manumekāgramāsīnamabhigamya maharṣayaḥ  |
pratipūjya yathānyāyamidaṃ vacanamabruvan  || 1.1 ||
bhagavan sarvavarṇānāṃ yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  |
antaraprabhavānāṃ ca dharmānno vaktumarhasi  || 1.2 ||
tvameko hyasya sarvasya vidhānasya svayaṃbhuvaḥ  |
acintyasyāprameyasya kāryatattvārthavitprabho  || 1.3 ||
sa taiḥ pṛṣṭastathā samyagamitaujā mahātmabhiḥ  |
pratyuvācārcya tān sarvānmaharṣīñśrūyatāmiti  || 1.4 ||
āsīdidaṃ tamobhūtamaprajñātamalakṣaṇam  |
apratarkyamavijñeyaṃ prasuptamiva sarvataḥ  || 1.5 ||
tataḥ svayaṃbhūrbhagavānavyakto vyañjayannidam  |
mahābhūtādi vṛttaujāḥ prādurāsīttamonudaḥ  || 1.6 ||
yo'sāvatīndriyagrāhyaḥ sūkṣmo'vyaktaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
sarvabhūtamayo'cintyaḥ sa eva svayamudbabhau  || 1.7 ||
so'bhidhyāya śarīrātsvātsisṛkṣurvividhāḥ prajāḥ  |
apa eva sasarjādau tāsu vīryamavāsṛjat || 1.8 ||
tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham  |
tasmiñjajñe svayaṃ brahmā sarvalokapitāmahaḥ  || 1.9 ||
āpo narā iti proktā āpo vai narasūnavaḥ  |
tā yadasyāyanaṃ pūrvaṃ tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 1.10 ||
yattatkāraṇamavyaktaṃ nityaṃ sadasadātmakaṃ  |
tadvisṛṣṭaḥ sa puruṣo loke brahmeti kīrtyate  || 1.11 ||
tasminnaṇḍe sa bhagavānuṣitvā parivatsaram  |
svayamevātmano dhyānāttadaṇḍamakaroddvidhā  || 1.12 ||
tābhyāṃ sa śakalābhyāṃ ca divaṃ bhūmiṃ ca nirmame  |
madhye vyoma diśaścāṣṭāvapāṃ sthānaṃ ca śāśvataṃ  || 1.13 ||
udbabarhātmanaścaiva manaḥ sadasadātmakam  |
manasaścāpyahaṃkāramabhimantāramīśvaram  || 1.14 ||
mahāntameva cātmānaṃ sarvāṇi triguṇāni ca  |
viṣayāṇāṃ grahītṝṇi śanaiḥ pañcendriyāṇi ca  || 1.15 ||
teṣāṃ tvavayavān sūkṣmān ṣaṇṇāmapyamitaujasām  |
saṃniveśyātmamātrāsu sarvabhūtāni nirmame  || 1.16 ||
yanmūrtyavayavāḥ sūkṣmāstānīmānyāśrayanti ṣaṭ |
tasmāccharīramityāhustasya mūrtiṃ manīṣiṇaḥ  || 1.17 ||
tadāviśanti bhūtāni mahānti saha karmabhiḥ  |
manaścāvayavaiḥ sūkṣmaiḥ sarvabhūtakṛdavyayam  || 1.18 ||
teṣāmidaṃ tu saptānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ mahaujasām  |
sūkṣmābhyo mūrtimātrābhyaḥ saṃbhavatyavyayādvyayam  || 1.19 ||
ādyādyasya guṇaṃ tveṣāmavāpnoti paraḥ paraḥ  |
yo yo yāvatithaścaiṣāṃ sa sa tāvadguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 1.20 ||
sarveṣāṃ tu sa nāmāni karmāṇi ca pṛthakpṛthak |
vedaśabdebhya evādau pṛthaksaṃsthāśca nirmame  || 1.21 ||
karmātmanāṃ ca devānāṃ so'sṛjatprāṇināṃ prabhuḥ  |
sādhyānāṃ ca gaṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ yajñaṃ caiva sanātanam  || 1.22 ||
agnivāyuravibhyastu trayaṃ brahma sanātanam  |
dudoha yajñasiddhyarthamṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇam  || 1.23 ||
kālaṃ kālavibhaktīśca nakṣatrāṇi grahāṃstathā  |
saritaḥ sāgarāñśailān samāni viṣamāni ca  || 1.24 ||
tapo vācaṃ ratiṃ caiva kāmaṃ ca krodhameva ca  |
sṛṣṭiṃ sasarja caivemāṃ sraṣṭumicchannimāḥ prajāḥ  || 1.25 ||
karmaṇāṃ ca vivekārthaṃ dharmādharmau vyavecayat |
dvandvairayojayaccemāḥ sukhaduḥkhādibhiḥ prajāḥ  || 1.26 ||
aṇvyo mātrā vināśinyo daśārdhānāṃ tu yāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
tābhiḥ sārdhamidaṃ sarvaṃ saṃbhavatyanupūrvaśaḥ  || 1.27 ||
yaṃ tu karmaṇi yasmin sa nyayuṅkta prathamaṃ prabhuḥ  |
sa tadeva svayaṃ bheje sṛjyamānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  || 1.28 ||
hiṃsrāhiṃsre mṛdukrūre dharmādharmāvṛtānṛte  |
yadyasya so'dadhātsarge tattasya svayamāviśat || 1.29 ||
yathā rtuliṅgānyṛtavaḥ svayameva rtuparyaye  |
svāni svānyabhipadyante tathā karmāṇi dehinaḥ  || 1.30 ||
lokānāṃ tu vivṛddhyarthaṃ mukhabāhūrupādataḥ  |
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ ca niravartayat || 1.31 ||
dvidhā kṛtvātmano dehamardhena puruṣo'bhavat |
ardhena nārī tasyāṃ sa virājamasṛjatprabhuḥ  || 1.32 ||
tapastaptvāsṛjadyaṃ tu sa svayaṃ puruṣo virāṭ |
taṃ māṃ vittāsya sarvasya sraṣṭāraṃ dvijasattamāḥ  || 1.33 ||
ahaṃ prajāḥ sisṛkṣustu tapastaptvā suduścaram  |
patīn prajānāmasṛjaṃ maharṣīnādito daśa  || 1.34 ||
marīcimatryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum  |
pracetasaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca bhṛguṃ nāradameva ca  || 1.35 ||
ete manūṃstu saptān yānasṛjan bhūritejasaḥ  |
devān devanikāyāṃśca maharṣīṃścāmitaujasaḥ  || 1.36 ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācāṃśca gandharvāpsaraso'surān  |
nāgān sarpān suparṇāṃśca pitṝṇāṃśca pṛthaggaṇam  || 1.37 ||
vidyuto'śanimeghāṃśca rohitendradhanūṃṣi ca  |
ulkānirghātaketūṃśca jyotīṃṣyuccāvacāni ca  || 1.38 ||
kinnarān vānarānmatsyān vividhāṃśca vihaṅgamān  |
paśūnmṛgānmanuṣyāṃśca vyālāṃścobhayatodataḥ  || 1.39 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgāṃśca yūkāmakṣikamatkuṇam  |
sarvaṃ ca daṃśamaśakaṃ sthāvaraṃ ca pṛthagvidham  || 1.40 ||
evametairidaṃ sarvaṃ manniyogānmahātmabhiḥ  |
yathākarma tapoyogātsṛṣṭaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam  || 1.41 ||
yeṣāṃ tu yādṛṣaṃ karma bhūtānāmiha kīrtitam  |
tattathā vo'bhidhāsyāmi kramayogaṃ ca janmani  || 1.42 ||
paśavaśca mṛgāścaiva vyālāścobhayatodataḥ  |
rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāśca manuṣyāśca jarāyujāḥ  || 1.43 ||
aṇḍājāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ sarpā nakrā matsyāśca kacchapāḥ  |
yāni caivam: prakārāṇi sthalajānyaudakāni ca  || 1.44 ||
svedajaṃ daṃśamaśakaṃ yūkāmakṣikamatkuṇam  |
ūṣmaṇaścopajāyante yaccānyatkiṃ cidīdṛṣam  || 1.45 ||
udbhijjāḥ sthāvarāḥ sarve bījakāṇḍaprarohiṇaḥ  |
oṣadhyaḥ phalapākāntā bahupuṣpaphalopagāḥ  || 1.46 ||
apuṣpāḥ phalavanto ye te vanaspatayaḥ smṛtāḥ  |
puṣpiṇaḥ phalinaścaiva vṛkṣāstūbhayataḥ smṛtāḥ  || 1.47 ||
gucchagulmaṃ tu vividhaṃ tathaiva tṛṇajātayaḥ  |
bījakāṇḍaruhāṇyeva pratānā vallya eva ca  || 1.48 ||
tamasā bahurūpeṇa veṣṭitāḥ karmahetunā  |
antaḥsaṃjñā bhavantyete sukhaduḥkhasamanvitāḥ  || 1.49 ||
etadantāstu gatayo brahmādyāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ  |
ghore'smin bhūtasaṃsāre nityaṃ satatayāyini  || 1.50 ||
evaṃ sarvaṃ sa sṛṣṭvedaṃ māṃ cācintyaparākramaḥ  |
ātmanyantardadhe bhūyaḥ kālaṃ kālena pīḍayan  || 1.51 ||
yadā sa devo jāgarti tadevaṃ ceṣṭate jagat |
yadā svapiti śāntātmā tadā sarvaṃ nimīlati  || 1.52 ||
tasmin svapiti tu svasthe karmātmānaḥ śarīriṇaḥ  |
svakarmabhyo nivartante manaśca glānimṛcchati  || 1.53 ||
yugapattu pralīyante yadā tasminmahātmani  |
tadāyaṃ sarvabhūtātmā sukhaṃ svapiti nirvṛtaḥ  || 1.54 ||
tamo'yaṃ tu samāśritya ciraṃ tiṣṭhati sendriyaḥ  |
na ca svaṃ kurute karma tadotkrāmati mūrtitaḥ  || 1.55 ||
yadāṇumātriko bhūtvā bījaṃ sthāsnu cariṣṇu ca  |
samāviśati saṃsṛṣṭastadā mūrtiṃ vimuñcati  || 1.56 ||
evaṃ sa jāgratsvapnābhyāmidaṃ sarvaṃ carācaram  |
saṃjīvayati cājasraṃ pramāpayati cāvyayaḥ  || 1.57 ||
idaṃ śāstraṃ tu kṛtvāsau māmeva svayamāditaḥ  |
vidhivadgrāhayāmāsa marīcyādīṃstvahaṃ munīn  || 1.58 ||
etadvo'yaṃ bhṛguḥ śāstraṃ śrāvayiṣyatyaśesataḥ  |
etaddhi matto'dhijage sarvameṣo'khilaṃ muniḥ  || 1.59 ||
tatastathā sa tenokto maharṣimanunā bhṛguḥ  |
tānabravīdṛṣīn sarvān prītātmā śrūyatāmiti  || 1.60 ||
svāyaṃbhuvasyāsya manoḥ ṣaḍvaṃśyā manavo'pare  |
sṛṣṭavantaḥ prajāḥ svāḥ svā mahātmāno mahaujasaḥ  || 1.61 ||
svārociṣaścottamaśca tāmaso raivatastathā  |
cākṣuṣaśca mahātejā vivasvatsuta eva ca  || 1.62 ||
svāyaṃbhuvādyāḥ saptaite manavo bhūritejasaḥ  |
sve sve'ntare sarvamidamutpādyāpuścarācaram  || 1.63 ||
nimeṣā daśa cāṣṭau ca kāṣṭhā triṃśattu tāḥ kalā  |
triṃśatkalā muhūrtaḥ syādahorātraṃ tu tāvataḥ  || 1.64 ||
ahorātre vibhajate sūryo mānuṣadaivike  |
rātriḥ svapnāya bhūtānāṃ ceṣṭāyai karmaṇāmahaḥ  || 1.65 ||
pitrye rātryahanī māsaḥ pravibhāgastu pakṣayoḥ  |
karmaceṣṭāsvahaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śuklaḥ svapnāya śarvarī  || 1.66 ||
daive rātryahanī varṣaṃ pravibhāgastayoḥ punaḥ  |
ahastatrodagayanaṃ rātriḥ syāddakṣiṇāyanam  || 1.67 ||
brāhmasya tu kṣapāhasya yatpramāṇaṃ samāsataḥ  |
ekaikaśo yugānāṃ tu kramaśastannibodhata  || 1.68 ||
catvāryāhuḥ sahasrāṇi varsāṇāṃ tatkṛtaṃ yugam  |
tasya tāvacchatī saṃdhyā saṃdhyāṃśaśca tathāvidhaḥ  || 1.69 ||
itareṣu sasaṃdhyeṣu sasaṃdhyāṃśeṣu ca triṣu  |
ekāpāyena vartante sahasrāṇi śatāni ca   || 1.70 ||
yadetatparisaṃkhyātamādāveva caturyugam  |
etaddvādaśasāhasraṃ devānāṃ yugamucyate  || 1.71 ||
daivikānāṃ yugānāṃ tu sahasraṃ parisaṃkhyayā  |
brāhmamekamaharjñeyaṃ tāvatīṃ rātrimeva ca  || 1.72 ||
tadvai yugasahasrāntaṃ brāhmaṃ puṇyamaharviduḥ  |
rātriṃ ca tāvatīmeva te'horātravido janāḥ  || 1.73 ||
tasya so'harniśasyānte prasuptaḥ pratibudhyate  |
pratibuddhaśca sṛjati manaḥ sadasadātmakam  || 1.74 ||
manaḥ sṛṣṭiṃ vikurute codyamānaṃ sisṛkṣayā  |
ākāśaṃ jāyate tasmāttasya śabdaṃ guṇaṃ viduḥ  || 1.75 ||
ākāśāttu vikurvāṇātsarvagandhavahaḥ śuciḥ  |
balavāñjāyate vāyuḥ sa vai sparśaguṇo mataḥ  || 1.76 ||
vāyorapi vikurvāṇādvirociṣṇu tamonudam  |
jyotirutpadyate bhāsvattadrūpaguṇamucyate  || 1.77 ||
jyotiṣaśca vikurvāṇādāpo rasaguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
adbhyo gandhaguṇā bhūmirityeṣā sṛṣṭirāditaḥ  || 1.78 ||
yadprāgdvādaśasāhasramuditaṃ daivikaṃ yugam  |
tadekasaptatiguṇaṃ manvantaramihocyate  || 1.79 ||
manvantarāṇyasaṃkhyāni sargaḥ saṃhāra eva ca  |
krīḍannivaitatkurute parameṣṭhī punaḥ punaḥ  || 1.80 ||
catuṣpātsakalo dharmaḥ satyaṃ caiva kṛte yuge  |
nādharmeṇāgamaḥ kaścinmanuṣyān prati vartate  || 1.81 ||
itareṣvāgamāddharmaḥ pādaśastvavaropitaḥ  |
caurikānṛtamāyābhirdharmaścāpaiti pādaśaḥ  || 1.82 ||
arogāḥ sarvasiddhārthāścaturvarṣaśatāyuṣaḥ  |
kṛte tretādiṣu hyeṣāmāyurhrasati pādaśaḥ  || 1.83 ||
vedoktamāyurmartyānāmāśiṣaścaiva karmaṇām  |
phalantyanuyugaṃ loke prabhāvaśca śarīriṇām  || 1.84 ||
anye kṛtayuge dharmāstretāyāṃ dvāpare'pare  |
anye kaliyuge nṝṇāṃ yugahrāsānurūpataḥ  || 1.85 ||
tapaḥ paraṃ kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ jñānamucyate  |
dvāpare yajñamevāhurdānamekaṃ kalau yuge  || 1.86 ||
sarvasyāsya tu sargasya guptyarthaṃ sa mahādyutiḥ  |
mukhabāhūrupajjānāṃ pṛthakkarmāṇyakalpayat || 1.87 ||
adhyāpanamadhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā  |
dānaṃ pratigrahaṃ caiva brāhmaṇānāmakalpayat || 1.88 ||
prajānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānamijyādhyayanameva ca  |
viṣayeṣvaprasaktiśca kṣatriyasya samāsataḥ  || 1.89 ||
paśūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ dānamijyādhyayanameva ca  |
vaṇikpathaṃ kusīdaṃ ca vaiśyasya kṛṣimeva ca  || 1.90 ||
ekameva tu śūdrasya prabhuḥ karma samādiśat |
eteṣāmeva varṇānāṃ śuśrūṣāmanasūyayā  || 1.91 ||
ūrdhvaṃ nābhermedhyataraḥ puruṣaḥ parikīrtitaḥ  |
tasmānmedhyatamaṃ tvasya mukhamuktaṃ svayaṃbhuvā  || 1.92 ||
uttamāṅgodbhavājjyeṣṭhyādbrahmaṇaścaiva dhāraṇāt |
sarvasyaivāsya sargasya dharmato brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ  || 1.93 ||
taṃ hi svayaṃbhūḥ svādāsyāttapastaptvādito'sṛjat |
havyakavyābhivāhyāya sarvasyāsya ca guptaye  || 1.94 ||
yasyāsyena sadāśnanti havyāni tridivaukasaḥ  |
kavyāni caiva pitaraḥ kiṃ bhūtamadhikaṃ tataḥ  || 1.95 ||
bhūtānāṃ prāṇinaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ prāṇināṃ buddhijīvinaḥ  |
buddhimatsu narāḥ śreṣṭhā nareṣu brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 1.96 ||
brāhmaṇeṣu ca vidvāṃso vidvatsu kṛtabuddhayaḥ  |
kṛtabuddhiṣu kartāraḥ kartṛṣu brahmavedinaḥ  || 1.97 ||
utpattireva viprasya mūrtirdharmasya śāśvatī  |
sa hi dharmārthamutpanno brahmabhūyāya kalpate  || 1.98 ||
brāhmaṇo jāyamāno hi pṛthivyāmadhijāyate  |
īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ dharmakośasya guptaye  || 1.99 ||
sarvaṃ svaṃ brāhmaṇasyedaṃ yatkiṃ cijjagatīgataṃ  |
śraiṣṭhyenābhijanenedaṃ sarvaṃ vai brāhmaṇo'rhati  || 1.100 ||
svameva brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte svaṃ vaste svaṃ dadāti ca  |
ānṛśaṃsyādbrāhmaṇasya bhuñjate hītare janāḥ  || 1.101 ||
tasya karmavivekārthaṃ śeṣāṇāmanupūrvaśaḥ  |
svāyaṃbhuvo manurdhīmānidaṃ śāstramakalpayat || 1.102 ||
viduṣā brāhmaṇenedamadhyetavyaṃ prayatnataḥ  |
śiśyebhyaśca pravaktavyaṃ samyaṅnānyena kena cit || 1.103 ||
idaṃ śāstramadhīyāno brāhmaṇaḥ śaṃsitavrataḥ  |
manovāggehajairnityaṃ karmadoṣairna lipyate  || 1.104 ||
punāti paṅktiṃ vaṃśyāṃśca saptasapta parāvarān  |
pṛthivīmapi caivemāṃ kṛtsnāmeko'pi so'rhati  || 1.105 ||
idaṃ svastyayanaṃ śreṣṭhamidaṃ buddhivivardhanam  |
idaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyamidaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ param  || 1.106 ||
asmin dharmo'khilenokto guṇadoṣau ca karmaṇām  |
caturṇāmapi varṇānāmācāraścaiva śāśvataḥ  || 1.107 ||
ācāraḥ paramo dharmaḥ śrutyuktaḥ smārta eva ca  |
tasmādasmin sadā yukto nityaṃ syādātmavān dvijaḥ  || 1.108 ||
ācārādvicyuto vipro na vedaphalamaśnute  |
ācāreṇa tu saṃyuktaḥ sampūrṇaphalabhājbhavet || 1.109 ||
evamācārato dṛṣṭvā dharmasya munayo gatiṃ  |
sarvasya tapaso mūlamācāraṃ jagṛhuḥ param  || 1.110 ||
jagataśca samutpattiṃ saṃskāravidhimeva ca   |
vratacaryopacāraṃ ca snānasya ca paraṃ vidhim  || 1.111 ||
dārādhigamanaṃ caiva vivāhānāṃ ca lakṣaṇam  |
mahāyajñavidhānaṃ ca śrāddhakalpaṃ ca śāśvatam  || 1.112 ||
vṛttīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ caiva snātakasya vratāni ca  |
bhakṣyābhakṣyaṃ ca śaucaṃ ca dravyāṇāṃ śuddhimeva ca  || 1.113 ||
strīdharmayogaṃ tāpasyaṃ mokṣaṃ saṃnyāsameva ca  |
rājñaśca dharmamakhilaṃ kāryāṇāṃ ca vinirṇayam  || 1.114 ||
sākṣipraśnavidhānaṃ ca dharmaṃ strīpuṃsayorapi  |
vibhāgadharmaṃ dyūtaṃ ca kaṇṭakānāṃ ca śodhanam  || 1.115 ||
vaiśyaśūdropacāraṃ ca saṃkīrṇānāṃ ca saṃbhavam  |
āpaddharmaṃ ca varṇānāṃ prāyaścittavidhiṃ tathā  || 1.116 ||
saṃsāragamanaṃ caiva trividhaṃ karmasaṃbhavam  |
niḥśreyasaṃ karmaṇāṃ ca guṇadoṣaparīkṣaṇam  || 1.117 ||
deśadharmāñjātidharmān kuladharmāṃśca śāśvatān  |
pāṣaṇḍagaṇadharmāṃśca śāstre'sminnuktavānmanuḥ  || 1.118 ||
yathedamuktavāñśāstraṃ purā pṛṣṭo manurmayā  |
tathedaṃ yūyamapyadya matsakāśānnibodhata  || 1.119 ||

vidvadbhiḥ sevitaḥ sadbhirnityamadveṣarāgibhiḥ  |
hṛdayenābhyanujñāto yo dharmastaṃ nibodhata  || 2.1 ||
kāmātmatā na praśastā na caivehāstyakāmatā  |
kāmyo hi vedādhigamaḥ karmayogaśca vaidikaḥ  || 2.2 ||
saṃkalpamūlaḥ kāmo vai yajñāḥ saṃkalpasaṃbhavāḥ  |
vratāni yamadharmāśca sarve saṃkalpajāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 2.3 ||
akāmasya kriyā kā ciddṛśyate neha karhi cit |
yadyaddhi kurute kiṃ cittattatkāmasya ceṣṭitam  || 2.4 ||
teṣu samyagvartamāno gacchatyamaralokatām  |
yathā saṃkalpitāṃśceha sarvān kāmān samaśnute  || 2.5 ||
vedo'khilo dharmamūlaṃ smṛtiśīle ca tadvidām  |
ācāraścaiva sādhūnāmātmanastuṣṭireva ca  || 2.6 ||
yaḥ kaścitkasya ciddharmo manunā parikīrtitaḥ  |
sa sarvo'bhihito vede sarvajñānamayo hi saḥ  || 2.7 ||
sarvaṃ tu samavekṣyedaṃ nikhilaṃ jñānacakṣuṣā  |
śrutiprāmāṇyato vidvān svadharme niviśeta vai  || 2.8 ||
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmamanutiṣṭhan hi mānavaḥ  |
iha kīrtimavāpnoti pretya cānuttamaṃ sukham  || 2.9 ||
śrutistu vedo vijñeyo dharmaśāstraṃ tu vai smṛtiḥ  |
te sarvārtheṣvamīmāṃsye tābhyāṃ dharmo hi nirbabhau  || 2.10 ||
yo'vamanyeta te mūle hetuśāstrāśrayāddvijaḥ  |
sa sādhubhirbahiṣkāryo nāstiko vedanindakaḥ  || 2.11 ||
vedaḥ smṛtiḥ sadācāraḥ svasya ca priyamātmanaḥ  |
etaccaturvidhaṃ prāhuḥ sākṣāddharmasya lakṣaṇam  || 2.12 ||
arthakāmeṣvasaktānāṃ dharmajñānaṃ vidhīyate  |
dharmaṃ jijñāsamānānāṃ pramāṇaṃ paramaṃ śrutiḥ  || 2.13 ||
śrutidvaidhaṃ tu yatra syāttatra dharmāvubhau smṛtau  |
ubhāvapi hi tau dharmau samyaguktau manīṣibhiḥ  || 2.14 ||
udite'nudite caiva samayādhyuṣite tathā  |
sarvathā vartate yajña itīyaṃ vaidikī śrutiḥ  || 2.15 ||
niṣekādiśmaśānānto mantrairyasyodito vidhiḥ  |
tasya śāstre'dhikāro'smiñjñeyo nānyasya kasya cit || 2.16 ||
sarasvatīdṛśadvatyordevanadyoryadantaram  |
taṃ devanirmitaṃ deśaṃ brahmāvartaṃ pracakṣate  || 2.17 ||
tasmin deśe ya ācāraḥ pāramparyakramāgataḥ  |
varṇānāṃ sāntarālānāṃ sa sadācāra ucyate  || 2.18 ||
kurukṣetraṃ ca matsyāśca pañcālāḥ śūrasenakāḥ  |
eṣa brahmarṣideśo vai brahmāvartādanantaraḥ  || 2.19 ||
etaddeśaprasūtasya sakāśādagrajanmanaḥ  |
svaṃ svaṃ caritraṃ śikṣeran pṛthivyāṃ sarvamānavāḥ  || 2.20 ||
himavadvindhyayormadhyaṃ yatprāgvinaśanādapi  |
pratyageva prayāgācca madhyadeśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 2.21 ||
ā samudrāttu vai pūrvādā samudrācca paścimāt |
tayorevāntaraṃ giryorāryāvartaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 2.22 ||
kṛṣṇasārastu carati mṛgo yatra svabhāvataḥ  |
sa jñeyo yajñiyo deśo mlecchadeśastvataḥ paraḥ  || 2.23 ||
etāṇdvijātayo deśān saṃśrayeran prayatnataḥ  |
śūdrastu yasmin kasmin vā nivasedvṛttikarśitaḥ  || 2.24 ||
eṣā dharmasya vo yoniḥ samāsena prakīrtitā  |
saṃbhavaścāsya sarvasya varṇadharmānnibodhata  || 2.25 ||
vaidikaiḥ karmabhiḥ puṇyairniṣekādirdvijanmanām  |
kāryaḥ śarīrasaṃskāraḥ pāvanaḥ pretya ceha ca  || 2.26 ||
gārbhairhomairjātakarma- cauḍamauñjīnibandhanaiḥ  |
baijikaṃ gārbhikaṃ caino dvijānāmapamṛjyate  || 2.27 ||
svādhyāyena vratairhomaistraividyenejyayā sutaiḥ  |
mahāyajñaiśca yajñaiśca brāhmīyaṃ kriyate tanuḥ  || 2.28 ||
prāṅnābhivardhanātpuṃso jātakarma vidhīyate  |
mantravatprāśanaṃ cāsya hiraṇyamadhusarpiṣām  || 2.29 ||
nāmadheyaṃ daśamyāṃ tu dvādaśyāṃ vāsya kārayet |
puṇye tithau muhūrte vā nakṣatre vā guṇānvite  || 2.30 ||
maṅgalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya syātkṣatriyasya balānvitam  |
vaiśyasya dhanasaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya tu jugupsitam  || 2.31 ||
śarmavadbrāhmaṇasya syādrājño rakṣāsamanvitam  |
vaiśyasya puṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya preṣyasaṃyutam  || 2.32 ||
strīṇāṃ sukhodyamakrūraṃ vispaṣṭārthaṃ manoharam  |
maṅgalyaṃ dīrghavarṇāntamāśīrvādābhidhānavat || 2.33 ||
caturthe māsi kartavyaṃ śiśorniṣkramaṇaṃ gṛhāt |
ṣaṣṭhe'nnaprāśanaṃ māsi yadveṣṭaṃ maṅgalaṃ kule  || 2.34 ||
cūḍākarma dvijātīnāṃ sarveṣāmeva dharmataḥ  |
prathame'bde tṛtīye vā kartavyaṃ śruticodanāt || 2.35 ||
garbhāṣṭame'bde kurvīta brāhmaṇasyopanāyanam  |
garbhādekādaśe rājño garbhāttu dvādaśe viśaḥ  || 2.36 ||
brahmavarcasakāmasya kāryo viprasya pañcame  |
rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyehārthino'ṣṭame  || 2.37 ||
ā ṣodaśādbrāhmaṇasya sāvitrī nātivartate  |
ā dvāviṃśātkṣatrabandhorā caturviṃśaterviśaḥ  || 2.38 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ trayo'pyete yathākālamasaṃskṛtāḥ  |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā bhavantyāryavigarhitāḥ  || 2.39 ||
naitairapūtairvidhivadāpadyapi hi karhi cit |
brāhmān yaunāṃśca saṃbandhānnācaredbrāhmaṇaḥ saha  || 2.40 ||
kārṣṇarauravabāstāni carmāṇi brahmacāriṇaḥ  |
vasīrannānupūrvyeṇa śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca  || 2.41 ||
mauñjī trivṛtsamā ślakṣṇā kāryā viprasya mekhalā  |
kṣatriyasya tu maurvī jyā vaiśyasya śaṇatāntavī  || 2.42 ||
muñjālābhe tu kartavyāḥ kuśāśmantakabalvajaiḥ  |
trivṛtā granthinaikena tribhiḥ pañcabhireva vā  || 2.43 ||
kārpāsamupavītaṃ syādviprasyordhvavṛtaṃ trivṛt |
śaṇasūtramayaṃ rājño vaiśyasyāvikasautrikam  || 2.44 ||
brāhmaṇo bailvapālāśau kṣatriyo vāṭakhādirau  |
pailavāudumbarau vaiśyo daṇḍānarhanti dharmataḥ  || 2.45 ||
keśāntiko brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ pramāṇataḥ  |
lalāṭasammito rājñaḥ syāttu nāsāntiko viśaḥ  || 2.46 ||
ṛjavaste tu sarve syuravraṇāḥ saumyadarśanāḥ  |
anudvegakarā nṝṇāṃ satvaco'nagnidūṣitāḥ  || 2.47 ||
pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍamupasthāya ca bhāskaram  |
pradakṣiṇaṃ parītyāgniṃ caredbhaikṣaṃ yathāvidhi  || 2.48 ||
bhavatpūrvaṃ caredbhaikṣamupanīto dvijottamaḥ  |
bhavanmadhyaṃ tu rājanyo vaiśyastu bhavaduttaram  || 2.49 ||
mātaraṃ vā svasāraṃ vā māturvā bhaginīṃ nijām  |
bhikṣeta bhikṣāṃ prathamaṃ yā cainaṃ nāvamānayet || 2.50 ||
samāhṛtya tu tadbhaikṣaṃ yāvadannamamāyayā  |
nivedya gurave'śnīyādācamya prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ  || 2.51 ||
āyuṣyaṃ prāṅmukho bhuṅkte yaśasyaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  |
śriyaṃ pratyaṅmukho bhuṅkte ṛtaṃ bhuṅkte hyudaṅmukhaḥ  || 2.52 ||
upaspṛśya dvijo nityamannamadyātsamāhitaḥ  |
bhuktvā copaspṛśetsamyagadbhiḥ khāni ca saṃspṛśet || 2.53 ||
pūjayedaśanaṃ nityamadyāccaitadakutsayan  |
dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyetprasīdecca pratinandecca sarvaśaḥ  || 2.54 ||
pūjitaṃ hyaśanaṃ nityaṃ balamūrjaṃ ca yacchati  |
apūjitaṃ tu tadbhuktamubhayaṃ nāśayedidam  || 2.55 ||
nocchiṣṭaṃ kasya ciddadyānnādyādetattathāntarā  |
na caivātyaśanaṃ kuryānna cocchiṣṭaḥ kva cidvrajet || 2.56 ||
anārogyamanāyuṣyamasvargyaṃ cātibhojanam  |
apuṇyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ tasmāttatparivarjayet || 2.57 ||
brāhmeṇa viprastīrthena nityakālamupaspṛśet |
kāyatraidaśikābhyāṃ vā na pitryeṇa kadā cana  || 2.58 ||
aṅguṣṭhamūlasya tale brāhmaṃ tīrthaṃ pracakṣate  |
kāyamaṅgulimūle'gre devaṃ pitryaṃ tayoradhaḥ  || 2.59 ||
trirācāmedapaḥ pūrvaṃ dviḥ pramṛjyāttato mukham  |
khāni caiva spṛśedadbhirātmānaṃ śira eva ca  || 2.60 ||
anuṣṇābhiraphenābhiradbhistīrthena dharmavit |
śaucepsuḥ sarvadācāmedekānte prāgudaṅmukhaḥ  || 2.61 ||
hṛdgābhiḥ pūyate vipraḥ kaṇṭhagābhistu bhūmipaḥ  |
vaiśyo'dbhiḥ prāśitābhistu śūdraḥ spṛṣṭābhirantataḥ  || 2.62 ||
uddhṛte dakṣine pāṇāvupavītyucyate dvijaḥ  |
savye prācīnāvītī nivītī kaṇṭhasajjane  || 2.63 ||
mekhalāmajinaṃ daṇḍamupavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum  |
apsu prāsya vinaṣṭāni gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat || 2.64 ||
keśāntaḥ ṣoḍaśe varṣe brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate  |
rājanyabandhordvāviṃśe vaiśyasya dvyadhike mataḥ  || 2.65 ||
amantrikā tu kāryeyaṃ strīṇāmāvṛdaśeṣataḥ  |
saṃskārārthaṃ śarīrasya yathākālaṃ yathākramam  || 2.66 ||
vaivāhiko vidhiḥ strīṇāṃ saṃskāro vaidikaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
patisevā gurau vāso gṛhārtho'gniparikriyā  || 2.67 ||
eṣa prokto dvijātīnāmaupanāyaniko vidhiḥ  |
utpattivyañjakaḥ puṇyaḥ karmayogaṃ nibodhata  || 2.68 ||
upanīya guruḥ śiṣyaṃ śikṣayecchaucamāditaḥ  |
ācāramagnikāryaṃ ca saṃdhyopāsanameva ca  || 2.69 ||
adhyeṣyamāṇastvācānto yathāśāstramudaṅmukhaḥ  |
brahmāñjalikṛto'dhyāpyo laghuvāsā jitendriyaḥ  || 2.70 ||
brahmārambhe'vasāne ca pādau grāhyau guroḥ sadā  |
saṃhatya hastāvadhyeyaṃ sa hi brahmāñjaliḥ smṛtaḥ  || 2.71 ||
vyatyastapāṇinā kāryamupasaṃgrahaṇaṃ guroḥ  |
savyena savyaḥ spraṣṭavyo dakṣiṇena ca dakṣiṇaḥ  || 2.72 ||
adhyeṣyamāṇaṃ tu gururnityakālamatandritaḥ  |
adhīṣva bho iti brūyādvirāmo'stviti cāramet || 2.73 ||
brahmanaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryādādāvante ca sarvadā  |
sravatyanoṃkṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ parastācca viśīryati  || 2.74 ||
prākkūlān paryupāsīnaḥ pavitraiścaiva pāvitaḥ  |
prāṇāyāmaistribhiḥ pūtastata oṃkāramarhati  || 2.75 ||
akāraṃ cāpyukāraṃ ca makāraṃ ca prajāpatiḥ  |
vedatrayānniraduhadbhūrbhuvaḥ svaritīti ca  || 2.76 ||
tribhya eva tu vedebhyaḥ pādaṃ pādamadūduhat |
tadityṛco'syāḥ sāvitryāḥ parameṣṭhī prajāpatiḥ  || 2.77 ||
etadakṣarametāṃ ca japan vyāhṛtipūrvikām  |
saṃdhyayorvedavidvipro vedapuṇyena yujyate  || 2.78 ||
sahasrakṛtvastvabhyasya bahiretattrikaṃ dvijaḥ  |
mahato'pyenaso māsāttvacevāhirvimucyate  || 2.79 ||
etayā rcā visaṃyuktaḥ kāle ca kriyayā svayā  |
brahmakṣatriyaviśyonirgarhaṇāṃ yāti sādhuṣu  || 2.80 ||
oṃkārapūrvikāstisro mahāvyāhṛtayo'vyayāḥ  |
tripadā caiva sāvitrī vijñeyaṃ brahmaṇo mukham  || 2.81 ||
yo'dhīte'hanyahanyetāṃ trīṇi varṣāṇyatandritaḥ  |
sa brahma paramabhyeti vāyubhūtaḥ khamūrtimān  || 2.82 ||
ekākṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma prāṇāyāmaḥ paraṃ tapaḥ  |
sāvitryāstu paraṃ nāsti maunātsatyaṃ viśiṣyate  || 2.83 ||
kṣaranti sarvā vaidikyo juhotiyajatikriyāḥ  |
akṣaraṃ duṣkaraṃ jñeyaṃ brahma caiva prajāpatiḥ  || 2.84 ||
vidhiyajñājjapayajño viśiṣṭo daśabhirguṇaiḥ  |
upāṃśuḥ syācchataguṇaḥ sāhasro mānasaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 2.85 ||
ye pākayajñāscatvāro vidhiyajñasamanvitāḥ  |
sarve te japayajñasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm  || 2.86 ||
japyenaiva tu saṃsidhyedbrāhmaṇo nātra saṃśayaḥ  |
kuryādanyanna vā kuryānmaitro brāhmaṇa ucyate  || 2.87 ||
indriyāṇāṃ vicaratāṃ viṣayeṣvapahāriṣu  |
saṃyame yatnamātiṣṭhedvidvān yanteva vājinām  || 2.88 ||
ekādaśendriyāṇyāhuryāni pūrve manīṣiṇaḥ  |
tāni samyakpravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  || 2.89 ||
śrotraṃ tvakcakṣuṣī jihvā nāsikā caiva pañcamī  |
pāyūpasthaṃ hastapādaṃ vākcaiva daśamī smṛtā  || 2.90 ||
buddhīndriyāṇi pañcaiṣāṃ śrotrādīnyanupūrvaśaḥ  |
karmendriyāṇi pañcaiṣāṃ pāyvādīni pracakṣate  || 2.91 ||
ekādaśaṃ mano jñeyaṃ svaguṇenobhayātmakam  |
yasmin jite jitāvetau bhavataḥ pañcakau gaṇau  || 2.92 ||
indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgena doṣamṛcchatyasaṃśayam  |
saṃniyamya tu tānyeva tataḥ siddhiṃ nigacchati  || 2.93 ||
na jātu kāmaḥ kāmānāmupabhogena śāmyati  |
haviṣā kṛṣṇavartmeva bhūya evābhivardhate  || 2.94 ||
yaścaitān prāpnuyātsarvān yaścaitān kevalāṃstyajet |
prāpaṇātsarvakāmānāṃ parityāgo viśiṣyate  || 2.95 ||
na tathaitāni śakyante saṃniyantumasevayā  |
viṣayeṣu prajuṣṭāni yathā jñānena nityaśaḥ  || 2.96 ||
vedāstyāgaśca yajñāśca niyamāśca tapāṃsi ca  |
na vipraduṣṭabhāvasya siddhiṃ gacchati karhi cit || 2.97 ||
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ  |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ  || 2.98 ||
indriyāṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ yadyekaṃ kṣaratīndriyam  |
tenāsya kṣarati prajñā dṛteḥ pādādivodakam  || 2.99 ||
vaśe kṛtvendriyagrāmaṃ saṃyamya ca manastathā  |
sarvān saṃsādhayedarthānakṣiṇvan yogatastanum  || 2.100 ||
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japāṃstiṣṭhetsāvitrīmārkadarśanāt |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīnaḥ samyagṛkṣavibhāvanāt || 2.101 ||
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃstiṣṭhannaiśameno vyapohati  |
paścimāṃ tu samāsīno malaṃ hanti divākṛtam  || 2.102 ||
na tiṣṭhati tu yaḥ pūrvāṃ nopāste yaśca paścimām  |
sa śūdravadbahiṣkāryaḥ sarvasmāddvijakarmaṇaḥ  || 2.103 ||
apāṃ samīpe niyato naityakaṃ vidhimāsthitaḥ  |
sāvitrīmapyadhīyīta gatvāraṇyaṃ samāhitaḥ  || 2.104 ||
vedopakaraṇe caiva svādhyāye caiva naityake  |
nānurodho'styanadhyāye homamantreṣu caiva hi  || 2.105 ||
naityake nāstyanadhyāyo brahmasattraṃ hi tatsmṛtam  |
brahmāhutihutaṃ puṇyamanadhyāyavaṣaṭkṛtam  || 2.106 ||
yaḥ svādhyāyamadhīte'bdaṃ vidhinā niyataḥ śuciḥ  |
tasya nityaṃ kṣaratyeṣa payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu  || 2.107 ||
agnīndhanaṃ bhaikṣacaryāmadhaḥśayyāṃ gurorhitam  |
ā samāvartanātkuryātkṛtopanayano dvijaḥ  || 2.108 ||
ācāryaputraḥ śuśrūṣurjñānado dhārmikaḥ śuciḥ  |
āptaḥ śakto'rthadaḥ sādhuḥ svo'dhyāpyā daśa dharmataḥ  || 2.109 ||
nāpṛṣṭaḥ kasya cidbrūyānna cānyāyena pṛcchataḥ  |
jānannapi hi medhāvī jaḍavalloka ācaret || 2.110 ||
adharmeṇa ca yaḥ prāha yaścādharmeṇa pṛcchati  |
tayoranyataraḥ praiti vidveṣaṃ vādhigacchati  || 2.111 ||
dharmārthau yatra na syātāṃ śuśrūṣā vāpi tadvidhā  |
tatra vidyā na vaptavyā śubhaṃ bījamivoṣare  || 2.112 ||
vidyayaiva samaṃ kāmaṃ martavyaṃ brahmavādinā  |
āpadyapi hi ghorāyāṃ na tvenāmiriṇe vapet || 2.113 ||
vidyā brāhmaṇametyāha śevadhiste'smi rakṣa mām  |
asūyakāya māṃ mādāstathā syāṃ vīryavattamā  || 2.114 ||
yameva tu śuciṃ vidyānniyatabrahmacāriṇam  |
tasmai māṃ brūhi viprāya nidhipāyāpramādine  || 2.115 ||
brahma yastvananujñātamadhīyānādavāpnuyāt |
sa brahmasteyasaṃyukto narakaṃ pratipadyate  || 2.116 ||
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ vāpi tathādhyātmikameva vā  |
ādadīta yato jñānaṃ taṃ pūrvamabhivādayet || 2.117 ||
sāvitrīmātrasāro'pi varaṃ vipraḥ suyantritaḥ  |
nāyantritastrivedo'pi sarvāśī sarvavikrayī  || 2.118 ||
śayyāsane'dhyācarite śreyasā na samāviśet |
śayyāsanasthaścaivainaṃ pratyutthāyābhivādayet || 2.119 ||
ūrdhvaṃ prāṇā hyutkrāmanti yūnaḥ sthavira āyati  |
pratyutthānābhivādābhyāṃ punastān pratipadyate  || 2.120 ||
abhivādanaśīlasya nityaṃ vṛddhopasevinaḥ  |
catvāri tasya vardhante āyurdharmo yaśo balam  || 2.121 ||
abhivādātparaṃ vipro jyāyāṃsamabhivādayan  |
asau nāmāhamasmīti svaṃ nāma parikīrtayet || 2.122 ||
nāmadheyasya ye ke cidabhivādaṃ na jānate  |
tān prājño'hamiti brūyātstriyaḥ sarvāstathaiva ca  || 2.123 ||
bhoḥśabdaṃ kīrtayedante svasya nāmno'bhivādane  |
nāmnāṃ svarūpabhāvo hi bhobhāva ṛṣibhiḥ smṛtaḥ  || 2.124 ||
āyuṣmān bhava saumyeti vācyo vipro'bhivādane  |
akāraścāsya nāmno'nte vācyaḥ pūrvākṣaraḥ plutaḥ  || 2.125 ||
yo na vettyabhivādasya vipraḥ pratyabhivādanam  |
nābhivādyaḥ sa viduṣā yathā śūdrastathaiva saḥ  || 2.126 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchetkṣatrabandhumanāmayam  |
vaiśyaṃ kṣemaṃ samāgamya śūdramārogyameva ca  || 2.127 ||
avācyo dīkṣito nāmnā yavīyānapi yo bhavet |
bhobhavatpūrvakaṃ tvenamabhibhāṣeta dharmavit || 2.128 ||
parapatnī tu yā strī syādasaṃbandhā ca yonitaḥ  |
tāṃ brūyādbhavatītyevaṃ subhage bhaginīti ca  || 2.129 ||
mātulāṃśca pitṛvyāṃśca śvaśurānṛtvijo gurūn  |
asāvahamiti brūyātpratyutthāya yavīyasaḥ  || 2.130 ||
mātṛśvasā mātulānī śvaśrūratha pitṛśvasā  |
saṃpūjyā gurupatnīvatsamāstā gurubhāryayā  || 2.131 ||
bhrāturbhāryopasaṃgrāhyā savarṇāhanyahanyapi  |
viproṣya tūpasaṃgrāhyā jñātisaṃbandhiyoṣitaḥ  || 2.132 ||
piturbhaginyāṃ mātuśca jyāyasyāṃ ca svasaryapi  |
mātṛvadvṛttimātiṣṭhenmātā tābhyo garīyasī  || 2.133 ||
daśābdākhyaṃ paurasakhyaṃ pañcābdākhyaṃ kalābhṛtām  |
tryabdapūrvaṃ śrotriyāṇāṃ svalpenāpi svayoniṣu  || 2.134 ||
brāhmaṇaṃ daśavarṣaṃ tu śatavarṣaṃ tu bhūmipam  |
pitāputrau vijānīyādbrāhmaṇastu tayoḥ pitā  || 2.135 ||
vittaṃ bandhurvayaḥ karma vidyā bhavati pañcamī  |
etāni mānyasthānāni garīyo yadyaduttaram  || 2.136 ||
pañcānāṃ triṣu varṇeṣu bhūyāṃsi guṇavanti ca  |
yatra syuḥ so'tra mānārhaḥ śūdro'pi daśamīṃ gataḥ  || 2.137 ||
cakriṇo daśamīsthasya rogiṇo bhāriṇaḥ striyāḥ  |
snātakasya ca rājñaśca panthā deyo varasya ca  || 2.138 ||
teṣāṃ tu samāvetānāṃ mānyau snātakapārthivau  |
rājasnātakayoścaiva snātako nṛpamānabhāk || 2.139 ||
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedamadhyāpayeddvijaḥ  |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tamācāryaṃ pracakṣate  || 2.140 ||
ekadeśaṃ tu vedasya vedāṅgānyapi vā punaḥ  |
yo'dhyāpayati vṛttyarthamupādhyāyaḥ sa ucyate  || 2.141 ||
niṣekādīni karmāṇi yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi  |
saṃbhāvayati cānnena sa vipro gururucyate  || 2.142 ||
agnyādheyaṃ pākayajñānagniṣṭomādikānmakhān  |
yaḥ karoti vṛto yasya sa tasya rtvigihocyate  || 2.143 ||
ya āvṛṇotyavitathaṃ brahmaṇā śravaṇāvubhau  |
sa mātā sa pitā jñeyastaṃ na druhyetkadā cana  || 2.144 ||
upādhyāyān daśācārya ācāryāṇāṃ śataṃ pitā  |
sahasraṃ tu pitṝnmātā gauraveṇātiricyate  || 2.145 ||
utpādakabrahmadātrorgarīyān brahmadaḥ pitā  |
brahmajanma hi viprasya pretya ceha ca śāśvatam  || 2.146 ||
kāmānmātā pitā cainaṃ yadutpādayato mithaḥ  |
saṃbhūtiṃ tasya tāṃ vidyādyadyonāvabhijāyate  || 2.147 ||
ācāryastvasya yāṃ jātiṃ vidhivadvedapāragaḥ  |
utpādayati sāvitryā sā satyā sājarāmarā  || 2.148 ||
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ  |
tamapīha guruṃ vidyācchrutopakriyayā tayā  || 2.149 ||
brāhmasya janmanaḥ kartā svadharmasya ca śāsitā  |
bālo'pi vipro vṛddhasya pitā bhavati dharmataḥ  || 2.150 ||
adhyāpayāmāsa pitṝn śiśurāṅgirasaḥ kaviḥ  |
putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān  || 2.151 ||
te tamarthamapṛcchanta devānāgatamanyavaḥ  |
devāścaitān sametyocurnyāyyaṃ vaḥ śiśuruktavān  || 2.152 ||
ajño bhavati vai bālaḥ pitā bhavati mantradaḥ  |
ajñaṃ hi bālamityāhuḥ pitetyeva tu mantradam  || 2.153 ||
na hāyanairna palitairna vittena na bandhubhiḥ  |
ṛṣayaścakrire dharmaṃ yo'nūcānaḥ sa no mahān  || 2.154 ||
viprāṇāṃ jñānato jyaiṣṭhyaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu vīryataḥ  |
vaiśyānāṃ dhānyadhanataḥ śūdrāṇāmeva janmataḥ  || 2.155 ||
na tena vṛddho bhavati yenāsya palitaṃ śiraḥ  |
yo vai yuvāpyadhīyānastaṃ devāḥ sthaviraṃ viduḥ  || 2.156 ||
yathā kāṣṭhamayo hastī yathā carmamayo mṛgaḥ  |
yaśca vipro'nadhīyānastrayaste nāma bibhrati  || 2.157 ||
yathā ṣaṇḍho'phalaḥ strīṣu yathā gaurgavi cāphalā  |
yathā cājñe'phalaṃ dānaṃ tathā vipro'nṛco'phalaḥ  || 2.158 ||
ahiṃsayaiva bhūtānāṃ kāryaṃ śreyo'nuśāsanam  |
vākcaiva madhurā ślakṣṇā prayojyā dharmamicchatā  || 2.159 ||
yasya vāṅganasī śuddhe samyaggupte ca sarvadā  |
sa vai sarvamavāpnoti vedāntopagataṃ phalam  || 2.160 ||
nāruṃtudaḥ syādārto'pi na paradrohakarmadhīḥ  |
yayāsyodvijate vācā nālokyāṃ tāmudīrayet || 2.161 ||
sammānādbrāhmaṇo nityamudvijeta viṣādiva  |
amṛtasyeva cākāṅkṣedavamānasya sarvadā  || 2.162 ||
sukhaṃ hyavamataḥ śete sukhaṃ ca pratibudhyate  |
sukhaṃ carati loke'sminnavamantā vinaśyati  || 2.163 ||
anena kramayogena saṃskṛtātmā dvijaḥ śanaiḥ  |
gurau vasan sañcinuyādbrahmādhigamikaṃ tapaḥ  || 2.164 ||
tapoviśeṣairvividhairvrataiśca vidhicoditaiḥ  |
vedaḥ kṛtsno'dhigantavyaḥ sarahasyo dvijanmanā  || 2.165 ||
vedameva sadābhyasyettapastapsyan dvijottamaḥ  |
vedābhyāso hi viprasya tapaḥ paramihocyate  || 2.166 ||
ā haiva sa nakhāgrebhyaḥ paramaṃ tapyate tapaḥ  |
yaḥ sragvyapi dvijo'dhīte svādhyāyaṃ śaktito'nvaham  || 2.167 ||
yo'nadhītya dvijo vedamanyatra kurute śramam  |
sa jīvanneva śūdratvamāśu gacchati sānvayaḥ  || 2.168 ||
māturagre'dhijananaṃ dvitīyaṃ mauñjibandhane  |
tṛtīyaṃ yajñadīkṣāyāṃ dvijasya śruticodanāt || 2.169 ||
tatra yadbrahmajanmāsya mauñjībandhanacihnitam  |
tatrāsya mātā sāvitrī pitā tvācārya ucyate  || 2.170 ||
vedapradānādācāryaṃ pitaraṃ paricakṣate  |
na hyasmin yujyate karma kiñcidā mauñjibandhanāt || 2.171 ||
nābhivyāhārayedbrahma svadhāninayanādṛte  |
śūdreṇa hi samastāvadyāvadvede na jāyate  || 2.172 ||
kṛtopanayanasyāsya vratādeśanamiṣyate  |
brahmaṇo grahaṇaṃ caiva krameṇa vidhipūrvakam  || 2.173 ||
yadyasya vihitaṃ carma yatsūtraṃ yā ca mekhalā  |
yo daṇḍo yacca vasanaṃ tattadasya vrateṣvapi  || 2.174 ||
sevetemāṃstu niyamān brahmacārī gurau vasan  |
sanniyamyendriyagrāmaṃ tapovṛddhyarthamātmanaḥ  || 2.175 ||
nityaṃ snātvā śuciḥ kuryāddevarṣipitṛtarpaṇam  |
devatābhyarcanaṃ caiva samidādhānameva ca  || 2.176 ||
varjayenmadhu māṃsaṃ ca gandhaṃ mālyaṃ rasān striyaḥ  |
śuktāni yāni sarvāṇi prāṇināṃ caiva hiṃsanam  || 2.177 ||
abhyaṅgamañjanaṃ cākṣṇorupānacchatradhāraṇam  |
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca nartanaṃ gītavādanam  || 2.178 ||
dyūtaṃ ca janavādaṃ ca parivādaṃ tathānṛtam  |
strīṇāṃ ca prekṣaṇālambhamupaghātaṃ parasya ca  || 2.179 ||
ekaḥ śayīta sarvatra na retaḥ skandayetkva cit |
kāmāddhi skandayan reto hinasti vratamātmanaḥ  || 2.180 ||
svapne siktvā brahmacārī dvijaḥ śukramakāmataḥ  |
snātvārkamarcayitvā triḥ punarmāmityṛcaṃ japet || 2.181 ||
udakumbhaṃ sumanaso gośakṛnmṛttikākuśān  |
āharedyāvadarthāni bhaikṣaṃ cāharahaścaret || 2.182 ||
vedayajñairahīnānāṃ praśastānāṃ svakarmasu  |
brahmacāryāharedbhaikṣaṃ gṛhebhyaḥ prayato'nvaham  || 2.183 ||
guroḥ kule na bhikṣeta na jñātikulabandhuṣu  |
alābhe tvanyagehānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ vivarjayet || 2.184 ||
sarvaṃ vāpi caredgrāmaṃ pūrvoktānāmasaṃbhave  |
niyamya prayato vācamabhiśastāṃstu varjayet || 2.185 ||
dūrādāhṛtya samidhaḥ sannidadhyādvihāyasi  |
sāyam: prātaśca juhuyāttābhiragnimatandritaḥ  || 2.186 ||
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇamasamidhya ca pāvakaṃ  |
anāturaḥ saptarātramavakīrṇivrataṃ caret || 2.187 ||
bhaikṣeṇa vartayennityaṃ naikānnādī bhavedvratī  |
bhaikṣeṇa vratino vṛttirupavāsasamā smṛtā  || 2.188 ||
vratavaddevadaivatye pitrye karmaṇyatha rṣivat |
kāmamabhyarthito'śnīyādvratamasya na lupyate  || 2.189 ||
brāhmaṇasyaiva karmaitadupadiṣṭaṃ manīṣibhiḥ  |
rājanyavaiśyayostvevaṃ naitatkarma vidhīyate  || 2.190 ||
codito guruṇā nityamapracodita eva vā  |
kuryādadhyayane yatnamācāryasya hiteṣu ca  || 2.191 ||
śarīraṃ caiva vācaṃ ca buddhīndriyamanāṃsi ca  |
niyamya prāñjalistiṣṭhedvīkṣamāṇo gurormukham  || 2.192 ||
nityamuddhṛtapāṇiḥ syātsādhvācāraḥ susaṃvṛtaḥ  |
āsyatāmiti coktaḥ sannāsītābhimukhaṃ guroḥ  || 2.193 ||
hīnānnavastraveṣaḥ syātsarvadā gurusannidhau  |
uttiṣṭhetprathamaṃ cāsya caramaṃ caiva saṃviśet || 2.194 ||
pratiśrāvaṇasaṃbhāṣe śayāno na samācaret |
nāsīno na ca bhuñjāno na tiṣṭhanna parāṅmukhaḥ  || 2.195 ||
āsīnasya sthitaḥ kuryādabhigacchaṃstu tiṣṭhataḥ  |
pratyudgamya tvāvrajataḥ paścāddhāvaṃstu dhāvataḥ  || 2.196 ||
parāṅmukhasyābhimukho dūrasthasyaitya cāntikam  |
praṇamya tu śayānasya nideśe caiva tiṣṭhataḥ  || 2.197 ||
nīcaṃ śayyāsanaṃ cāsya nityaṃ syādgurusannidhau  |
gurostu cakṣurviṣaye na yatheṣṭāsano bhavet || 2.198 ||
nodāharedasya nāma parokṣamapi kevalam  |
na caivāsyānukurvīta gatibhāṣitaceṣṭitam  || 2.199 ||
guroryatra parivādo nindā vāpi pravartate  |
karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato'nyataḥ  || 2.200 ||
parīvādātkharo bhavati śvā vai bhavati nindakaḥ  |
paribhoktā kṛmirbhavati kīṭo bhavati matsarī  || 2.201 ||
dūrastho nārcayedenaṃ na kruddho nāntike striyāḥ  |
yānāsanasthaścaivainamavaruhyābhivādayet || 2.202 ||
prativāte'nuvāte ca nāsīta guruṇā saha  |
asaṃśrave caiva gurorna kiṃ cidapi kīrtayet || 2.203 ||
go'śvoṣṭrayānaprāsāda- prastareṣu kaṭeṣu ca  |
āsīta guruṇā sārdhaṃ śilāphalakanauṣu ca  || 2.204 ||
gurorgurau sannihite guruvadvṛttimācaret |
na cānisṛṣṭo guruṇā svān gurūnabhivādayet || 2.205 ||
vidyāguruṣvevameva nityā vṛttiḥ svayoniṣu  |
pratiṣedhatsu cādharmāddhitaṃ copadiśatsvapi  || 2.206 ||
śreyaḥsu guruvadvṛttiṃ nityameva samācaret |
guruputreṣu cāryeṣu guroścaiva svabandhuṣu  || 2.207 ||
bālaḥ samānajanmā vā śiṣyo vā yajñakarmaṇi  |
adhyāpayan gurusuto guruvanmānamarhati  || 2.208 ||
utsādanaṃ ca gātrāṇāṃ snāpanocchiṣṭabhojane  |
na kuryādguruputrasya pādayoścāvanejanam  || 2.209 ||
guruvatpratipūjyāḥ syuḥ savarṇā guruyoṣitaḥ  |
asavarṇāstu sampūjyāḥ pratyutthānābhivādanaiḥ  || 2.210 ||
abhyañjanaṃ snāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanameva ca  |
gurupatnyā na kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam  || 2.211 ||
gurupatnī tu yuvatirnābhivādyeha pādayoḥ  |
pūrṇaviṃśativarṣeṇa guṇadoṣau vijānatā  || 2.212 ||
svabhāva eṣa nārīṇāṃ narāṇāmiha dūṣaṇam  |
ato'rthānna pramādyanti pramadāsu vipaścitaḥ  || 2.213 ||
avidvāṃsamalaṃ loke vidvāṃsamapi vā punaḥ  |
pramadā hyutpathaṃ netuṃ kāmakrodhavaśānugam  || 2.214 ||
mātrā svasrā duhitrā vā na viviktāsano bhavet |
balavānindriyagrāmo vidvāṃsamapi karṣati  || 2.215 ||
kāmaṃ tu gurupatnīnāṃ yuvatīnāṃ yuvā bhuvi  |
vidhivadvandanaṃ kuryādasāvahamiti bruvan  || 2.216 ||
viproṣya pādagrahaṇamanvahaṃ cābhivādanam  |
gurudāreṣu kurvīta satāṃ dharmamanusmaran  || 2.217 ||
yathā khanan khanitreṇa naro vāryadhigacchati  |
tathā gurugatāṃ vidyāṃ śuśrūṣuradhigacchati  || 2.218 ||
muṇḍo vā jaṭilo vā syādatha vā syācchikhājaṭaḥ  |
nainaṃ grāme'bhinimlocetsūryo nābhyudiyātkva cit || 2.219 ||
taṃ cedabhyudiyātsūryaḥ śayānaṃ kāmacārataḥ  |
nimlocedvāpyavijñānājjapannupavaseddinam  || 2.220 ||
sūryeṇa hyabhinirmuktaḥ śayāno'bhyuditaśca yaḥ  |
prāyaścittamakurvāṇo yuktaḥ syānmahatainasā  || 2.221 ||
ācamya prayato nityamubhe saṃdhye samāhitaḥ  |
śucau deśe japañjapyamupāsīta yathāvidhi  || 2.222 ||
yadi strī yadyavarajaḥ śreyaḥ kiṃ citsamācaret |
tatsarvamācaredyukto yatra cāsya ramenmanaḥ  || 2.223 ||
dharmārthāvucyate śreyaḥ kāmārthau dharma eva ca  |
artha eveha vā śreyastrivarga iti tu sthitiḥ  || 2.224 ||
ācāryaśca pitā caiva mātā bhrātā ca pūrvajaḥ  |
nārtenāpyavamantavyā brāhmaṇena viśeṣataḥ  || 2.225 ||
ācāryo brahmaṇo mūrtiḥ pitā mūrtiḥ prajāpateḥ  |
mātā pṛthivyā mūrtistu bhrātā svo mūrtirātmanaḥ  || 2.226 ||
yaṃ mātāpitarau kleśaṃ sahete saṃbhave nṛṇām  |
na tasya niṣkṛtiḥ śakyā kartuṃ varṣaśatairapi  || 2.227 ||
tayornityaṃ priyaṃ kuryādācāryasya ca sarvadā  |
teṣveva triṣu tuṣṭeṣu tapaḥ sarvaṃ samāpyate  || 2.228 ||
teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ śuśrūṣā paramaṃ tapa ucyate  |
na tairanabhyanujñāto dharmamanyaṃ samācaret || 2.229 ||
ta eva hi trayo lokāsta eva traya āśramāḥ  |
ta eva hi trayo vedāsta evoktāstrayo'gnayaḥ  || 2.230 ||
pitā vai gārhapatyo'gnirmātāgnirdakṣiṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
gururāhavanīyastu sāgnitretā garīyasī  || 2.231 ||
triṣvapramādyanneteṣu trīn lokān vijayedgṛhī  |
dīpyamānaḥ svavapuṣā devavaddivi modate  || 2.232 ||
imaṃ lokaṃ mātṛbhaktyā pitṛbhaktyā tu madhyamam  |
guruśuśrūṣayā tvevaṃ brahmalokaṃ samaśnute  || 2.233 ||
sarve tasyādṛtā dharmā yasyaite traya ādṛtāḥ  |
anādṛtāstu yasyaite sarvāstasyāphalāḥ kriyāḥ  || 2.234 ||
yāvattrayaste jīveyustāvannānyaṃ samācaret |
teṣveva nityaṃ śuśrūṣāṃ kuryātpriyahite rataḥ  || 2.235 ||
teṣāmanuparodhena pāratryaṃ yadyadācaret |
tattannivedayettebhyo manovacanakarmabhiḥ  || 2.236 ||
triṣveteṣvitikṛtyaṃ hi puruṣasya samāpyate  |
eṣa dharmaḥ paraḥ sākṣādupadharmo'nya ucyate  || 2.237 ||
śraddadhānaḥ śubhāṃ vidyāmādadītāvarādapi  |
anyādapi paraṃ dharmaṃ strīratnaṃ duṣkulādapi  || 2.238 ||
viṣādapyamṛtaṃ grāhyaṃ bālādapi subhāṣitam  |
amitrādapi sadvṛttamamedhyādapi kāñcanam  || 2.239 ||
striyo ratnānyatho vidyā dharmaḥ śaucaṃ subhāṣitam  |
vividhāni ca śīlpāni samādeyāni sarvataḥ  || 2.240 ||
abrāhmaṇādadhyāyanamāpatkāle vidhīyate  |
anuvrajyā ca śuśrūṣā yāvadadhyāyanaṃ guroḥ  || 2.241 ||
nābrāhmaṇe gurau śiṣyo vāsamātyantikaṃ vaset |
brāhmaṇe vānanūcāne kāṅkṣan gatimanuttamām  || 2.242 ||
yadi tvātyantikaṃ vāsaṃ rocayeta guroḥ kule  |
yuktaḥ paricaredenamā śarīravimokṣaṇāt || 2.243 ||
ā samāpteḥ śarīrasya yastu śuśrūṣate gurum  |
sa gacchatyañjasā vipro brahmaṇaḥ sadma śāśvatam  || 2.244 ||
na pūrvaṃ gurave kiṃ cidupakurvīta dharmavit |
snāsyaṃstu guruṇājñaptaḥ śaktyā gurvarthamāharet || 2.245 ||
kṣetraṃ hiraṇyaṃ gāmaśvaṃ chatropānahamāsanam  |
dhānyaṃ śākaṃ ca vāsāṃsi gurave prītimāvahet || 2.246 ||
ācārye tu khalu prete guruputre guṇānvite  |
gurudāre sapiṇḍe vā guruvadvṛttimācaret || 2.247 ||
eteṣvavidyamāneṣu sthānāsanavihāravān  |
prayuñjāno'gniśuśrūṣāṃ sādhayeddehamātmanaḥ  || 2.248 ||
evaṃ carati yo vipro brahmacaryamaviplutaḥ  |
sa gacchatyuttamasthānaṃ na ceha jāyate punaḥ  || 2.249 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśadābdikaṃ caryaṃ gurau traivedikaṃ vratam  |
tadardhikaṃ pādikaṃ vā grahaṇāntikameva vā  || 3.1 ||
vedānadhītya vedau vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam  |
aviplutabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśramamāvaset || 3.2 ||
taṃ pratītaṃ svadharmeṇa brahmadāyaharaṃ pituḥ  |
sragviṇaṃ talpa āsīnamarhayetprathamaṃ gavā  || 3.3 ||
guruṇānumataḥ snātvā samāvṛtto yathāvidhi  |
udvaheta dvijo bhāryāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām  || 3.4 ||
asapiṇḍā ca yā māturasagotrā ca yā pituḥ  |
sā praśastā dvijātīnāṃ dārakarmaṇi maithune  || 3.5 ||
mahāntyapi samṛddhāni go'jāvidhanadhānyataḥ  |
strīsaṃbandhe daśaitāni kulāni parivarjayet || 3.6 ||
hīnakriyaṃ niṣpuruṣaṃ niśchando romaśārśasam  |
kṣayāmayāvyapasmāri- śvitrikuṣṭhikulāni ca  || 3.7 ||
nodvahetkapilāṃ kanyāṃ nādhikāṅgīṃ na rogiṇīm  |
nālomikāṃ nātilomāṃ na vācāṭāṃ na piṅgalām  || 3.8 ||
na rkṣavṛkṣanadīnāmnīṃ nāntyaparvatanāmikām  |
na pakṣyahipreṣyanāmnīṃ na ca bhīṣananāmikām  || 3.9 ||
avyaṅgāṅgīṃ saumyanāmnīṃ haṃsavāraṇagāminīm  |
tanulomakeśadaśanāṃ mṛdvaṅgīmudvahetstriyam  || 3.10 ||
yasyāstu na bhavedbhrātā na vijñāyeta vā pitā  |
nopayaccheta tāṃ prājñaḥ putrikādharmaśaṅkayā  || 3.11 ||
savarṇāgre dvijātīnāṃ praśastā dārakarmaṇi  |
kāmatastu pravṛttānāmimāḥ syuḥ kramaśo'varāḥ  || 3.12 ||
śūdraiva bhāryā śūdrasya sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛte  |
te ca svā caiva rājñaśca tāśca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ  || 3.13 ||
na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayorāpadyapi hi tiṣṭhatoḥ  |
kasmiṃścidapi vṛttānte śūdrā bhāryopadiśyate  || 3.14 ||
hīnajātistriyaṃ mohādudvahanto dvijātayaḥ  |
kulānyeva nayantyāśu sasantānāni śūdratām  || 3.15 ||
śūdrāvedī patatyatrerutathyatanayasya ca  |
śaunakasya sutotpattyā tadapatyatayā bhṛgoḥ  || 3.16 ||
śūdrāṃ śayanamāropya brāhmaṇo yātyadhogatim  |
janayitvā sutaṃ tasyāṃ brāhmaṇyādeva hīyate  || 3.17 ||
daivapitryātitheyāni tatpradhānāni yasya tu  |
nāśnanti pitṛdevāstanna ca svargaṃ sa gacchati  || 3.18 ||
vṛṣalīphenapītasya niḥśvāsopahatasya ca  |
tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasya niṣkṛtirna vidhīyate  || 3.19 ||
caturṇāmapi varṇānaṃ pretya ceha hitāhitān  |
aṣṭāvimān samāsena strīvivāhānnibodhata  || 3.20 ||
brāhmo daivastathaivārṣaḥ prājāpatyastathāsuraḥ  |
gāndharvo rākṣasaścaiva paiśācaścāṣṭamo'dhamaḥ  || 3.21 ||
yo yasya dharmyo varṇasya guṇadoṣau ca yasya yau  |
tadvaḥ sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi prasave ca guṇāguṇān  || 3.22 ||
ṣaḍānupūrvyā viprasya kṣatrasya caturo'varān  |
viṭśūdrayostu tāneva vidyāddharmyānarākṣasān  || 3.23 ||
caturo brāhmaṇasyādyān praśastān kavayo viduḥ  |
rākṣasaṃ kṣatriyasyaikamāsuraṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ  || 3.24 ||
pañcānāṃ tu trayo dharmyā dvāvadharmyau smṛtāviha  |
paiśācaścāsuraścaiva na kartavyau kadā cana  || 3.25 ||
pṛthakpṛthagvā miśrau vā vivāhau pūrvacoditau  |
gāndharvo rākṣasaścaiva dharmyau kṣatrasya tau smṛtau  || 3.26 ||
ācchādya cārcayitvā ca śrutaśīlavate svayam  |
āhūya dānaṃ kanyāyā brāhmo dharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ  || 3.27 ||
yajñe tu vitate samyagṛtvije karma kurvate  |
alaṅkṛtya sutādānaṃ daivaṃ dharmaṃ pracakṣate  || 3.28 ||
ekaṃ gomithunaṃ dve vā varādādāya dharmataḥ  |
kanyāpradānaṃ vidhivadārṣo dharmaḥ sa ucyate  || 3.29 ||
sahobhau caratāṃ dharmamiti vācānubhāṣya ca  |
kanyāpradānamabhyarcya prājāpatyo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ  || 3.30 ||
jñātibhyo draviṇaṃ dattvā kanyāyai caiva śaktitaḥ  |
kanyāpradānaṃ svācchandyādāsuro dharma ucyate  || 3.31 ||
icchayānyonyasaṃyogaḥ kanyāyāśca varasya ca  |
gāndharvaḥ sa tu vijñeyo maithunyaḥ kāmasaṃbhavaḥ  || 3.32 ||
hatvā chittvā ca bhittvā ca krośantīṃ rudantīṃ gṛhāt |
prasahya kanyāharaṇaṃ rākṣaso vidhirucyate  || 3.33 ||
suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ vā raho yatropagacchati  |
sa pāpiṣṭho vivāhānāṃ paiśācaścāṣṭamo'dhamaḥ  || 3.34 ||
adbhireva dvijāgryāṇāṃ kanyādānaṃ viśiṣyate  |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāmitaretarakāmyayā  || 3.35 ||
yo yasyaiṣāṃ vivāhānāṃ manunā kīrtito guṇaḥ  |
sarvaṃ śṛṇuta taṃ viprāḥ sarvaṃ kīrtayato mama  || 3.36 ||
daśa pūrvān parān vaṃśyānātmānaṃ caikaviṃśakam  |
brāhmīputraḥ sukṛtakṛnmocayatyenasaḥ pitṝn  || 3.37 ||
daivoḍhājaḥ sutaścaiva sapta sapta parāvarān  |
ārṣoḍhājaḥ sutastrīṃstrīn ṣaṭṣaṭkāyoḍhajaḥ sutaḥ  || 3.38 ||
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu caturṣvevānupūrvaśaḥ  |
brahmavarcasvinaḥ putrā jāyante śiṣṭasammatāḥ  || 3.39 ||
rūpasattvaguṇopetā dhanavanto yaśasvinaḥ  |
paryāptabhogā dharmiṣṭhā jīvanti ca śataṃ samāḥ  || 3.40 ||
itareṣu tu śiṣṭeṣu nṛśaṃsānṛtavādinaḥ  |
jāyante durvivāheṣu brahmadharmadviṣaḥ sutāḥ  || 3.41 ||
aninditaiḥ strīvivāhairanindyā bhavati prajā  |
ninditairninditā nṝṇāṃ tasmānnindyān vivarjayet || 3.42 ||
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskāraḥ savarṇāsūpadiśyate  |
asavarṇāsvayaṃ jñeyo vidhirudvāhakarmaṇi  || 3.43 ||
śaraḥ kṣatriyayā grāhyaḥ pratodo vaiśyakanyayā  |
vasanasya daśā grāhyā śūdrayotkṛṣṭavedane  || 3.44 ||
ṛtukālābhigāmī syātsvadāranirataḥ sadā  |
parvavarjaṃ vrajeccaināṃ tadvrato ratikāmyayā  || 3.45 ||
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ  |
caturbhiritaraiḥ sārdhamahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ  || 3.46 ||
tāsāmādyāścatasrastu ninditaikādaśī ca yā  |
trayodaśī ca śeṣāstu praśastā daśarātrayaḥ  || 3.47 ||
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo'yugmāsu rātriṣu  |
tasmādyugmāsu putrārthī saṃviśedārtave striyam  || 3.48 ||
pumān puṃso'dhike śukre strī bhavatyadhike striyāḥ  |
same'pumān puṃ striyau vā kṣīṇe'lpe ca viparyayaḥ  || 3.49 ||
nindyāsvaṣṭāsu cānyāsu striyo rātriṣu varjayan  |
brahmacāryeva bhavati yatra tatrāśrame vasan  || 3.50 ||
na kanyāyāḥ pitā vidvān gṛhṇīyācchulkamaṇvapi  |
gṛhṇañśulkaṃ hi lobhena syānnaro'patyavikrayī  || 3.51 ||
strīdhanāni tu ye mohādupajīvanti bāndhavāḥ  |
nārī yānāni vastraṃ vā te pāpā yāntyadhogatim  || 3.52 ||
ārṣe gomithunaṃ śulkaṃ ke cidāhurmṛṣaiva tat |
alpo'pyevaṃ mahān vāpi vikrayastāvadeva saḥ  || 3.53 ||
yāsāṃ nādadate śulkaṃ jñātayo na sa vikrayaḥ  |
arhaṇaṃ tatkumārīṇāmānṛśaṃsyaṃ ca kevalam  || 3.54 ||
pitṛbhirbhrātṛbhiścaitāḥ patibhirdevaraistathā  |
pūjyā bhūṣayitavyāśca bahukalyāṇamīpsubhiḥ  || 3.55 ||
yatra nāryastu pūjyante ramante tatra devatāḥ  |
yatraitāstu na pūjyante sarvāstatrāphalāḥ kriyāḥ  || 3.56 ||
śocanti jāmayo yatra vinaśyatyāśu tatkulam  |
na śocanti tu yatraitā vardhate taddhi sarvadā  || 3.57 ||
jāmayo yāni gehāni śapantyapratipūjitāḥ  |
tāni kṛtyāhatānīva vinaśyanti samantataḥ  || 3.58 ||
tasmādetāḥ sadā pūjyā bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ  |
bhūtikāmairnarairnityaṃ satkareṣūtsaveṣu ca  || 3.59 ||
saṃtuṣṭo bhāryayā bhartā bhartrā bhāryā tathaiva ca  |
yasminneva kule nityaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tatra vai dhruvam  || 3.60 ||
yadi hi strī na roceta pumāṃsaṃ na pramodayet |
apramodātpunaḥ puṃsaḥ prajanaṃ na pravartate  || 3.61 ||
striyāṃ tu rocamānāyāṃ sarvaṃ tadrocate kulaṃ  |
tasyāṃ tvarocamānāyāṃ sarvameva na rocate  || 3.62 ||
kuvivāhaiḥ kriyālopairvedānadhyayanena ca  |
kulānyakulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca  || 3.63 ||
śilpena vyavahāreṇa śūdrāpatyaiśca kevalaiḥ  |
gobhiraśvaiśca yānaiśca kṛṣyā rājopasevayā  || 3.64 ||
ayājyayājanaiścaiva nāstikyena ca karmaṇām  |
kulānyāśu vinaśyanti yāni hīnāni mantrataḥ  || 3.65 ||
mantratastu samṛddhāni kulānyalpadhanānyapi  |
kulasaṃkhyāṃ ca gacchanti karṣanti ca mahadyaśaḥ  || 3.66 ||
vaivāhike'gnau kurvīta gṛhyaṃ karma yathāvidhi  |
pañcayajñavidhānaṃ ca paktiṃ cānvāhikīṃ gṛhī  || 3.67[57 ] ||
pañca sūnā gṛhasthasya cullī peṣaṇyupaskaraḥ  |
kaṇḍanī codakumbhaśca badhyate yāstu vāhayan  || 3.68[58 ] ||
tāsāṃ krameṇa sarvāsāṃ niṣkṛtyarthaṃ maharṣibhiḥ  |
pañca kḷptā mahāyajñāḥ pratyahaṃ gṛhamedhinām  || 3.69[59 ] ||
adhyāpanaṃ brahmayajñaḥ pitṛyajñastu tarpaṇam  |
homo daivo balirbhauto nṛyajño'tithipūjanam  || 3.70[60 ] ||
pañcaitān yo mahāayajñānna hāpayati śaktitaḥ  |
sa gṛhe'pi vasannityaṃ sūnādoṣairna lipyate  || 3.71[61 ] ||
devatātithibhṛtyānāṃ pitṝṇāmātmanaśca yaḥ  |
na nirvapati pañcānāmucchvasanna sa jīvati  || 3.72[62 ] ||
ahutaṃ ca hutaṃ caiva tathā prahutameva ca  |
brāhmyaṃ hutaṃ prāśitaṃ ca pañcayajñān pracakṣate  || 3.73[63 ] ||
japo'huto huto homaḥ prahuto bhautiko baliḥ  |
brāhmyaṃ hutaṃ dvijāgryārcā prāśitaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam  || 3.74[64 ] ||
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syāddaive caiveha karmaṇi  |
daivakarmaṇi yukto hi bibhartīdaṃ carācaram  || 3.75[65 ] ||
agnau prāstāhutiḥ samyagādityamupatiṣṭhate  |
ādityājjāyate vṛṣṭirvṛṣṭerannaṃ tataḥ prajāḥ  || 3.76[66 ] ||
yathā vāyuṃ samāśritya vartante sarvajantavaḥ  |
tathā gṛhasthamāśritya vartante sarva āśramāḥ  || 3.77[67 ] ||
yasmāttrayo'pyāśramiṇo jñānenānnena cānvaham  |
gṛhasthenaiva dhāryante tasmājjyeṣṭhāśramo gṛhī  || 3.78[68 ] ||
sa saṃdhāryaḥ prayatnena svargamakṣayamicchatā  |
sukhaṃ cehecchatātyantaṃ yo'dhāryo durbalendriyaiḥ  || 3.79[69 ] ||
ṛṣayaḥ pitaro devā bhūtānyatithayastathā  |
āśāsate kuṭumbibhyastebhyaḥ kāryaṃ vijānatā  || 3.80[70 ] ||
svādhyāyenārcayeta rṣīn homairdevān yathāvidhi  |
pitṝñśrāddhaiśca nṝnannairbhūtāni balikarmaṇā  || 3.81[71 ] ||
kuryādaharahaḥ śrāddhamannādyenodakena vā  |
payomūlaphalairvāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītimāvahan  || 3.82[72 ] ||
ekamapyāśayedvipraṃ pitrarthe pāñcayajñike  |
na caivātrāśayetkiṃ cidvaiśvadevaṃ prati dvijam  || 3.83[73 ] ||
vaiśvadevasya siddhasya gṛhye'gnau vidhipūrvakam  |
ābhyaḥ kuryāddevatābhyo brāhmaṇo homamanvaham  || 3.84[74 ] ||
agneḥ somasya caivādau tayoścaiva samastayoḥ  |
viśvebhyaścaiva devebhyo dhanvantaraya eva ca  || 3.85[75 ] ||
kuhvai caivānumatyai ca prajāpataya eva ca   |
saha dyāvāpṛthivyośca tathā sviṣṭakṛte'ntataḥ  || 3.86[76 ] ||
evaṃ samyagghavirhutvā sarvadikṣu pradakṣiṇam  |
indrāntakāppatīndubhyaḥ sānugebhyo baliṃ haret || 3.87[77 ] ||
marudbhya iti tu dvāri kṣipedapsvadbhya ityapi  |
vanaspatibhya ityevaṃ musalolūkhale haret || 3.88[78 ] ||
ucchīrṣake śriyai kuryādbhadrakālyai ca pādataḥ  |
brahmavāstoṣpatibhyāṃ tu vāstumadhye baliṃ haret || 3.89[79 ] ||
viśvebhyaścaiva devebhyo balimākāśa utkṣipet |
divācarebhyo bhūtebhyo naktaṃcāribhya eva ca  || 3.90[80 ] ||
pṛṣṭhavāstuni kurvīta baliṃ sarvātmabhūtaye  |
pitṛbhyo baliśeṣaṃ tu sarvaṃ dakṣiṇato haret || 3.91[81 ] ||
śūnāṃ ca patitānāṃ ca śvapacāṃ pāparogiṇām  |
vayasānāṃ kṛmīṇāṃ ca śanakairnirvapedbhuvi  || 3.92[82 ] ||
evaṃ yaḥ sarvabhūtāni brāhmaṇo nityamarcati  |
sa gacchati paraṃ sthānaṃ tejomūrtiḥ pathā rjunā  || 3.93[83 ] ||
kṛtvaitadbalikarmaivamatithiṃ pūrvamāśayet |
bhikṣāṃ ca bhikṣave dadyādvidhivadbrahmacāriṇe  || 3.94[84 ] ||
yatpuṇyaphalamāpnoti gāṃ dattvā vidhivadguroḥ  |
tatpuṇyaphalamāpnoti bhikṣāṃ dattvā dvijo gṛhī  || 3.95[85 ] ||
bhikṣāmapyudapātraṃ vā satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam  |
vedatattvārthaviduṣe brāhmaṇāyopapādayet || 3.96[86 ] ||
naśyanti havyakavyāni narāṇāmavijānatām  |
bhasmībhūteṣu vipreṣu mohāddattāni dātṛbhiḥ  || 3.97[87 ] ||
vidyātapaḥsamṛddheṣu hutaṃ vipramukhāgniṣu  |
nistārayati durgācca mahataścaiva kilbiṣāt || 3.98[88 ] ||
saṃprāptāya tvatithaye pradadyādāsanodake  |
annaṃ caiva yathāśakti satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam  || 3.99[89 ] ||
śilānapyuñchato nityaṃ pañcāgnīnapi juhvataḥ  |
sarvaṃ sukṛtamādatte brāhmaṇo'narcito vasan  || 3.100[90 ] ||
tṛṇāni bhūmirudakaṃ vākcaturthī ca sūnṛtā  |
etānyapi satāṃ gehe nocchidyante kadā cana  || 3.101[91 ] ||
ekarātraṃ tu nivasannatithirbrāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāttasmādatithirucyate  || 3.102[92 ] ||
naikagrāmīṇamatithiṃ vipraṃ sāṅgatikaṃ tathā  |
upasthitaṃ gṛhe vidyādbhāryā yatrāgnayo'pi vā  || 3.103[93 ] ||
upāsate ye gṛhasthāḥ parapākamabuddhayaḥ  |
tena te pretya paśutāṃ vrajantyannādidāyinaḥ  || 3.104[94 ] ||
apraṇodyo'tithiḥ sāyaṃ sūryoḍho gṛhamedhinā  |
kāle prāptastvakāle vā nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset || 3.105[95 ] ||
na vai svayaṃ tadaśnīyādatithiṃ yanna bhojayet |
dhanyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ svargyaṃ vātithipūjanam  || 3.106[96 ] ||
āsanāvasathau śayyāmanuvrajyāmupāsanām  |
uttameṣūttamaṃ kuryāddhīne hīnaṃ same samam  || 3.107[97 ] ||
vaiśvadeve tu nirvṛtte yadyanyo'tithirāvrajet |
tasyāpyannaṃ yathāśakti pradadyānna baliṃ haret || 3.108[98 ] ||
na bhojanārthaṃ sve vipraḥ kulagotre nivedayet |
bhojanārthaṃ hi te śaṃsan vāntāśītyucyate budhaiḥ  || 3.109[99 ] ||
na brāhmaṇasya tvatithirgṛhe rājanya ucyate  |
vaiśyaśūdrau sakhā caiva jñātayo gurureva ca  || 3.110[100 ] ||
yadi tvatithidharmeṇa kṣatriyo gṛhamāvrajet |
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu kāmaṃ tamapi bhojayet || 3.111[101 ] ||
vaiśyaśūdrāvapi prāptau kuṭumbe'tithidharmiṇau  |
bhojayetsaha bhṛtyaistāvānṛśaṃsyaṃ prayojayan  || 3.112[102 ] ||
itarānapi sakhyādīn samprītyā gṛhamāgatān  |
prakṛtyānnaṃ yathāśakti bhojayetsaha bhāryayā  || 3.113[103 ] ||
suvāsinīḥ kumārīśca rogiṇo garbhiṇīḥ striyaḥ  |
atithibhyo'gra evaitān bhojayedavicārayan  || 3.114[104 ] ||
adattvā tu ya etebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte'vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
sa bhuñjāno na jānāti śvagṛdhrairjagdhimātmanaḥ  || 3.115[105 ] ||
bhuktavatsvatha vipreṣu sveṣu bhṛtyeṣu caiva hi  |
bhuñjīyātāṃ tataḥ paścādavaśiṣṭaṃ tu dampatī  || 3.116[106 ] ||
devānṛṣīnmanuṣyāṃśca pitṝn gṛhyāśca devatāḥ  |
pūjayitvā tataḥ paścādgṛhasthaḥ śeṣabhugbhavet || 3.117[107 ] ||
aghaṃ sa kevalaṃ bhuṅkte yaḥ pacatyātmakāraṇāt |
yajñaśiṣṭāśanaṃ hyetatsatāmannaṃ vidhīyate  || 3.118[108 ] ||
rājartviksnātakagurūn priyaśvaśuramātulān  |
arhayenmadhuparkeṇa parisaṃvatsarātpunaḥ  || 3.119[109 ] ||
rājā ca śrotriyaścaiva yajñakarmaṇyupasthitau  |
madhuparkeṇa saṃpūjyau na tvayajña iti sthitiḥ  || 3.120[110 ] ||
sāyaṃ tvannasya siddhasya patnyamantraṃ baliṃ haret |
vaiśvadevaṃ hi nāmaitatsāyaṃ prātarvidhīyate  || 3.121[111 ] ||
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya vipraścandrakṣaye'gnimān  |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryānmāsānumāsikam  || 3.122[112 ] ||
pitṝṇāṃ māsikaṃ śrāddhamanvāhāryaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  |
taccāmiṣeṇā kartavyaṃ praśastena prayatnataḥ  || 3.123[113 ] ||
tatra ye bhojanīyāḥ syurye ca varjyā dvijottamāḥ  |
yāvantaścaiva yaiścānnaistān pravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 3.124[114 ] ||
dvau daive pitṛkārye trīnekaikamubhayatra vā  |
bhojayetsusamṛddho'pi na prasajjeta vistare  || 3.125[115 ] ||
satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadaḥ  |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmānneheta vistaram  || 3.126[116 ] ||
prathitā pretakṛtyaiṣā pitryaṃ nāma vidhukṣaye  |
tasmin yuktasyaiti nityaṃ pretakṛtyaiva laukikī  || 3.127[117 ] ||
śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni dātṛbhiḥ  |
arhattamāya viprāya tasmai dattaṃ mahāphalam  || 3.128[118 ] ||
ekaikamapi vidvāṃsaṃ daive pitrye ca bhojayet |
puṣkalaṃ phalamāpnoti nāmantrajñān bahūnapi  || 3.129[119 ] ||
dūrādeva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam  |
tīrthaṃ taddhavyakavyānāṃ pradāne so'tithiḥ smṛtaḥ  || 3.130[120 ] ||
sahasraṃ hi sahasrāṇāmanṛcāṃ yatra bhuñjate  |
ekastānmantravitprītaḥ sarvānarhati dharmataḥ  || 3.131[121 ] ||
jñānotkṛṣṭāya deyāni kavyāni ca havīṃṣi ca  |
na hi hastāvasṛgdigdhau rudhireṇaiva śudhyataḥ  || 3.132[122 ] ||
yāvato grasate grāsān havyakavyeṣvamantravit |
tāvato grasate preto dīptaśūlarṣṭyayoguḍān  || 3.133[123 ] ||
jñānaniṣṭhā dvijāḥ ke cittaponiṣṭhāstathāpare  |
tapaḥsvādhyāyaniṣṭhāśca karmaniṣṭhāstathāpare  || 3.134[124 ] ||
jñānaniṣṭheṣu kavyāni pratiṣṭhāpyāni yatnataḥ  |
havyāni tu yathānyāyaṃ sarveṣveva caturṣvapi  || 3.135[125 ] ||
aśrotriyaḥ pitā yasya putraḥ syādvedapāragaḥ  |
aśrotriyo vā putraḥ syātpitā syādvedapāragaḥ  || 3.136[126 ] ||
jyāyāṃsamanayorvidyādyasya syācchrotriyaḥ pitā  |
mantrasaṃpūjanārthaṃ tu satkāramitaro'rhati  || 3.137[127 ] ||
na śrāddhe bhojayenmitraṃ dhanaiḥ kāryo'sya saṃgrahaḥ  |
nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāttaṃ śrāddhe bhojayeddvijam  || 3.138[128 ] ||
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca  |
tasya pretya phalaṃ nāsti śrāddheṣu ca haviḥṣu ca  || 3.139[129 ] ||
yaḥ saṃgatāni kurute mohācchrāddhena mānavaḥ  |
sa svargāccyavate lokācchrāddhamitro dvijādhamaḥ  || 3.140[130 ] ||
saṃbhojāni sābhihitā paiśācī dakṣiṇā dvijaiḥ  |
ihaivāste tu sā loke gaurandhevaikaveśmani  || 3.141[131 ] ||
yatheriṇe bījamuptvā na vaptā labhate phalam  |
tathānṛce havirdattvā na dātā labhate phalam  || 3.142[132 ] ||
dātṝn pratigrahītṝṃśca kurute phalabhāginaḥ  |
viduṣe dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā vidhivatpretya ceha ca  || 3.143[133 ] ||
kāmaṃ śrāddhe'rcayenmitraṃ nābhirūpamapi tvarim  |
dviṣatā hi havirbhuktaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalam  || 3.144[134 ] ||
yatnena bhojayecchrāddhe bahvṛcaṃ vedapāragam  |
śākhāntagamathādhvaryuṃ chandogaṃ tu samāptikam  || 3.145[135 ] ||
eṣāmanyatamo yasya bhuñjīta śrāddhamarcitaḥ  |
pitṝṇāṃ tasya tṛptiḥ syācchāśvatī sāptapauruṣī  || 3.146[136 ] ||
eṣa vai prathamaḥ kalpaḥ pradāne havyakavyayoḥ  |
anukalpastvayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhiranuṣṭhitaḥ  || 3.147[137 ] ||
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svasrīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum  |
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhumṛtvigyājyau ca bhojayet || 3.148[138 ] ||
na brāhmaṇaṃ parīkṣeta daive karmaṇi dharmavit |
pitrye karmaṇi tu prāpte parīkṣeta prayatnataḥ  || 3.149[139 ] ||
ye stenapatitaklībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ  |
tān havyakavyayorviprānanarhānmanurabravīt || 3.150[140 ] ||
jaṭilaṃ cānadhīyānaṃ durbālaṃ kitavaṃ tathā  |
yājayanti ca ye pūgāṃstāṃśca śrāddhe na bhojayet || 3.151[141 ] ||
cikitsakān devalakānmāṃsavikrayiṇastathā  |
vipaṇena ca jīvanto varjyāḥ syurhavyakavyayoḥ  || 3.152[142 ] ||
preṣyo grāmasya rājñaśca kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ  |
pratiroddhā guroścaiva tyaktāgnirvārdhuṣistathā  || 3.153[143 ] ||
yakṣmī ca paśupālaśca parivettā nirākṛtiḥ  |
brahmadviṭparivittiśca gaṇābhyantara eva ca  || 3.154[144 ] ||
kuśīlavo'vakīrṇī ca vṛṣalīpatireva ca  |
paunarbhavaśca kāṇaśca yasya copapatirgṛhe  || 3.155[145 ] ||
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaśca bhṛtakādhyāpitastathā  |
śūdraśiṣyo guruścaiva vāgduṣṭaḥ kuṇḍagolakau  || 3.156[146 ] ||
akāraṇe parityaktā mātāpitrorgurostathā  |
brāhmairyaunaiśca saṃbandhaiḥ saṃyogaṃ patitairgataḥ  || 3.157[147 ] ||
agāradāhī garadaḥ kuṇḍāśī somavikrayī  |
samudrayāyī bandī ca tailikaḥ kūṭakārakaḥ  || 3.158[148 ] ||
pitrā vivadamānaśca kitavo madyapastathā  |
pāparogyabhiśastaśca dāmbhiko rasavikrayī  || 3.159[149 ] ||
dhanuḥśarāṇāṃ kartā ca yaścāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ  |
mitradhrugdyūtavṛttiśca putrācāryastathaiva ca  || 3.160[150 ] ||
bhrāmarī ganḍamālī ca śvitryatho piśunastathā  |
unmatto'ndhaśca varjyāḥ syurvedanindaka eva ca  || 3.161[151 ] ||
hastigo'śvoṣṭradamako nakṣatrairyaśca jīvati  |
pakṣiṇāṃ poṣako yaśca yuddhācāryastathaiva ca  || 3.162[152 ] ||
srotasāṃ bhedako yaśca teṣāṃ cāvaraṇe rataḥ  |
gṛhasaṃveśako dūto vṛkṣāropaka eva ca  || 3.163[153 ] ||
śvakrīḍī śyenajīvī ca kanyādūṣaka eva ca  |
hiṃsro vṛṣalavṛttiśca gaṇānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ  || 3.164[154 ] ||
ācārahīnaḥ klībaśca nityaṃ yācanakastathā  |
kṛṣijīvī ślīpadī ca sadbhirnindita eva ca  || 3.165[155 ] ||
aurabhriko māhiṣikaḥ parapūrvāpatistathā  |
pretaniryāpakaścaiva varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ  || 3.166[156 ] ||
etān vigarhitācārānapāṅkteyān dvijādhamān  |
dvijātipravaro vidvānubhayatra vivarjayet || 3.167[157 ] ||
brāhmaṇo tvanadhīyānastṛṇāgniriva śāmyati  |
tasmai havyaṃ na dātavyaṃ na hi bhasmani hūyate  || 3.168[158 ] ||
apāṅktadāne yo dāturbhavatyūrdhvaṃ phalodayaḥ  |
daive haviṣi pitrye vā taṃ pravaksyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 3.169[159 ] ||
avratairyaddvijairbhuktaṃ parivetrādibhistathā  |
apāṅkteyairyadanyaiśca tadvai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate  || 3.170[160 ] ||
dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo'graje sthite  |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittistu pūrvajaḥ  || 3.171[161 ] ||
parivittiḥ parivettā yayā ca parividyate  |
sarve te narakaṃ yānti dātṛyājakapañcamāḥ  || 3.172[162 ] ||
bhrāturmṛtasya bhāryāyāṃ yo'nurajyeta kāmataḥ  |
dharmeṇāpi niyuktāyāṃ sa jñeyo didhiṣūpatiḥ  || 3.173[163 ] ||
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau  |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍaḥ syānmṛte bhartari golakaḥ  || 3.174[164 ] ||
tau tu jātau parakṣetre prāṇinau pretya ceha ca  |
dattāni havyakavyāni nāśayanti pradāyinām  || 3.175[165 ] ||
apāṅktyo yāvataḥ paṅktyān bhuñjānānanupaśyati  |
tāvatāṃ na phalaṃ tatra dātā prāpnoti bāliśaḥ  || 3.176[166 ] ||
vīkṣyāndho navateḥ kāṇaḥ ṣaṣṭeḥ śvitrī śatasya tu  |
pāparogī sahasrasya dāturnāśayate phalam  || 3.177[167 ] ||
yāvataḥ saṃspṛśedaṅgairbrāhmaṇāñśūdrayājakaḥ  |
tāvatāṃ na bhaveddātuḥ phalaṃ dānasya paurtikam  || 3.168[168 ] ||
vedaviccāpi vipro'sya lobhātkṛtvā pratigraham  |
vināśaṃ vrajati kṣipramāmapātramivāmbhasi  || 3.179[169 ] ||
somavikrayiṇe viṣṭhā bhiṣaje pūyaśoṇitam  |
naṣṭaṃ devalake dattamapratiṣṭhaṃ tu vārdhuṣau  || 3.180[170 ] ||
yattu vāṇijake dattaṃ neha nāmutra tadbhavet |
bhasmanīva hutaṃ dravyaṃ tathā paunarbhave dvije  || 3.181[171 ] ||
itareṣu tvapāṅktyeṣu yathoddiṣṭeṣvasādhuṣu  |
medo'sṛṅmāṃsamajjāsthi vadantyannaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ  || 3.182[172 ] ||
apāṅktyopahatā paṅktiḥ pāvyate yairdvijottamaiḥ  |
tānnibodhata kārtsnyena dvijāgryān paṅktipāvanān  || 3.183[173 ] ||
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvapravacaneṣu ca  |
śrotriyānvayajāścaiva vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ  || 3.184[174 ] ||
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnistrisuparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
brahmadeyātmasantāno jyeṣṭhasāmaga eva ca  || 3.185[175 ] ||
vedārthavitpravaktā ca brahmacārī sahasradaḥ  |
śatāyuścaiva vijñeyā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ  || 3.186[176 ] ||
pūrvedyuraparedyurvā śrāddhakarmaṇyupasthite  |
nimantrayeta tryavarān samyagviprān yathoditān  || 3.187[177 ] ||
nimantrito dvijaḥ pitrye niyatātmā bhavetsadā  |
na ca chandāṃsyadhīyīta yasya śrāddhaṃ ca tadbhavet || 3.188[178 ] ||
nimantritān hi pitara upatiṣṭhanti tān dvijān  |
vāyuvaccānugacchanti tathāsīnānupāsate  || 3.189[179 ] ||
ketitastu yathānyāyaṃ havye kavye dvijottamaḥ  |
kathaṃ cidapyatikrāman pāpaḥ sūkaratāṃ vrajet || 3.190[180 ] ||
āmantritastu yaḥ śrāddhe vṛśalyā saha modate  |
dāturyadduṣkṛtaṃ kiṃ cittatsarvaṃ pratipadyate  || 3.191[181 ] ||
akrodhanāḥ śaucaparāḥ satataṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ  |
nyastaśastrā mahābhāgāḥ pitaraḥ pūrvadevatāḥ  || 3.192[182 ] ||
yasmādutpattireteṣāṃ sarveṣāmapyaśeṣataḥ  |
ye ca yairupacaryāḥ syurniyamaistānnibodhata  || 3.193[183 ] ||
manorhairaṇyagarbhasya ye marīcyādayaḥ sutāḥ  |
teṣāmṛṣīṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ putrāḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 3.194[184 ] ||
virāṭsutāḥ somasadaḥ sādhyānāṃ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ  |
agniṣvāttāśca devānāṃ mārīcā lokaviśrutāḥ  || 3.195[185 ] ||
daityadānavayakṣāṇāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām  |
suparṇakinnarāṇāṃ ca smṛtā barhiṣado'trijāḥ  || 3.196[186 ] ||
somapā nāma viprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ havirbhujaḥ  |
vaiśyānāmājyapā nāma śūdrāṇāṃ tu sukālinaḥ  || 3.197[187 ] ||
somapāstu kaveḥ putrā haviṣmanto'ṅgiraḥsutāḥ  |
pulastyasyājyapāḥ putrā vasiṣṭhasya sukālinaḥ  || 3.198[188 ] ||
agnidagdhānagnidagdhān kāvyān barhiṣadastathā  |
agniṣvāttāṃśca saumyāṃśca viprāṇāmeva nirdiśet || 3.199[189 ] ||
ya ete tu gaṇā mukhyāḥ pitṝṇāṃ parikīrtitāḥ  |
teṣāmapīha vijñeyaṃ putrapautramanantakam  || 3.200[190 ] ||
ṛṣibhyaḥ pitaro jātāḥ pitṛbhyo devamānavāḥ  |
devebhyastu jagatsarvaṃ caraṃ sthāṇvanupūrvaśaḥ  || 3.201[191 ] ||
rājatairbhājanaireṣāmatho vā rajatānvitaiḥ  |
vāryapi śraddhayā dattamakṣayāyopakalpate  || 3.202[192 ] ||
daivakāryāddvijātīnāṃ pitṛkāryaṃ viśiṣyate  |
daivaṃ hi pitṛkāryasya pūrvamāpyāyanaṃ smṛtam  || 3.203[193 ] ||
teṣāmārakṣabhūtaṃ tu pūrvaṃ daivaṃ niyojayet |
raksāṃsi vipralumpanti śrāddhamārakṣavarjitam  || 3.204[194 ] ||
daivādyantaṃ tadīheta pitrādyantaṃ na tadbhavet |
pitrādyantaṃ tvīhamānaḥ kṣipraṃ naśyati sānvayaḥ  || 3.205[195 ] ||
śuciṃ deśaṃ viviktaṃ ca gomayenopalepayet |
dakṣināpravaṇaṃ caiva prayatnenopapādayet || 3.206[196 ] ||
avakāśeṣu cokṣeṣu jalatīreṣu caiva hi  |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti dattena pitaraḥ sadā  || 3.207[197 ] ||
āsaneṣūpakḷpteṣu barhiṣmatsu pṛthakpṛthak |
upaspṛṣṭodakān samyagviprāṃstānupaveśayet || 3.208[198 ] ||
upaveśya tu tān viprānāsaneṣvajugupsitān  |
gandhamālyaiḥ surabhibhirarcayeddaivapūrvakam  || 3.209[199 ] ||
teṣāmudakamānīya sapavitrāṃstilānapi  |
agnau kuryādanujñāto brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaiḥ saha  || 3.210[200 ] ||
agneḥ somayamābhyāṃ ca kṛtvāpyāyanamāditaḥ  |
havirdānena vidhivatpaścātsaṃtarpayetpitṝn  || 3.211[201 ] ||
agnyabhāve tu viprasya pāṇāvevopapādayet |
yo hyagniḥ sa dvijo viprairmantradarśibhirucyate  || 3.212[202 ] ||
akrodhanān suprasādān vadantyetān purātanān  |
lokasyāpyāyane yuktān śrāddhadevān dvijottamān  || 3.213[203 ] ||
apasavyamagnau kṛtvā sarvamāvṛtya vikramam  |
apasavyena hastena nirvapedudakaṃ bhuvi  || 3.214[204 ] ||
trīṃstu tasmāddhaviḥśeṣātpiṇḍān kṛtvā samāhitaḥ  |
audakenaiva vidhinā nirvapeddakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  || 3.215[205 ] ||
nyupya piṇḍāṃstatastāṃstu prayato vidhipūrvakam  |
teṣu darbheṣu taṃ hastaṃ nirmṛjyāllepabhāginām  || 3.216[206 ] ||
ācamyodakparāvṛtya trirāyamya śanairasūn  |
ṣaḍṛtūṃśca namaskuryātpitṝneva ca mantravat || 3.217[207 ] ||
udakaṃ ninayeccheṣaṃ śanaiḥ piṇḍāntike punaḥ  |
avajighrecca tān piṇḍān yathānyuptān samāhitaḥ  || 3.218[208 ] ||
piṇḍebhyastvalpikāṃ mātrāṃ samādāyānupūrvaśaḥ  |
tāneva viprānāsīnān vidhivatpūrvamāśayet || 3.219[209 ] ||
dhriyamāṇe tu pitari pūrveṣāmeva nirvapet |
vipravadvāpi taṃ śrāddhe svakaṃ pitaramāśayet || 3.220[210 ] ||
pitā yasya nivṛttaḥ syājjīveccāpi pitāmahaḥ  |
pituḥ sa nāma saṅkīrtya kīrtayetprapitāmaham  || 3.221[211 ] ||
pitāmaho vā tacchrāddhaṃ bhuñjītetyabravīnmanuḥ  |
kāmaṃ vā samanujñātaḥ svayameva samācaret || 3.222[212 ] ||
teṣāṃ dattvā tu hasteṣu sapavitraṃ tilodakam  |
tatpiṇḍāgraṃ prayaccheta svadhaiṣāmastviti bruvan  || 3.223[213 ] ||
pāṇibhyāṃ tūpasaṃgṛhya svayamannasya vardhitam  |
viprāntike pitṝn dhyāyan śanakairupanikṣipet || 3.224[214 ] ||
ubhayorhastayormuktaṃ yadannamupanīyate  |
tadvipralumpantyasurāḥ sahasā duṣṭacetasaḥ  || 3.225[215 ] ||
guṇāṃśca sūpaśākādyān payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu  |
vinyasetprayataḥ pūrvaṃ bhūmāveva samāhitaḥ  || 3.226[216 ] ||
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ mūlāni ca phalāni ca  |
hṛdyāni caiva māṃsāni pānāni surabhīṇi ca  || 3.227[217 ] ||
upanīya tu tatsarvaṃ śanakaiḥ susamāhitaḥ  |
pariveṣayeta prayato guṇān sarvān pracodayan  || 3.228[218 ] ||
nāsramāpātayejjātu na kupyennānṛtaṃ vadet |
na pādena spṛśedannaṃ na caitadavadhūnayet || 3.229[219 ] ||
asraṃ gamayati pretān kopo'rīnanṛtaṃ śunaḥ  |
pādasparśastu rakṣāṃsi duṣkṛtīnavadhūnanam  || 3.230[220 ] ||
yadyadroceta viprebhyastattaddadyādamatsaraḥ  |
brahmodyāśca kathāḥ kuryātpitṝṇāmetadīpsitam  || 3.231[221 ] ||
svādhyāyaṃ śrāvayetpitrye dharmaśāstrāṇi caiva hi  |
ākhyānānītihāsāṃśca purāṇāni khilāni ca  || 3.232[222 ] ||
harṣayedbrāhmaṇāṃstuṣṭo bhojayecca śanaiḥśanaiḥ  |
annādyenāsakṛccaitān guṇaiśca paricodayet || 3.233[223 ] ||
vratasthamapi dauhitraṃ śrāddhe yatnena bhojayet |
kutapaṃ cāsanaṃ dadyāttilaiśca vikirenmahīm  || 3.234[224 ] ||
trīṇi śrāddhe pavitrāṇi dauhitraḥ kutapastilāḥ  |
trīṇi cātra praśaṃsanti śaucamakrodhamatvarām  || 3.235[225 ] ||
atyuṣṇaṃ sarvamannaṃ syādbhuñjīraṃste ca vāgyatāḥ  |
na ca dvijātayo brūyurdātrā pṛṣṭā havirguṇān  || 3.236[226 ] ||
yāvaduṣmā bhavatyannaṃ yāvadaśnanti vāgyatāḥ  |
pitarastāvadaśnanti yāvannoktā havirguṇāḥ  || 3.237[227 ] ||
yadveṣṭitaśirā bhuṅkte yadbhuṅkte dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ  |
sopānatkaśca yadbhuṅkte tadvai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate  || 3.238[228 ] ||
cāṇḍālaśca varāhaśca kukkuṭaḥ śvā tathaiva ca  |
rajasvalā ca ṣaṇḍhaśca nekṣerannaśnato dvijān  || 3.239[229 ] ||
home pradāne bhojye ca yadebhirabhivīkṣyate  |
daive haviṣi pitrye vā tadgacchatyayathātatham  || 3.240[230 ] ||
ghrāṇena sūkaro hanti pakṣavātena kukkuṭaḥ  |
śvā tu dṛṣṭinipātena sparśeṇāvaravarṇajaḥ  || 3.241[231 ] ||
khañjo vā yadi vā kāṇo dātuḥ preṣyo'pi vā bhavet |
hīnātiriktagātro vā tamapyapanayetpunaḥ  || 3.242[232 ] ||
brāhmaṇaṃ bhikṣukaṃ vāpi bhojanārthamupasthitam  |
brāhmaṇairabhyanujñātaḥ śaktitaḥ pratipūjayet || 3.243[233 ] ||
sārvavarṇikamannādyaṃ saṃnīyāplāvya vāriṇā  |
samutsṛjedbhuktavatāmagrato vikiran bhuvi  || 3.244[234 ] ||
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām  |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syāddarbheṣu vikiraśca yaḥ  || 3.245[235 ] ||
uccheṣaṇāṃ bhūmigatamajihmasyāśaṭhasya ca  |
dāsavargasya tatpitrye bhāgadheyaṃ pracakṣate  || 3.246[236 ] ||
āsapiṇḍakriyākarma dvijāteḥ saṃsthitasya tu  |
adaivaṃ bhojayecchrāddhaṃ piṇḍamekaṃ ca nirvapet || 3.247[237 ] ||
sahapiṇḍakriyāyāṃ tu kṛtāyāmasya dharmataḥ  |
anayaivāvṛtā kāryaṃ piṇḍanirvapanaṃ sutaiḥ  || 3.248[238 ] ||
śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā ya ucchiṣṭaṃ vṛṣalāya prayacchati  |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti kālasūtramavākśirāḥ  || 3.249[239 ] ||
śrāddhabhugvṛṣalītalpaṃ tadaharyo'dhigacchati  |
tasyāḥ purīṣe taṃ māsaṃ pitarastasya śerate  || 3.250[240 ] ||
pṛṣṭvā svaditamityevaṃ tṛptānācāmayettataḥ  |
ācāntāṃścānujānīyādabhito ramyatāmiti  || 3.251[241 ] ||
svadhāstvityeva taṃ brūyurbrāhmaṇāstadanantaram  |
svadhākāraḥ parā hyāṣīḥ sarveṣu pitṛkarmasu  || 3.252[242 ] ||
tato bhuktavatāṃ teṣāmannaśeṣaṃ nivedayet |
yathā brūyustathā kuryādanujñātastato dvijaiḥ  || 3.253[243 ] ||
pitrye svaditamityeva vācyaṃ goṣṭhe tu suśṛtam  |
saṃpannamityabhyudaye daive rucitamityapi  || 3.254[244 ] ||
aparāhṇastathā darbhā vāstusaṃpādanaṃ tilāḥ  |
sṛṣṭirmṛṣṭirdvijāścāgryāḥ śrāddhakarmasu saṃpadaḥ  || 3.255[245 ] ||
darbhāḥ pavitraṃ pūrvāhṇo haviṣyāṇi ca sarvaśaḥ  |
pavitraṃ yacca pūrvoktaṃ vijñeyā havyasaṃpadaḥ  || 3.256[246 ] ||
munyannāni payaḥ somo māṃsaṃ yaccānupaskṛtam  |
aksāralavaṇaṃ caiva prakṛtyā havirucyate  || 3.257[247 ] ||
visṛjya brāhmaṇāṃstāṃstu niyato vāgyataḥ śuciḥ  |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamākāṅkṣan yācetemān varān pitṝn  || 3.258[248 ] ||
dātāro no'bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ saṃtatireva ca  |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamadbahudeyaṃ ca no'stviti  || 3.259[249 ] ||
evaṃ nirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍāṃstāṃstadanantaram  |
gāṃ vipramajamagniṃ vā prāśayedapsu vā kṣipet || 3.260[250 ] ||
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ ke citparastādeva kurvate  |
vayobhiḥ khādayantyanye prakṣipantyanale'psu vā  || 3.261[251 ] ||
pativratā dharmapatnī pitṛpūjanatatparā  |
madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ piṇḍamadyātsamyaksutārthinī  || 3.262[252 ] ||
āyuṣmantaṃ sutaṃ sūte yaśomedhāsamanvitam  |
dhanavantaṃ prajāvantaṃ sāttvikaṃ dhārmikaṃ tathā  || 3.263[253 ] ||
praksālya hastāvācāmya jñātiprāyaṃ prakalpayet |
jñātibhyaḥ satkṛtaṃ dattvā bāndhavānapi bhojayet || 3.264[254 ] ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ tu tattiṣṭhedyāvadviprā visarjitāḥ  |
tato gṛhabaliṃ kuryāditi dharmo vyavasthitaḥ  || 3.265[255 ] ||
haviryaccirarātrāya yaccānantyāya kalpate  |
pitṛbhyo vidhivaddattaṃ tatpravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 3.266[256 ] ||
tilairvrīhiyavairmāṣairadbhirmūlaphalena vā  |
dattena māsaṃ tṛpyanti vidhivatpitaro nṛnām  || 3.267[257 ] ||
dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīnmāsān hāriṇena tu  |
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākunenātha pañca vai  || 3.268[258 ] ||
ṣaṇmāsāṃśchāgamāṃsena pārṣatena ca sapta vai  |
aṣṭāvenasya māṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu  || 3.269[259 ] ||
daśamāsāṃstu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ  |
śaśakūrmayostu māṃsena māsānekādaśaiva tu  || 3.270[260 ] ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena ca  |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptirdvādaśavārṣikī  || 3.271[261 ] ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkāḥ khaṅgalohāmiṣaṃ madhu  |
ānantyāyaiva kalpyante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ  || 3.272[262 ] ||
yatkiṃ cinmadhunā miśraṃ pradadyāttu trayodaśīm  |
tadapyakṣayameva syādvarṣāsu ca maghāsu ca  || 3.273[263 ] ||
api naḥ sa kule bhūyādyo no dadyāttrayodaśīm  |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ prākchāye kuñjarasya ca  || 3.274[264 ] ||
yadyaddadāti vidhivatsamyakśraddhāsamanvitaḥ  |
tattatpitṝṇāṃ bhavati paratrānantamakṣayam  || 3.275[265 ] ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe daśamyādau varjayitvā caturdaśīm  |
śrāddhe praśastāstithayo yathaitā na tathetarāḥ  || 3.276[266 ] ||
yukṣu kurvan dinarkṣeṣu sarvān kāmān samaśnute  |
ayukṣu tu pitṝn sarvān prajāṃ prāpnoti puṣkalām  || 3.277[267 ] ||
yathā caivāparaḥ pakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣādviśiṣyate  |
tathā śrāddhasya pūrvāhṇādaparāhṇo viśiṣyate  || 3.278[268 ] ||
prācīnāvītinā samyagapasavyamatandriṇā  |
pitryamā nidhanātkāryaṃ vidhivaddarbhapāṇinā  || 3.279[269 ] ||
rātrau śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta rākṣasī kīrtitā hi sā  |
saṃdhyayorubhayoścaiva sūrye caivācirodite  || 3.280[270 ] ||
anena vidhinā śrāddhaṃ trirabdasyeha nirvapet |
hemantagrīṣmavarṣāsu pāñcayajñikamanvaham  || 3.281[271 ] ||
na paitṛyajñiyo homo laukike'gnau vidhīyate  |
na darśena vinā śrāddhamāhitāgnerdvijanmanaḥ  || 3.282[272 ] ||
yadeva tarpayatyadbhiḥ pitṝn snātvā dvijottamaḥ  |
tenaiva kṛtsnamāpnoti pitṛyajñakriyāphalam  || 3.283[273 ] ||
vasūn vadanti tu pitṝn rudrāṃścaiva pitāmahān  |
prapitāmahāṃstathādityān śrutireṣā sanātanī  || 3.284[274 ] ||
vighasāśī bhavennityaṃ nityaṃ vāmṛtabhojanaḥ  |
vighaso bhuktaśeṣaṃ tu yajñaśeṣaṃ tathāmṛtam  || 3.285[275 ] ||
etadvo'bhihitaṃ sarvaṃ vidhānaṃ pāñcayajñikam  |
dvijātimukhyavṛttīnāṃ vidhānaṃ śrūyatāmiti  || 3.286[276 ] ||

caturthamāyuṣo bhāgamuṣitvādyaṃ gurau dvijāḥ   |
dvitīyamāyuṣo bhāgaṃ kṛtadāro gṛhe vaset || 4.1 ||
adroheṇaiva bhūtānāmalpadroheṇa vā punaḥ  |
yā vṛttistāṃ samāsthāya vipro jīvedanāpadi  || 4.2 ||
yātrāmātraprasiddhyarthaṃ svaiḥ karmabhiragarhitaiḥ  |
akleśena śarīrasya kurvīta dhanasaṃcayam  || 4.3 ||
ṛtāmṛtābhyāṃ jīvettu mṛtena pramṛtena vā  |
satyānṛtābhyāmapi vā na śvavṛttyā kadā cana  || 4.4 ||
ṛtamuñchaśilaṃ jñeyamamṛtaṃ syādayācitam  |
mṛtaṃ tu yācitaṃ bhaikṣaṃ pramṛtaṃ karṣaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 4.5 ||
satyānṛtaṃ tu vāṇijyaṃ tena caivāpi jīvyate  |
sevā śvavṛttirākhyātā tasmāttāṃ parivarjayet || 4.6 ||
kusūladhānyako vā syātkumbhīdhānyaka eva vā  |
tryahaihiko vāpi bhavedaśvastanika eva vā  || 4.7 ||
caturṇāmapi caiteṣāṃ dvijānāṃ gṛhamedhinām  |
jyāyān paraḥ paro jñeyo dharmato lokajittamaḥ  || 4.8 ||
ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavatyeṣāṃ tribhiranyaḥ pravartate  |
dvābhyāmekaścaturthastu brahmasattreṇa jīvati  || 4.9 ||
vartayaṃśca śiloñchābhyāmagnihotraparāyaṇaḥ  |
iṣṭīḥ pārvāyaṇāntīyāḥ kevalā nirvapetsadā  || 4.10 ||
na lokavṛttaṃ varteta vṛttihetoḥ kathaṃ cana  |
ajihmāmaśathāṃ śuddhāṃ jīvedbrāhmaṇajīvikām  || 4.11 ||
saṃtoṣaṃ paramāsthāya sukhārthī saṃyato bhavet |
saṃtoṣamūlaṃ hi sukhaṃ duḥkhamūlaṃ viparyayaḥ  || 4.12 ||
ato'nyatamayā vṛttyā jīvaṃstu snātako dvijaḥ  |
svargāyuṣyayaśasyāni vratāṇīmāni dhārayet || 4.13 ||
vedoditaṃ svakaṃ karma nityaṃ kuryādatandritaḥ  |
taddhi kurvan yathāśakti prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim  || 4.14 ||
nehetārthān prasaṅgena na viruddhena karmaṇā  |
na vidyamāneṣvartheṣu nārtyāmapi yatastataḥ  || 4.15 ||
indriyārtheṣu sarveṣu na prasajyeta kāmataḥ  |
atiprasaktiṃ caiteṣāṃ manasā saṃnivartayet || 4.16 ||
sarvān parityajedarthān svādhyāyasya virodhinaḥ  |
yathā tathādhyāpayaṃstu sā hyasya kṛtakṛtyatā  || 4.17 ||
vayasaḥ karmaṇo'rthasya śrutasyābhijanasya ca  |
veṣavāgbuddhisārūpyamācaran vicarediha  || 4.18 ||
buddhivṛddhikarāṇyāśu dhanyāni ca hitāni ca  |
nityaṃ śāstrāṇyavekṣeta nigamāṃścaiva vaidikān  || 4.19 ||
yathā yathā hi puruṣaḥ śāstraṃ samadhigacchati  |
tathā tathā vijānāti vijñānaṃ cāsya rocate  || 4.20 ||
ṛṣiyajñaṃ devayajñaṃ bhūtayajñaṃ ca sarvadā  |
nṛyajñaṃ pitṛyajñaṃ ca yathāśakti na hāpayet || 4.21 ||
etāneke mahāyajñān yajñaśāstravido janāḥ  |
anīhamānāḥ satatamindriyeṣveva juhvati  || 4.22 ||
vācyeke juhvati prāṇaṃ prāṇe vācaṃ ca sarvadā  |
vāci prāṇe ca paśyanto yajñanirvṛttimakṣayām  || 4.23 ||
jñānenaivāpare viprā yajantyetairmakhaiḥ sadā  |
jñānamūlāṃ kriyāmeṣāṃ paśyanto jñānacakṣuṣā  || 4.24 ||
agnihotraṃ ca juhuyādādyante dyuniśoḥ sadā  |
darśena cārdhamāsānte paurṇāmāsena caiva hi  || 4.25 ||
sasyānte navasasyeṣṭyā tathā rtvante dvijo'dhvaraiḥ  |
paśunā tvayanasyādau samānte saumikairmakhaiḥ  || 4.26 ||
nāniṣṭvā navasasyeṣṭyā paśunā cāgnimān dvijaḥ  |
navānnamadyānmāṃsaṃ vā dīrghamāyurjijīviṣuḥ  || 4.27 ||
navenānarcitā hyasya paśuhavyena cāgnayaḥ  |
prāṇānevāttumicchanti navānnāmiṣagardhinaḥ  || 4.28 ||
āsanāśanaśayyābhiradbhirmūlaphalena vā  |
nāsya kaścidvasedgehe śaktito'narcito'tithiḥ  || 4.29 ||
pāṣāṇḍino vikarmasthān baiḍālavratikāñśaṭhān  |
haitukān bakavṛttīṃśca vāṅgātreṇāpi nārcayet || 4.30 ||
vedavidyāvratasnātāñśrotriyān gṛhamedhinaḥ  |
pūjayeddhavyakavyena viparītāṃśca varjayet || 4.31 ||
śaktito'pacamānebhyo dātavyaṃ gṛhamedhinā  |
saṃvibhāgaśca bhūtebhyaḥ kartavyo'nuparodhataḥ  || 4.32 ||
rājato dhanamanvicchetsaṃsīdan snātakaḥ kṣudhā  |
yājyāntevāsinorvāpi na tvanyata iti sthitiḥ  || 4.33 ||
na sīdetsnātako vipraḥ kṣudhā śaktaḥ kathaṃ cana  |
na jīrṇamalavadvāsā bhavecca vibhave sati  || 4.34 ||
kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśrurdāntaḥ śuklāmbaraḥ śuciḥ  |
svādhyāye caiva yuktaḥ syānnityamātmahiteṣu ca  || 4.35 ||
vaiṇavīṃ dhārayedyaṣṭiṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum  |
yajñopavītaṃ vedaṃ ca śubhaṃ raukme ca kuṇḍale  || 4.36 ||
nekṣetodyantamādityaṃ nāstaṃ yāntaṃ kadā cana  |
nopasṛṣṭaṃ na vāristhaṃ na madhyaṃ nabhaso gatam  || 4.37 ||
na laṅghayedvatsatantrīṃ na pradhāvecca varṣati  |
na codake nirīkṣeta svarūpamiti dhāraṇā  || 4.38 ||
mṛdaṃ gāṃ daivataṃ vipraṃ ghṛtaṃ madhu catuṣpatham  |
pradakṣiṇāni kurvīta prajñātāṃśca vanaspatīn  || 4.39 ||
nopagacchetpramatto'pi striyamārtavadarśane  |
samānaśayane caiva na śayīta tayā saha  || 4.40 ||
rajasābhiplutāṃ nārīṃ narasya hyupagacchataḥ  |
prajñā tejo balaṃ cakṣurāyuścaiva prahīyate  || 4.41 ||
tāṃ vivarjayatastasya rajasā samabhiplutām  |
prajñā tejo balaṃ cakṣurāyuścaiva pravardhate  || 4.42 ||
nāśnīyādbhāryayā sārdhaṃ naināmīkṣeta cāśnatīm  |
kṣuvatīṃ jṛmbhamāṇāṃ vā na cāsīnāṃ yathāsukham  || 4.43 ||
nāñjayantīṃ svake netre na cābhyaktāmanāvṛtām  |
na paśyetprasavantīṃ ca tejaskāmo dvijottamaḥ  || 4.44 ||
nānnamadyādekavāsā na nagnaḥ snānamācaret |
na mūtraṃ pathi kurvīta na bhasmani na govraje  || 4.45 ||
na phālakṛṣṭe na jale na cityāṃ na ca parvate  |
na jīrṇadevāyatane na valmīke kadā cana  || 4.46 ||
na sasattveṣu garteṣu na gacchannapi na sthitaḥ  |
na nadītīramāsādya na ca parvatamastake  || 4.47 ||
vāyvagnivipramādityamapaḥ paśyaṃstathaiva gāḥ  |
na kadā cana kurvīta viṇmūtrasya visarjanam  || 4.48 ||
tiraskṛtyoccaretkāṣṭha- loṣṭhapatratṛṇādinā  |
niyamya prayato vācaṃ saṃvītāṅgo'vaguṇṭhitaḥ  || k4.49[50 ] ||
mūtroccārasamutsargaṃ divā kuryādudaṅmukhaḥ  |
dakṣiṇābhimukho rātrau saṃdhyāyośca yathā divā  || k4.50[51 ] ||
chāyāyāmandhakāre vā rātrāvahani vā dvijaḥ  |
yathāsukhamukhaḥ kuryātprāṇabādhabhayeṣu ca  || k4.51[52 ] ||
pratyagniṃ pratisūryaṃ ca pratisomodakadvijam  |
pratigu prativātaṃ ca prajñā naśyati mehataḥ  || k4.52[49 ] ||
nāgniṃ mukhenopadhamennagnāṃ nekṣeta ca striyam  |
nāmedhyaṃ prakṣipedagnau na ca pādau pratāpayet || 4.53 ||
adhastānnopadadhyācca na cainamabhilaṅghayet |
na cainaṃ pādataḥ kuryānna prāṇābādhamācaret || 4.54 ||
nāśnīyātsaṃdhivelāyāṃ na gacchennāpi saṃviśet |
na caiva pralikhedbhūmiṃ nātmano'paharetsrajam  || 4.55 ||
nāpsu mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā ṣṭhīvanaṃ vā samutsṛjet |
amedhyaliptamanyadvā lohitaṃ vā viṣāṇi vā  || 4.56 ||
naikaḥ supyācchūnyagehe na śreyāṃsaṃ prabodhayet |
nodakyayābhibhāṣeta yajñaṃ gacchenna cāvṛtaḥ  || 4.57 ||
agnyagāre gavāṃ goṣṭhe brāhmaṇānāṃ ca saṃnidhau  |
svādhyāye bhojane caiva dakṣinaṃ pāṇimuddharet || 4.58 ||
na vārayedgāṃ dhayantīṃ na cācakṣīta kasya cit |
na divīndrāyudhaṃ dṛṣṭvā kasya ciddarśayedbudhaḥ  || 4.59 ||
nādharmike vasedgrāme na vyādhibahule bhṛśam  |
naikaḥ prapadyetādhvānaṃ na ciraṃ parvate vaset || 4.60 ||
na śūdrarājye nivasennādhārmikajanāvṛte  |
na pāṣaṇḍigaṇākrānte nopasṣṛṭe'ntyajairnṛbhiḥ  || 4.61 ||
na bhuñjītoddhṛtasnehaṃ nātisauhityamācaret |
nātiprage nātisāyaṃ na sāyaṃ prātarāśitaḥ  || 4.62 ||
na kurvīta vṛthāceṣṭāṃ na vāryañjalinā pibet |
notsaṅge bhakṣayedbhakṣyānna jātu syātkutūhalī  || 4.63 ||
na nṛtyedatha vā gāyenna vāditrāṇi vādayet]  |
nāsphoṭayenna ca kṣveḍenna ca rakto virāvayet || 4.64 ||
na pādau dhāvayetkāṃsye kadā cidapi bhājane  |
na bhinnabhāṇḍe bhuñjīta na bhāvapratidūṣite  || 4.65 ||
upānahau ca vāsaśca dhṛtamanyairna dhārayet |
upavītamalaṅkāraṃ srajaṃ karakameva ca  || 4.66 ||
nāvinītairbhajeddhuryairna ca kṣudhvyādhipīḍitaiḥ  |
na bhinnaśṛṅgākṣikhurairna vāladhivirūpitaiḥ  || 4.67 ||
vinītaistu vrajennityamāśugairlakṣaṇānvitaiḥ  |
varṇarūpopasaṃpannaiḥ pratodenātudan bhṛśam  || 4.68 ||
bālātapaḥ pretadhūmo varjyaṃ bhinnaṃ tathāsanam  |
na chindyānnakharomāṇi dantairnotpāṭayennakhān  || 4.69 ||
na mṛlloṣṭhaṃ ca mṛdnīyānna chindyātkarajaistṛṇam  |
na karma niṣphalaṃ kuryānnāyatyāmasukhodayam  || 4.70 ||
loṣṭhamardī tṛṇacchedī nakhakhādī ca yo naraḥ  |
sa vināśaṃ vrajatyāśu sūcakāśucireva ca  || 4.71 ||
na vigarhya kathāṃ kuryādbahirmālyaṃ na dhārayet |
gavāṃ ca yānaṃ pṛṣṭhena sarvathaiva vigarhitam  || 4.72 ||
advāreṇa ca nātīyādgrāmaṃ vā veśma vāvṛtam  |
rātrau ca vṛkṣamūlāni dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 4.73 ||
nākṣairdīvyetkadā cittu svayaṃ nopānahau haret |
śayanastho na bhuñjīta na pāṇisthaṃ na cāsane  || 4.74 ||
sarvaṃ ca tilasaṃbaddhaṃ nādyādastamite ravau  |
na ca nagnaḥ śayīteha na cocchiṣṭaḥ kva cidvrajet || 4.75 ||
ārdrapādastu bhuñjīta nārdrapādastu saṃviśet |
ārdrapādastu bhuñjāno dīrghamāyuravāpnuyāt || 4.76 ||
acakṣurviṣayaṃ durgaṃ  na prapadyeta karhi cit |
na viṇmūtramudīkṣeta na bāhubhyāṃ nadīṃ taret || 4.77 ||
adhitiṣṭhenna keśāṃstu na bhasmāsthikapālikāḥ  |
na kārpāsāsthi na tuṣān dīrghamāyurjijīviṣuḥ  || 4.78 ||
na saṃvasecca patitairna cāṇḍālairna pulkasaiḥ  |
na mūrkhairnāvaliptaiśca nāntyairnāntyāvasāyibhiḥ  || 4.79 ||
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyānnocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam  |
na cāsyopadiśeddharmaṃ na cāsya vratamādiśet || 4.80 ||
yo hyasya dharmamācaṣṭe yaścaivādiśati vratam  |
so'saṃvṛtaṃ nāma tamaḥ saha tenaiva majjati  || 4.81 ||
na saṃhatābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ kaṇḍūyedātmanaḥ śiraḥ  |
na spṛśeccaitaducchiṣṭo na ca snāyādvinā tataḥ  || 4.82 ||
keśagrahān prahārāṃśca śirasyetān vivarjayet |
śiraḥsnātaśca tailena nāṅgaṃ kiṃ cidapi spṛśet || 4.83 ||
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyādarājanyaprasūtitaḥ  |
sūnācakradhvajavatāṃ veśenaiva ca jīvatām  || 4.84 ||
daśasūnāsamaṃ cakraṃ daśacakrasamo dhvajaḥ  |
daśadhvajasamo veśo daśaveśasamo nṛpaḥ  || 4.85 ||
daśa sūṇāsahasrāṇi yo vāhayati saunikaḥ  |
tena tulyaḥ smṛto rājā ghorastasya pratigrahaḥ  || 4.86 ||
yo rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇāti lubdhasyocchāstravartinaḥ  |
sa paryāyeṇa yātīmānnarakānekaviṃśatim  || 4.87 ||
tāmisramandhatāmisraṃ mahārauravarauravau  |
narakaṃ kālasūtraṃ ca mahānarakameva ca  || 4.88 ||
saṃjīvanaṃ mahāvīciṃ tapanaṃ saṃpratāpanam  |
saṃhātaṃ ca sakākolaṃ kuḍmalaṃ pratimūrtikam  || 4.89 ||
lohaśaṅkumṛjīṣaṃ ca panthānaṃ śālmalīṃ nadīm  |
asipatravanaṃ caiva lohadārakameva ca  || 4.90 ||
etadvidanto vidvāṃso brāhmaṇā brahmavādinaḥ  |
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇanti pretya śreyo'bhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ  || 4.91 ||
brāhme muhūrte budhyeta dharmārthau cānucintayet |
kāyakleśāṃśca tanmūlān vedatattvārthameva ca  || 4.92 ||
utthāyāvaśyakaṃ kṛtvā kṛtaśaucaḥ samāhitaḥ  |
pūrvāṃ saṃdhyāṃ japaṃstiṣṭhetsvakāle cāparāṃ ciram  || 4.93 ||
ṛṣayo dīrghasaṃdhyatvāddīrghamāyuravāpnuyuḥ  |
prajñāṃ yaśaśca kīrtiṃ ca brahmavarcasameva ca  || 4.94 ||
śrāvaṇyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vāpyupākṛtya yathāvidhi  |
yuktaśchandāṃsyadhīyīta māsān vipro'rdhapañcamān  || 4.95 ||
