puṣye tu chandasāṃ kuryādbahirutsarjanaṃ dvijaḥ  |
māghaśuklasya vā prāpte pūrvāhṇe prathame'hani  || 4.96 ||
yathāśāstraṃ tu kṛtvaivamutsargaṃ chandasāṃ bahiḥ  |
virametpakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ tadevaikamaharniśam  || 4.97 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ tu chandāṃsi śukleṣu niyataḥ paṭhet |
vedāṅgāni ca sarvāṇi kṛṣṇapakṣeṣu saṃpaṭhet || 4.98 ||
nāvispaṣṭamadhīyīta na śūdrajanasannidhau  |
na niśānte pariśrānto brahmādhītya punaḥ svapet || 4.99 ||
yathoditena vidhinā nityaṃ chandaskṛtaṃ paṭhet |
brahma chandaskṛtaṃ caiva dvijo yukto hyanāpadi  || 4.100 ||
imānnityamanadhyāyānadhīyāno vivarjayet |
adhyāpanaṃ ca kurvāṇaḥ śiṣyāṇāṃ vidhipūrvakam  || 4.101 ||
karṇaśrave'nile rātrau divā pāṃsusamūhane  |
etau varṣāsvanadhyāyāvadhyāyajñāḥ pracakṣate  || 4.102 ||
vidyutstanitavarṣeṣu maholkānāṃ ca saṃplave  |
ākālikamanadhyāyameteṣu manurabravīt || 4.103 ||
etāṃstvabhyuditān vidyādyadā prāduṣkṛtāgniṣu  |
tadā vidyādanadhyāyamanṛtau cābhradarśane  || 4.104 ||
nirghāte bhūmicalane jyotiṣāṃ copasarjane  |
etānākālikān vidyādanadhyāyānṛtāvapi  || 4.105 ||
prāduṣkṛteṣvagniṣu tu vidyutstanitaniḥsvane  |
sajyotiḥ syādanadhyāyaḥ śeṣe rātrau yathā divā  || 4.106 ||
nityānadhyāya eva syādgrāmeṣu nagareṣu ca  |
dharmanaipuṇyakāmānāṃ pūtigandhe ca sarvadā  || 4.107 ||
antargataśave grāme vṛṣalasya ca sannidhau  |
anadhyāyo rudyamāne samavāye janasya ca  || 4.108 ||
udake madhyarātre ca viṇmūtrasya visarjane  |
ucchiṣṭaḥ śrāddhabhukcaiva manasāpi na cintayet || 4.109 ||
pratigṛhya dvijo vidvānekoddiṣṭasya ketanam  |
tryahaṃ na kīrtayedbrahma rājño rāhośca sūtake  || 4.110 ||
yāvadekānudiṣṭasya gandho lepaśca tiṣṭhati  |
viprasya viduṣo dehe tāvadbrahma na kīrtayet || 4.111 ||
śayānaḥ prauḍhapādaśca kṛtvā caivāvasakthikām  |
nādhīyītāmiṣaṃ jagdhvā sūtakānnādyameva ca  || 4.112 ||
nīhāre bāṇaśabde ca saṃdhyayoreva cobhayoḥ  |
amāvāsyācaturdaśyoḥ paurṇamāsyaṣṭakāsu ca  || 4.113 ||
amāvāsyā guruṃ hanti śiṣyaṃ hanti caturdaśī  |
brahmāṣṭakapaurṇamāsyau tasmāttāḥ parivarjayet || 4.114 ||
pāṃsuvarṣe diśāṃ dāhe gomāyuvirute tathā  |
śvakharoṣṭre ca ruvati paṅktau ca na paṭheddvijaḥ  || 4.115 ||
nādhīyīta śmaśānānte grāmānte govraje'pi vā  |
vasitvā maithunaṃ vāsaḥ śrāddhikaṃ pratigṛhya ca  || 4.116 ||
prāṇi vā yadi vāprāṇi yatkiṃ cicchrāddhikaṃ bhavet |
tadālabhyāpyanadhyāyaḥ pāṇyāsyo hi dvijaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 4.117 ||
corairupadrute grāme saṃbhrame cāgnikārite  |
ākālikamanadhyāyaṃ vidyātsarvādbhuteṣu ca  || 4.118 ||
upākarmaṇi cotsarge trirātraṃ kṣepaṇaṃ smṛtam  |
aṣṭakāsu tvahorātramṛtvantāsu ca rātriṣu  || 4.119 ||
nādhīyītāśvamārūḍho na vṛkṣaṃ na ca hastinam  |
na nāvaṃ na kharaṃ noṣṭraṃ neriṇastho na yānagaḥ  || 4.120 ||
na vivāde na kalahe na senāyāṃ na saṃgare  |
na bhuktamātre nājīrṇe na vamitvā na śuktake  || 4.121 ||
atithiṃ cānanujñāpya mārute vāti vā bhṛśam  |
rudhire ca srute gātrācchastreṇa ca parikṣate  || 4.122 ||
sāmadhvanāvṛgyajuṣī nādhīyīta kadā cana  |
vedasyādhītya vāpyantamāraṇyakamadhītya ca  || 4.123 ||
ṛgvedo devadaivatyo yajurvedastu mānuṣaḥ  |
sāmavedaḥ smṛtaḥ pitryastasmāttasyāśucirdhvaniḥ  || 4.124 ||
etadvidvanto vidvāṃsastrayīniṣkarṣamanvaham  |
kramataḥ pūrvamabhyasya paścādvedamadhīyate  || 4.125 ||
paśumaṇḍūkamārjāra- śvasarpanakulākhubhiḥ  |
antarāgamane vidyādanadhyāyamaharniśam  || 4.126 ||
dvāveva varjayennityamanadhyāyau prayatnataḥ  |
svādhyāyabhūmiṃ cāśuddhamātmānaṃ cāśuciṃ dvijaḥ  || 4.127 ||
amāvāsyāmaṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm  |
brahmacārī bhavennityamapyṛtau snātako dvijaḥ  || 4.128 ||
na snānamācaredbhuktvā nāturo na mahāniśi  |
na vāsobhiḥ sahājasraṃ nāvijñāte jalāśaye  || 4.129 ||
devatānāṃ guro rājñaḥ snātakācāryayostathā  |
nākrāmetkāmataśchāyāṃ babhruṇo dīkṣitasya ca  || 4.130 ||
madhyaṃdine'rdharātre ca śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā ca sāmiṣam  |
saṃdhyayorubhayoścaiva na seveta catuṣpatham  || 4.131 ||
udvartanamapasnānaṃ viṇmūtre raktameva ca  |
śleśmaniṣṭhyūtavāntāni nādhitiṣṭhettu kāmataḥ  || 4.132 ||
vairiṇaṃ nopaseveta sahāyaṃ caiva vairiṇaḥ  |
adhārmikaṃ taskaraṃ ca parasyaiva ca yoṣitaṃ  || 4.133 ||
na hīdṛśamanāyuṣyaṃ loke kiṃ cana vidyate  |
yādṛśaṃ puruṣasyeha paradāropasevanam  || 4.134 ||
kṣatriyaṃ caiva sarpaṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ ca bahuśrutam  |
nāvamanyeta vai bhūṣṇuḥ kṛśānapi kadā cana  || 4.135 ||
etattrayaṃ hi puruṣaṃ nirdahedavamānitam  |
tasmādetattrayaṃ nityaṃ nāvamanyeta buddhimān  || 4.136 ||
nātmānamavamanyeta purvābhirasamṛddhibhiḥ  |
ā mṛtyoḥ śriyamanvicchennaināṃ manyeta durlabhām  || 4.137 ||
satyaṃ brūyātpriyaṃ brūyānna brūyātsatyamapriyam  |
priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyādeṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ  || 4.138 ||
bhadraṃ bhadramiti brūyādbhadramityeva vā vadet |
śuṣkavairaṃ vivādaṃ ca na kuryātkena citsaha  || 4.139 ||
nātikalyaṃ nātisāyaṃ nātimadhyaṃdine sthite  |
nājñātena samaṃ gacchennaiko na vṛṣalaiḥ saha  || 4.140 ||
hīnāṅgānatiriktāṅgān vidyāhīnān vayo'dhikān  |
rūpadraviṇahīnāṃśca jātihīnāṃśca nākṣipet || 4.141 ||
na spṛśetpāṇinocchiṣṭo vipro gobrāhmaṇānalāṇ |
na cāpi paśyedaśuciḥ sustho jyotirgaṇān divā  || 4.142 ||
spṛṣṭvaitānaśucirnityamadbhiḥ prāṇānupaspṛśet |
gātrāṇi caiva sarvāṇi nābhiṃ pāṇitalena tu  || 4.143 ||
anāturaḥ svāni khāni na spṛśedanimittataḥ  |
romāṇi ca rahasyāni sarvāṇyeva vivarjayet || 4.144 ||
maṅgalācārayuktaḥ syātprayatātmā jitendriyaḥ  |
japecca juhuyāccaiva nityamagnimatandritaḥ  || 4.145 ||
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ nityaṃ ca prayatātmanām  |
japatāṃ juhvatāṃ caiva vinipāto na vidyate  || 4.146 ||
vedamevābhyasennityaṃ yathākālamatandritaḥ  |
taṃ hyasyāhuḥ paraṃ dharmamupadharmo'nya ucyate  || 4.147 ḥḥ
vedābhyāsena satataṃ śaucena tapasaiva ca  |
adroheṇa ca bhūtānāṃ jātiṃ smarati paurvikīm  || 4.148 ||
paurvikīṃ saṃsmaran jātiṃ brahmaivābhyasyate punaḥ  |
brahmābhyāsena cājasramanantaṃ sukhamaśnute  || 4.149 ||
sāvitrāñśāntihomāṃśca kuryātparvasu nityaśaḥ  |
pitṝṃścaivāṣṭakāsvarcennityamanvaṣṭakāsu ca  || 4.150 ||
dūrādāvasathānmūtraṃ dūrātpādāvasecanam  |
ucchiṣṭānnaniṣekaṃ ca dūrādeva samācaret || 4.151 ||
maitraṃ prasādhanaṃ snānaṃ dantadhāvanamañjanam  |
pūrvāhṇa eva kurvīta devatānāṃ ca pūjanam  || 4.152 ||
daivatānyabhigacchettu dhārmikāṃśca dvijottamān  |
īśvaraṃ caiva rakṣārthaṃ gurūneva ca parvasu  || 4.153 ||
abhivādayedvṛddhāṃśca dadyāccaivāsanaṃ svakam  |
kṛtāñjalirupāsīta gacchataḥ pṛṣṭhato'nviyāt || 4.154 ||
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ samyaṅnibaddhaṃ sveṣu karmasu  |
dharmamūlaṃ niṣeveta sadācāramatandritaḥ  || 4.155 ||
ācārāllabhate hyāyurācārādīpsitāḥ prajāḥ  |
ācārāddhanamakṣayyamācāro hantyalakṣaṇam  || 4.156 ||
durācāro hi puruṣo loke bhavati ninditaḥ  |
duḥkhabhāgī ca satataṃ vyādhito'lpāyureva ca  || 4.157 ||
sarvalakṣaṇahīno'pi yaḥ sadācāravānnaraḥ  |
śraddadhāno'nasūyaśca śataṃ varṣāṇi jīvati  || 4.158 ||
yadyatparavaśaṃ karma tattadyatnena varjayet |
yadyadātmavaśaṃ tu syāttattatseveta yatnataḥ  || 4.159 ||
sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkhaṃ sarvamātmavaśaṃ sukham  |
etadvidyātsamāsena lakṣaṇaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ  || 4.160 ||
yatkarma kurvato'sya syātparitoṣo'ntarātmanaḥ  |
tatprayatnena kurvīta viparītaṃ tu varjayet || 4.161 ||
ācāryaṃ ca pravaktāraṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum  |
na hiṃsyādbrāhmaṇān gāśca sarvāṃścaiva tapasvinaḥ  || 4.162 ||
nāstikyaṃ vedanindāṃ ca devatānāṃ ca kutsanam  |
dveṣaṃ dambhaṃ ca mānaṃ ca krodhaṃ taikṣṇyaṃ ca varjayet || 4.163 ||
parasya daṇḍaṃ nodyacchetkruddho nainaṃ nipātayet |
anyatra putrācchiṣyādvā śiṣṭyarthaṃ tāḍayettu tau  || 4.164 ||
brāhmaṇāyāvaguryaiva dvijātirvadhakāmyayā  |
śataṃ varṣāṇi tāmisre narake parivartate  || 4.165 ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi saṃrambhānmatipūrvakam  |
ekaviṃśatīmājātīḥ pāpayoniṣu jāyate  || 4.166 ||
ayudhyamānasyotpādya brāhmaṇasyāsṛgaṅgataḥ  |
duḥkhaṃ sumahadāpnoti pretyāprājñatayā naraḥ  || 4.167 ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāti mahītalāt |
tāvato'bdānamutrānyaiḥ śoṇitotpādako'dyate  || 4.168 ||
na kadā ciddvije tasmādvidvānavaguredapi  |
na tāḍayettṛṇenāpi na gātrātsrāvayedasṛk || 4.169 ||
adhārmiko naro yo hi yasya cāpyanṛtaṃ dhanam  |
hiṃsārataśca yo nityaṃ nehāsau sukhamedhate  || 4.170 ||
na sīdannapi dharmeṇa mano'dharme niveśayet |
adhārmikānāṃ pāpānāmāśu paśyan viparyayam  || 4.171 ||
nādharmaścarito loke sadyaḥ phalati gauriva  |
śanairāvartyamānastu karturmūlāni kṛntati  || 4.172 ||
yadi nātmani putreṣu na cetputreṣu naptṛṣu  |
na tveva tu kṛto'dharmaḥ karturbhavati niṣphalaḥ  || 4.173 ||
adharmeṇaidhate tāvattato bhadrāṇi paśyati  |
tataḥ sapatnān jayati samūlastu vinaśyati  || 4.174 ||
satyadharmāryavṛtteṣu śauce caivārametsadā  |
śiṣyāṃśca śiṣyāddharmeṇa vāgbāhūdarasaṃyataḥ  || 4.175 ||
parityajedarthakāmau yau syātāṃ dharmavarjitau  |
dharmaṃ cāpyasukhodarkaṃ lokasaṃkruṣṭameva ca  || 4.176 ||
na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo'nṛjuḥ  |
na syādvākcapalaścaiva na paradrohakarmadhīḥ  || 4.177 ||
yenāsya pitaro yātā yena yātāḥ pitāmahāḥ  |
tena yāyātsatāṃ mārgaṃ tena gacchanna riṣyati  || 4.178 ||
ṛtvikpurohitācāryairmātulātithisaṃśritaiḥ  |
bālavṛddhāturairvaidyairjñātisaṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ  || 4.179 ||
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ jāmībhirbhrātrā putreṇa bhāryayā  |
duhitrā dāsavargeṇa vivādaṃ na samācaret || 4.180 ||
etairvivādān saṃtyajya sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  |
etairjitaiśca jayati sarvāṃllokānimān gṛhī  || 4.181 ||
ācāryo brahmalokeśaḥ prājāpatye pitā prabhuḥ  |
atithistvindralokeśo devalokasya ca rtvijaḥ  || 4.182 ||
jāmayo'psarasāṃ loke vaiśvadevasya bāndhavāḥ  |
saṃbandhino hyapāṃ loke pṛthivyāṃ mātṛmātulau  || 4.183 ||
ākāśeśāstu vijñeyā bālavṛddhakṛśāturāḥ  |
bhrātā jyeṣṭhaḥ samaḥ pitrā bhāryā putraḥ svakā tanuḥ  || 4.184 ||
chāyā svo dāsavargaśca duhitā kṛpaṇaṃ param  |
tasmādetairadhikṣiptaḥ sahetāsaṃjvaraḥ sadā  || 4.185 ||
pratigrahasamartho'pi prasaṅgaṃ tatra varjayet |
pratigraheṇa hyasyāśu brāhmaṃ tejaḥ praśāmyati  || 4.186 ||
na dravyāṇāmavijñāya vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ pratigrahe  |
prājñaḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryādavasīdannapi kṣudhā  || 4.187 ||
hiraṇyaṃ bhūmimaśvaṃ gāmannaṃ vāsastilān ghṛtam  |
pratigṛhṇannavidvāṃstu bhasmībhavati dāruvat || 4.188 ||
hiraṇyamāyurannaṃ ca bhūrgauścāpyoṣatastanum  |
aśvaścakṣustvacaṃ vāso ghṛtaṃ tejastilāhprajāḥ  || 4.189 ||
atapāstvanadhīyānaḥ pratigraharucirdvijaḥ  |
ambhasyaśmaplaveneva saha tenaiva majjati  || 4.190 ||
tasmādavidvān bibhiyādyasmāttasmātpratigrahāt |
svalpakenāpyavidvān hi paṅke gauriva sīdati  || 4.191 ||
na vāryapi prayacchettu baiḍālavratike dvije  |
na bakavratike pāpe nāvedavidi dharmavit || 4.192 ||
triṣvapyeteṣu dattaṃ hi vidhināpyarjitaṃ dhanam  |
dāturbhavatyanarthāya paratrādātureva ca  || 4.193 ||
yathā plavenāupalena nimajjatyudake taran  |
tathā nimajjato'dhastādajñau dātṛpratīcchakau  || 4.194 ||
dharmadhvajī sadā lubdhaśchādmiko lokadambhakaḥ  |
baiḍālavratiko jñeyo hiṃsraḥ sarvābhisaṃdhakaḥ  || 4.195 ||
adhodṛṣṭirnaiṣkṛtikaḥ svārthasādhanatatparaḥ  |
śaṭho mithyāvinītaśca bakavratacaro dvijaḥ  || 4.196[197 ] ||
ye bakavratino viprā ye ca mārjāraliṅginaḥ  |
te patantyandhatāmisre tena pāpena karmaṇā  || 4.197[198 ] ||
na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret |
vratena pāpaṃ pracchādya kurvan strīśūdradambhanam  || 4.198[199 ] ||
pretyeha cedṛśā viprā garhyante brahmavādibhiḥ  |
chadmanā caritaṃ yacca vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati  || 4.199[200 ] ||
aliṅgī liṅgiveṣeṇa yo vṛttimupajīvati  |
sa liṅgināṃ haratyenastiryagyonau ca jāyate  || 4.200[201 ] ||
parakīyanipāneṣu na snāyāddhi kadā cana  |
nipānakartuḥ snātvā tu duṣkṛtāṃśena lipyate  || 4.201[202 ] ||
yānaśayyāsanānyasya kūpodyānagṛhāṇi ca  |
adattānyupayuñjāna enasaḥ syātturīyabhāk || 4.202[203 ] ||
nadīṣu devakhāteṣu taḍāgeṣu saraḥsu ca  |
snānaṃ samācarennityaṃ gartaprasravaṇeṣu ca  || 4.203[204 ] ||
yamān seveta satataṃ na nityaṃ niyamān budhaḥ  |
yamān patatyakurvāṇo niyamān kevalān bhajan  || 4.204[205 ] ||
nāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājikṛte tathā  |
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kva cit || 4.205[206 ] ||
aślīkametatsādhūnāṃ yatra juhvatyamī haviḥ  |
pratīpametaddevānāṃ tasmāttatparivarjayet || 4.206[207 ] ||
mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca na bhuñjīta kadā cana  |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca padā spṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ  || 4.207[208 ] ||
bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitaṃ caiva saṃspṛṣṭaṃ cāpyudakyayā  |
patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ ca śunā saṃspṛṣṭameva ca  || 4.208[209 ] ||
gavā cānnamupaghrātaṃ ghuṣṭānnaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca viduṣā ca jugupsitam  || 4.209[210 ] ||
stenagāyanayoścānnaṃ takṣṇo vārdhuṣikasya ca  |
dīkṣitasya kadaryasya baddhasya nigaḍasya ca  || 4.210[211 ] ||
abhiśastasya ṣaṇḍhasya puṃścalyā dāmbhikasya ca  |
śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ caiva śūdrasyocchiṣṭameva ca  || 4.211[212 ] ||
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ krūrasyocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ  |
ugrānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca paryācāntamanirdaśam  || 4.212[213 ] ||
anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsamavīrāyāśca yoṣitaḥ  |
dviṣadannaṃ nagaryannaṃ patitānnamavakṣutam  || 4.213[214 ] ||
piśunānṛtinoścānnaṃ kratuvikrayiṇastathā  |
śailūṣatunnavāyānnaṃ kṛtaghnasyānnameva ca  || 4.214[215 ] ||
karmārasya niṣādasya raṅgāvatārakasya ca  |
suvarṇakarturveṇasya śastravikrayiṇastathā  || 4.215[216 ] ||
śvavatāṃ śauṇḍikānāṃ ca cailanirṇejakasya ca  |
rañjakasya nṛśaṃsasya yasya copapatirgṛhe  || 4.216[217 ] ||
mṛṣyanti ye copapatiṃ strījitānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  |
anirdaśaṃ ca pretānnamatuṣṭikarameva ca  || 4.217[218 ] ||
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam  |
āyuḥ suvarṇakārānnaṃ yaśaścarmāvakartinaḥ  || 4.218[219 ] ||
kārukānnaṃ prajāṃ hanti balaṃ nirṇejakasya ca  |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati  || 4.219[220 ] ||
pūyaṃ cikitsakasyānnaṃ puṃścalyāstvannamindriyam  |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ śastravikrayiṇo malam  || 4.220[221 ] ||
ya ete'nye tvabhojyānnāḥ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitāḥ  |
teṣāṃ tvagasthiromāṇi vadantyannaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ  || 4.221[222 ] ||
bhuktvāto'nyatamasyānnamamatyā kṣapaṇaṃ tryaham  |
matyā bhuktvācaretkṛcchraṃ retoviṇmūtrameva ca  || 4.222[223 ] ||
nādyācchūdrasya pakvānnaṃ vidvānaśrāddhino dvijaḥ  |
ādadītāmamevāsmādavṛttāvekarātrikam  || 4.223[224 ] ||
śrotriyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vārdhuṣeḥ  |
mīmāṃsitvobhayaṃ devāḥ samamannamakalpayan  || 4.224[225 ] ||
tān prajāpatirāhaitya mā kṛdhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam  |
śraddhāpūtaṃ vadānyasya hatamaśraddhayetarat || 4.225[226 ] ||
śraddhayeṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ca nityaṃ kuryādatandritaḥ  |
śraddhākṛte hyakṣaye te bhavataḥ svāgatairdhanaiḥ  || 4.226[227 ] ||
dānadharmaṃ niṣeveta nityamaiṣṭikapaurtikam  |
parituṣṭena bhāvena pātramāsādya śaktitaḥ  || 4.227[228 ] ||
yatkiṃ cidapi dātavyaṃ yācitenānasūyayā  |
utpatsyate hi tatpātraṃ yattārayati sarvataḥ  || 4.228[229 ] ||
vāridastṛptimāpnoti sukhamakṣayyamannadaḥ  |
tilapradaḥ prajāmiṣṭāṃ dīpadaścakṣuruttamam  || 4.229[230 ] ||
bhūmido bhūmimāpnoti dīrghamāyurhiraṇyadaḥ  |
gṛhado'gryāṇi veśmāni rūpyado rūpamuttamam  || 4.230[231 ] ||
vāsodaścandrasālokyamaśvisālokyamaśvadaḥ  |
anaḍuhaḥ śriyaṃ puṣṭāṃ godo bradhnasya viṣṭapam  || 4.231[232 ] ||
yānaśayyāprado bhāryāmaiśvaryamabhayapradaḥ  |
dhānyadaḥ śāśvataṃ saukhyaṃ brahmado brahmasārṣṭitām  || 4.232[233 ] ||
sarveṣāmeva dānānāṃ brahmadānaṃ viśiṣyate  |
vāryannagomahīvāsas- tilakāñcanasarpiṣām  || 4.233[234 ] ||
yena yena tu bhāvena yadyaddānaṃ prayacchati  |
tattattenaiva bhāvena prāpnoti pratipūjitaḥ  || 4.234[235 ] ||
yo'rcitaṃ pratigṛhṇāti dadātyarcitameva vā  |
tāvubhau gacchataḥ svargaṃ narakaṃ tu viparyaye  || 4.235[236 ] ||
na vismayeta tapasā vadediṣṭvā ca nānṛtam  |
nārto'pyapavadedviprānna dattvā parikīrtayet || 4.236[237 ] ||
yajño'nṛtena kṣarati tapaḥ kṣarati vismayāt |
āyurviprāpavādena dānaṃ ca parikīrtanāt || 4.237[238 ] ||
dharmaṃ śanaiḥ saṃcinuyādvalmīkamiva puttikāḥ  |
paralokasahāyārthaṃ sarvabhūtānyapīḍayan  || 4.238[239 ] ||
nāmutra hi sahāyārthaṃ pitā mātā ca tiṣṭhataḥ  |
na putradāraṃ na jñātirdharmastiṣṭhati kevalaḥ  || 4.239[240 ] ||
ekaḥ prajāyate jantureka eva pralīyate  |
eko'nubhuṅkte sukṛtameka eva ca duṣkṛtam  || 4.240[241 ] ||
mṛtaṃ śarīramutsṛjya kāṣṭhaloṣṭasamaṃ kṣitau  |
vimukhā bāndhavā yānti dharmastamanugacchati  || 4.241[242 ] ||
tasmāddharmaṃ sahāyārthaṃ nityaṃ saṃcinuyācchanaiḥ  |
dharmeṇa hi sahāyena tamastarati dustaram  || 4.242[243 ] ||
dharmapradhānaṃ puruṣaṃ tapasā hatakilbiṣam  |
paralokaṃ nayatyāśu bhāsvantaṃ khaśarīriṇam  || 4.243[244 ] ||
uttamairuttamairnityaṃ saṃbandhānācaretsaha  |
ninīṣuḥ kulamutkarṣamadhamānadhamāṃstyajet || 4.244[245 ] ||
uttamānuttamāneva gacchan hīnāṃstu varjayan  |
brāhmaṇaḥ śreṣṭhatāmeti pratyavāyena śūdratām  || 4.245[246 ] ||
dṛḍhakārī mṛdurdāntaḥ krūrācārairasaṃvasan  |
ahiṃsro damadānābhyāṃ jayetsvargaṃ tathāvrataḥ  || 4.246[247 ] ||
edhodakaṃ mūlaphalamannamabhyudyataṃ ca yat |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyānmadhvathābhayadakṣiṇām  || 4.247[248 ] ||
āhṛtābhyudyatāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastādapracoditām  |
mene prajāpatirgrāhyāmapi duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ  || 4.248[249 ] ||
nāśnanti pitarastasya daśavarṣāṇi pañca ca  |
na ca havyaṃ vahatyagniryastāmabhyavamanyate  || 4.249[250 ] ||
śayyāṃ gṛhān kuśān gandhānapaḥ puṣpaṃ maṇīn dadhi  |
dhānā matsyān payo māṃsaṃ śākaṃ caiva na nirṇudet || 4.250[251 ] ||
gurūn bhṛtyāṃścojjihīrṣannarciṣyan devatātithīn  |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyānna tu tṛpyetsvayaṃ tataḥ  || 4.251[252 ] ||
guruṣu tvabhyatīteṣu vinā vā tairgṛhe vasan  |
ātmano vṛttimanvicchan gṛhṇīyātsādhutaḥ sadā  || 4.252[253 ] ||
ārdhikaḥ kulamitraṃ ca gopālo dāsanāpitau  |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yāścātmānaṃ nivedayet || 4.253[254 ] ||
yādṛśo'sya bhavedātmā yādṛśaṃ ca cikīrṣitam  |
yathā copacaredenaṃ tathātmānaṃ nivedayet || 4.254[255 ] ||
yo'nyathā santamātmānamanyathā satsu bhāṣate  |
sa pāpakṛttamo loke stena ātmāpahārakaḥ  || 4.255[256 ] ||
vācyarthā niyatāḥ sarve vāṅgūlā vāgviniḥsṛtāḥ  |
tāṃstu yaḥ stenayedvācaṃ sa sarvasteyakṛnnaraḥ  || 4.256[257 ] ||
maharṣipitṛdevānāṃ gatvānṛṇyaṃ yathāvidhi  |
putre sarvaṃ samāsajya vasenmādhyasthyamāśritaḥ  || 4.257[258 ] ||
ekākī cintayennityaṃ vivikte hitamātmanaḥ  |
ekākī cintayāno hi paraṃ śreyo'dhigacchati  || 4.258[259 ] ||
eṣoditā gṛhasthasya vṛttirviprasya śāśvatī  |
snātakavratakalpaśca sattvavṛddhikaraḥ śubhaḥ  || 4.259[260 ] ||
anena vipro vṛttena vartayan vedaśāstravit |
vyapetakalmaṣo nityaṃ brahmaloke mahīyate  || 4.260[261 ] ||

śrutvaitānṛṣayo dharmān snātakasya yathoditān  |
idamūcurmahātmānamanalaprabhavaṃ bhṛgum  || 5.1 ||
evaṃ yathoktaṃ viprāṇāṃ svadharmamanutiṣṭhatām  |
kathaṃ mṛtyuḥ prabhavati vedaśāstravidāṃ prabho  || 5.2 ||
sa tānuvāca dharmātmā maharṣīnmānavo bhṛguḥ  |
śrūyatāṃ yena doṣeṇa mṛtyurviprān jighāṃsati  || 5.3 ||
anabhyāsena vedānāmācārasya ca varjanāt |
ālasyādannadoṣācca mṛtyurviprāñjighāṃsati  || 5.4 ||
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palāṇḍuṃ kavakāni ca  |
abhakṣyāṇi dvijātīnāmamedhyaprabhavāni ca  || 5.5 ||
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vṛścanaprabhavāṃstathā  |
śeluṃ gavyaṃ ca peyūṣaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || 5.6 ||
vṛthā kṛsarasaṃyāvaṃ pāyasāpūpameva ca  |
anupākṛtamāṃsāni devānnāni havīṃṣi ca  || 5.7 ||
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīramauṣṭramaikaśaphaṃ tathā  |
āvikaṃ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāśca goḥ payaḥ  || 5.8 ||
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ māhiṣaṃ vinā  |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśuktāni caiva hi  || 5.9 ||
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ ca śukteṣu sarvaṃ ca dadhisaṃbhavam  |
yāni caivābhiṣūyante puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 5.10 ||
kravyādāñśakunān sarvāṃstathā grāmanivāsinaḥ  |
anirdiṣṭāṃścaikaśaphāṃṣṭiṭṭibhaṃ ca vivarjayet || 5.11 ||
kalaviṅkaṃ plavaṃ haṃsaṃ cakrāhvaṃ grāmakukkuṭam  |
sārasaṃ rajjuvālaṃ ca dātyūhaṃ śukasārike  || 5.12 ||
pratudāñjālapādāṃśca koyaṣṭinakhaviṣkirān  |
nimajjataśca matsyādān saunaṃ vallūrameva ca  || 5.13 ||
bakaṃ caiva balākāṃ ca kākolaṃ khañjarīṭakam  |
matsyādān viḍvarāhāṃśca matsyāneva ca sarvaśaḥ  || 5.14 ||
yo yasya māṃsamaśnāti sa tanmāṃsāda ucyate  |
matsyādaḥ sarvamāṃsādastasmānmatsyān vivarjayet || 5.15 ||
pāṭhīnarohitāvādyau niyuktau havyakavyayoḥ  |
rājīvān siṃhatuṇḍāśca saśalkāścaiva sarvaśaḥ  || 5.16 ||
na bhakṣayedekacarānajñātāṃśca mṛgadvijān  |
bhakṣyeṣvapi samuddiṣṭān sarvān pañcanakhāṃstathā  || 5.17 ||
śvāvidhaṃ śalyakaṃ godhāṃ khaḍgakūrmaśaśāṃstathā  |
bhakṣyān pañcanakheṣvāhuranuṣṭrāṃścaikatodatah || 5.18 ||
chatrākaṃ viḍvarāhaṃ ca laśunaṃ grāmakukkuṭam  |
palāṇḍuṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva matyā jagdhvā pateddvijaḥ  || 5.19 ||
amatyaitāni ṣaḍjagdhvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret |
yaticāndrāyāṇaṃ vāpi śeṣeṣūpavasedahaḥ  || 5.20 ||
saṃvatsarasyaikamapi caretkṛcchraṃ dvijottamaḥ  |
ajñātabhuktaśuddhyarthaṃ jñātasya tu viṣeśataḥ  || 5.21 ||
yajñārthaṃ brāhmaṇairvadhyāḥ praśastā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ  |
bhṛtyānāṃ caiva vṛttyarthamagastyo hyācaratpurā  || 5.22 ||
babhūvurhi puroḍāśā bhakṣyāṇāṃ mṛgapakṣiṇām  |
purāṇeṣvapi yajñeṣu brahmakṣatrasaveṣu ca  || 5.23 ||
yatkiṃ citsnehasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyamagarhitam  |
tatparyuṣitamapyādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca yadbhavet || 5.24 ||
cirasthitamapi tvādyamasnehāktaṃ dvijātibhiḥ  |
yavagodhūmajaṃ sarvaṃ payasaścaiva vikriyā  || 5.25 ||
etaduktaṃ dvijātīnāṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyamaśeṣataḥ  |
māṃsasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇavarjane  || 5.26 ||
prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayenmāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā  |
yathāvidhi niyuktastu prāṇānāmeva cātyaye  || 5.27 ||
prāṇasyānnamidaṃ sarvaṃ prajāpatirakalpayat |
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva sarvaṃ prāṇasya bhojanam  || 5.28 ||
carāṇāmannamacarā daṃṣṭriṇāmapyadaṃṣṭriṇaḥ  |
ahastāśca sahastānāṃ śūrāṇāṃ caiva bhīravaḥ  || 5.29 ||
nāttā duṣyatyadannādyān prāṇino'hanyahanyapi  |
dhātraiva sṛṣṭā hyādyāśca prāṇino'ttāra eva ca  || 5.30 ||
yajñāya jagdhirmāṃsasyetyeṣa daivo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ  |
ato'nyathā pravṛttistu rākṣaso vidhirucyate  || 5.31 ||
krītvā svayaṃ vāpyutpādya paropakṛtameva vā  |
devān pitṝṃścārcayitvā khādanmāṃsaṃ na duṣyati  || 5.32 ||
nādyādavidhinā māṃsaṃ vidhijño'nāpadi dvijaḥ  |
jagdhvā hyavidhinā māṃsaṃ pretastairadyate'vaśaḥ  || 5.33 ||
na tādṛśaṃ bhavatyeno mṛgahanturdhanārthinaḥ  |
yādṛśaṃ bhavati pretya vṛthāmāṃsāni khādataḥ  || 5.34 ||
niyuktastu yathānyāyaṃ yo māṃsaṃ nātti mānavaḥ  |
sa pretya paśutāṃ yāti saṃbhavānekaviṃśatim  || 5.35 ||
asaṃskṛtān paśūnmantrairnādyādvipraḥ kadā cana  |
mantraistu saṃskṛtānadyācchāśvataṃ vidhimāsthitaḥ  || 5.36 ||
kuryādghṛtapaśuṃ saṅge kuryātpiṣṭapaśuṃ tathā  |
na tveva tu vṛthā hantuṃ paśumicchetkadā cana  || 5.37 ||
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvatkṛtvo ha māraṇam  |
vṛthāpaśughnaḥ prāpnoti pretya janmani janmani  || 5.38 ||
yajñārthaṃ paśavaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ svayameva svayaṃbhuvā  |
yajño'sya bhūtyai sarvasya tasmādyajñe vadho'vadhaḥ  || 5.39 ||
oṣadhyaḥ paśavo vṛkṣāstiryañcaḥ pakṣiṇastathā  |
yajñārthaṃ nidhanaṃ prāptāḥ prāpnuvantyutsṛtīḥ punaḥ  || 5.40 ||
madhuparke ca yajñe ca pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi  |
atraiva paśavo hiṃsyā nānyatretyabravīnmanuḥ  || 5.41 ||
eṣvartheṣu paśūn hiṃsan vedatattvārthaviddvijaḥ  |
ātmānaṃ ca paśuṃ caiva gamayatyuttamaṃ gatim  || 5.42 ||
gṛhe gurāvaraṇye vā nivasannātmavān dvijaḥ  |
nāvedavihitāṃ hiṃsāmāpadyapi samācaret || 5.43 ||
yā vedavihitā hiṃsā niyatāsmiṃścarācare  |
ahiṃsāmeva tāṃ vidyādvedāddharmo hi nirbabhau  || 5.44 ||
yo'hiṃsakāni bhūtāni hinastyātmasukhecchayā  |
sa jīvāṃśca mṛtaścaiva na kva citsukhamedhate  || 5.45 ||
yo bandhanavadhakleśān prāṇināṃ na cikīrṣati  |
sa sarvasya hitaprepsuḥ sukhamatyantamaśnute  || 5.46 ||
yaddhyāyati yatkurute ratiṃ badhnāti yatra ca  |
tadavāpnotyayatnena yo hinasti na kiṃ cana  || 5.47 ||
nākṛtvā prāṇināṃ hiṃsāṃ māṃsamutpadyate kva cit |
na ca prāṇivadhaḥ svargyastasmānmāṃsaṃ vivarjayet || 5.48 ||
samutpattiṃ ca māṃsasya vadhabandhau ca dehinām  |
prasamīkṣya nivarteta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt || 5.49 ||
na bhakṣayati yo māṃsaṃ vidhiṃ hitvā piśācavat |
na loke priyatāṃ yāti vyādhibhiśca na pīḍyate  || 5.50 ||
anumantā viśasitā nihantā krayavikrayī  |
saṃskartā copahartā ca khādakaśceti ghātakāḥ  || 5.51 ||
svamāṃsaṃ paramāṃsena yo vardhayitumicchati  |
anabhyarcya pitṝn devāṃstato'nyo nāstyapuṇyakṛt || 5.52 ||
varṣe varṣe'śvamedhena yo yajeta śataṃ samāḥ  |
māṃsāni ca na khādedyastayoḥ puṇyaphalaṃ samam  || 5.53 ||
phalamūlāśanairmedhyairmunyannānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ  |
na tatphalamavāpnoti yanmāṃsaparivarjanāt || 5.54 ||
māṃ sa bhakṣayitāmutra yasya māṃsamihādmyaham  |
etanmāṃsasya māṃsatvaṃ pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ  || 5.55 ||
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune  |
pravṛttireṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttistu mahāphalā  || 5.56 ||
pretaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi dravyaśuddhiṃ tathaiva ca  |
caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  || 5.57 ||
dantajāte'nujāte ca kṛtacūḍe ca saṃsthite  |
aśuddhā bāndhavāḥ sarve sūtake ca tathocyate  || 5.58 ḥḥ
daśāhaṃ śāvamāśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate  |
arvāksaṃcayanādasthnāṃ tryahamekāhameva vā  || 5.59 ||
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate  |
samānodakabhāvastu janmanāmnoravedane  || 5.60 ||
yathedaṃ śāvamāśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate  |
janane'pyevameva syānnipuṇaṃ śuddhimicchatām  || 5.61 ||
sarveṣāṃ śāvamāśaucaṃ mātāpitrostu sūtakam  |
sūtakaṃ mātureva syādupaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ  || 5.62[61 ] ||
nirasya tu pumāñśukramupaspṛsyaiva śudhyati  |
baijikādabhisaṃbandhādanurundhyādaghaṃ tryaham  || 5.63[62 ] ||
ahnā caikena rātryā ca trirātraireva ca tribhiḥ  |
śavaspṛśo viśudhyanti tryahādudakadāyinaḥ  || 5.64[63 ] ||
guroḥ pretasya śiṣyastu pitṛmedhaṃ samācaran  |
pretahāraiḥ samaṃ tatra daśarātreṇa śudhyati  || 5.65[64 ] ||
rātribhirmāsatulyābhirgarbhasrāve viśudhyati  |
rajasyuparate sādhvī snānena strī rajasvalā  || 5.66[65 ] ||
nṛṇāmakṛtacūḍānāṃ viśuddhirnaiśikī smṛtā  |
nirvṛttacūḍakānāṃ tu trirātrācchuddhiriṣyate  || 5.67[66 ] ||
ūnadvivārṣikaṃ pretaṃ nidadhyurbāndhavā bahiḥ  |
alaṃkṛtya śucau bhūmāvasthisaṃcayanādṛte  || 5.68[67 ] ||
nāsya kāryo'gnisaṃskāro na ca kāryodakakriyā  |
araṇye kāṣṭhavattyaktvā kṣapeyustryahameva tu  || 5.69[68 ] ||
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavairudakakriyā  |
jātadantasya vā kuryurnāmni vāpi kṛte sati  || 5.70[69 ] ||
sabrahmacāriṇyekāhamatīte kṣapaṇaṃ smṛtam  |
janmanyekodakānāṃ tu trirātrācchuddhiriṣyate  || 5.71[70 ] ||
strīṇāmasaṃskṛtānāṃ tu tryahācchudhyanti bāndhavāḥ  |
yathoktenaiva kalpena śudhyanti tu sanābhayaḥ  || 5.72[71 ] ||
akṣāralavaṇānnāḥ syurnimajjeyuśca te tryaham  |
māṃsāśanaṃ ca nāśnīyuḥ śayīraṃśca pṛthakkṣitau  || 5.73[72 ] ||
saṃnidhāveṣa vai kalpaḥ śāvāśaucasya kīrtitaḥ  |
asaṃnidhāvayaṃ jñeyo vidhiḥ saṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ  || 5.74[73 ] ||
vigataṃ tu videśasthaṃ śṛṇuyādyo hyanirdaśam  |
yaccheṣaṃ daśarātrasya tāvadevāśucirbhavet || 5.75[74 ] ||
atikrānte daśāhe ca trirātramaśucirbhavet |
saṃvatsare vyatīte tu spṛṣṭvaivāpo viśudhyati  || 5.76[75 ] ||
nirdaśaṃ jñātimaraṇaṃ śrutvā putrasya janma ca  |
savāsā jalamāplutya śuddho bhavati mānavaḥ  || 5.77[76 ] ||
bāle deśāntarasthe ca pṛthakpiṇḍe ca saṃsthite  |
savāsā jalamāplutya sadya eva viśudhyati  || 5.78[77 ] ||
antardaśāhe syātāṃ cetpunarmaraṇajanmanī  |
tāvatsyādaśucirvipro yāvattatsyādanirdaśam  || 5.79[78 ] ||
trirātramāhurāśaucamācārye saṃsthite sati  |
tasya putre ca patnyāṃ ca divārātramiti sthitiḥ  || 5.80[79 ] ||
śrotriye tūpasaṃpanne trirātramaśucirbhavet |
mātule pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ śiṣyartvigbāndhaveṣu ca  || 5.81[80 ] ||
prete rājani sajyotiryasya syādviṣaye sthitaḥ  |
aśrotriye tvahaḥ kṛtsnamanūcāne tathā gurau  || 5.82[81 ] ||
śuddhyedvipro daśāhena dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ  |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śudhyati  || 5.83[82 ] ||
na vardhayedaghāhāni pratyūhennāgniṣu kriyāḥ  |
na ca tatkarma kurvāṇaḥ sanābhyo'pyaśucirbhavet || 5.84[83 ] ||
divākīrtimudakyāṃ ca patitaṃ sūtikāṃ tathā  |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyati  || 5.85[84 ] ||
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japedaśucidarśane  |
saurānmantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīśca śaktitaḥ  || 5.86[85 ] ||
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati  |
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gāmālabhyārkamīkṣya vā  || 5.87[86 ] ||
ādiṣṭī nodakaṃ kuryādā vratasya samāpanāt |
samāpte tūdakaṃ kṛtvā trirātreṇaiva śudhyati  || 5.88[87 ] ||
vṛthāsaṃkarajātānāṃ pravrajyāsu ca tiṣṭhatām  |
ātmanastyāgināṃ caiva nivartetodakakriyā  || 5.89[88 ] ||
pāṣaṇḍamāśritānāṃ ca carantīnāṃ ca kāmataḥ  |
garbhabhartṛdruhāṃ caiva surāpīnāṃ ca yoṣitām  || 5.90[89 ] ||
ācāryaṃ svamupādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum  |
nirhṛtya tu vratī pretānna vratena viyujyate  || 5.91[90 ] ||
dakṣiṇena mṛtaṃ śūdraṃ puradvāreṇa nirharet |
paścimottarapūrvaistu yathāyogaṃ dvijanmanaḥ  || 5.92[91 ] ||
na rājñāmaghadoṣo'sti vratināṃ na ca sattriṇām  |
aindraṃ sthānamupāsīnā brahmabhūtā hi te sadā  || 5.93[92 ] ||
rājño mahātmike sthāne sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate  |
prajānāṃ parirakṣārthamāsanaṃ cātra kāraṇam  || 5.94[93 ] ||
ḍimbhāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca  |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ  || 5.95[94 ] ||
somāgnyarkānilendrāṇāṃ vittāppatyoryamasya ca  |
aṣṭānāṃ lokapālānāṃ vapurdhārayate nṛpaḥ  || 5.96[95 ] ||
lokeśādhiṣṭhito rājā nāsyāśaucaṃ vidhīyate  |
śaucāśaucaṃ hi martyānāṃ lokebhyaḥ prabhavāpyayau  || 5.97[96 ] ||
udyatairāhave śastraiḥ kṣatradharmahatasya ca  |
sadyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate yajñastathāśaucamiti sthitiḥ  || 5.98[97 ] ||
vipraḥ śudhyatyapaḥ spṛṣṭvā kṣatriyo vāhanāyudham  |
vaiśyaḥ pratodaṃ raśmīn vā yaṣṭiṃ śūdraḥ kṛtakriyaḥ  || 5.99[98 ] ||
etadvo'bhihitaṃ śaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu dvijottamāḥ  |
asapiṇḍeṣu sarveṣu pretaśuddhiṃ nibodhata  || 5.100[99 ] ||
asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa māturāptāṃśca bāndhavān  || 5.101[100 ] ||
yadyannamatti teṣāṃ tu daśāhenaiva śudhyati  |
anadannannamahnaiva na cettasmin gṛhe vaset || 5.102[101 ] ||
anugamyecchayā pretaṃ jñātimajñātimeva ca  |
snātvā sacailaḥ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati  || 5.103[102 ] ||
na vipraṃ sveṣu tiṣṭhatsu mṛtaṃ śūdreṇa nāyayet |
asvargyā hyāhutiḥ sā syācchūdrasaṃsparśadūṣitā  || 5.104[103 ] ||
jñānaṃ tapo'gnirāhāro mṛnmano vāryupāñjanam  |
vāyuḥ karmārkakālau ca śuddheḥ kartṝṇi dehinām  || 5.105[104 ] ||
sarveṣāmeva śaucānāmarthaśaucaṃ paraṃ smṛtaṃ  |
yo'rthe śucirhi sa śucirna mṛdvāriśuciḥ śuciḥ  || 5.106[105 ] ||
kṣāntyā śudhyanti vidvāṃso dānenākāryakāriṇaḥ  |
pracchannapāpā japyena tapasā vedavittamāḥ  || 5.107[106 ] ||
mṛttoyaiḥ śudhyate śodhyaṃ nadī vegena śudhyati  |
rajasā strī manoduṣṭā saṃnyāsena dvijottamāḥ  || 5.108[107 ] ||
adbhirgātrāṇi śudhyanti manaḥ satyena śudhyati  |
vidyātapobhyāṃ bhūtātmā buddhirjñānena śudhyati  || 5.109[108 ] ||
eṣa śaucasya vaḥ proktaḥ śarīrasya vinirṇayaḥ  |
nānāvidhānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śuddheḥ śṛṇuta nirṇayam  || 5.110[109 ] ||
taijasānāṃ maṇīnāṃ ca sarvasyāśmamayasya ca  |
bhasmanādbhirmṛdā caiva śuddhiruktā manīṣibhiḥ  || 5.111[110 ] ||
nirlepaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhāṇḍamadbhireva viśudhyati  |
abjamaśmamayaṃ caiva rājataṃ cānupaskṛtam  || 5.112[111 ] ||
apāmagneśca saṃyogāddhaimaṃ raupyaṃ ca nirbabhau  |
tasmāttayoḥ svayonyaiva nirṇeko guṇavattaraḥ  || 5.113[112 ] ||
tāmrāyaḥkāṃsyaraityānāṃ trapuṇaḥ sīsakasya ca  |
śaucaṃ yathārhaṃ kartavyaṃ kṣārāmlodakavāribhiḥ  || 5.114[113 ] ||
dravāṇāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ śuddhirutpavanaṃ smṛtam  |
prokṣaṇaṃ saṃhatānāṃ ca dāravāṇāṃ ca takṣaṇam  || 5.115[114 ] ||
mārjanaṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā yajñakarmaṇi  |
camasānāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca śuddhiḥ prakṣālanena tu  || 5.116[115 ] ||
carūṇāṃ sruksruvāṇāṃ ca śuddhiruṣṇena vāriṇā  |
sphyaśūrpaśakaṭānāṃ ca musalolūkhalasya ca  || 5.117[116 ] ||
adbhistu prokṣaṇaṃ śaucaṃ bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsasām  |
prakṣālanena tvalpānāmadbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate  || 5.118[117 ] ||
cailavaccarmaṇāṃ śuddhirvaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca  |
śākamūlaphalānāṃ ca dhānyavacchuddhiriṣyate  || 5.119[118 ] ||
kauśeyāvikayorūṣaiḥ kutapānāmariṣṭakaiḥ  |
śrīphalairaṃśupaṭṭānāṃ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ  || 5.120[119 ] ||
kṣaumavacchaṅkhaśṛṅgāṇāmasthidantamayasya ca  |
śuddhirvijānatā kāryā gomūtreṇodakena vā  || 5.121[120 ] ||
prokṣaṇāttṛṇakāṣṭhaṃ ca palālaṃ caiva śudhyati  |
mārjanopāñjanairveśma punaḥpākena mṛnmayam  || 5.122[121 ] ||
madyairmūtraiḥ purīṣairvā ṣṭhīvanaihpūyaśoṇitaiḥ  |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śuddhyeta punaḥpākena mṛnmayam  || 5.123 ||
saṃmārjanopāñjanena sekenollekhanena ca  |
gavāṃ ca parivāsena bhūmiḥ śudhyati pañcabhiḥ  || 5.124[122 ] ||
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavā ghrātamavadhūtamavakṣutam  |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiśca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati  || 5.125[123 ] ||
yāvannāpaityamedhyāktādgandho lepaśca tatkṛtaḥ  |
tāvanmṛdvāri cādeyaṃ sarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu  || 5.126[124 ] ||
trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānāmakalpayan  |
adṛṣṭamadbhirnirṇiktaṃ yacca vācā praśasyate  || 5.127[125 ] ||
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatā vaitṛṣṇyaṃ yāsu gorbhavet |
avyāptāścedamedhyena gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ  || 5.128[126 ] ||
nityaṃ śuddhaḥ kāruhastaḥ paṇye yacca prasāritam  |
brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣyaṃ nityaṃ medhyamiti sthitiḥ  || 5.129[127 ] ||
nityamāsyaṃ śuci strīṇāṃ śakuniḥ phalapātane  |
prasrave ca śucirvatsaḥ śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ  || 5.130[128 ] ||
śvabhirhatasya yanmāṃsaṃ śuci tanmanurabravīt |
kravyādbhiśca hatasyānyaiścaṇḍālādyaiśca dasyubhiḥ  || 5.131[129 ] ||
ūrdhvaṃ nābheryāni khāni tāni medhyāni sarvaśaḥ  |
yānyadhastānyamedhyāni dehāccaiva malāścyutāḥ  || 5.132[130 ] ||
makṣikā vipruṣaśchāyā gauraśvaḥ sūryaraśmayaḥ  |
rajo bhūrvāyuragniśca sparśe medhyāni nirdiśet || 5.133[131 ] ||
viṇmūtrotsargaśuddhyarthaṃ mṛdvāryādeyamarthavat |
daihikānāṃ malānāṃ ca śuddhiṣu dvādaśasvapi  || 5.134[132 ] ||
vasā śukramasṛṅmajjā mūtraviṭghrāṇakarṇaviṭ |
śleśma aśru dūṣikā svedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ  || 5.135[133 ] ||
ekā liṅge gude tisrastathaikatra kare daśa  |
ubhayoḥ sapta dātavyā mṛdaḥ śuddhimabhīpsatā  || 5.136[134 ] ||
etacchaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām  |
triguṇaṃ syādvanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam  || 5.137[135 ] ||
kṛtvā mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā khānyācānta upaspṛśet |
vedamadhyeṣyamāṇaśca annamaśnaṃśca sarvadā  || 5.138[136 ] ||
trirācāmedapaḥ pūrvaṃ dviḥ pramṛjyāttato mukham  |
śarīraṃ śaucamicchan hi strī śūdrastu sakṛtsakṛt || 5.139[137 ] ||
śūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartinām  |
vaiśyavacchaucakalpaśca dvijocchiṣṭaṃ ca bhojanam  || 5.140[138 ] ||
nocchiṣṭaṃ kurvate mukhyā vipruṣo'ṅgaṃ na yānti yāḥ  |
na śmaśrūṇi gatānyāsyaṃ na dantāntaradhiṣṭhitam  || 5.141[139 ] ||
spṛśanti bindavaḥ pādau ya ācāmayataḥ parān  |
bhaumikaiste samā jñeyā na tairāprayato bhavet || 5.142[140 ] ||
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭo dravyahastaḥ kathaṃ cana  |
anidhāyaiva taddravyamācāntaḥ śucitāmiyāt || 5.143[141 ] ||
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprāśanamācaret |
ācāmedeva bhuktvānnaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam  || 5.144[142 ] ||
suptvā kṣutvā ca bhuktvā ca niṣṭhīvyoktvānṛtāni ca  |
pītvāpo'dhyeṣyamāṇaśca ācāmetprayato'pi san  || 5.145[143 ] ||
eṣāṃ śaucavidhiḥ kṛtsno dravyaśuddhistathaiva ca  |
ukto vaḥ sarvavarṇānāṃ strīṇāṃ dharmānnibodhata  || 5.146[144 ] ||
bālayā vā yuvatyā vā vṛddhayā vāpi yoṣitā  |
na svātantryeṇa kartavyaṃ kiṃ cidkāryaṃ gṛheṣvapi  || 5.147[145 ] ||
bālye piturvaśe tiṣṭhetpāṇigrāhasya yauvane  |
putrāṇāṃ bhartari prete na bhajetstrī svatantratām  || 5.148[146 ] ||
pitrā bhartrā sutairvāpi necchedvirahamātmanaḥ  |
eṣāṃ hi viraheṇa strī garhye kuryādubhe kule  || 5.149[147 ] ||
sadā prahṛṣṭayā bhāvyaṃ gṛhakārye ca dakṣayā  |
susaṃskṛtopaskarayā vyaye cāmuktahastayā  || 5.150[148 ] ||
yasmai dadyātpitā tvenāṃ bhrātā vānumate pituḥ  |
taṃ śuśrūṣeta jīvantaṃ saṃsthitaṃ ca na laṅghayet || 5.151[149 ] ||
maṅgalārthaṃ svastyayanaṃ yajñaścāsāṃ prajāpateḥ  |
prayujyate vivāhe tu pradānaṃ svāmyakāraṇam  || 5.152[150 ] ||
anṛtāvṛtukāle ca mantrasaṃskārakṛtpatiḥ  |
sukhasya nityaṃ dāteha paraloke ca yoṣitaḥ  || 5.153[151 ] ||
viśīlaḥ kāmavṛtto vā guṇairvā parivarjitaḥ  |
upacāryaḥ striyā sādhvyā satataṃ devavatpatiḥ  || 5.154[152 ] ||
nāsti strīṇāṃ pṛthagyajño na vrataṃ nāpyupoṣaṇam  |
patiṃ śuśrūṣate yena tena svarge mahīyate  || 5.155[153 ] ||
pāṇigrāhasya sādhvī strī jīvato vā mṛtasya vā  |
patilokamabhīpsantī nācaretkiṃ cidapriyam  || 5.156[154 ] ||
kāmaṃ tu ksapayeddehaṃ puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ  |
na tu nāmāpi gṛhṇīyātpatyau prete parasya tu  || 5.157[155 ] ||
āsītā maraṇātksāntā niyatā brahmacāriṇī  |
yo dharma ekapatnīnāṃ kāṅkṣantī tamanuttamam  || 5.158[156 ] ||
anekāni sahasrāṇi kumārabrahmacāriṇām  |
divaṃ gatāni viprāṇāmakṛtvā kulasaṃtatim  || 5.159[157 ] ||
mṛte bhartari sāḍhvī strī brahmacarye vyavasthitā  |
svargaṃ gacchatyaputrāpi yathā te brahmacāriṇaḥ  || 5.160[158 ] ||
apatyalobhādyā tu strī bhartāramativartate  |
seha nindāmavāpnoti paralokācca hīyate  || 5.161[159 ] ||
nānyotpannā prajāstīha na cāpyanyaparigrahe  |
na dvitīyaśca sādhvīnāṃ kva cidbhartopadiśyate  || 5.162[160 ] ||
patiṃ hitvāpakṛṣṭaṃ svamutkṛṣṭaṃ yā niṣevate  |
nindyaiva sā bhavelloke parapūrveti cocyate  || 5.163[161 ] ||
vyabhicārāttu bhartuḥ strī loke prāpnoti nindyatām  |
śṛgālayoniṃ prāpnoti pāparogaiśca pīḍyate  || 5.164[162 ] ||
patiṃ yā nābhicarati manovāgdehasaṃyutā  |
sā bhartṛlokamāpnoti sadbhiḥ sādhvīti cocyate  || 5.165[163 ] ||
anena nārī vṛttena manovāgdehasaṃyatā  |
ihāgryāṃ kīrtimāpnoti patilokaṃ paratra ca  || 5.166[164 ] ||
evaṃ vṛttāṃ savarṇāṃ strīṃ dvijātiḥ pūrvamāriṇīm  |
dāhayedagnihotreṇa yajñapātraiśca dharmavit || 5.167[165 ] ||
bhāryāyai pūrvamāriṇyai dattvāgnīnantyakarmaṇi  |
punardārakriyāṃ kuryātpunarādhānameva ca  || 5.168[166 ] ||
anena vidhinā nityaṃ pañcayajñānna hāpayet |
dvitīyamāyuṣo bhāgaṃ kṛtadāro gṛhe vaset || 5.169[167 ] ||

evaṃ gṛhāśrame sthitvā vidhivatsnātako dvijaḥ  |
vane vasettu niyato yathāvadvijitendriyaḥ  || 6.1 ||
gṛhasthastu yathā paśyedvalīpalitamātmanaḥ  |
apatyasyaiva cāpatyaṃ tadāraṇyaṃ samāśrayet || 6.2 ||
saṃtyajya grāmyamāhāraṃ sarvaṃ caiva paricchadam  |
putreṣu bhāryāṃ nikṣipya vanaṃ gacchetsahaiva vā  || 6.3 ||
agnihotraṃ samādāya gṛhyaṃ cāgniparicchadam  |
grāmādaraṇyaṃ niḥsṛtya nivasenniyatendriyaḥ  || 6.4 ||
munyannairvividhairmedhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena vā  |
etāneva mahāyajñānnirvapedvidhipūrvakam  || 6.5 ||
vasīta carma cīraṃ vā sāyaṃ snāyātprage tathā  |
jaṭāśca bibhṛyānnityaṃ śmaśrulomanakhāni ca  || 6.6 ||
yadbhakṣyaṃ syādtato dadyādbaliṃ bhikṣāṃ ca śaktitaḥ  |
ammūlaphalabhikṣābhirarcayedāśramāgatān  || 6.7 ||
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syāddānto maitraḥ samāhitaḥ  |
dātā nityamanādātā sarvabhūtānukampakaḥ  || 6.8 ||
vaitānikaṃ ca juhuyādagnihotraṃ yathāvidhi  |
darśamaskandayan parva paurṇamāsaṃ ca yogataḥ  || 6.9 ||
ṛkṣeṣṭyāgrayaṇaṃ caiva cāturmāsyāni cāharet |
turāyaṇaṃ ca kramaśo dakṣasyāyanameva ca  || 6.10 ||
vāsantaśāradairmedhyairmunyannaiḥ svayamāhṛtaiḥ  |
puroḍāśāṃścarūṃścaiva vidhivannirvapetpṛthak || 6.11 ||
devatābhyastu taddhutvā vanyaṃ medhyataraṃ haviḥ  |
śeṣamātmani yuñjīta lavaṇaṃ ca svayaṃ kṛtam  || 6.12 ||
sthalajāudakaśākāni puṣpamūlaphalāni ca  |
medhyavṛkṣodbhavānyadyātsnehāṃśca phalasaṃbhavān  || 6.13 ||
varjayenmadhu māṃsaṃ ca bhaumāni kavakāni ca  |
bhūstṛṇaṃ śigrukaṃ caiva śleśmātakaphalāni ca  || 6.14 ||
tyajedāśvayuje māsi munyannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam  |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi śākamūlaphalāni ca  || 6.15 ||
na phālakṛṣṭamaśnīyādutsṛṣṭamapi kena cit |
na grāmajātānyārto'pi mūlāṇi ca phalāni ca  || 6.16 ||
agnipakvāśano vā syātkālapakvabhugeva vā  |
aśmakuṭṭo bhavedvāpi dantolūkhaliko'pi vā  || 6.17 ||
sadyaḥ prakṣālako vā syānmāsasaṃcayiko'pi vā  |
ṣaṇmāsanicayo vā syātsamānicaya eva vā  || 6.18 ||
naktaṃ cānnaṃ samaśnīyāddivā vāhṛtya śaktitaḥ  |
caturthakāliko vā syātsyādvāpyaṣṭamakālikaḥ  || 6.19 ||
cāndrāyaṇavidhānairvā śuklakṛṣṇe ca vartayet |
pakṣāntayorvāpyaśnīyādyavāgūṃ kvathitāṃ sakṛt || 6.20 ||
puṣpamūlaphalairvāpi kevalairvartayetsadā  |
kālapakvaiḥ svayaṃ śīrṇairvaikhānasamate sthitaḥ  || 6.21 ||
bhūmau viparivarteta tiṣṭhedvā prapadairdinam  |
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharetsavaneṣūpayannapaḥ  || 6.22 ||
grīṣme pañcatapāstu syādvarṣāsvabhrāvakāśikaḥ  |
ārdravāsāstu hemante kramaśo vardhayaṃstapaḥ  || 6.23 ||
upaspṛśaṃstriṣavaṇaṃ pitṝn devāṃśca tarpayet |
tapaścaraṃścogrataraṃ śoṣayeddehamātmanaḥ  || 6.24 ||
agnīnātmani vaitānān samāropya yathāvidhi  |
anagniraniketaḥ syānmunirmūlaphalāśanaḥ  || 6.25 ||
aprayatnaḥ sukhārtheṣu brahmacārī dharāśayaḥ  |
śaraṇeṣvamamaścaiva vṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ  || 6.26 ||
tāpaseṣveva vipreṣu yātrikaṃ bhaikṣamāharet |
gṛhamedhiṣu cānyeṣu dvijeṣu vanavāsiṣu  || 6.27 ||
grāmādāhṛtya vāśnīyādaṣṭau grāsān vane vasan  |
pratigṛhya puṭenaiva pāṇinā śakalena vā  || 6.28 ||
etāścānyāśca seveta dīkṣā vipro vane vasan  |
vividhāścāupaniṣadīrātmasaṃsiddhaye śrutīḥ  || 6.29 ||
ṛṣibhirbrāhmaṇaiścaiva gṛhasthaireva sevitāḥ  |
vidyātapovivṛddhyarthaṃ śarīrasya ca śuddhaye  || 6.30 ||
aparājitāṃ vāsthāya vrajeddiśamajihmagaḥ  |
ā nipātāccharīrasya yukto vāryanilāśanaḥ  || 6.31 ||
āsāṃ maharṣicaryāṇāṃ tyaktvānyatamayā tanum  |
vītaśokabhayo vipro brahmaloke mahīyate  || 6.32 ||
vaneṣu ca vihṛtyaivaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bhāgamāyuṣaḥ  |
caturthamāyuṣo bhāgaṃ tyakvā saṅgān parivrajet || 6.33 ||
āśramādāśramaṃ gatvā hutahomo jitendriyaḥ  |
bhikṣābalipariśrāntaḥ pravrajan pretya vardhate  || 6.34 ||
ṛṇāni trīṇyapākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu sevamāno vrajatyadhaḥ  || 6.35 ||
adhītya vidhivadvedān putrāṃścotpādya dharmataḥ  |
iṣṭvā ca śaktito yajñairmano mokṣe niveśayet || 6.36 ||
anadhītya dvijo vedānanutpādya tathā sutān  |
aniṣṭvā caiva yajñaiśca mokṣamicchan vrajatyadhaḥ  || 6.37 ||
prājāpatyaṃ nirupyeṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām  |
ātmanyagnīn samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajedgṛhāt || 6.38 ||
yo dattvā sarvabhūtebhyaḥ pravrajatyabhayaṃ gṛhāt |
tasya tejomayā lokā bhavanti brahmavādinaḥ  || 6.39 ||
yasmādaṇvapi bhūtānāṃ dvijānnotpadyate bhayam  |
tasya dehādvimuktasya bhayaṃ nāsti kutaścana  || 6.40 ||
agārādabhiniṣkrāntaḥ pavitropacito muniḥ  |
samupoḍheṣu kāmeṣu nirapekṣaḥ parivrajet || 6.41 ||
eka eva carennityaṃ siddhyarthamasahāyavān  |
siddhimekasya saṃpaśyanna jahāti na hīyate  || 6.42 ||
anagniraniketaḥ syādgrāmamannārthamāśrayet |
upekṣako'saṃkusuko munirbhāvasamāhitaḥ  || 6.43 ||
kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucelamasahāyatā  |
samatā caiva sarvasminnetanmuktasya lakṣaṇam  || 6.44 ||
nābhinandeta maraṇaṃ nābhinandeta jīvitam  |
kālameva pratīkṣeta nirveśaṃ bhṛtako yathā  || 6.45 ||
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyasetpādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
satyapūtāṃ vadedvācaṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret || 6.46 ||
ativādāṃstitikṣeta nāvamanyeta kaṃ cana  |
na cemaṃ dehamāśritya vairaṃ kurvīta kena cit || 6.47 ||
kruddhyantaṃ na pratikrudhyedākruṣṭaḥ kuśalaṃ vadet |
saptadvārāvakīrṇāṃ ca na vācamanṛtāṃ vadet || 6.48 ||
adhyātmaratirāsīno nirapekṣo nirāmiṣaḥ  |
ātmanaiva sahāyena sukhārthī vicarediha  || 6.49 ||
na cotpātanimittābhyāṃ na nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā  |
nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhi cit || 6.50 ||
na tāpasairbrāhmaṇairvā vayobhirapi vā śvabhiḥ  |
ākīrṇaṃ bhikṣukairvānyairagāramupasaṃvrajet || 6.51 ||
kḷptakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ pātrī daṇḍī kusumbhavān  |
vicarenniyato nityaṃ sarvabhūtānyapīḍayan  || 6.52 ||
ataijasāni pātrāṇi tasya syurnirvraṇāni ca  |
teṣāmadbhiḥ smṛtaṃ śaucaṃ camasānāmivādhvare  || 6.53 ||
alābuṃ dārupātraṃ ca mṛṇmayaṃ vaidalaṃ tathā  |
etāṇi yatipātrāṇi manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo'bravīt || 6.54 ||
ekakālaṃ caredbhaikṣaṃ na prasajjeta vistare  |
bhaikṣe prasakto hi yatirviṣayeṣvapi sajjati  || 6.55 ||
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane  |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiścaret || 6.56 ||
alābhe na viṣadī syāllābhe caiva na harṣayet |
prāṇayātrikamātraḥ syānmātrāsaṅgādvinirgataḥ  || 6.57 ||
abhipūjitalābhāṃstu jugupsetaiva sarvaśaḥ  |
abhipūjitalābhaiśca yatirmukto'pi badhyate  || 6.58 ||
alpānnābhyavahāreṇa rahaḥsthānāsanena ca  |
hriyamāṇāni viṣayairindriyāṇi nivartayet || 6.59 ||
indriyāṇāṃ nirodhena rāgadveśakṣayeṇa ca  |
ahiṃsayā ca bhūtānāmamṛtatvāya kalpate  || 6.60 ||
avekṣeta gatīrnṝṇāṃ karmadoṣasamudbhavāḥ  |
niraye caiva patanaṃ yātanāśca yamakṣaye  || 6.61 ||
viprayogaṃ priyaiścaiva saṃyogaṃ ca tathāpriyaiḥ  |
jarayā cābhibhavanaṃ vyādhibhiścopapīḍanaṃ  || 6.62 ||
dehādutkramaṇaṃ cāṣmātpunargarbhe ca saṃbhavam  |
yonikoṭisahasreṣu sṛtīścāsyāntarātmanaḥ  || 6.63 ||
adharmaprabhavaṃ caiva duḥkhayogaṃ śarīriṇām  |
dharmārthaprabhavaṃ caiva sukhasaṃyogamakṣayam  || 6.64 ||
sūkṣmatāṃ cānvavekṣeta yogena paramātmanaḥ  |
deheṣu ca samutpattimuttameṣvadhameṣu ca  || 6.65 ||
dūṣito'pi careddharmaṃ yatra tatrāśrame rataḥ  |
samaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu na liṅgaṃ dharmakāraṇam  || 6.66 ||
phalaṃ katakavṛkṣasya yadyapyambuprasādakam  |
na nāmagrahaṇādeva tasya vāri prasīdati  || 6.67 ||
saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jantūnāṃ rātrāvahani vā sadā  |
śarīrasyātyaye caiva samīkṣya vasudhāṃ caret || 6.68 ||
ahnā rātryā ca yāñjantūn hinastyajñānato yatiḥ  |
teṣāṃ snātvā viśuddhyarthaṃ prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍācaret || 6.69 ||
prāṇāyāmā brāhmaṇasya trayo'pi vidhivatkṛtāḥ  |
vyāhṛtipraṇavairyuktā vijñeyaṃ paramaṃ tapaḥ  || 6.70 ||
dahyante dhmāyamānānāṃ dhātūnāṃ hi yathā malāḥ  |
tathendriyāṇāṃ dahyante doṣāḥ prāṇasya nigrahāt || 6.71 ||
prāṇāyamairdaheddoṣān dhāraṇābhiśca kilbiṣam  |
pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānenānīśvarān guṇān  || 6.72 ||
uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu durjñeyāmakṛtātmabhiḥ  |
dhyānayogena saṃpaśyedgatimasyāntarātmanaḥ  || 6.73 ||
samyagdarśanasaṃpannaḥ karmabhirna nibadhyate  |
darśanena vihīnastu saṃsāraṃ pratipadyate  || 6.74 ||
ahiṃsayendriyāsaṅgairvaidikaiścaiva karmabhiḥ  |
tapasaścaraṇaiścograiḥ sādhayantīha tatpadam  || 6.75 ||
asthisthūṇaṃ snāyuyutaṃ māṃsaśoṇitalepanam  |
carmāvanaddhaṃ durgandhi pūrṇaṃ mūtrapurīṣayoḥ  || 6.76 ||
jarāśokasamāviṣṭaṃ rogāyatanamāturam  |
rajasvalamanityaṃ ca bhūtāvāsamimaṃ tyajet || 6.77 ||
nadīkūlaṃ yathā vṛkṣo vṛkṣaṃ vā śakuniryathā  |
tathā tyajannimaṃ dehaṃ kṛcchrādgrāhādvimucyate  || 6.78 ||
priyeṣu sveṣu sukṛtamapriyeṣu ca duṣkṛtam  |
visṛjya dhyānayogena brahmābhyeti sanātanam  || 6.79 ||
yadā bhāvena bhavati sarvabhāveṣu niḥspṛhaḥ  |
tadā sukhamavāpnoti pretya ceha ca śāśvatam  || 6.80 ||
anena vidhinā sarvāṃstyaktvā saṅgāñśanaiḥ śanaiḥ  |
sarvadvandvavinirmukto brahmaṇyevāvatiṣṭhate  || 6.81 ||
dhyānikaṃ sarvamevaitadyadetadabhiśabditam  |
na hyanadhyātmavitkaścitkriyāphalamupāśnute  || 6.82 ||
adhiyajñaṃ brahma japedādhidaivikameva ca  |
ādhyātmikaṃ ca satataṃ vedāntābhihitaṃ ca yat || 6.83 ||
idaṃ śaraṇamajñānāmidameva vijānatām  |
idamanvicchatāṃ svargamidamānantyamicchatām  || 6.84 ||
anena kramayogena parivrajati yo dvijaḥ  |
sa vidhūyeha pāpmānaṃ paraṃ brahmādhigacchati  || 6.85 ||
eṣa dharmo'nuśiṣṭo vo yatīnāṃ niyatātmanām  |
vedasaṃnyāsikānāṃ tu karmayogaṃ nibodhata  || 6.86 ||
brahmacārī gṛhasthaśca vānaprastho yatistathā  |
ete gṛhasthaprabhavāścatvāraḥ pṛthagāśramāḥ  || 6.87 ||
sarve'pi kramaśastvete yathāśāstraṃ niṣevitāḥ  |
yathoktakāriṇaṃ vipraṃ nayanti paramāṃ gatim  || 6.88 ||
sarveṣāmapi caiteṣāṃ vedasmṛtividhānataḥ  |
gṛhastha ucyate śreṣṭhaḥ sa trīnetān bibharti hi  || 6.89 ||
yathā nadīnadāḥ sarve sāgare yānti saṃsthitim  |
tathaivāśramiṇaḥ sarve gṛhasthe yānti saṃsthitim  || 6.90 ||
caturbhirapi caivaitairnityamāśramibhirdvijaiḥ  |
daśalakṣaṇako dharmaḥ sevitavyaḥ prayatnataḥ  || 6.91 ||
dhṛtiḥ kṣamā damo'steyaṃ śaucamindriyanigrahaḥ  |
dhīrvidyā satyamakrodho daśakaṃ dharmalakṣaṇam  || 6.92 ||
daśa lakṣaṇāni dharmasya ye viprāḥ samadhīyate  |
adhītya cānuvartante te yānti paramāṃ gatim  || 6.93 ||
daśalakṣaṇakaṃ dharmamanutiṣṭhan samāhitaḥ  |
vedāntaṃ vidhivacchrutvā saṃnyasedanṛṇo dvijaḥ  || 6.94 ||
saṃnyasya sarvakarmāṇi karmadoṣānapānudan  |
niyato vedamabhyasya putraiśvarye sukhaṃ vaset || 6.95 ||
evaṃ saṃnyasya karmāṇi svakāryaparamo'spṛhaḥ  |
saṃnyāsenāpahatyainaḥ prāpnoti paramaṃ gatim  || 6.96 ||
eṣa vo'bhihito dharmo brāhmaṇasya caturvidhaḥ  |
puṇyo'kṣayaphalaḥ pretya rājñāṃ dharmaṃ nibodhata  || 6.97 ||

rājadharmān pravakṣyāmi yathāvṛtto bhavennṛpaḥ  |
saṃbhavaśca yathā tasya siddhiśca paramā yathā  || 7.1 ||
brāhmaṃ prāptena saṃskāraṃ kṣatriyeṇa yathāvidhi  |
sarvasyāsya yathānyāyaṃ kartavyaṃ parirakṣaṇam  || 7.2 ||
arājake hi loke'smin sarvato vidruto bhayāt |
rakṣārthamasya sarvasya rājānamasṛjatprabhuḥ  || 7.3 ||
indrānilayamārkāṇāmagneśca varuṇasya ca  |
candravitteśayoścaiva mātrā nirhṛtya śāśvatīḥ  || 7.4 ||
yasmādeṣāṃ surendrāṇāṃ mātrābhyo nirmito nṛpaḥ  |
tasmādabhibhavatyeṣa sarvabhūtāni tejasā  || 7.5 ||
tapatyādityavaccaiṣa cakṣūṃṣi ca manāṃsi ca  |
na cainaṃ bhuvi śaknoti kaścidapyabhivīkṣitum  || 7.6 ||
so'gnirbhavati vāyuśca so'rkaḥ somaḥ sa dharmarāṭ |
sa kuberaḥ sa varuṇaḥ sa mahendraḥ prabhāvataḥ  || 7.7 ||
bālo'pi nāvamāntavyo manuṣya iti bhūmipaḥ  |
mahatī devatā hyeṣā nararūpeṇa tiṣṭhati  || 7.8 ||
ekameva dahatyagnirnaraṃ durupasarpiṇam  |
kulaṃ dahati rājāgniḥ sapaśudravyasaṃcayam  || 7.9 ||
kāryaṃ so'vekṣya śaktiṃ ca deśakālau ca tattvataḥ  |
kurute dharmasiddhyarthaṃ viśvarūpaṃ punaḥ punaḥ  || 7.10 ||
yasya prasāde padmā śrīrvijayaśca parākrame  |
mṛtyuśca vasati krodhe sarvatejomayo hi saḥ  || 7.11 ||
taṃ yastu dveṣṭi saṃmohātsa vinaśyatyasaṃśayam  |
tasya hyāśu vināśāya rājā prakurute manaḥ  || 7.12 ||
tasmāddharmaṃ yamiṣṭeṣu sa vyavasyennarādhipaḥ  |
aniṣṭaṃ cāpyaniṣṭeṣu taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet || 7.13 ||
tasyārthe sarvabhūtānāṃ goptāraṃ dharmamātmajam  |
brahmatejomayaṃ daṇḍamasṛjatpūrvamīśvaraḥ  || 7.14 ||
tasya sarvāṇi bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca  |
bhayādbhogāya kalpante svadharmānna calanti ca  || 7.15 ||
taṃ deśakālau śaktiṃ ca vidyāṃ cāvekṣya tattvataḥ  |
yathārhataḥ saṃpraṇayennareṣvanyāyavartiṣu  || 7.16 ||
sa rājā puruṣo daṇḍaḥ sa netā śāsitā ca saḥ  |
caturṇāmāśramāṇāṃ ca dharmasya pratibhūḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.17 ||
daṇḍaḥ śāsti prajāḥ sarvā daṇḍa evābhirakṣati  |
daṇḍaḥ supteṣu jāgarti daṇḍaṃ dharmaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  || 7.18 ||
samīkṣya sa dhṛtaḥ samyaksarvā rañjayati prajāḥ  |
asamīkṣya praṇītastu vināśayati sarvataḥ  || 7.19 ||
yadi na praṇayedrājā daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣvatandritaḥ  |
śūle matsyānivāpakṣyan durbalān balavattarāḥ  || 7.20 ||
adyātkākaḥ puroḍāśaṃ śvā ca lihyāddhavistathā  |
svāmyaṃ ca na syātkasmiṃścitpravartetādharottaram  || 7.21 ||
sarvo daṇḍajito loko durlabho hi śucirnaraḥ  |
daṇḍasya hi bhayātsarvaṃ jagadbhogāya kalpate  || 7.22 ||
devadānavagandharvā rakṣāṃsi patagoragāḥ  |
te'pi bhogāya kalpante daṇḍenaiva nipīḍitāḥ  || 7.23 ||
duṣyeyuḥ sarvavarṇāśca bhidyeran sarvasetavaḥ  |
sarvalokaprakopaśca bhaveddaṇḍasya vibhramāt || 7.24 ||
yatra śyāmo lohitākṣo daṇḍaścarati pāpahā  |
prajāstatra na muhyanti netā cetsādhu paśyati  || 7.25 ||
tasyāhuḥ saṃpraṇetāraṃ rājānaṃ satyavādinam  |
samīkṣyakāriṇaṃ prājñaṃ dharmakāmārthakovidam  || 7.26 ||
taṃ rājā praṇayan samyaktrivargeṇābhivardhate  |
kāmātmā viṣamaḥ kṣudro daṇḍenaiva nihanyate  || 7.27 ||
daṇḍo hi sumahattejo durdharaścākṛtātmabhiḥ  |
dharmādvicalitaṃ hanti nṛpameva sabāndhavam  || 7.28 ||
tato durgaṃ ca rāṣṭraṃ ca lokaṃ ca sacarācaram  |
antarikṣagatāṃścaiva munīn devāṃśca pīḍayet || 7.29 ||
so'sahāyena mūḍhena lubdhenākṛtabuddhinā  |
na śakyo nyāyato netuṃ saktena viṣayeṣu ca  || 7.30 ||
śucinā satyasaṃdhena yathāśāstrānusāriṇā  |
praṇetuṃ śakyate daṇḍaḥ susahāyena dhīmatā  || 7.31 ||
svarāṣṭre nyāyavṛttaḥ syādbhṛśadaṇḍaśca śatruṣu  |
suhṛtsvajihmaḥ snigdheṣu brāhmaṇeṣu kṣamānvitaḥ  || 7.32 ||
evaṃvṛttasya nṛpateḥ śiloñchenāpi jīvataḥ  |
vistīryate yaśo loke tailabindurivāmbhasi  || 7.33 ||
atastu viparītasya nṛpaterajitātmanaḥ  |
saṃkṣipyate yaśo loke ghṛtabindurivāmbhasi  || 7.34 ||
sve sve dharme niviṣṭānāṃ sarveṣāmanupūrvaśaḥ  |
varṇānāmāśramāṇāṃ ca rājā sṛṣṭo'bhirakṣitā  || 7.35 ||
tena yadyatsabhṛtyena kartavyaṃ rakṣatā prajāḥ  |
tattadvo'haṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvadanupūrvaśaḥ  || 7.36 ||
brāhmaṇān paryupāsīta prātarutthāya pārthivaḥ  |
traividyavṛddhān viduṣastiṣṭhetteṣāṃ ca śāsane  || 7.37 ||
vṛddhāṃśca nityaṃ seveta viprān vedavidaḥ śucīn  |
vṛddhasevī hi satataṃ rakṣobhirapi pūjyate  || 7.38 ||
tebhyo'dhigacchedvinayaṃ vinītātmāpi nityaśaḥ  |
vinītātmā hi nṛpatirna vinaśyati karhi cit || 7.39 ||
bahavo'vinayānnaṣṭā rājānaḥ saparicchadāḥ  |
vanasthā api rājyāni vinayātpratipedire  || 7.40 ||
veno vinaṣṭo'vinayānnahuṣaścaiva pārthivaḥ  |
sudāḥ paijavanaścaiva sumukho nimireva ca  || 7.41 ||
pṛthustu vinayādrājyaṃ prāptavānmanureva ca  |
kuberaśca dhanaiśvaryaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ caiva gādhijaḥ  || 7.42 ||
traividyebhyastrayīṃ vidyāṃ daṇḍanītiṃ ca śāśvatīm  |
ānvīkṣikīṃ cātmavidyāṃ vārtārambhāṃśca lokataḥ  || 7.43 ||
indriyāṇāṃ jaye yogaṃ samātiṣṭheddivāniśam  |
jitendriyo hi śaknoti vaśe sthāpayituṃ prajāḥ  || 7.44 ||
daśa kāmasamutthāni tathāṣṭau krodhajāni ca  |
vyasanāni durantāni prayatnena vivarjayet || 7.45 ||
kāmajeṣu prasakto hi vyasaneṣu mahīpatiḥ  |
viyujyate'rthadharmābhyāṃ krodhajeṣvātmanaiva tu  || 7.46 ||
mṛgayākṣo divāsvapnaḥ parivādaḥ striyo madaḥ  |
tauryatrikaṃ vṛthāṭyā ca kāmajo daśako gaṇaḥ  || 7.47 ||
paiśunyaṃ sāhasaṃ droha īrṣyāsūyārthadūṣaṇam  |
vāgdaṇḍajaṃ ca pāruṣyaṃ krodhajo'pi gaṇo'ṣṭakaḥ  || 7.48 ||
dvayorapyetayormūlaṃ yaṃ sarve kavayo viduḥ  |
taṃ yatnena jayellobhaṃ tajjāvetāvubhau gaṇau  || 7.49 ||
pānamakṣāḥ striyaścaiva mṛgayā ca yathākramam  |
etatkaṣṭatamaṃ vidyāccatuṣkaṃ kāmaje gaṇe  || 7.50 ||
daṇḍasya pātanaṃ caiva vākpāruṣyārthadūṣaṇe  |
krodhaje'pi gaṇe vidyātkaṣṭametattrikaṃ sadā  || 7.51 ||
saptakasyāsya vargasya sarvatraivānuṣaṅgiṇaḥ  |
pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ gurutaraṃ vidyādvyasanamātmavān  || 7.52 ||
vyasanasya ca mṛtyośca vyasanaṃ kaṣṭamucyate  |
vyasanyadho'dho vrajati svaryātyavyasanī mṛtaḥ  || 7.53 ||
maulāñśāstravidaḥ śūrāṃllabdhalakṣān kulodbhavān  |
sacivān sapta cāṣṭau vā prakurvīta parīkṣitān  || 7.54 ||
api yatsukaraṃ karma tadapyekena duṣkaram  |
viśeṣato'sahāyena kiṃ tu rājyaṃ mahodayam  || 7.55 ||
taiḥ sārdhaṃ cintayennityaṃ sāmānyaṃ saṃdhivigraham  |
sthānaṃ samudayaṃ guptiṃ labdhapraśamanāni ca  || 7.56 ||
teṣāṃ svaṃ svamabhiprāyamupalabhya pṛthakpṛthak |
samastānāṃ ca kāryeṣu vidadhyāddhitamātmanaḥ  || 7.57 ||
sarveṣāṃ tu viśiṣṭena brāhmaṇena vipaścitā  |
mantrayetparamaṃ mantraṃ rājā ṣāḍguṇyasaṃyutam  || 7.58 ||
nityaṃ tasmin samāśvastaḥ sarvakāryāṇi niḥkṣipet |
tena sārdhaṃ viniścitya tataḥ karma samārabhet || 7.59 ||
anyānapi prakurvīta śucīn prājñānavasthitān  |
samyagarthasamāhartṝnamātyān suparīkṣitān  || 7.60 ||
nirvartetāsya yāvadbhiritikartavyatā nṛbhiḥ  |
tāvato'tandritān dakṣān prakurvīta vicakṣaṇān  || 7.61 ||
teṣāmarthe niyuñjīta śūrān dakṣān kulodgatān  |
śucīnākarakarmānte bhīrūnantarniveśane  || 7.62 ||
dūtaṃ caiva prakurvīta sarvaśāstraviśāradam  |
iṅgitākāraceṣṭajñaṃ śuciṃ dakṣaṃ kulodgatam  || 7.63 ||
anuraktaḥ śucirdakṣaḥ smṛtimān deśakālavit |
vapuṣmān vītabhīrvāgmī dūto rājñaḥ praśasyate  || 7.64 ||
amātye daṇḍa āyatto daṇḍe vainayikī kriyā  |
nṛpatau kośarāṣṭre ca dūte saṃdhiviparyayau  || 7.65 ||
dūta eva hi saṃdhatte bhinattyeva ca saṃhatān  |
dūtastatkurute karma bhidyante yena mānavaḥ  || 7.66 ||
sa vidyādasya kṛtyeṣu nirgūḍheṅgitaceṣṭitaiḥ  |
ākāramiṅgitaṃ ceṣṭāṃ bhṛtyeṣu ca cikīrṣitam  || 7.67 ||
buddhvā ca sarvaṃ tattvena pararājacikīrṣitam  |
tathā prayatnamātiṣṭhedyathātmānaṃ na pīḍayet || 7.68 ||
jāṅgalaṃ sasyasaṃpannamāryaprāyamanāvilam  |
ramyamānatasāmantaṃ svājīvyaṃ deśamāvaset || 7.69 ||
dhanvadurgaṃ mahīdurgamabdurgaṃ vārkṣameva vā  |
nṛdurgaṃ giridurgaṃ vā samāśritya vasetpuram  || 7.70 ||
sarveṇa tu prayatnena giridurgaṃ samāśrayet |
eṣāṃ hi bāhuguṇyena giridurgaṃ viśiṣyate  || 7.71 ||
triṇyādyānyāśritāstveṣāṃ mṛgagartāśrayāpcarāḥ  |
trīṇyuttarāṇi kramaśaḥ plavaṃgamanarāmarāḥ  || 7.72 ||
yathā durgāśritānetānnopahiṃsanti śatravaḥ  |
tathārayo na hiṃsanti nṛpaṃ durgasamāśritam  || 7.73 ||
ekaḥ śataṃ yodhayati prākārastho dhanurdharaḥ  |
śataṃ daśasahasrāṇi tasmāddurgaṃ vidhīyate  || 7.74 ||
tatsyādāyudhasaṃpannaṃ dhanadhānyena vāhanaiḥ  |
brāhmaṇaiḥ śilpibhiryantrairyavasenodakena ca  || 7.75 ||
tasya madhye suparyāptaṃ kārayedgṛhamātmanaḥ  |
guptaṃ sarvartukaṃ śubhraṃ jalavṛkṣasamanvitam  || 7.76 ||
tadadhyāsyodvahedbhāryāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām  |
kule mahati saṃbhūtāṃ hṛdyāṃ rūpaguṇānvītām  || 7.77 ||
purohitaṃ ca kurvīta vṛṇuyādeva ca rtvijaḥ  |
te'sya gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi kuryurvaitānikāni ca  || 7.78 ||
yajeta rājā kratubhirvividhairāptadakṣiṇaiḥ  |
dharmārthaṃ caiva viprebhyo dadyādbhogān dhanāni ca  || 7.79 ||
sāṃvatsarikamāptaiśca rāṣṭrādāhārayedbalim  |
syāccāmnāyaparo loke varteta pitṛvannṛṣu  || 7.80 ||
adhyakṣān vividhān kuryāttatra tatra vipaścitaḥ  |
te'sya sarvāṇyavekṣerannṛṇāṃ kāryāṇi kurvatām  || 7.81 ||
āvṛttānāṃ gurukulādviprāṇāṃ pūjako bhavet |
nṛpāṇāmakṣayo hyeṣa nidhirbrāhmo'bhidhīyate  || 7.82 ||
na taṃ stenā na cāmitrā haranti na ca naśyati  |
tasmādrājñā nidhātavyo brāhmaṇeṣvakṣayo nidhiḥ  || 7.83 ||
na skandate na vyathate na vinaśyati karhi cit |
variṣṭhamagnihotrebhyo brāhmaṇasya mukhe hutam  || 7.84 ||
samamabrāhmaṇe dānaṃ dviguṇaṃ brāhmaṇabruve  |
prādhīte śatasāhasramanantaṃ vedapārage  || 7.85 ||
pātrasya hi viśeṣeṇa śraddadhānatayaiva ca  |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā pretya dānasya phalamaśnute  || 7.86 ||
deśakālavidhānena dravyaṃ śraddhāsamanvitam  |
pātre pradīyate yattu taddharmasya prasādhanam  || 7.87  ||
samottamādhamai rājā tvāhūtaḥ pālayan prajāḥ  |
na nivarteta saṃgrāmātkṣātraṃ dharmamanusmaran  || 7.87[88 ] ||
saṃgrāmeṣvanivartitvaṃ prajānāṃ caiva pālanam  |
śuśrūṣā brāhmaṇānāṃ ca rājñāṃ śreyaskaraṃ param  || 7.88[89 ] ||
āhaveṣu mitho'nyonyaṃ jighāṃsanto mahīkṣitaḥ  |
yudhyamānāḥ paraṃ śaktyā svargaṃ yāntyaparāṅmukhāḥ  || 7.89[90 ] ||
na kūṭairāyudhairhanyādyudhyamāno raṇe ripūn  |
na karṇibhirnāpi digdhairnāgnijvalitatejanaiḥ  || 7.90[91 ] ||
na ca hanyātsthalārūḍhaṃ na klībaṃ na kṛtāñjalim  |
na muktakeśaṃ nāsīnaṃ na tavāsmīti vādinam  || 7.91[92 ] ||
na suptaṃ na visaṃnāhaṃ na nagnaṃ na nirāyudham  |
nāyudhyamānaṃ paśyantaṃ na pareṇa samāgatam  || 7.92[93 ] ||
nāyudhavyasanaprāptaṃ nārtaṃ nātiparikṣataṃ  |
na bhītaṃ na parāvṛttaṃ satāṃ dharmamanusmaran  || 7.93[94 ] ||
yastu bhītaḥ parāvṛttaḥ saṃgrāme hanyate paraiḥ  |
bharturyadduṣkṛtaṃ kiṃ cittatsarvaṃ pratipadyate  || 7.94[95 ] ||
yaccāsya sukṛtaṃ kiṃ cidamutrārthamupārjitam  |
bhartā tatsarvamādatte parāvṛttahatasya tu  || 7.95[96 ] ||
rathāśvaṃ hastinaṃ chatraṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ paśūn striyaḥ  |
sarvadravyāṇi kupyaṃ ca yo yajjayati tasya tat || 7.96[97 ] ||
rājñaśca dadyuruddhāramityeṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ  |
rājñā ca sarvayodhebhyo dātavyamapṛthagjitam  || 7.97[98 ] ||
eṣo'nupaskṛtaḥ prokto yodhadharmaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
asmāddharmānna cyaveta kṣatriyo ghnan raṇe ripūn  || 7.98[99 ] ||
alabdhaṃ caiva lipseta labdhaṃ rakṣetprayatnataḥ  |
rakṣitaṃ vardhayeccaiva vṛddhaṃ pātreṣu nikṣipet || 7.99[100 ] ||
etaccaturvidhaṃ vidyātpuruṣārthaprayojanam  |
asya nityamanuṣṭhānaṃ samyakkuryādatandritaḥ  || 7.100[101 ] ||
alabdhamiccheddaṇḍena labdhaṃ rakṣedavekṣayā  |
rakṣitaṃ vardhayedvṛddhyā vṛddhaṃ pātreṣu nikṣipet || 7.101[102 ] ||
nityamudyatadaṇḍaḥ syānnityaṃ vivṛtapauruṣaḥ  |
nityaṃ saṃvṛtasaṃvāryo nityaṃ chidrānusāryareḥ  || 7.102[103 ] ||
nityamudyatadaṇḍasya kṛtsnamudvijate jagat |
tasmātsarvāṇi bhūtāni daṇḍenaiva prasādhayet || 7.103[104 ] ||
amāyayaiva varteta na kathaṃ cana māyayā  |
budhyetāriprayuktāṃ ca māyāṃ nityaṃ susaṃvṛtaḥ  || 7.104[105 ] ||
nāsya chidraṃ paro vidyādvidyācchidraṃ parasya ca  |
gūhetkūrma ivāṅgāni rakṣedvivaramātmanaḥ  || 7.105[106 ] ||
bakavaccintayedarthān siṃhavacca parākrame  |
vṛkavaccāvalumpeta śaśavacca viniṣpatet || 7.106[107 ] ||
evaṃ vijayamānasya ye'sya syuḥ paripanthinaḥ  |
tānānayedvaśaṃ sarvān sāmādibhirupakramaiḥ  || 7.107[108 ] ||
yadi te tu na tiṣṭheyurupāyaiḥ prathamaistribhiḥ  |
daṇḍenaiva prasahyaitāñśanakairvaśamānayet || 7.108[109 ] ||
sāmādīnāmupāyānāṃ caturṇāmapi paṇḍitāḥ  |
sāmadaṇḍau praśaṃsanti nityaṃ rāṣṭrābhivṛddhaye  || 7.109[110 ] ||
yathoddharati nirdātā kakṣaṃ dhānyaṃ ca rakṣati  |
tathā rakṣennṛpo rāṣṭraṃ hanyācca paripanthinaḥ  || 7.110[111 ] ||
mohādrājā svarāṣṭraṃ yaḥ karṣayatyanavekṣayā  |
so'cirādbhraśyate rājyājjīvitācca sabāndhavaḥ  || 7.111[112 ] ||
śarīrakarṣaṇātprāṇāḥ kṣīyante prāṇināṃ yathā  |
tathā rājñāmapi prāṇāḥ kṣīyante rāṣṭrakarṣaṇāt || 7.112[113 ] ||
rāṣṭrasya saṃgrahe nityaṃ vidhānamidamācaret |
susaṃgṛhītarāṣṭre hi pārthivaḥ sukhamedhate  || 7.113[114 ] ||
dvayostrayāṇāṃ pañcānāṃ madhye gulmamadhiṣṭhitam  |
tathā grāmaśatānāṃ ca kuryādrāṣṭrasya saṃgraham  || 7.114[115 ] ||
grāmasyādhipatiṃ kuryāddaśagrāmapatiṃ tathā  |
viṃśatīśaṃ śateśaṃ ca sahasrapatimeva ca  || 7.115[116 ] ||
grāmadoṣān samutpannān grāmikaḥ śanakaiḥ svayam  |
śaṃsedgrāmadaśeśāya daśeśo viṃśatīśine  || 7.116[117 ] ||
viṃśatīśastu tatsarvaṃ śateśāya nivedayet |
śaṃsedgrāmaśateśastu sahasrapataye svayam  || 7.117[118 ] ||
yāni rājapradeyāni pratyahaṃ grāmavāsibhiḥ  |
annapānendhanādīni grāmikastānyavāpnuyāt || 7.118[119 ] ||
daśī kulaṃ tu bhuñjīta viṃśī pañca kulāni ca  |
grāmaṃ grāmaśatādhyakṣaḥ sahasrādhipatiḥ puram  || 7.119[120 ] ||
teṣāṃ grāmyāṇi kāryāni pṛthakkāryāṇi caiva hi  |
rājño'nyaḥ sacivaḥ snigdhastāni paśyedatandritaḥ  || 7.120[121 ] ||
nagare nagare caikaṃ kuryātsarvārthacintakam  |
uccaiḥsthānaṃ ghorarūpaṃ nakṣatrāṇāmiva graham  || 7.121[122 ] ||
sa tānanuparikrāmetsarvāneva sadā svayam  |
teṣāṃ vṛttaṃ pariṇayetsamyagrāṣṭreṣu taccaraiḥ  || 7.122[123 ] ||
rājño hi rakṣādhikṛtāḥ parasvādāyinaḥ śaṭhāḥ  |
bhṛtyā bhavanti prāyeṇa tebhyo rakṣedimāḥ prajāḥ  || 7.123[124 ] ||
ye kāryikebhyo'rthameva gṛhṇīyuḥ pāpacetasaḥ  |
teṣāṃ sarvasvamādāya rājā kuryātpravāsanam  || 7.124[125 ] ||
rājā karmasu yuktānāṃ strīṇāṃ preṣyajanasya ca   |
pratyahaṃ kalpayedvṛttiṃ sthānaṃ karmānurūpataḥ  || 7.125[126 ] ||
paṇo deyo'vakṛṣṭasya ṣaḍutkṛṣṭasya vetanam  |
ṣāṇmāsikastathācchādo dhānyadroṇastu māsikaḥ  || 7.126[127 ] ||
krayavikrayamadhvānaṃ bhaktaṃ ca saparivyayam  |
yogakṣemaṃ ca saṃprekṣya vaṇijo dāpayetkarān  || 7.127[128 ] ||
yathā phalena yujyeta rājā kartā ca karmaṇām  |
tathāvekṣya nṛpo rāṣṭre kalpayetsatataṃ karān  || 7.128[129 ] ||
yathālpālpamadantyādyaṃ vāryokovatsaṣaṭpadāḥ  |
tathālpālpo grahītavyo rāṣṭrādrājñābdikaḥ karaḥ  || 7.129[130 ] ||
pañcāśadbhāga ādeyo rājñā paśuhiraṇyayoḥ  |
dhānyānāmaṣṭamo bhāgaḥ ṣaṣṭho dvādaśa eva vā  || 7.130[131 ] ||
ādadītātha ṣaḍbhāgaṃ drumāṃsamadhusarpiṣām  |
gandhauṣadhirasānāṃ ca puṣpamūlaphalasya ca  || 7.131[132 ] ||
patraśākatṛṇānāṃ ca carmaṇāṃ vaidalasya ca  |
mṛnmayānāṃ ca bhāṇḍānāṃ sarvasyāśmamayasya ca  || 7.132[133 ] ||
mriyamāṇo'pyādadīta na rājā śrotriyātkaram  |
na ca kṣudhāsya saṃsīdecchrotriyo viṣaye vasan  || 7.133[134 ] ||
yasya rājñastu viṣaye śrotriyaḥ sīdati kṣudhā  |
tasyāpi tatkṣudhā rāṣṭramacirenaiva sīdati  || 7.134[135 ] ||
śrutavṛtte viditvāsya vṛttiṃ dharmyāṃ prakalpayet |
saṃrakṣetsarvataścainaṃ pitā putramivāurasam  || 7.135[136 ] ||
saṃrakṣyamāṇo rājñā yaṃ] kurute dharmamanvaham  |
tenāyurvardhate rājño draviṇaṃ rāṣṭrameva ca  || 7.136[137 ] ||
yatkiṃ cidapi varṣasya dāpayetkarasaṃjñitam  |
vyavahāreṇa jīvantaṃ rājā rāṣṭre pṛthagjanam  || 7.137[138 ] ||
kārukāñśilpinaścaiva śūdrāṃscātmopajīvinaḥ  |
ekaikaṃ kārayetkarma māsi māsi mahīpatiḥ  || 7.138[139 ] ||
nocchindyādātmano mūlaṃ pareṣāṃ cātitṛṣṇayā  |
ucchindan hyātmano mūlamāṭmānaṃ tāṃśca pīdayet || 7.139[140 ] ||
tīkṣṇaścaiva mṛduśca syātkāryaṃ vīkṣya mahīpatiḥ  |
tīkṣṇaścaiva mṛduścaiva rāja bhavati sammataḥ  || 7.140[141 ] ||
amātyamukhyaṃ dharmajñaṃ prājñaṃ dāntaṃ kulodgatam  |
sthāpayedāsane tasmin khinnaḥ kāryekṣaṇe nṛṇām  || 7.141[142 ] ||
evaṃ sarvaṃ vidhāyedamitikartavyamātmanaḥ  |
yuktaścaivāpramattaśca parirakṣedimāḥ prajāḥ  || 7.142[143 ] ||
vikrośantyo yasya rāṣṭrādhriyante dasyubhiḥ prajāḥ  |
saṃpaśyataḥ sabhṛtyasya mṛtaḥ sa na tu jīvati  || 7.143[144 ] ||
kṣatriyasya paro dharmaḥ prājānāmeva pālanam  |
nirdiṣṭaphalabhoktā hi rājā dharmeṇa yujyate  || 7.144[145 ] ||
utthāya paścime yāme kṛtaśaucaḥ samāhitaḥ  |
hutāgnirbrāhmaṇāṃścārcya praviśetsa śubhāṃ sabhām  || 7.145[146 ] ||
tatra sthitaḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ pratinandya visarjayet |
visṛjya ca prajāḥ sarvā mantrayetsaha mantribhiḥ  || 7.146[147 ] ||
giripṛṣṭhaṃ samāruhya prasādaṃ vā rahogataḥ  |
araṇye niḥśalāke vā mantrayedavibhāvitaḥ  || 7.147[148 ] ||
yasya mantraṃ na jānanti samāgamya pṛthagjanāḥ  |
sa kṛtsnāṃ pṛthivīṃ bhuṅkte kośahīno'pi pārthivaḥ  || 7.148[149 ] ||
jaḍamūkāndhabadhirāṃstairyagyonān vayo'tigān  |
strīmlecchavyādhitavyaṅgānmantrakāle'pasārayet || 7.149[150 ] ||
bhindantyavamatā mantraṃ tairyagyonāstathaiva ca  |
striyaścaiva viśeṣeṇa tasmāttatrādṛto bhavet || 7.150[151 ] ||
madhyaṃdine'rdharātre vā viśrānto vigataklamaḥ  |
cintayeddharmakāmārthān sārdhaṃ taireka eva vā  || 7.151[152 ] ||
parasparaviruddhānāṃ teṣāṃ ca samupārjanam  |
kanyānāṃ saṃpradānaṃ ca kumārāṇāṃ ca rakṣaṇaṃ  || 7.152[153 ] ||
dūtasaṃpreṣaṇaṃ caiva kāryaśeṣaṃ tathaiva ca  |
antaḥpurapracāraṃ ca praṇidhīnāṃ ca ceṣṭitam  || 7.153[154 ] ||
kṛtsnaṃ cāṣṭavidhaṃ karma pañcavargaṃ ca tattvataḥ  |
anurāgāparāgau ca pracāraṃ maṇḍalasya ca  || 7.154[155 ] ||
madhyamasya pracāraṃ ca vijīgiṣośca ceṣṭitam  |
udāsīnapracāraṃ ca śatroścaiva prayatnataḥ  || 7.155[156 ] ||
etāḥ prakṛtayo mūlaṃ maṇḍalasya samāsataḥ  |
aṣṭau cānyāḥ samākhyātā dvādaśaiva tu tāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 7.156[157 ] ||
amātyarāṣṭradurgārtha- daṇḍākhyāḥ pañca cāparāḥ  |
pratyekaṃ kathitā hyetāḥ saṃkṣepeṇa dvisaptatiḥ  || 7.157[158 ] ||
anantaramariṃ vidyādarisevinameva ca  |
areranantaraṃ mitramudāsīnaṃ tayoḥ param  || 7.158[159 ] ||
tān sarvānabhisaṃdadhyātsāmādibhirupakramaiḥ  |
vyastaiścaiva samastaiśca pauruṣeṇa nayena ca  || 7.159[160 ] ||
saṃdhiṃ ca vigrahaṃ caiva yānamāsanameva ca  |
dvaidhībhāvaṃ saṃśrayaṃ ca ṣaḍguṇāṃścintayetsadā  || 7.160[161 ] ||
āsanaṃ caiva yānaṃ ca saṃdhiṃ vigrahameva ca  |
kāryaṃ vīkṣya prayuñjīta dvaidhaṃ saṃśrayameva ca  || 7.161[162 ] ||
saṃdhiṃ tu dvividhaṃ vidyādrājā vigrahameva ca  |
ubhe yānāsane caiva dvividhaḥ saṃśrayaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.162[163 ] ||
samānayānakarmā ca viparītastathaiva ca  |
tadā tvāyatisaṃyuktaḥ saṃdhirjñeyo dvilakṣaṇaḥ  || 7.163[164 ] ||
svayaṃkṛtaśca kāryārthamakāle kāla eva vā  |
mitrasya caivāpakṛte dvividho vigrahaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.164[165 ] ||
ekākinaścātyayike kārye prāpte yadṛcchayā  |
saṃhatasya ca mitreṇa dvividhaṃ yānamucyate  || 7.165[166 ] ||
kṣīṇasya caiva kramaśo daivātpūrvakṛtena vā  |
mitrasya cānurodhena dvividhaṃ smṛtamāsanam  || 7.166[167 ] ||
balasya svāminaścaiva sthitiḥ kāryārthasiddhaye  |
dvividhaṃ kīrtyate dvaidhaṃ ṣāḍguṇyaguṇavedibhiḥ  || 7.167[168 ] ||
arthasaṃpādanārthaṃ ca pīḍyamānasya śatrubhiḥ  |
sādhuṣu vyapadeśaśca dvividhaḥ saṃśrayaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 7.168[169 ] ||
yadāvagacchedāyatyāmādhikyaṃ dhruvamātmanaḥ  |
tadātve cālpikāṃ pīḍāṃ tadā saṃdhiṃ samāśrayet || 7.169[170 ] ||
yadā prahṛṣṭā manyeta sarvāstu prakṛtīrbhṛśam  |
atyucchritaṃ tathātmānaṃ tadā kurvīta vigraham  || 7.170[171 ] ||
yadā manyeta bhāvena hṛṣṭaṃ puṣṭaṃ balaṃ svakam  |
parasya viparītaṃ ca tadā yāyādripuṃ prati  || 7.171[172 ] ||
yadā tu syātparikṣīṇo vāhanena balena ca  |
tadāsīta prayatnena śanakaiḥ sāntvayannarīn  || 7.172[173 ] ||
manyetāriṃ yadā rājā sarvathā balavattaram  |
tadā dvidhā balaṃ kṛtvā sādhayetkāryamātmanaḥ  || 7.173[174 ] ||
yadā parabalānāṃ tu gamanīyatamo bhavet |
tadā tu saṃśrayetkṣipraṃ dhārmikaṃ balinaṃ nṛpam  || 7.174[175 ] ||
nigrahaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ ca kuryādyo'ribalasya ca  |
upaseveta taṃ nityaṃ sarvayatnairguruṃ yathā  || 7.175[176 ] ||
yadi tatrāpi saṃpaśyeddoṣaṃ saṃśrayakāritam  |
suyuddhameva tatrāpi nirviśaṅkaḥ samācaret || 7.176[177 ] ||
sarvopāyaistathā kuryānnītijñaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ  |
yathāsyābhyadhikā na syurmitrodāsīnaśatravaḥ  || 7.177[178 ] ||
āyatiṃ sarvakāryāṇāṃ tadātvaṃ ca vicārayet |
atītānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ guṇadoṣau ca tattvataḥ  || 7.178[179 ] ||
āyatyāṃ guṇadoṣajñastadātve kṣipraniścayaḥ  |
atīte kāryaśeṣajñaḥ śatrubhirnābhibhūyate  || 7.179[180 ] ||
yathainaṃ nābhisaṃdadhyurmitrodāsīnaśatravaḥ  |
tathā sarvaṃ saṃvidadhyādeṣa sāmāsiko nayaḥ  || 7.180[181 ] ||
tadā tu yānamātiṣṭhedarirāṣṭraṃ prati prabhuḥ  |
tadānena vidhānena yāyādaripuraṃ śanaiḥ  || 7.181[182 ] ||
mārgaśīrṣe śubhe māsi yāyādyātrāṃ mahīpatiḥ  |
phālgunaṃ vātha caitraṃ vā māsau prati yathābalam  || 7.182[183 ] ||
anyeṣvapi tu kāleṣu yadā paśyeddhruvaṃ jayam  |
tadā yāyādvigṛhyaiva vyasane cotthite ripoḥ  || 7.183[184 ] ||
kṛtvā vidhānaṃ mūle tu yātrikaṃ ca yathāvidhi  |
upagṛhyāspadaṃ caiva cārān samyagvidhāya ca  || 7.184[185 ] ||
saṃśodhya trividhaṃ mārgaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ ca balaṃ svakam  |
sāṃparāyikakalpena yāyādaripuraṃ prati  || 7.185[186 ] ||
śatrusevini mitre ca gūḍhe yuktataro bhavet |
gatapratyāgate caiva sa hi kaṣṭataro ripuḥ  || 7.186[187 ] ||
daṇḍavyūhena tanmārgaṃ yāyāttu śakaṭena vā  |
varāhamakarābhyāṃ vā sūcyā vā garuḍena vā  || 7.187[188 ] ||
yataśca bhayamāśaṅkettato vistārayedbalam  |
padmena caiva vyūhena niviśeta sadā svayam  || 7.188[189 ] ||
senāpatibalādhyakṣau sarvadikṣu niveśayet |
yataśca bhayamāśaṅketprācīṃ tāṃ kalpayeddiśam  || 7.189[190 ] ||
gulmāṃśca sthāpayedāptān kṛtasaṃjñān samantataḥ  |
sthāne yuddhe ca kuśalānabhīrūnavikāriṇaḥ  || 7.190[191 ] ||
saṃhatān yodhayedalpān kāmaṃ vistārayedbahūn  |
sūcyā vajreṇa caivaitān vyūhena vyūhya yodhayet || 7.191[192 ] ||
syandanāśvaiḥ same yudhyedanūpe nau dvipaistathā  |
vṛkṣagulmāvṛte cāpairasicarmāyudhaiḥ sthale  || 7.192[193 ] ||
kurukṣetrāṃśca matsyāṃśca pañcālāñśūrasenajān  |
dīrghāṃllaghūṃścaiva narānagrānīkeṣu yojayet || 7.193[194 ] ||
praharṣayedbalaṃ vyūhya tāṃśca samyakparīkṣayet |
ceṣṭāścaiva vijānīyādarīn yodhayatāmapi  || 7.194[195 ] ||
uparudhyārimāsīta rāṣṭraṃ cāsyopapīḍayet |
dūṣayeccāsya satataṃ yavasānnodakendhanam  || 7.195[196 ] ||
bhindyāccaiva taḍāgāni prākāraparikhāstathā  |
samavaskandayeccainaṃ rātrau vitrāsayettathā  || 7.196[197 ] ||
upajapyānupajapedbudhyetaiva ca tatkṛtam  |
yukte ca daive yudhyeta jayaprepsurapetabhīḥ  || 7.197[198 ] ||
sāmnā dānena bhedena samastairatha vā pṛthak |
vijetuṃ prayatetārīnna yuddhena kadā cana  || 7.198[199 ] ||
anityo vijayo yasmāddṛśyate yudhyamānayoḥ  |
parājayaśca saṃgrāme tasmādyuddhaṃ vivarjayet || 7.199[200 ] ||
trayāṇāmapyupāyānāṃ pūrvoktānāmasaṃbhave  |
tathā yudhyeta saṃpanno vijayeta ripūn yathā  || 7.200[201 ] ||
jitvā saṃpūjayeddevān brāhmaṇāṃścaiva dhārmikān  |
pradadyātparihārārthaṃ khyāpayedabhayāni ca  || 7.201[202 ] ||
sarveṣāṃ tu viditvaiṣāṃ samāsena cikīrṣitam  |
sthāpayettatra tadvaṃśyaṃ kuryācca samayakriyām  || 7.202[203 ] ||
pramāṇāni ca kurvīta teṣāṃ dharmān yathoditān  |
ratnaiśca pūjayedenaṃ pradhānapuruṣaiḥ saha  || 7.203[204 ] ||
ādānamapriyakaraṃ dānaṃ ca priyakārakam  |
abhīpsitānāmarthānāṃ kāle yuktaṃ praśasyate  || 7.204[205 ] ||
sarvaṃ karmedamāyattaṃ vidhāne daivamānuṣe  |
tayordaivamacintyaṃ tu mānuṣe vidyate kriyā  || 7.205[206 ] ||
daivena vidhinā yuktaṃ mānuṣyaṃ yatpravartate  |
parikleśena mahatā tadarthasya samādhakam  || 7.207  ||
saṃyuktasyāpi daivena puruṣakāreṇa varjitam  |
vinā puruṣakāreṇa phalaṃ kṣetraṃ prayacchati  || 7.208  ||
candrārkādyā grahā vāyuragnirāpastathaiva ca  |
iha daivena sādhyante pauruṣeṇa prayatnataḥ  || 7.209  ||
saha vāpi vrajedyuktaḥ saṃdhiṃ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ  |
mitraṃ hiraṇyaṃ bhūmiṃ vā saṃpaśyaṃstrividhaṃ phalam  || 7.206[210 ] ||
pārṣṇigrāhaṃ ca saṃprekṣya tathākrandaṃ ca maṇḍale  |
mitrādathāpyamitrādvā yātrāphalamavāpnuyāt || 7.207[211 ] ||
hiraṇyabhūmisaṃprāptyā pārthivo na tathaidhate  |
yathā mitraṃ dhruvaṃ labdhvā kṛśamapyāyatikṣamam  || 7.208[212 ] ||
dharmajñaṃ ca kṛtajñaṃ ca tuṣṭaprakṛtimeva ca  |
anuraktaṃ sthirārambhaṃ laghumitraṃ praśasyate  || 7.209[213 ] ||
prājñaṃ kulīnaṃ śūraṃ ca dakṣaṃ dātārameva ca  |
kṛtajñaṃ dhṛtimantaṃ ca kaṣṭamāhurariṃ budhāḥ  || 7.210[214 ] ||
āryatā puruṣajñānaṃ śauryaṃ karuṇaveditā  |
sthaulalakṣyaṃ ca satatamudāsīnaguṇodayaḥ  || 7.211[215 ] ||
ksemyāṃ sasyapradāṃ nityaṃ paśuvṛddhikarīmapi  |
parityajennṛpo bhūmimātmārthamavicārayan  || 7.212[216 ] ||
āpadarthaṃ dhanaṃ rakṣeddārān rakṣeddhanairapi  |
ātmānaṃ satataṃ rakṣeddārairapi dhanairapi  || 7.213[217 ] ||
saha sarvāḥ samutpannāḥ prasamīkṣyāpado bhṛśam  |
saṃyuktāṃśca viyuktāṃśca sarvopāyān sṛjedbudhaḥ  || 7.214[218 ] ||
upetāramupeyaṃ ca sarvopāyāṃśca kṛtsnaśaḥ  |
etattrayaṃ samāśritya prayatetārthasiddhaye  || 7.215[219 ] ||
evaṃ sarvamidaṃ rājā saha sammantrya mantribhiḥ  |
vyāyamyāplutya madhyāhne bhoktumantaḥpuraṃ viśet || 7.216[220 ] ||
tatrātmabhūtaiḥ kālajñairahāryaiḥ paricārakaiḥ  |
suparīkṣitamannādyamadyānmantrairviṣāpahaiḥ  || 7.217[221 ] ||
viṣaghnairagadaiścāsya sarvadravyāṇi yojayet |
viṣaghnāni ca ratnāni niyato dhārayetsadā  || 7.218[222 ] ||
parīkṣitāḥ striyaścainaṃ vyajanodakadhūpanaiḥ  |
veṣābharaṇasaṃśuddhāḥ spṛśeyuḥ susamāhitāḥ  || 7.219[223 ] ||
evaṃ prayatnaṃ kurvīta yānaśayyāsanāśane  |
snāne prasādhane caiva sarvālaṅkārakeṣu ca  || 7.220[224 ] ||
bhuktavān vihareccaiva strībhirantaḥpure saha  |
vihṛtya tu yathākālaṃ punaḥ kāryāṇi cintayet || 7.221[225 ] ||
alaṃkṛtaśca saṃpaśyedāyudhīyaṃ punarjanam  |
vāhanāni ca sarvāṇi śastrāṇyābharaṇāni ca  || 7.222[226 ] ||
saṃdhyāṃ copāsya śṛṇuyādantarveśmani śastrabhṛt |
rahasyākhyāyināṃ caiva praṇidhīnāṃ ca ceṣṭitam  || 7.223[227 ] ||
gatvā kakṣāntaraṃ tvanyatsamanujñāpya taṃ janam  |
praviśedbhojanārthaṃ ca strīvṛto'ntaḥpuraṃ punaḥ  || 7.224[228 ] ||
tatra bhuktvā punaḥ kiṃ cittūryaghoṣaiḥ praharṣitaḥ  |
saṃviśettaṃ yathākālamuttiṣṭhecca gataklamaḥ  || 7.255[229 ] ||
etadvidhānamātiṣṭhedarogaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ  |
asvasthaḥ sarvametattu bhṛtyeṣu viniyojayet || 7.226[230 ] ||

vyavahārān didṛkṣustu brāhmaṇaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ  |
mantrajñairmantribhiścaiva vinītaḥ praviśetsabhām  || 8.1 ||
tatrāsīnaḥ sthito vāpi pāṇimudyamya dakṣiṇam  |
vinītaveṣābharaṇaḥ paśyetkāryāṇi kāryiṇām  || 8.2 ||
pratyahaṃ deśadṛṣṭaiśca śāstradṛṣṭaiśca hetubhiḥ  |
aṣṭādaśasu mārgeṣu nibaddhāni pṛthakpṛthak || 8.3 ||
teṣāmādyamṛṇādānaṃ nikṣepo'svāmivikrayaḥ  |
saṃbhūya ca samutthānaṃ dattasyānapakarma ca  || 8.4 ||
vetanasyaiva cādānaṃ saṃvidaśca vyatikramaḥ  |
krayavikrayānuśayo vivādaḥ svāmipālayoḥ  || 8.5 ||
sīmāvivādadharmaśca pāruṣye daṇḍavācike  |
steyaṃ ca sāhasaṃ caiva strīsaṃgrahaṇameva ca  || 8.6 ||
strīpuṃdharmo vibhāgaśca dyūtamāhvaya eva ca  |
padānyaṣṭādaśaitāni vyavahārasthitāviha  || 8.7 ||
eṣu sthāneṣu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ vivādaṃ caratāṃ nṛṇām  |
dharmaṃ śāśvatamāśritya kuryātkāryavinirṇayam  || 8.8 ||
yadā svayaṃ na kuryāttu nṛpatiḥ kāryadarśanam  |
tadā niyuñjyādvidvāṃsaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kāryadarśane  || 8.9 ||
so'sya kāryāṇi saṃpaśyetsabhyaireva tribhirvṛtaḥ  |
sabhāmeva praviśyāgryāmāsīnaḥ sthita eva vā  || 8.10 ||
yasmin deśe niṣīdanti viprā vedavidastrayaḥ  |
rājñaścādhikṛto vidvān brahmaṇastāṃ sabhāṃ viduḥ  || 8.11 ||
dharmo viddhastvadharmeṇa sabhāṃ yatropatiṣṭhate  |
śalyaṃ cāsya na kṛntanti viddhāstatra sabhāsadaḥ  || 8.12 ||
sabhāṃ vā na praveṣṭavyaṃ vaktavyaṃ vā samañjasam  |
abruvan vibruvan vāpi naro bhavati kilbiṣī  || 8.13 ||
yatra dharmo hyadharmeṇa satyaṃ yatrānṛtena ca  |
hanyate prekṣamāṇānāṃ hatāstatra sabhāsadaḥ  || 8.14 ||
dharma eva hato hanti dharmo rakṣati rakṣitaḥ  |
tasmāddharmo na hantavyo mā no dharmo hato'vadhīt || 8.15 ||
vṛṣo hi bhagavān dharmastasya yaḥ kurute hyalam  |
vṛṣalaṃ taṃ vidurdevāstasmāddharmaṃ na lopayet || 8.16 ||
eka eva suhṛddharmo nidhāne'pyanuyāti yaḥ  |
śarīreṇa samaṃ nāśaṃ sarvamanyaddhi gacchati  || 8.17 ||
pādo'dharmasya kartāraṃ pādaḥ sākṣiṇamṛcchati  |
pādaḥ sabhāsadaḥ sarvān pādo rājānamṛcchati  || 8.18 ||
rājā bhavatyanenāstu mucyante ca sabhāsadaḥ  |
eno gacchati kartāraṃ nindārho yatra nindyate  || 8.19 ||
jātimātropajīvī vā kāmaṃ syādbrāhmaṇabruvaḥ  |
dharmapravaktā nṛpaterna tu śūdraḥ kathaṃ cana  || 8.20 ||
yasya śūdrastu kurute rājño dharmavivecanam  |
tasya sīdati tadrāṣṭraṃ paṅke gauriva paśyataḥ  || 8.21 ||
yadrāṣṭraṃ śūdrabhūyiṣṭhaṃ nāstikākrāntamadvijam  |
vinaśyatyāśu tatkṛtsnaṃ durbhikṣavyādhipīḍitam  || 8.22 ||
dharmāsanamadhiṣṭhāya saṃvītāṅgaḥ samāhitaḥ  |
praṇamya lokapālebhyaḥ kāryadarśanamārabhet || 8.23 ||
arthānarthāvubhau buddhvā dharmādharmau ca kevalau  |
varṇakrameṇa sarvāṇi paśyetkāryāṇi kāryiṇām  || 8.24 ||
bāhyairvibhāvayelliṅgairbhāvamantargataṃ nṛṇām  |
svaravarṇeṅgitākāraiścakṣuṣā ceṣṭitena ca  || 8.25 ||
ākārairiṅgitairgatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣitena ca  |
netravaktravikāraiśca gṛhyate'ntargataṃ manaḥ  || 8.26 ||
bāladāyādikaṃ rikthaṃ tāvadrājānupālayet |
yāvatsa syātsamāvṛtto yāvaccātītaśaiśavaḥ  || 8.27 ||
vaśāputrāsu caivaṃ syādrakṣaṇaṃ niṣkulāsu ca  |
pativratāsu ca strīṣu vidhavāsvāturāsu ca  || 8.28 ||
jīvantīnāṃ tu tāsāṃ ye taddhareyuḥ svabāndhavāḥ  |
tāñśiṣyāccauradaṇḍena dhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ  || 8.29 ||
praṇaṣṭasvāmikaṃ rikthaṃ rājā tryabdaṃ nidhāpayet |
arvāktryabdāddharetsvāmī pareṇa nṛpatirharet || 8.30 ||
mamedamiti yo brūyātso'nuyojyo yathāvidhi  |
saṃvādya rūpasaṃkhyādīn svāmī taddravyamarhati  || 8.31 ||
avedayāno naṣṭasya deśaṃ kālaṃ ca tattvataḥ  |
varṇaṃ rūpaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca tatsamaṃ daṇḍamarhati  || 8.32 ||
ādadītātha ṣaḍbhāgaṃ pranaṣṭādhigatānnṛpaḥ  |
daśamaṃ dvādaśaṃ vāpi satāṃ dharmamanusmaran  || 8.33 ||
pranaṣṭādhigataṃ dravyaṃ tiṣṭhedyuktairadhiṣṭhitam  |
yāṃstatra caurān gṛhṇīyāttān rājebhena ghātayet || 8.34 ||
mamāyamiti yo brūyānnidhiṃ satyena mānavaḥ  |
tasyādadīta ṣaḍbhāgaṃ rājā dvādaśameva vā  || 8.35 ||
anṛtaṃ tu vadan daṇḍyaḥ svavittasyāṃśamaṣṭamam  |
tasyaiva vā nidhānasya saṃkhyayālpīyasīṃ kalām  || 8.36 ||
vidvāṃstu brāhmaṇo dṛṣṭvā pūrvopanihitaṃ nidhim  |
aśeṣato'pyādadīta sarvasyādhipatirhi saḥ  || 8.38 ||
yaṃ tu paśyennidhiṃ rājā purāṇaṃ nihitaṃ kṣitau  |
tasmāddvijebhyo dattvārdhamardhaṃ kośe praveśayet || 8.38 ||
nidhīnāṃ tu purāṇānāṃ dhātūnāmeva ca kṣitau  |
ardhabhāgrakṣaṇādrājā bhūmeradhipatirhi saḥ  || 8.39 ||
dātavyaṃ sarvavarṇebhyo rājñā caurairhṛtaṃ dhanam  |
rājā tadupayuñjānaścaurasyāpnoti kilbiṣam  || 8.40 ||
jātijānapadān dharmān śreṇīdharmāṃśca dharmavit |
samīkṣya kuladharmāṃśca svadharmaṃ pratipādayet || 8.41 ||
svāni karmāṇi kurvāṇā dūre santo'pi mānavāḥ  |
priyā bhavanti lokasya sve sve karmaṇyavasthitāḥ  || 8.42 ||
notpādayetsvayaṃ kāryaṃ rājā nāpyasya puruṣaḥ  |
na ca prāpitamanyena grasedarthaṃ kathaṃ cana  || 8.43 ||
yathā nayatyasṛkpātairmṛgasya mṛgayuḥ padam  |
nayettathānumānena dharmasya nṛpatiḥ padam  || 8.44 ||
satyamarthaṃ ca saṃpaśyedātmānamatha sākṣiṇaḥ  |
deśaṃ rūpaṃ ca kālaṃ ca vyavahāravidhau sthitaḥ  || 8.45 ||
sadbhirācaritaṃ yatsyāddhārmikaiśca dvijātibhiḥ  |
taddeśakulajātīnāmaviruddhaṃ prakalpayet || 8.46 ||
adhamarṇārthasiddhyarthamuttamarṇena coditaḥ  |
dāpayeddhanikasyārthamadhamarṇādvibhāvitam  || 8.47 ||
yairyairupāyairarthaṃ svaṃ prāpnuyāduttamarṇikaḥ  |
tairtairupāyaiḥ saṃgṛhya dāpayedadhamarṇikam  || 8.48 ||
dharmeṇa vyavahāreṇa chalenācaritena ca  |
prayuktaṃ sādhayedarthaṃ pañcamena balena ca  || 8.49 ||
yaḥ svayaṃ sādhayedarthamuttamarṇo'dhamarṇikāt |
na sa rājñābhiyoktavyaḥ svakaṃ saṃsādhayan dhanam  || 8.50 ||
arthe'pavyayamānaṃ tu karaṇena vibhāvitam  |
dāpayeddhanikasyārthaṃ daṇḍaleśaṃ ca śaktitaḥ  || 8.51 ||
apahnave'dhamarṇasya dehītyuktasya saṃsadi  |
abhiyoktā diśeddeśyaṃ karaṇaṃ vānyaduddiśet || 8.52 ||
adeśyaṃ yaśca diśati nirdiśyāpahnute ca yaḥ  |
yaścādharottarānarthān vigītānnāvabudhyate  || 8.53 ||
apadiśyāpadeśyaṃ ca punaryastvapadhāvati  |
samyakpraṇihitaṃ cārthaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ sannābhinandati  || 8.54 ||
asaṃbhāṣye sākṣibhiśca deśe saṃbhāṣate mithaḥ  |
nirucyamānaṃ praśnaṃ ca necchedyaścāpi niṣpatet || 8.55 ||
brūhītyuktaśca na brūyāduktaṃ ca na vibhāvayet |
na ca pūrvāparaṃ vidyāttasmādarthātsa hīyate  || 8.56 ||
sākṣiṇaḥ santi metyuktvā diśetyukto diśenna yaḥ  |
dharmasthaḥ kāraṇairetairhīnaṃ tamapi nirdiśet || 8.57 ||
abhiyoktā na cedbrūyādbadhyo daṇḍyaśca dharmataḥ  |
na cettripakṣātprabrūyāddharmaṃ prati parājitaḥ  || 8.58 ||
yo yāvannihnuvītārthaṃ mithyā yāvati vā vadet |
tau nṛpeṇa hyadharmajñau dāpyo taddviguṇaṃ damam  || 8.59 ||
pṛṣṭo'pavyayamānastu kṛtāvastho dhanaiṣiṇā  |
tryavaraiḥ sākṣibhirbhāvyo nṛpabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau  || 8.60 ||
yādṛśā dhanibhiḥ kāryā vyavahāreṣu sākṣiṇaḥ  |
tādṛśān saṃpravakṣyāmi yathā vācyamṛtaṃ ca taiḥ  || 8.61 ||
gṛhiṇaḥ putriṇo maulāḥ kṣatraviśśūdrayonayaḥ  |
arthyuktāḥ sākṣyamarhanti na ye ke cidanāpadi  || 8.62 ||
āptāḥ sarveṣu varṇeṣu kāryāḥ kāryeṣu sākṣiṇaḥ  |
sarvadharmavido'lubdhā viparītāṃstu varjayet || 8.63 ||
nārthasaṃbandhino nāptā na sahāyā na vairiṇaḥ  |
na dṛṣṭadoṣāḥ kartavyā na vyādhyārtā na dūṣitāḥ  || 8.64 ||
na sākṣī nṛpatiḥ kāryo na kārukakuśīlavau  |
na śrotriyo na liṅgastho na saṅgebhyo vinirgataḥ  || 8.65 ||
nādhyadhīno na vaktavyo na dasyurna vikarmakṛt |
na vṛddho na śiśurnaiko nāntyo na vikalendriyaḥ  || 8.66 ||
nārto na matto nonmatto na kṣuttṛṣṇopapīḍitaḥ  |
na śramārto na kāmārto na kruddho nāpi taskaraḥ  || 8.67 ||
strīṇāṃ sākṣyaṃ striyaḥ kuryurdvijānāṃ sadṛśā dvijāḥ  |
śūdrāśca santaḥ śūdrāṇāmantyānāmantyayonayaḥ  || 8.68 ||
anubhāvī tu yaḥ kaścitkuryātsākṣyaṃ vivādinām  |
antarveśmanyaraṇye vā śarīrasyāpi cātyaye  || 8.69 ||
striyāpyasaṃbhāve kāryaṃ bālena sthavireṇa vā  |
śiṣyeṇa bandhunā vāpi dāsena bhṛtakena vā  || 8.70 ||
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca sākṣyeṣu vadatāṃ mṛṣā  |
jānīyādasthirāṃ vācamutsiktamanasāṃ tathā  || 8.71 ||
sāhaseṣu ca sarveṣu steyasaṃgrahaṇeṣu ca  |
vāgdaṇḍayośca pāruṣye na parīkṣeta sākṣiṇaḥ  || 8.72 ||
bahutvaṃ parigṛhṇīyātsākṣidvaidhe narādhipaḥ  |
sameṣu tu guṇotkṛṣṭān guṇidvaidhe dvijottamān  || 8.73 ||
samakṣadarśanātsākṣyaṃ śravaṇāccaiva sidhyati  |
tatra satyaṃ bruvan sākṣī dharmārthābhyāṃ na hīyate  || 8.74 ||
sākṣī dṛṣṭaśrutādanyadvibruvannāryasaṃsadi  |
avāṅnarakamabhyeti pretya svargācca hīyate  || 8.75 ||
yatrānibaddho'pīkṣeta śṛṇuyādvāpi kiṃ cana  |
pṛṣṭastatrāpi tadbrūyādyathādṛṣṭaṃ yathāśrutam  || 8.76 ||
eko'lubdhastu sākṣī syādbahvyaḥ śucyo'pi na striyaḥ  |
strībuddherasthiratvāttu doṣaiścānye'pi ye vṛtāḥ  || 8.77 ||
svabhāvenaiva yadbrūyustadgrāhyaṃ vyāvahārikam  |
ato yadanyadvibrūyurdharmārthaṃ tadapārthakam  || 8.78 ||
sabhāntaḥ sākṣiṇaḥ prāptānarthipratyarthisaṃnidhau  |
prāḍvivāko'nuyuñjīta vidhinānena sāntvayan  || 8.79 ||
yaddvayoranayorvettha kārye'smiṃśceṣṭitaṃ mithaḥ  |
tadbrūta sarvaṃ satyena yuṣmākaṃ hyatra sākṣitā  || 8.80 ||
satyaṃ sākṣye bruvan sākṣī lokānāpnoti puṣkalān  |
iha cānuttamāṃ kīrtiṃ vāgeṣā brahmapūjitā  || 8.81 ||
sākṣye'nṛtaṃ vadan pāśairbadhyate vāruṇairbhṛśam  |
vivaśaḥ śatamājātīstasmātsākṣyaṃ vadedṛtam  || 8.82 ||
satyena pūyate sākṣī dharmaḥ satyena vardhate  |
