tasmātsatyaṃ hi vaktavyaṃ sarvavarṇeṣu sākṣibhiḥ  || 8.83 ||
ātmaiva hyātmanaḥ sākṣī gatirātmā tathātmanaḥ  |
māvamaṃsthāḥ svamātmānaṃ nṛṇāṃ sākṣiṇamuttamam  || 8.84 ||
manyante vai pāpakṛto na kaścitpaśyatīti naḥ  |
tāṃstu devāḥ prapaśyanti svasyaivāntarapūruṣaḥ  || 8.85 ||
dyaurbhūmirāpo hṛdayaṃ candrārkāgniyamānilāḥ  |
rātriḥ saṃdhye ca dharmaśca vṛttajñāḥ sarvadehinām  || 8.86 ||
devabrāhmaṇasāṃnidhye sākṣyaṃ pṛcchedṛtaṃ dvijān  |
udaṅmukhān prāṅmukhān vā pūrvāhṇe vai śuciḥ śucīn  || 8.87 ||
brūhīti brāhmaṇaṃ pṛcchetsatyaṃ brūhīti pārthivam  |
gobījakāñcanairvaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvaistu pātakaiḥ  || 8.88 ||
brahmaghno ye smṛtā lokā ye ca strībālaghātinaḥ  |
mitradruhaḥ kṛtaghnasya te te syurbruvato mṛṣā  || 8.89 ||
janmaprabhṛti yatkiṃ citpuṇyaṃ bhadra tvayā kṛtam  |
tatte sarvaṃ śuno gacchedyadi brūyāstvamanyathā  || 8.90 ||
eko'hamasmītyātmānaṃ yastvaṃ kalyāṇa manyase  |
nityaṃ sthitaste hṛdyeṣa puṇyapāpekṣitā muniḥ  || 8.91 ||
yamo vaivasvato devo yastavaiṣa hṛdi sthitaḥ  |
tena cedavivādaste mā gaṅgāṃ mā kurūn gamaḥ  || 8.92 ||
nagno muṇḍaḥ kapālena ca bhikṣārthī kṣutpipāsitaḥ  |
andhaḥ śatrukulaṃ gacchedyaḥ sākṣyamanṛtaṃ vadet || 8.93 ||
avākśirāstamasyandhe kilbiṣī narakaṃ vrajet |
yaḥ praśnaṃ vitathaṃ brūyātpṛṣṭaḥ san dharmaniścaye  || 8.94 ||
andho matsyānivāśnāti sa naraḥ kaṇṭakaiḥ saha  |
yo bhāṣate'rthavaikalyamapratyakṣaṃ sabhāṃ gataḥ  || 8.95 ||
yasya vidvān hi vadataḥ kṣetrajño nābhiśaṅkate  |
tasmānna devāḥ śreyāṃsaṃ loke'nyaṃ puruṣaṃ viduḥ  || 8.96 ||
yāvato bāndhavān yasmin hanti sākṣye'nṛtaṃ vadan  |
tāvataḥ saṃkhyayā tasmin śṛṇu saumyānupūrvaśaḥ  || 8.97 ||
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte  |
śatamaśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte  || 8.98 ||
hanti jātānajātāṃśca hiraṇyārthe'nṛtaṃ vadan  |
sarvaṃ bhūmyanṛte hanti mā sma bhūmyanṛtaṃ vadīḥ  || 8.99 ||
apsu bhūmivadityāhuḥ strīṇāṃ bhoge ca maithune  |
abjeṣu caiva ratneṣu sarveṣvaśmamayeṣu ca  || 8.100 ||
etān doṣānavekṣya tvaṃ sarvānanṛtabhāṣaṇe  |
yathāśrutaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ sarvamevāñjasā vada  || 8.101 ||
gorakṣakān vāṇijikāṃstathā kārukuśīlavān  |
preṣyān vārdhuṣikāṃścaiva viprān śūdravadācaret || 8.102 ||
tadvadan dharmato'rtheṣu jānannapyanythā naraḥ  |
na svargāccyavate lokāddaivīṃ vācaṃ vadanti tām  || 8.103 ||
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatra rtoktau bhavedvadhaḥ  |
tatra vaktavyamanṛtaṃ taddhi satyādviśiṣyate  || 8.104 ||
vāggaivatyaiśca carubhiryajeraṃste sarasvatīm  |
anṛtasyainasastasya kurvāṇā niṣkṛtiṃ parām  || 8.105 ||
kūṣmāṇḍairvāpi juhuyādghṛtamagnau yathāvidhi  |
udityṛcā vā vāruṇyā tṛcenābdaivatena vā  || 8.106 ||
tripakṣādabruvan sākṣyamṛṇādiṣu naro'gadaḥ  |
tadṛṇaṃ prāpnuyātsarvaṃ daśabandhaṃ ca sarvataḥ  || 8.107 ||
yasya dṛśyeta saptāhāduktavākyasya sākṣiṇaḥ  |
rogo'gnirjñātimaraṇamṛṇaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ  || 8.108 ||
asākṣikeṣu tvartheṣu mitho vivādamānayoḥ  |
avindaṃstattvataḥ satyaṃ śapathenāpi lambhayet || 8.109 ||
maharṣibhiśca devaiśca kāryārthaṃ śapathāḥ kṛtāḥ  |
vasiṣṭhaścāpi śapathaṃ śepe paijavane nṛpe  || 8.110 ||
na vṛthā śapathaṃ kuryātsvalpe'pyarthe naro budhaḥ  |
vṛthā hi śapathaṃ kurvan pretya ceha ca naśyati  || 8.111 ||
kāminīṣu vivāheṣu gavāṃ bhakṣye tathendhane  |
brāhmaṇābhyupapattau ca śapathe nāsti pātakam  || 8.112 ||
satyena śāpayedvipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ  |
gobījakāñcanairvaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvaistu pātakaiḥ  || 8.113 ||
agniṃ vāhārayedenamapsu cainaṃ nimajjayet |
putradārasya vāpyenaṃ śirāṃsi sparśayetpṛthak || 8.114 ||
yamiddho na dahatyagnirāpo nonmajjayanti ca  |
na cārtimṛcchati kṣipraṃ sa jñeyaḥ śapathe śuciḥ  || 8.115 ||
vatsasya hyabhiśastasya purā bhrātrā yavīyasā  |
nāgnirdadāha romāpi satyena jagataḥ spaśaḥ  || 8.116 ||
yasmin yasmin vivāde tu kauṭasākṣyaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet |
tattatkāryaṃ nivarteta kṛtaṃ cāpyakṛtaṃ bhavet || 8.117 ||
lobhānmohādbhayānmaitrātkāmātkrodhāttathaiva ca  |
ajñānādbālabhāvācca sākṣyaṃ vitathamucyate  || 8.118 ||
eṣāmanyatame sthāne yaḥ sākṣyamanṛtaṃ vadet |
tasya daṇḍaviśeṣāṃstu pravakṣyāmyanupūrvaśaḥ  || 8.119 ||
lobhātsahasraṃ daṇḍyastu mohātpūrvaṃ tu sāhasam  |
bhayāddvau madhyamau daṇḍau maitrātpūrvaṃ caturguṇam  || 8.120 ||
kāmāddaśaguṇaṃ pūrvaṃ krodhāttu triguṇaṃ param  |
ajñānāddve śate pūrṇe bāliśyācchatameva tu  || 8.121 ||
etānāhuḥ kauṭasākṣye proktān daṇḍānmanīṣibhiḥ  |
dharmasyāvyabhicārārthamadharmaniyamāya ca  || 8.122 ||
kauṭasākṣyaṃ tu kurvāṇāṃstrīn varṇān dhārmiko nṛpaḥ  |
pravāsayeddaṇḍayitvā brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet || 8.123 ||
daśa sthānāni daṇḍasya manuḥ svayaṃbhuvo'bravīt |
triṣu varṇeṣu yāni syurakṣato brāhmaṇo vrajet || 8.124 ||
upasthamudaraṃ jihvā hastau pādau ca pañcamam  |
cakṣurnāsā ca karṇau ca dhanaṃ dehastathaiva ca  || 8.125 ||
anubandhaṃ parijñāya deśakālau ca tattvataḥ  |
sārāparādho cālokya daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣu pātayet || 8.126 ||
adharmadaṇḍanaṃ loke yaśoghnaṃ kīrtināśanam  |
asvargyaṃ ca paratrāpi tasmāttatparivarjayet || 8.127 ||
adaṇḍyān daṇḍayan rājā daṇḍyāṃścaivāpyadaṇḍayan  |
ayaśo mahadāpnoti narakaṃ caiva gacchati  || 8.128 ||
vāgdaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ kuryāddhigdaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram  |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍamataḥ param  || 8.129 ||
vadhenāpi yadā tvetānnigrahītuṃ na śaknuyāt |
tadaiṣu sarvamapyetatprayuñjīta catuṣṭayam  || 8.130 ||
lokasaṃvyavahārārthaṃ yāḥ saṃjñāḥ prathitā bhuvi  |
tāmrarūpyasuvarṇānāṃ tāḥ pravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 8.131 ||
jālāntaragate bhānau yatsūkṣmaṃ dṛśyate rajaḥ  |
prathamaṃ tatpramāṇānāṃ trasareṇuṃ pracakṣate  || 8.132 ||
trasareṇavo'ṣṭau vijñeyā likṣaikā parimāṇataḥ  |
tā rājasarṣapastisraste trayo gaurasarṣapaḥ  || 8.133 ||
sarṣapāḥ ṣaḍyavo madhyastriyavaṃ tvekakṛṣṇalam  |
pañcakṛṣṇalako māṣaste suvarṇastu ṣoḍaśa  || 8.134 ||
palaṃ suvarṇāścatvāraḥ palāni dharaṇaṃ daśa  |
dve kṛṣṇale samadhṛte vijñeyo raupyamāṣakaḥ  || 8.135 ||
te ṣoḍaśa syāddharaṇaṃ purāṇaścaiva rājataḥ  |
kārṣāpaṇastu vijñeyastāmrikaḥ kārṣikaḥ paṇaḥ  || 8.136 ||
dharaṇāni daśa jñeyaḥ śatamānastu rājataḥ  |
catuḥsauvarṇiko niṣko vijñeyastu pramāṇataḥ  || 8.137 ||
paṇānāṃ dve śate sārdhe prathamaḥ sāhasaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
madhyamaḥ pañca vijñeyaḥ sahasraṃ tveva cottamaḥ  || 8.138 ||
ṛṇe deye pratijñāte pañcakaṃ śatamarhati  |
apahnave taddviguṇaṃ tanmanoranuśāsanam  || 8.139 ||
vasiṣṭhavihitāṃ vṛddhiṃ sṛjedvittavivardhinīm  |
aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyānmāsādvārdhuṣikaḥ śate  || 8.140 ||
dvikaṃ śataṃ vā gṛhṇīyātsatāṃ dharmamanusmaran  |
dvikaṃ śataṃ hi gṛhṇāno na bhavatyarthakilbiṣī  || 8.141 ||
dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ samam  |
māsasya vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyādvarṇānāmanupūrvaśaḥ  || 8.142 ||
na tvevādhau sopakāre kausīdīṃ vṛddhimāpnuyāt |
na cādheḥ kālasaṃrodhānnisargo'sti na vikrayaḥ  || 8.143 ||
na bhoktavyo balādādhirbhuñjāno vṛddhimutsṛjet |
mūlyena toṣayeccainamādhisteno'nyathā bhavet || 8.144 ||
ādhiścopanidhiścobhau na kālātyayamarhataḥ  |
avahāryau bhavetāṃ tau dīrghakālamavasthitau  || 8.145 ||
saṃprītyā bhujyamānāni na naśyanti kadā cana  |
dhenuruṣṭro vahannaśvo yaśca damyaḥ prayujyate  || 8.146 ||
yatkiṃ ciddaśavarṣāṇi saṃnidhau prekṣate dhanī  |
bhujyamānaṃ paraistūṣṇīṃ na sa tallabdhumarhati  || 8.147 ||
ajaḍaścedapogaṇḍo viṣaye cāsya bhujyate  |
bhagnaṃ tadvyavahāreṇa bhoktā taddravyamarhati  || 8.148 ||
ādhiḥ sīmā bāladhanaṃ nikṣepopanidhiḥ striyaḥ  |
rājasvaṃ śrotriyasvaṃ ca na bhogena praṇaśyati  || 8.149 ||
yaḥ svāminānanujñātamādhiṃ bhūṅkte'vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
tenārdhavṛddhirmoktavyā tasya bhogasya niṣkṛtiḥ  || 8.150 ||
kusīdavṛddhirdvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti sakṛdāhṛtā  |
dhānye sade lave vāhye nātikrāmati pañcatām  || 8.151 ||
kṛtānusārādadhikā vyatiriktā na sidhyati  |
kusīdapathamāhustaṃ pañcakaṃ śatamarhati  || 8.152 ||
nātisāṃvatsarīṃ vṛddhiṃ na cādṛṣṭāṃ punarharet |
cakravṛddhiḥ kālavṛddhiḥ kāritā kāyikā ca yā  || 8.153 ||
ṛṇaṃ dātumaśakto yaḥ kartumicchetpunaḥ kriyām  |
sa dattvā nirjitāṃ vṛddhiṃ karaṇaṃ parivartayet || 8.154 ||
adarśayitvā tatraiva hiraṇyaṃ parivartayet |
yāvatī saṃbhavedvṛddhistāvatīṃ dātumarhati  || 8.155 ||
cakravṛddhiṃ samārūḍho deśakālavyavasthitaḥ  |
atikrāman deśakālau na tatphalamavāpnuyāt || 8.156 ||
samudrayānakuśalā deśakālārthadarśinaḥ  |
sthāpayanti tu yāṃ vṛddhiṃ sā tatrādhigamaṃ prati  || 8.157 ||
yo yasya pratibhūstiṣṭheddarśanāyeha mānavaḥ  |
adarśayan sa taṃ tasya prayacchetsvadhanādṛṇam  || 8.158 ||
prātibhāvyaṃ vṛthādānamākṣikaṃ saurikāṃ ca yat |
daṇḍaśulkāvaśeṣaṃ ca na putro dātumarhati  || 8.159 ||
darśanaprātibhāvye tu vidhiḥ syātpūrvacoditaḥ  |
dānapratibhuvi prete dāyādānapi dāpayet || 8.160 ||
adātari punardātā vijñātaprakṛtāvṛṇam  |
paścātpratibhuvi prete parīpsetkena hetunā  || 8.161 ||
nirādiṣṭadhanaścettu pratibhūḥ syādalaṃdhanaḥ  |
svadhanādeva taddadyānnirādiṣṭa iti sthitiḥ  || 8.162 ||
mattonmattārtādhyadhīnairbālena sthavireṇa vā  |
asaṃbaddhakṛtaścaiva vyāvahāro na sidhyati  || 8.163 ||
satyā na bhāṣā bhavati yadyapi syātpratiṣṭhitā  |
bahiścedbhāṣyate dharmānniyatādvyavahārikāt || 8.164 ||
yogādhamanavikrītaṃ yogadānapratigraham  |
yatra vāpyupadhiṃ paśyettatsarvaṃ vinivartayet || 8.165 ||
grahītā yadi naṣṭaḥ syātkuṭumbārthe kṛto vyayaḥ  |
dātavyaṃ bāndhavaistatsyātpravibhaktairapi svataḥ  || 8.166 ||
kuṭumbārthe'dhyadhīno'pi vyavahāraṃ yamācaret |
svadeśe vā videśe vā taṃ jyāyānna vicālayet || 8.167 ||
balāddattaṃ balādbhuktaṃ balādyaccāpi lekhitam  |
sarvān balakṛtānarthānakṛtānmanurabravīt || 8.168 ||
trayaḥ parārthe kliśyanti sākṣiṇaḥ pratibhūḥ kulam  |
catvārastūpacīyante vipra āḍhyo vaṇiṅnṛpaḥ  || 8.169 ||
anādeyaṃ nādadīta parikṣīṇo'pi pārthivaḥ  |
na cādeyaṃ samṛddho'pi sūkṣmamapyarthamutsṛjet || 8.170 ||
anādeyasya cādānādādeyasya ca varjanāt |
daurbalyaṃ khyāpyate rājñaḥ sa pretyeha ca naśyati  || 8.171 ||
svādānādvarṇasaṃsargāttvabalānāṃ ca rakṣaṇāt |
balaṃ saṃjāyate rājñaḥ sa pretyeha ca vardhate  || 8.172 ||
tasmādyama iva svāmī svayaṃ hitvā priyāpriye  |
varteta yāmyayā vṛttyā jitakrodho jitendriyaḥ  || 8.173 ||
yastvadharmeṇa kāryāṇi mohātkuryānnarādhipaḥ  |
acirāttaṃ durātmānaṃ vaśe kurvanti śatravaḥ  || 8.174 ||
kāmakrodhau tu saṃyamya yo'rthān dharmeṇa paśyati  |
prajāstamanuvartante samudramiva sindhavaḥ  || 8.175 ||
yaḥ sādhayantaṃ chandena vedayeddhanikaṃ nṛpe  |
sa rājñā taccaturbhāgaṃ dāpyastasya ca taddhanam  || 8.176 ||
karmaṇāpi samaṃ kuryāddhanikāyādhamarṇikaḥ  |
samo'vakṛṣṭajātistu dadyācchreyāṃstu tacchanaiḥ  || 8.177 ||
anena vidhinā rājā mitho vivadatāṃ nṛṇām  |
sākṣipratyayasiddhāni kāryāṇi samatāṃ nayet || 8.178 ||
kulaje vṛttasaṃpanne dharmajñe satyavādini  |
mahāpakṣe dhaninyārye nikṣepaṃ nikṣipedbudhaḥ  || 8.179 ||
yo yathā nikṣipeddhaste yamarthaṃ yasya mānavaḥ  |
sa tathaiva grahītavyo yathā dāyastathā grahaḥ  || 8.180 ||
yo nikṣepaṃ yācyamāno nikṣepturna prayacchati  |
sa yācyaḥ prāḍvivākena tannikṣepturasaṃnidhau  || 8.181 ||
sākṣyabhāve praṇidhibhirvayorūpasamanvitaiḥ  |
apadeśaiśca saṃnyasya hiraṇyaṃ tasya tattvataḥ  || 8.182 ||
sa yadi pratipadyeta yathānyastaṃ yathākṛtam  |
na tatra vidyate kiṃ cidyatparairabhiyujyate  || 8.183 ||
teṣāṃ na dadyādyadi tu taddhiraṇyaṃ yathāvidhi  |
ubhau nigṛhya dāpyaḥ syāditi dharmasya dhāraṇā  || 8.184 ||
nikṣepopanidhī nityaṃ na deyau pratyanantare  |
naśyato vinipāte tāvanipāte tvanāśinau  || 8.185 ||
svayameva tu yau dadyānmṛtasya pratyanantare  |
na sa rājñābhiyoktavyo na nikṣeptuśca bandhubhiḥ  || 8.186 ||
acchalenaiva cānvicchettamarthaṃ prītipūrvakam  |
vicārya tasya vā vṛttaṃ sāmnaiva parisādhayet || 8.187 ||
nikṣepeṣveṣu sarveṣu vidhiḥ syātparisādhane  |
samudre nāpnuyātkiṃ cidyadi tasmānna saṃharet || 8.188 ||
caurairhṛtaṃ jalenoḍhamagninā dagdhameva vā  |
na dadyādyadi tasmātsa na saṃharati kiṃ cana  || 8.189 ||
nikṣepasyāpahartāramanikṣeptārameva ca  |
sarvairupāyairanvicchecchapathaiścaiva vaidikaiḥ  || 8.190 ||
yo nikṣepaṃ nārpayati yaścānikṣipya yācate  |
tāvubhau cauravacchāsyau dāpyau vā tatsamaṃ damam  || 8.191 ||
nikṣepasyāpahartāraṃ tatsamaṃ dāpayeddamam  |
tathopanidhihartāramaviśeṣeṇa pārthivaḥ  || 8.192 ||
upadhābhiśca yaḥ kaścitparadravyaṃ harennaraḥ  |
sasahāyaḥ sa hantavyaḥ prakāśaṃ vividhairvadhaiḥ  || 8.193 ||
nikṣepo yaḥ kṛto yena yāvāṃśca kulasaṃnidhau  |
tāvāneva sa vijñeyo vibruvan daṇḍamarhati  || 8.194 ||
mitho dāyaḥ kṛto yena gṛhīto mitha eva vā  |
mitha eva pradātavyo yathā dāyastathā grahaḥ  || 8.195 ||
nikṣiptasya dhanasyaivaṃ prītyopanihitasya ca  |
rājā vinirṇayaṃ kuryādakṣiṇvannyāsadhāriṇam  || 8.196 ||
vikrīṇīte parasya svaṃ yo'svāmī svāmyasammataḥ  |
na taṃ nayeta sākṣyaṃ tu stenamastenamāninam  || 8.197 ||
avahāryo bhaveccaiva sānvayaḥ ṣaṭśataṃ damam  |
niranvayo'napasaraḥ prāptaḥ syāccaurakilbiṣam  || 8.198 ||
asvāminā kṛto yastu dāyo vikraya eva vā  |
akṛtaḥ sa tu vijñeyo vyavahāre yathā sthitiḥ  || 8.199 ||
saṃbhogo dṛśyate yatra na dṛśyetāgamaḥ kva cit |
āgamaḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra na saṃbhoga iti sthitiḥ  || 8.200 ||
vikrayādyo dhanaṃ kiṃ cidgṛhṇīyatkulasaṃnidhau  |
krayeṇa sa viśuddhaṃ hi nyāyato labhate dhanam  || 8.201 ||
atha mūlamanāhāryaṃ prakāśakrayaśodhitaḥ  |
adaṇḍyo mucyate rājñā nāṣṭiko labhate dhanam  || 8.202 ||
nānyadanyena saṃsṛṣṭa- rūpaṃ vikrayamarhati  |
na cāsāraṃ na ca nyūnaṃ na dūreṇa tirohitam  || 8.203 ||
anyāṃ ceddarśayitvānyā voḍhuḥ kanyā pradīyate  |
ubhe ta ekaśulkena vahedityabravīnmanuḥ  || 8.204 ||
nonmattāyā na kuṣṭhinyā na ca yā spṛṣṭamaithunā  |
pūrvaṃ doṣānabhikhyāpya pradātā daṇḍamarhati  || 8.205 ||
ṛtvigyadi vṛto yajñe svakarma parihāpayet |
tasya karmānurūpeṇa deyo'ṃśaḥ sahakartṛbhiḥ  || 8.206 ||
dakṣiṇāsu ca dattāsu svakarma parihāpayan  |
kṛtsnameva labhetāṃśamanyenaiva ca kārayet || 8.207 ||
yasmin karmaṇi yāstu syuruktāḥ pratyaṅgadakṣiṇāḥ  |
sa eva tā ādidīta bhajeran sarva eva vā  || 8.208 ||
rathaṃ haretcādhvaryurbrahmādhāne ca vājinam  |
hotā vāpi haredaśvamudgātā cāpyanaḥ kraye  || 8.209 ||
sarveṣāmardhino mukhyāstadardhenārdhino'pare  |
tṛtīyinastṛtīyāṃśāścaturthāṃśāśca pādinaḥ  || 8.210 ||
saṃbhūya svāni karmāṇi kurvadbhiriha mānavaiḥ  |
anena vidhiyogena kartavyāṃśaprakalpanā  || 8.211 ||
dharmārthaṃ yena dattaṃ syātkasmai cidyācate dhanam  |
paścācca na tathā tatsyānna deyaṃ tasya tadbhavet || 8.212 ||
yadi saṃsādhayettattu darpāllobhena vā punaḥ  |
rājñā dāpyaḥ suvarṇaṃ syāttasya steyasya niṣkṛtiḥ  || 8.213 ||
dattasyaiṣoditā dharmyā yathāvadanapakriyā  |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi vetanasyānapakriyām  || 8.214 ||
bhṛto nārto na kuryādyo darpātkarma yathoditam  |
sa daṇḍyaḥ kṛṣṇalānyaṣṭau na deyaṃ cāsya vetanam  || 8.215 ||
ārtastu kuryātsvasthaḥ san yathābhāṣitamāditaḥ  |
sa dīrghasyāpi kālasya tallabhetaiva vetanam  || 8.216 ||
yathoktamārtaḥ sustho vā yastatkarma na kārayet |
na tasya vetanaṃ deyamalponasyāpi karmaṇaḥ  || 8.217 ||
eṣa dharmo'khilenokto vetanādānakarmaṇaḥ  |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi dharmaṃ samayabhedinām  || 8.218 ||
yo grāmadeśasaṃghānāṃ kṛtvā satyena saṃvidam  |
visaṃvadennaro lobhāttaṃ rāṣṭrādvipravāsayet || 8.219 ||
nigṛhya dāpayeccainaṃ samayavyabhicāriṇam  |
catuḥsuvarṇān ṣaṇniṣkāṃśchatamānaṃ ca rājakam  || 8.220 ||
etaddaṇḍavidhiṃ kuryāddhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ  |
grāmajātisamūheṣu samayavyabhicāriṇām  || 8.221 ||
krītvā vikrīya vā kiṃ cidyasyehānuśayo bhavet |
so'ntardaśāhāttaddravyaṃ dadyāccaivādadīta vā  || 8.222 ||
pareṇa tu daśāhasya na dadyānnāpi dāpayet |
ādadāno dadatcaiva rājñā daṇḍyau śatāni ṣaṭ || 8.223 ||
yastu doṣavatīṃ kanyāmanākhyāya prayacchati  |
tasya kuryānnṛpo daṇḍaṃ svayaṃ ṣaṇṇavatiṃ paṇān  || 8.224 ||
akanyeti tu yaḥ kanyāṃ brūyāddveṣeṇa mānavaḥ  |
sa śataṃ prāpnuyāddaṇḍaṃ tasyā doṣamadarśayan  || 8.225 ||
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ kanyāsveva pratiṣṭhitāḥ  |
nākanyāsu kva cinnṝṇāṃ luptadharmakriyā hi tāḥ  || 8.226 ||
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrā niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam  |
teṣāṃ niṣṭhā tu vijñeyā vidvadbhiḥ saptame pade  || 8.227 ||
yasmin yasmin kṛte kārye yasyehānuśayo bhavet |
tamanena vidhānena dharmye pathi niveśayet || 8.228 ||
paśuṣu svāmināṃ caiva pālānāṃ ca vyatikrame  |
vivādaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathāvaddharmatattvataḥ  || 8.229 ||
divā vaktavyatā pāle rātrau svāmini tadgṛhe  |
yogakṣeme'nyathā cettu pālo vaktavyatāmiyāt || 8.230 ||
gopaḥ kṣīrabhṛto yastu sa duhyāddaśato varām  |
gosvāmyanumate bhṛtyaḥ sā syātpāle'bhṛte bhṛtiḥ  || 8.231 ||
naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ kṛmibhiḥ śvahataṃ viṣame mṛtam  |
hīnaṃ puruṣakāreṇa pradadyātpāla eva tu  || 8.232 ||
vighuṣya tu hṛtaṃ caurairna pālo dātumarhati  |
yadi deśe ca kāle ca svāminaḥ svasya śaṃsati  || 8.233 ||
karṇau carma ca vālāṃśca bastiṃ snāyuṃ ca rocanām  |
paśuṣu svāmināṃ dadyānmṛteṣvaṅkāni darśayet || 8.234 ||
ajāvike tu saṃruddhe vṛkaiḥ pāle tvanāyati  |
yāṃ prasahya vṛko hanyātpāle tatkilbiṣaṃ bhavet || 8.235 ||
tāsāṃ cedavaruddhānāṃ carantīnāṃ mitho vane  |
yāmutplutya vṛko hanyānna pālastatra kilbiṣī  || 8.236 ||
dhanuḥśataṃ parīhāro grāmasya syātsamantataḥ  |
śamyāpātāstrayo vāpi triguṇo nagarasya tu  || 8.237 ||
tatrāparivṛtaṃ dhānyaṃ vihiṃsyuḥ paśavo yadi  |
na tatra praṇayeddaṇḍaṃ nṛpatiḥ paśurakṣiṇām  || 8.238 ||
vṛtiṃ tatra prakurvīta yāmuṣṭro na vilokayet |
chidraṃ ca vārayetsarvaṃ śvasūkaramukhānugam  || 8.239 ||
pathi kṣetre parivṛte grāmāntīye'tha vā punaḥ  |
sapālaḥ śatadaṇḍārho vipālān vārayetpaśūn  || 8.240 ||
kṣetreṣvanyeṣu tu paśuḥ sapādaṃ paṇamarhati  |
sarvatra tu sado deyaḥ kṣetrikasyeti dhāraṇā  || 8.241 ||
anirdaśāhāṃ gāṃ sūtāṃ vṛṣān devapaśūṃstathā  |
sapālān vā vipālān vā na daṇḍyānmanurabravīt || 8.242 ||
kṣetriyasyātyaye daṇḍo bhāgāddaśaguṇo bhavet |
tato'rdhadaṇḍo bhṛtyānāmajñānātkṣetrikasya tu  || 8.243 ||
etadvidhānamātiṣṭheddhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ  |
svāmināṃ ca paśūnāṃ ca pālānāṃ ca vyatikrame  || 8.244 ||
sīmāṃ prati samutpanne vivāde grāmayordvayoḥ  |
jyeṣṭhe māsi nayetsīmāṃ suprakāśeṣu setuṣu  || 8.245 ||
sīṃāvṛkṣāṃśca kurvīta nyagrodhāśvatthakiṃśukān  |
śālmalīn sālatālāṃśca kṣīriṇaścaiva pādapān  || 8.246 ||
gulmān veṇūṃśca vividhān śamīvallīsthalāni ca  |
śarān kubjakagulmāṃśca tathā sīmā na naśyati  || 8.247 ||
taḍāgānyudapānāni vāpyaḥ prasravaṇāni ca  || 8.248 [m250] ||
sīmāsaṃdhiṣu kāryāṇi devatāyatanāni ca  || 8.248[m250 ] ||
upachannāni cānyāni sīmāliṅgāni kārayet |
sīmājñāne nṛṇāṃ vīkṣya nityaṃ loke viparyayam  || 8.249 ||
aśmano'sthīni govālāṃstuṣān bhasma kapālikāḥ  |
karīṣamiṣṭakāṅgārāṃśśarkarā vālukāstathā  || 8.250[m248] ||
yāni caivaṃprakārāṇi kālādbhūmirna bhakṣayet |
tāni saṃdhiṣu sīmāyāmaprakāśāni kārayet || 8.251 ||
etairliṅgairnayetsīmāṃ rājā vivadamānayoḥ  |
pūrvabhuktyā ca satatamudakasyāgamena ca  || 8.252 ||
yadi sṃśaya eva syālliṅgānāmapi darśane  |
sākṣipratyaya eva syātsīmāvādavinirṇayaḥ  || 8.253 ||
grāmīyakakulānāṃ ca samakṣaṃ sīmni sākṣiṇaḥ  |
praṣṭavyāḥ sīmaliṅgāni tayoścaiva vivādinoḥ  || 8.254 ||
te pṛṣṭāstu yathā brūyuḥ samastāḥ sīmni niścayam  |
nibadhnīyāttathā sīmāṃ sarvāṃstāṃścaiva nāmataḥ  || 8.255 ||
śirobhiste gṛhītvorvīṃ sragviṇo raktavāsasaḥ  |
sukṛtaiḥ śāpithāḥ svaiḥ svairnayeyuste samañjasam  || 8.256 ||
yathoktena nayantaste pūyante satyasākṣiṇaḥ  |
viparītaṃ nayantastu dāpyāḥ syurdviśataṃ damam  || 8.257 ||
sākṣyabhāve tu catvāro grāmāḥ sāmantavāsinaḥ  |
sīmāvinirṇayaṃ kuryuḥ prayatā rājasaṃnidhau  || 8.258 ||
sāmantānāmabhāve tu maulānāṃ sīmni sākṣiṇām  |
imānapyanuyuñjīta puruṣān vanagocarān  || 8.259 ||
vyādhāñśākunikān gopān kaivartānmūlakhānakān  |
vyālagrāhānuñchavṛttīnanyāṃśca vanacāriṇaḥ  || 8.260 ||
te pṛṣṭāstu yathā brūyuḥ sīmāsaṃdhiṣu lakṣaṇam  |
tattathā sthāpayedrājā dharmeṇa grāmayordvayoḥ  || 8.261 ||
kṣetrakūpataḍāgānāmārāmasya gṛhasya ca  |
sāmantapratyayo jñeyaḥ sīmāsetuvinirṇayaḥ  || 8.262 ||
sāmantāścenmṛṣā brūyuḥ setau vivādatāṃ nṛṇām  |
sarve pṛthakpṛthagdaṇḍyā rājñā madhyamasāhasam  || 8.263 ||
gṛhaṃ taḍāgamārāmaṃ kṣetraṃ vā bhīṣayā haran  |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syādajñānāddviśato damaḥ  || 8.264 ||
sīmāyāmaviṣahyāyāṃ svayaṃ rājaiva dharmavit |
pradiśedbhūmimekeṣāmupakārāditi sthitiḥ  || 8.265 ||
eṣo'khilenābhihito dharmaḥ sīmāvinirṇaye  |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi vākpāruṣyavinirṇayam  || 8.266 ||
śataṃ brāhmaṇamākruśya kṣatriyo daṇḍamarhati  |
vaiśyo'pyardhaśataṃ dve vā śūdrastu vadhamarhati  || 8.267 ||
pañcāśadbrāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ kṣatriyasyābhiśaṃsane  |
vaiśye syādardhapañcāśacchūdre dvādaśako damaḥ  || 8.268 ||
samavarṇe dvijātīnāṃ dvādaśaiva vyatikrame  |
vādeṣvavacanīyeṣu tadeva dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 8.269 ||
ekajātirdvijātīṃstu vācā dāruṇayā kṣipan  |
jihvāyāḥ prāpnuyācchedaṃ jaghanyaprabhavo hi saḥ  || 8.270 ||
nāmajātigrahaṃ tveṣāmabhidroheṇa kurvataḥ  |
nikṣepyo'yomayaḥ śaṅkurjvalannāsye daśāṅgulaḥ  || 8.271 ||
dharmopadeśaṃ darpeṇa viprāṇāmasya kurvataḥ  |
taptamāsecayettailaṃ vaktre śrotre ca pārthivaḥ  || 8.272 ||
śrutaṃ deśaṃ ca jātiṃ ca karma śarīrameva ca  |
vitathena bruvan darpāddāpyaḥ syāddviśataṃ damam  || 8.273 ||
kāṇaṃ vāpyatha vā khañjamanyaṃ vāpi tathāvidham  |
tathyenāpi bruvan dāpyo daṇḍaṃ kārṣāpaṇāvaram  || 8.274 ||
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ jāyāṃ bhrātaraṃ tanayaṃ gurum  |
ākṣārayañśataṃ dāpyaḥ panthānaṃ cādadadguroḥ  || 8.275 ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyābhyāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ kāryo vijānatā  |
brāhmaṇe sāhasaḥ pūrvaḥ kṣatriye tveva madhyamaḥ  || 8.276 ||
viṭśūdrayorevameva svajātiṃ prati tattvataḥ  |
chedavarjaṃ praṇayanaṃ daṇḍasyeti viniścayaḥ  || 8.277 ||
eṣa daṇḍavidhiḥ prokto vākpāruṣyasya tattvataḥ  |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi daṇḍapāruṣyanirṇayam  || 8.278 ||
yena kena cidaṅgena hiṃsyāccecchreṣṭhamantyajaḥ  |
chettavyaṃ tadtadevāsya tanmanoranuśāsanam  || 8.279 ||
pāṇimudyamya daṇḍaṃ vā pāṇicchedanamarhati  |
pādena praharan kopātpādacchedanamarhati  || 8.280 ||
sahāsanamabhiprepsurutkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭajaḥ  |
kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ sphicaṃ vāsyāvakartayet || 8.281 ||
avaniṣṭhīvato darpāddvāvoṣṭhau chedayennṛpaḥ  |
avamūtrayato meḍhramavaśardhayato gudam  || 8.282 ||
keśeṣu gṛhṇato hastau chedayedavicārayan  |
pādayordāḍhikāyāṃ ca grīvāyāṃ vṛṣaṇeṣu ca  || 8.283 ||
tvagbhedakaḥ śataṃ daṇḍyo lohitasya ca darśakaḥ  |
māṃsabhettā tu ṣaṇniṣkān pravāsyastvasthibhedakaḥ  || 8.284 ||
vanaspatīnāṃ sarveṣāmupabhogo yathā yathā  |
yathā tathā damaḥ kāryo hiṃsāyāmiti dhāraṇā  || 8.285 ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ ca duḥkhāya prahṛte sati  |
yathā yathā mahadduḥkhaṃ daṇḍaṃ kuryāttathā tathā  || 8.286 ||
aṅgāvapīḍanāyāṃ ca vraṇaśonitayostathā  |
samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyaḥ sarvadaṇḍamathāpi vā  || 8.287 ||
dravyāṇi hiṃsyādyo yasya jñānato'jñānato'pi vā  |
sa tasyotpādayettuṣṭiṃ rājñe dadyācca tatsamam  || 8.288 ||
carmacārmikabhāṇḍeṣu kāṣṭhaloṣṭamayeṣu  |
mūlyātpañcaguṇo daṇḍaḥ puṣpamūlaphaleṣu ca  || 8.289 ||
yānasya caiva yātuśca yānasvāmina eva ca  |
daśātivartanānyāhuḥ śeṣe daṇḍo vidhīyate  || 8.290 ||
chinnanāsye bhagnayuge tiryakpratimukhāgate  |
akṣabhaṅge ca yānasya cakrabhaṅge tathaiva ca  || 8.291 ||
chedane caiva yantrāṇāṃ yoktraraśmyostathaiva ca  |
ākrande cāpyapaihīti na daṇḍaṃ manurabravīt || 8.292 ||
yatrāpavartate yugyaṃ vaiguṇyātprājakasya tu  |
tatra svāmī bhaveddaṇḍyo hiṃsāyāṃ dviśataṃ damam  || 8.293 ||
prājakaścedbhavedāptaḥ prājako daṇḍamarhati  |
yugyasthāḥ prājake'nāpte sarve daṇḍyāḥ śataṃ śatam  || 8.294 ||
sa cettu pathi saṃruddhaḥ paśubhirvā rathena vā  |
pramāpayetprāṇabhṛtastatra daṇḍo'vicāritaḥ  || 8.295 ||
manuṣyamāraṇe kṣipraṃ cauravatkilbiṣaṃ bhavet |
prāṇabhṛtsu mahatsvardhaṃ gogajoṣṭrahayādiṣu  || 8.296 ||
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ  |
pañcāśattu bhaveddaṇḍaḥ śubheṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu  || 8.297 ||
gardhabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syātpañcamāṣikaḥ  |
māṣikastu bhaveddaṇḍaḥ śvasūkaranipātane  || 8.298 ||
bhāryā putraśca dāsaśca preṣyo bhrātrā ca sodaraḥ  |
prāptāparādhāstāḍyāḥ syū rajjvā veṇudalena vā  || 8.299 ||
pṛṣṭhatastu śarīrasya nottamāṅge kathaṃ cana  |
ato'nyathā tu praharan prāptaḥ syāccaurakilbiṣam  || 8.300 ||
eṣo'khilenābhihito daṇḍapāruṣyanirṇayaḥ  |
stenasyātaḥ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ daṇḍavinirṇaye  || 8.301 ||
paramaṃ yatnamātiṣṭhetstenānāṃ nigrahe nṛpaḥ  |
stenānāṃ nigrahādasya yaśo rāṣṭraṃ ca vardhate  || 8.302 ||
abhayasya hi yo dātā sa pūjyaḥ satataṃ nṛpaḥ  |
sattraṃ hi vardhate tasya sadaivābhayadakṣiṇam  || 8.303 ||
sarvato dharmaṣaḍbhāgo rājño bhavati rakṣataḥ  |
adharmādapi ṣaḍbhāgo bhavatyasya hyarakṣataḥ  || 8.304 ||
yadadhīte yadyajate yaddadāti yadarcati  |
tasya ṣaḍbhāgabhāgrājā samyagbhavati rakṣaṇāt || 8.305 ||
rakṣan dharmeṇa bhūtāni rājā vadhyāṃśca ghātayan  |
yajate'haraharyajñaiḥ sahasraśatadakṣiṇaiḥ  || 8.306 ||
yo'rakṣan balimādatte karaṃ śulkaṃ ca pārthivaḥ  |
pratibhāgaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca sa sadyo narakaṃ vrajet || 8.307 ||
arakṣitāraṃ rājānaṃ baliṣaḍbhāgahāriṇam  |
tamāhuḥ sarvalokasya samagramalahārakam  || 8.308 ||
anapekṣitamaryādaṃ nāstikaṃ vipraluṃpakam  |
arakṣitāramattāraṃ nṛpaṃ vidyādadhogatim  || 8.309 ||
adhārmikaṃ tribhirnyāyairnigṛhṇīyātprayatnataḥ  |
nirodhanena bandhena vividhena vadhena ca  || 8.310 ||
nigraheṇa hi pāpānāṃ sādhūnāṃ saṃgraheṇa ca  |
dvijātaya ivejyābhiḥ pūyante satataṃ nṛpāḥ  || 8.311 ||
kṣantavyaṃ prabhuṇā nityaṃ kṣipatāṃ kāryiṇāṃ nṛṇām  |
bālavṛddhāturāṇāṃ ca kurvatā hitamātmanaḥ  || 8.312 ||
yaḥ kṣipto marṣayatyārtaistena svarge mahīyate  |
yastvaiśvaryānna kṣamate narakaṃ tena gacchati  || 8.313 ||
rājā stenena gantavyo muktakeśena dhāvatā  |
ācakṣāṇena tatsteyamevaṃkarmāsmi śādhi mām  || 8.314 ||
skandhenādāya musalaṃ laguḍaṃ vāpi khādiram  |
śaktiṃ cobhayatastīkṣṇāmāyasaṃ daṇḍameva vā  || 8.315 ||
śāsanādvā vimokṣādvā stenaḥ steyādvimucyate  |
aśāsitvā tu taṃ rājā stenasyāpnoti kilbiṣam  || 8.316 ||
annāde bhrūṇahā mārṣṭi patyau bhāryāpacāriṇī  |
gurau śiṣyaśca yājyaśca steno rājani kilbiṣam  || 8.317 ||
rājabhiḥ kṛtadaṇḍāstu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ  |
nirmalāḥ svargamāyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā  || 8.318 ||
yastu rajjuṃ ghaṭaṃ kūpāddharedbhindyācca yaḥ prapām  |
sa daṇḍaṃ prāpnuyānmāṣaṃ tacca tasmin samāharet || 8.319 ||
dhānyaṃ daśabhyaḥ kumbhebhyo harato'bhyadhikaṃ vadhaḥ  |
śeṣe'pyekādaśaguṇaṃ dāpyastasya ca taddhanam  || 8.320 ||
tathā dharimameyānāṃ śatādabhyadhike vadhaḥ  |
suvarṇarajatādīnāmuttamānāṃ ca vāsasām  || 8.321 ||
pañcāśatastvabhyadhike hastacchedanamiṣyate  |
śeṣe tvekādaśaguṇaṃ mūlyāddaṇḍaṃ prakalpayet || 8.322 ||
puruṣāṇāṃ kulīnānāṃ nārīṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ  |
mukhyānāṃ caiva ratnānāṃ haraṇe vadhamarhati  || 8.323 ||
mahāpaśūnāṃ haraṇe śastrāṇāmauṣadhasya ca  |
kālamāsādya kāryaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ rājā prakalpayet || 8.324 ||
goṣu brāhmaṇasaṃsthāsu churikāyāśca bhedane  |
paśūnāṃ haraṇe caiva sadyaḥ kāryo'rdhapādikaḥ  || 8.325 ||
sūtrakārpāsakiṇvānāṃ gomayasya guḍasya ca  |
dadhnaḥ kṣīrasya takrasya pānīyasya tṛṇasya ca  || 8.326 ||
veṇuvaidalabhāṇḍānāṃ lavaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca  |
mṛṇmayānāṃ ca haraṇe mṛdo bhasmana eva ca  || 8.327 ||
matsyānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva tailasya ca ghṛtasya ca  |
māṃsasya madhunaścaiva yaccānyatpaśusaṃbhavam  || 8.328 ||
anyeṣāṃ caivamādīnāṃ madyānāmodanasya ca  |
pakvānnānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tanmulyāddviguṇo damaḥ  || 8.329 ||
puṣpeṣu harite dhānye gulmavallīnageṣu ca  |
anyeṣvaparipūteṣu daṇḍaḥ syātpañcakṛṣṇalaḥ  || 8.330 ||
paripūteṣu dhānyeṣu śākamūlaphaleṣu ca  |
niranvaye śataṃ daṇḍaḥ sānvaye'rdhaśataṃ damaḥ  || 8.331 ||
syātsāhasaṃ tvanvayavatprasabhaṃ karma yatkṛtam  |
niranvayaṃ bhavetsteyaṃ hṛtvāpavyayate ca yat || 8.332 ||
yastvetānyupakḷptāni dravyāṇi stenayennaraḥ  |
tamādyaṃ daṇḍayedrājā yaścāgniṃ corayedgṛhāt || 8.333 ||
yena yena yathāṅgena steno nṛṣu viceṣṭate  |
tattadeva harettasya pratyādeśāya pārthivaḥ  || 8.334 ||
pitācāryaḥ suhṛnmātā bhāryā putraḥ purohitaḥ  |
nādaṇḍyo nāma rājño'sti yaḥ svadharme na tiṣṭhati  || 8.335 ||
kārṣāpaṇaṃ bhaveddaṇḍyo yatrānyaḥ prākṛto janaḥ  |
tatra rājā bhaveddaṇḍyaḥ sahasramiti dhāraṇā  || 8.336 ||
aṣṭāpādyaṃ tu śūdrasya steye bhavati kilbiṣam  |
ṣoḍaśaiva tu vaiśyasya dvātriṃśatkṣatriyasya ca  || 8.337 ||
brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ pūrṇaṃ vāpi śataṃ bhavet |
dviguṇā vā catuḥṣaṣṭistaddoṣaguṇaviddhi saḥ  || 8.338 ||
vānaspatyaṃ mūlaphalaṃ dārvagnyarthaṃ tathaiva ca  |
tṛṇaṃ ca gobhyo grāsārthamasteyaṃ manurabravīt || 8.339 ||
yo'dattādāyino hastāllipseta brāhmaṇo dhanam  |
yājanādhyāpanenāpi yathā stenastathaiva saḥ  || 8.340 ||
dvijo'dhvagaḥ kṣīṇavṛttirdvāvikṣū dve ca mūlake  |
ādadānaḥ parakṣetrānna daṇḍaṃ dātumarhati  || 8.341 ||
asaṃditānāṃ saṃdātā saṃditānāṃ ca mokṣakaḥ  |
dāsāśvarathahartā ca prāptaḥ syāccorakilbiṣam  || 8.342 ||
anena vidhinā rājā kurvāṇaḥ stenanigraham  |
yaśo'smin prāpnuyālloke pretya cānuttamaṃ sukham  || 8.343 ||
aindraṃ sthānamabhiprepsuryaśaścākṣayamavyayam  |
nopekṣeta kṣaṇamapi rājā sāhasikaṃ naram  || 8.344 ||
vāgduṣṭāttaskarāccaiva daṇḍenaiva ca hiṃsataḥ  |
sāhasasya naraḥ kartā vijñeyaḥ pāpakṛttamaḥ  || 8.345 ||
sāhase vartamānaṃ tu yo marṣayati pārthivaḥ  |
sa vināśaṃ vrajatyāśu vidveṣaṃ cādhigacchati  || 8.346 ||
na mitrakāraṇādrājā vipulādvā dhanāgamāt |
samutsṛjetsāhasikān sarvabhūtabhayāvahān  || 8.347 ||
śastraṃ dvijātibhirgrāhyaṃ dharmo yatroparudhyate  |
dvijātīnāṃ ca varṇānāṃ viplave kālakārite  || 8.348 ||
ātmanaśca paritrāṇe dakṣiṇānāṃ ca saṃgare  |
strīviprābhyupapattau ca ghnan dharmeṇa na duṣyati  || 8.349 ||
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam  |
ātatāyinamāyāntaṃ hanyādevāvicārayan  || 8.350 ||
nātatāyivadhe doṣo hanturbhavati kaścana  |
prakāśaṃ vāprakāśaṃ vā manyustaṃ manyumṛcchati  || 8.351 ||
paradārābhimarśeṣu pravṛttānnṝnmahīpatiḥ  |
udvejanakarairdaṇḍaiśchinnayitvā pravāsayet || 8.352 ||
tatsamuttho hi lokasya jāyate varṇasaṃkaraḥ  |
yena mūlaharo'dharmaḥ sarvanāśāya kalpate  || 8.353 ||
parasya patnyā puruṣaḥ saṃbhāṣāṃ yojayan rahaḥ  |
pūrvamākṣārito doṣaiḥ prāpnuyātpūrvasāhasam  || 8.354 ||
yastvanākṣāritaḥ pūrvamabhibhāṣate kāraṇāt |
na doṣaṃ prāpnuyātkiṃ cinna hi tasya vyatikramaḥ  || 8.355 ||
parastriyaṃ yo'bhivadettīrthe'raṇye vane'pi vā  |
nadīnāṃ vāpi saṃbhede sa saṃgrahaṇamāpnuyāt || 8.356 ||
upacārakriyā keliḥ sparśo bhūṣaṇavāsasām  |
saha khaṭvāsanaṃ caiva sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 8.357 ||
striyaṃ spṛśedadeśe yaḥ spṛṣṭo vā marṣayettayā  |
parasparasyānumate sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 8.358 ||
abrāhmaṇaḥ saṃgrahaṇe prāṇāntaṃ daṇḍamarhati  |
caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ dārā rakṣyatamāḥ sadā  || 8.359 ||
bhikṣukā bandinaścaiva dīkṣitāḥ kāravastathā  |
saṃbhāṣanaṃ saha strībhiḥ kuryuraprativāritāḥ  || 8.360 ||
na saṃbhāṣāṃ parastrībhiḥ pratiṣiddhaḥ samācaret |
niṣiddho bhāṣamāṇastu suvarṇaṃ daṇḍamarhati  || 8.361 ||
naiṣa cāraṇadāreṣu vidhirnātmopajīviṣu  |
sajjayanti hi te nārīrnigūḍhāścārayanti ca  || 8.362 ||
kiṃ cideva tu dāpyaḥ syātsaṃbhāṣāṃ tābhirācaran  |
praiṣyāsu caikabhaktāsu rahaḥ pravrajitāsu ca  || 8.363 ||
yo'kāmāṃ dūṣayetkanyāṃ sa sadyo vadhamarhati  |
sakāmāṃ dūṣayaṃstulyo na vadhaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ  || 8.364 ||
kanyāṃ bhajantīmutkṛṣṭaṃ na kiṃ cidapi dāpayet |
jaghanyaṃ sevamānāṃ tu saṃyatāṃ vāsayedgṛhe  || 8.365 ||
uttamāṃ sevamānastu jaghanyo vadhamarhati  |
śulkaṃ dadyātsevamānaḥ samāmicchetpitā yadi  || 8.366 ||
abhiṣahya tu yaḥ kanyāṃ kuryāddarpeṇa mānavaḥ  |
tasyāśu kartye aṅgulyau daṇḍaṃ cārhati ṣaṭśatam  || 8.367 ||
sakāmāṃ dūṣayaṃstulyo nāṅgulicchedamāpnuyāt |
dviśataṃ tu damaṃ dāpyaḥ prasaṅgavinivṛttaye  || 8.368 ||
kanyaiva kanyāṃ yā kuryāttasyāḥ syāddviśato damaḥ  |
śulkaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ dadyācchiphāścaivāpnuyāddaśa  || 8.369 ||
yā tu kanyāṃ prakuryātstrī sā sadyo mauṇḍyamarhati  |
aṅgulyoreva vā chedaṃ khareṇodvahanaṃ tathā  || 8.370 ||
bhartāraṃ laṅghayedyā tu strī jñātiguṇadarpitā  |
tāṃ śvabhiḥ khādayedrājā saṃsthāne bahusaṃsthite  || 8.371 ||
pumāṃsaṃ dāhayetpāpaṃ śayane tapta āyase  |
abhyādadhyuśca kāṣṭhāni tatra dahyeta pāpakṛt || 8.372 ||
saṃvatsarābhiśastasya duṣṭasya dviguṇo damaḥ  |
vrātyayā saha saṃvāse cāṇḍālyā tāvadeva tu  || 8.373 ||
śūdro guptamaguptaṃ vā dvaijātaṃ varṇamāvasan  |
aguptamaṅgasarvasvairguptaṃ sarveṇa hīyate  || 8.374 ||
vaiśyaḥ sarvasvadaṇḍaḥ syātsaṃvatsaranirodhataḥ  |
sahasraṃ kṣatriyo daṇḍyo mauṇḍyaṃ mūtreṇa cārhati  || 8.375 ||
brāhmaṇīṃ yadyaguptāṃ tu gacchetāṃ vaiśyapārthivau  |
vaiśyaṃ pañcaśataṃ kuryātkṣatriyaṃ tu sahasriṇam  || 8.376 ||
ubhāvapi tu tāveva brāhmaṇyā guptayā saha  |
viplutau śūdravaddaṇḍyau dagdhavyau vā kaṭāgninā  || 8.377 ||
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyo guptāṃ viprāṃ balādvrajan  |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syādicchantyā saha saṃgataḥ  || 8.378 ||
mauṇḍyaṃ prāṇāntikaṃ daṇḍo brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate  |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ daṇḍaḥ prāṇāntiko bhavet || 8.379 ||
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyātsarvapāpeṣvapi sthitam  |
rāṣṭrādenaṃ bahiḥ kuryātsamagradhanamakṣatam  || 8.380 ||
na brāhmaṇavadhādbhūyānadharmo vidyate bhuvi  |
tasmādasya vadhaṃ rājā manasāpi na cintayet || 8.381 ||
vaiśyaścetkṣatriyāṃ guptāṃ vaiśyāṃ vā kṣatriyo vrajet |
yo brāhmaṇyāmaguptāyāṃ tāvubhau daṇḍamarhataḥ  || 8.382 ||
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍaṃ dāpyo gupte tu te vrajan  |
śūdrāyāṃ kṣatriyaviśoḥ sāhasro vai bhaveddamaḥ  || 8.383 ||
kṣatriyāyāmaguptāyāṃ vaiśye pañcaśataṃ damaḥ  |
mūtreṇa mauṇḍyamicchettu kṣatriyo daṇḍameva vā  || 8.384 ||
agupte kṣatriyāvaiśye śūdrāṃ vā brāhmaṇo vrajan  |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syātsahasraṃ tvantyajastriyam  || 8.385 ||
yasya stenaḥ pure nāsti nānyastrīgo na duṣṭavāk |
na sāhasikadaṇḍaghno sa rājā śakralokabhāk || 8.386 ||
eteṣāṃ nigraho rājñaḥ pañcānāṃ viṣaye svake  |
sāṃrājyakṛtsajātyeṣu loke caiva yaśaskaraḥ  || 8.387 ||
ṛtvijaṃ yastyajedyājyo yājyaṃ ca rtviktyajedyadi  |
śaktaṃ karmaṇyaduṣṭaṃ ca tayordaṇḍaḥ śataṃ śatam  || 8.388 ||
na mātā na pitā na strī na putrastyāgamarhati  |
tyajannapatitānetān rājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || 8.389 ||
āśrameṣu dvijātīnāṃ kārye vivadatāṃ mithaḥ  |
na vibrūyānnṛpo dharmaṃ cikīrṣan hitamātmanaḥ  || 8.390 ||
yathārhametānabhyarcya brāhmaṇaiḥ saha pārthivaḥ  |
sāntvena praśamayyādau svadharmaṃ pratipādayet || 8.391 ||
prativeśyānuveśyau ca kalyāṇe viṃśatidvije  |
arhāvabhojayan vipro daṇḍamarhati māṣakam  || 8.392 ||
śrotriyaḥ śrotriyaṃ sādhuṃ bhūtikṛtyeṣvabhojayan  |
tadannaṃ dviguṇaṃ dāpyo hiraṇyaṃ caiva māṣakam  || 8.393 ||
andho jaḍaḥ pīṭhasarpī saptatyā sthaviraśca yaḥ  |
śrotriyeṣūpakurvaṃśca na dāpyāḥ kena citkaram  || 8.394 ||
śrotriyaṃ vyādhitārtau ca bālavṛddhāvakiṃcanam  |
mahākulīnamāryaṃ ca rājā saṃpūjayetsadā  || 8.395 ||
śālmalīphalake ślakṣṇe nenijyānnejakaḥ śanaiḥ  |
na ca vāsāṃsi vāsobhirnirharenna ca vāsayet || 8.396 ||
tantuvāyo daśapalaṃ dadyādekapalādhikam  |
ato'nyathā vartamāno dāpyo dvādaśakaṃ damam  || 8.397 ||
śulkasthāneṣu kuśalāḥ sarvapaṇyavicakṣaṇāḥ  |
kuryurarghaṃ yathāpaṇyaṃ tato viṃśaṃ nṛpo haret || 8.398 ||
rājñaḥ prakhyātabhāṇḍāni pratiṣiddhāni yāni ca  |
tāṇi nirharato lobhātsarvahāraṃ harennṛpaḥ  || 8.399 ||
śulkasthānaṃ pariharannakāle krayavikrayī  |
mithyāvādī ca saṃkhyāne dāpyo'ṣṭaguṇamatyayam  || 8.400 ||
āgamaṃ nirgamaṃ sthānaṃ tathā vṛddhikṣayāvubhau  |
vicārya sarvapaṇyānāṃ kārayetkrayavikrayau  || 8.401 ||
pañcarātre pañcarātre pakṣe pakṣe'tha vā gate  |
kurvīta caiṣāṃ pratyakṣamarghasaṃsthāpanaṃ nṛpaḥ  || 8.402 ||
tulāmānaṃ pratīmānaṃ sarvaṃ ca syātsulakṣitam  |
ṣaṭsu ṣaṭsu ca māseṣu punareva parīkṣayet || 8.403 ||
paṇaṃ yānaṃ tare dāpyaṃ pauruṣo'rdhapaṇaṃ tare  |
pādaṃ paśuśca yoṣicca pādārdhaṃ riktakaḥ pumān  || 8.404 ||
bhāṇḍapūrṇāni yānāni tāryaṃ dāpyāni sārataḥ  |
riktabhāṇḍāni yatkiṃ citpumāṃsaścāparicchadāḥ  || 8.405 ||
dīrghādhvani yathādeśaṃ yathākālaṃ taro bhavet |
nadītīreṣu tadvidyātsamudre nāsti lakṣaṇam  || 8.406 ||
garbhiṇī tu dvimāsādistathā pravrajito muniḥ  |
brāhmaṇā liṅginaścaiva na dāpyāstārikaṃ tare  || 8.407 ||
yannāvi kiṃ ciddāśānāṃ viśīryetāparādhataḥ  |
taddāśaireva dātavyaṃ samāgamya svato'ṃśataḥ  || 8.408 ||
eṣa nauyāyināmukto vyavahārasya nirṇayaḥ  |
dāśāparādhatastoye daivike nāsti nigrahaḥ  || 8.409 ||
vāṇijyaṃ kārayedvaiśyaṃ kusīdaṃ kṛṣimeva ca  |
paśūnāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ caiva dāsyaṃ śūdraṃ dvijanmanām  || 8.410 ||
kṣatriyaṃ caiva vaiśyaṃ ca brāhmaṇo vṛttikarśitau  |
bibhṛyādānṛśaṃsyena svāni karmāṇi kārayet || 8.411 ||
dāsyaṃ tu kārayaṃllobhādbrāhmaṇaḥ saṃskṛtān dvijān  |
anicchataḥ prābhavatyādrājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || 8.412 ||
śūdraṃ tu kārayeddāsyaṃ krītamakrītameva vā  |
dāsyāyaiva hi sṛṣṭo'sau brāhmaṇasya svayaṃbhuvā  || 8.413 ||
na svāminā nisṛṣṭo'pi śūdro dāsyādvimucyate  |
nisargajaṃ hi tattasya kastasmāttadapohati  || 8.414 ||
dhvajāhṛto bhaktadāso gṛhajaḥ krītadattrimau  |
paitriko daṇḍadāsaśca saptaite dāsayonayaḥ  || 8.415 ||
bhāryā putraśca dāsaśca traya evādhanāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
yatte samadhigacchanti yasya te tasya taddhanam  || 8.416 ||
visrabdhaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ śūdrāddravyopādānamācaret |
na hi tasyāsti kiṃ citsvaṃ bhartṛhāryadhano hi saḥ  || 8.417 ||
vaiśyaśūdrau prayatnena svāni karmāṇi kārayet |
tau hi cyutau svakarmabhyaḥ kṣobhayetāmidaṃ jagat || 8.418 ||
ahanyahanyavekṣeta karmāntān vāhanāni ca  |
āyavyayau ca niyatāvākarān kośameva ca  || 8.419 ||
evaṃ sarvānimān rājā vyavahārān samāpayan  |
vyapohya kilbiṣaṃ sarvaṃ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim  || 8.420 ||

puruṣasya striyāścaiva dharme vartmani tiṣṭhatoḥ  |
saṃyoge viprayoge ca dharmān vakṣyāmi śāśvatān  || 9.1 ||
asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ kāryāḥ puruṣaiḥ svairdivāniśam  |
viṣayeṣu ca sajjantyaḥ saṃsthāpyā ātmano vaśe  || 9.2 ||
pitā rakṣati kaumāre bhartā rakṣati yauvane  |
rakṣanti sthavire putrā na strī svātantryamarhati  || 9.3 ||
kāle'dātā pitā vācyo vācyaścānupayan patiḥ  |
mṛte bhartari putrastu vācyo māturarakṣitā  || 9.4 ||
sūkṣmebhyo'pi prasaṅgebhyaḥ striyo rakṣyā viśeṣataḥ  |
dvayorhi kulayoḥ śokamāvaheyurarakṣitāḥ  || 9.5 ||
imaṃ hi sarvavarṇānāṃ paśyanto dharmamuttamam  |
yatante rakṣituṃ bhāryāṃ bhartāro durbalā api  || 9.6 ||
svāṃ prasūtiṃ caritraṃ ca kulamātmānameva ca  |
svaṃ ca dharmaṃ prayatnena jāyāṃ rakṣan hi rakṣati  || 9.7 ||
patirbhāryāṃ saṃpraviśya garbho bhūtveha jāyate  |
jāyāyāstaddhi jāyātvaṃ yadasyāṃ jāyate punaḥ  || 9.8 ||
yādṛśaṃ bhajate hi strī sutaṃ sūte tathāvidham  |
tasmātprajāviśuddhyarthaṃ striyaṃ rakṣetprayatnataḥ  || 9.9 ||
na kaścidyoṣitaḥ śaktaḥ prasahya parirakṣitum  |
etairupāyayogaistu śakyāstāḥ parirakṣitum  || 9.10 ||
arthasya saṃgrahe caināṃ vyaye caiva niyojayet |
śauce dharme'nnapaktyāṃ ca pāriṇāhyasya vekṣaṇe  || 9.11 ||
arakṣitā gṛhe ruddhāḥ puruṣairāptakāribhiḥ  |
ātmānamātmanā yāstu rakṣeyustāḥ surakṣitāḥ  || 9.12 ||
pānaṃ durjanasaṃsargaḥ patyā ca viraho'ṭanam  |
svapno'nyagehavāsaśca nārīsaṃdūṣaṇāni ṣaṭ || 9.13 ||
naitā rūpaṃ parīkṣante nāsāṃ vayasi saṃsthitiḥ  |
surūpaṃ vā virūpaṃ vā pumānityeva bhuñjate  || 9.14 ||
pauṃścalyāccalacittācca naisnehyācca svabhāvataḥ  |
rakṣitā yatnato'pīha bhartṛṣvetā vikurvate  || 9.15 ||
evaṃ svabhāvaṃ jñātvāsāṃ prajāpatinisargajam  |
paramaṃ yatnamātiṣṭhetpuruṣo rakṣaṇaṃ prati  || 9.16 ||
śayyāsanamalaṅkāraṃ kāmaṃ krodhamanārjavaṃ m:anāryatām]  |
drohabhāvaṃ kucaryāṃ ca strībhyo manurakalpayat || 9.17 ||
nāsti strīṇāṃ kriyā mantrairiti dharme vyavasthitiḥ  |
nirindriyā hyamantrāśca strībhyo'nṛtamiti sthitiḥ  || 9.18 ||
tathā ca śrutayo bahvyo nigītā nigameṣvapi  |
svālakṣaṇyaparīkṣārthaṃ tāsāṃ śṛṇuta niṣkṛtīḥ  || 9.19 ||
yanme mātā pralulubhe vicarantyapativratā  |
tanme retaḥ pitā vṛṅktāmityasyaitannidarśanam  || 9.20 ||
dhyāyatyaniṣṭaṃ yatkiṃ citpāṇigrāhasya cetasā  |
tasyaiṣa vyabhicārasya nihnavaḥ samyagucyate  || 9.21 ||
yādṛgguṇena bhartrā strī saṃyujyeta yathāvidhi  |
tādṛgguṇā sā bhavati samudreṇeva nimnagā  || 9.22 ||
akṣamālā vasiṣṭhena saṃyuktādhamayonijā  |
śāraṅgī mandapālena jagāmābhyarhaṇīyatām  || 9.23 ||
etāścānyāśca loke'sminnapakṛṣṭaprasūtayaḥ  |
utkarṣaṃ yoṣitaḥ prāptāḥ svaiḥ svairbhartṛguṇaiḥ śubhaiḥ  || 9.24 ||
eṣoditā lokayātrā nityaṃ strīpuṃsayoḥ śubhā  |
pretyeha ca sukhodarkān prajādharmānnibodhata  || 9.25 ||
prajanārthaṃ mahābhāgāḥ pūjārhā gṛhadīptayaḥ  |
striyaḥ śriyaśca geheṣu na viśeṣo'sti kaścana  || 9.26 ||
utpādanamapatyasya jātasya paripālanam  |
pratyahaṃ lokayātrāyāḥ pratyakṣaṃ strī nibandhanam  || 9.27 ||
apatyaṃ dharmakāryāṇi śuśrūṣā ratiruttamā  |
dārādhīnastathā svargaḥ pitṝṇāmātmanaśca ha  || 9.28 ||
patiṃ yā nābhicarati manovāgdehasaṃyatā  |
sā bhartṛlokānāpnoti sadbhiḥ sādhvīti cocyate  || 9.29 ||
vyabhicārāttu bhartuḥ strī loke prāpnoti nindyatām  |
sṛgālayoniṃ cāpnoti pāparogaiśca pīḍyate  || 9.30 ||
putraṃ pratyuditaṃ sadbhiḥ pūrvajaiśca maharṣibhiḥ  |
viśvajanyamimaṃ puṇyamupanyāsaṃ nibodhata  || 9.31 ||
bhartari putraṃ vijānanti śrutidvaidhaṃ tu kartari  |
āhurutpādakaṃ ke cidapare kṣetriṇaṃ viduḥ  || 9.32 ||
kṣetrabhūtā smṛtā nārī bījabhūtaḥ smṛtaḥ pumān  |
kṣetrabījasamāyogātsaṃbhavaḥ sarvadehinām  || 9.33 ||
viśiṣṭaṃ kutra cidbījaṃ strīyonistveva kutra cit |
ubhayaṃ tu samaṃ yatra sā prasūtiḥ praśasyate  || 9.34 ||
bījasya caiva yonyāśca bījamutkṛṣṭamucyate  |
sarvabhūtaprasūtirhi bījalakṣaṇalakṣitā  || 9.35 ||
yādṛśaṃ tūpyate bījaṃ kṣetre kālopapādite  |
tādṛgrohati tattasmin bījaṃ svairvyañjitaṃ guṇaiḥ  || 9.36 ||
iyaṃ bhūmirhi bhūtānāṃ śāśvatī yonirucyate  |
na ca yoniguṇān kāṃścidbījaṃ puṣyati puṣṭiṣu  || 9.37 ||
bhūmāvapyekakedāre kāloptāni kṛṣīvalaiḥ  |
nānārūpāṇi jāyante bījānīha svabhāvataḥ  || 9.38 ||
vrīhayaḥ śālayo mudgāstilā māṣāstathā yavāḥ  |
yathābījaṃ prarohanti laśunānīkṣavastathā  || 9.39 ||
anyaduptaṃ jātamanyadityetannopapadyate  |
upyate yaddhi yadbījaṃ tattadeva prarohati  || 9.40 ||
tatprājñena vinītena jñānavijñānavedinā  |
āyuṣkāmena vaptavyaṃ na jātu parayoṣiti  || 9.41 ||
atra gāthā vāyugītāḥ kīrtayanti purāvidaḥ  |
yathā bījaṃ na vaptavyaṃ puṃsā paraparigrahe  || 9.42 ||
naśyatīṣuryathā viddhaḥ khe viddhamanuvidhyataḥ  |
tathā naśyati vai kṣipraṃ bījaṃ paraparigrahe  || 9.43 ||
pṛthorapīmāṃ pṛthivīṃ bhāryāṃ pūrvavido viduḥ  |
sthāṇucchedasya kedāramāhuḥ śālyavato mṛgam  || 9.44 ||
etāvāneva puruṣo yajjāyātmā prajeti ha  |
viprāḥ prāhustathā caitadyo bhartā sā smṛtāṅganā  || 9.45 ||
na niṣkrayavisargābhyāṃ bharturbhāryā vimucyate  |
evaṃ dharmaṃ vijānīmaḥ prākprajāpatinirmitam  || 9.46 ||
sakṛdaṃśo nipatati sakṛtkanyā pradīyate  |
sakṛdāha dadānīti trīṇyetāni satāṃ sakṛt || 9.47 ||
yathā go'śvoṣṭradāsīṣu mahiṣyajāvikāsu ca  |
notpādakaḥ prajābhāgī tathaivānyāṅganāsvapi  || 9.48 ||
ye'kṣetriṇo bījavantaḥ parakṣetrapravāpiṇaḥ  |
te vai sasyasya jātasya na labhante phalaṃ kva cit || 9.49 ||
yadanyagoṣu vṛṣabho vatsānāṃ janayecchatam  |
gomināmeva te vatsā moghaṃ skanditamārṣabham  || 9.50 ||
tathaivākṣetriṇo bījaṃ parakṣetrapravāpiṇaḥ  |
kurvanti kṣetriṇāmarthaṃ na bījī labhate phalam  || 9.51 ||
phalaṃ tvanabhisaṃdhāya kṣetriṇāṃ bījināṃ tathā  |
pratyakṣaṃ kṣetriṇāmartho bījādyonirgalīyasī  || 9.52 ||
kriyābhyupagamāttvetadbījārthaṃ yatpradīyate  |
tasyeha bhāginau dṛṣṭau bījī kṣetrika eva ca  || 9.53 ||
oghavātāhṛtaṃ bījaṃ yasya kṣetre prarohati  |
kṣetrikasyaiva tadbījaṃ na vaptā labhate phalam  || 9.54 ||
eṣa dharmo gavāśvasya dāsyuṣṭrājāvikasya ca  |
vihaṃgamahiṣīṇāṃ ca vijñeyaḥ prasavaṃ prati  || 9.55 ||
etadvaḥ sāraphalgutvaṃ bījayonyoḥ prakīrtitam  |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi yoṣitāṃ dharmamāpadi  || 9.56 ||
bhrāturjyeṣṭhasya bhāryā yā gurupatnyanujasya sā  |
yavīyasastu yā bhāryā snuṣā jyeṣṭhasya sā smṛtā  || 9.57 ||
jyeṣṭho yavīyaso bhāryāṃ yavīyān vāgrajastriyam  |
patitau bhavato gatvā niyuktāvapyanāpadi  || 9.58 ||
devarādvā sapiṇḍādvā striyā samyaṅniyuktayā  |
prajepsitāādhigantavyā saṃtānasya parikṣaye  || 9.59 ||
vidhavāyāṃ niyuktastu ghṛtākto vāgyato niśi  |
ekamutpādayetputraṃ na dvitīyaṃ kathaṃ cana  || 9.60 ||
dvitīyameke prajanaṃ manyante strīṣu tadvidaḥ  |
anirvṛtaṃ niyogārthaṃ paśyanto dharmatastayoḥ  || 9.61 ||
vidhavāyāṃ niyogārthe nirvṛtte tu yathāvidhi  |
guruvacca snuṣāvacca varteyātāṃ parasparam  || 9.62 ||
niyuktau yau vidhiṃ hitvā varteyātāṃ tu kāmataḥ  |
tāvubhau patitau syātāṃ snuṣāgagurutalpagau  || 9.63 ||
nānyasmin vidhavā nārī niyoktavyā dvijātibhiḥ  |
anyasmin hi niyuñjānā dharmaṃ hanyuḥ sanātanam  || 9.64 ||
nodvāhikeṣu mantreṣu niyogaḥ kīrtyate kva cit |
na vivāhavidhāvuktaṃ vidhavāvedanaṃ punaḥ  || 9.65 ||
ayaṃ dvijairhi vidvadbhiḥ paśudharmo vigarhitaḥ  |
manuṣyāṇāmapi prokto vene rājyaṃ praśāsati  || 9.66 ||
sa mahīmakhilāṃ bhuñjan rājarṣipravaraḥ purā  |
varṇānāṃ saṃkaraṃ cakre kāmopahatacetanaḥ  || 9.67 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti yo mohātpramītapatikāṃ striyam  |
niyojayatyapatyārthaṃ taṃ vigarhanti sādhavaḥ  || 9.68 ||
yasyā mriyeta kanyāyā vācā satye kṛte patiḥ  |
tāmanena vidhānena nijo vindeta devaraḥ  || 9.69 ||
yathāvidhyadhigamyaināṃ śuklavastrāṃ śucivratām  |
mitho bhajetā prasavātsakṛtsakṛdṛtāvṛtau  || 9.70 ||
na dattvā kasya citkanyāṃ punardadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ  |
dattvā punaḥ prayacchan hi prāpnoti puruṣānṛtam  || 9.71 ||
vidhivatpratigṛhyāpi tyajetkanyāṃ vigarhitām  |
vyādhitāṃ vipraduṣṭāṃ vā chadmanā copapāditām  || 9.72 ||
yastu doṣavatīṃ kanyāmanākhyāyopapādayet |
tasya tadvitathaṃ kuryātkanyādāturdurātmanaḥ  || 9.73 ||
vidhāya vṛttiṃ bhāryāyāḥ pravasetkāryavānnaraḥ  |
avṛttikarśitā hi strī praduṣyetsthitimatyapi  || 9.74 ||
vidhāya proṣite vṛttiṃ jīvenniyamamāsthitā  |
proṣite tvavidhāyaiva jīvecchilpairagarhitaiḥ  || 9.75 ||
proṣito dharmakāryārthaṃ pratīkṣyo'ṣṭau naraḥ samāḥ  |
vidyārthaṃ ṣaḍyaśo'rthaṃ vā kāmārthaṃ trīṃstu vatsarān  || 9.76 ||
saṃvatsaraṃ pratīkṣeta dviṣantīṃ yoṣitaṃ patiḥ  |
ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāttvenāṃ dāyaṃ hṛtvā na saṃvaset || 9.77 ||
atikrāmetpramattaṃ yā mattaṃ rogārtameva vā  |
sā trīnmāsān parityājyā vibhūṣaṇaparicchadā  || 9.78 ||
unmattaṃ patitaṃ klībamabījaṃ pāparogiṇam  |
na tyāgo'sti dviṣantyāśca na ca dāyāpavartanam  || 9.79 ||
madyapāsādhuvṛttā ca pratikūlā ca yā bhavet |
vyādhitā vādhivettavyā hiṃsrārthaghnī ca sarvadā  || 9.80 ||
vandhyāṣṭame'dhivedyā'abde daśame tu mṛtaprajā  |
ekādaśe strījananī sadyastvapriyavādinī  || 9.81 ||
yā rogiṇī syāttu hitā saṃpannā caiva śīlataḥ  |
sānujñāpyādhivettavyā nāvamānyā ca karhi cit || 9.82 ||
adhivinnā tu yā nārī nirgacchedruṣitā gṛhāt |
sā sadyaḥ saṃniroddhavyā tyājyā vā kulasaṃnidhau  || 9.83 ||
pratiṣiddhāpi cedyā tu madyamabhyudayeṣvapi  |
prekṣāsamājaṃ gacchedvā sā daṇḍyā kṛṣṇalāni ṣaṭ || 9.84 ||
yadi svāścāparāścaiva vinderan yoṣito dvijāḥ  |
tāsāṃ varṇakrameṇa syājjyeṣṭhyaṃ pūjā ca veśma ca  || 9.85 ||
bhartuḥ śarīraśuśrūṣāṃ dharmakāryaṃ ca naityakam  |
svā caiva kuryātsarveṣāṃ nāsvajātiḥ kathaṃ cana  || 9.86 ||
yastu tatkārayenmohātsajātyā sthitayānyayā  |
yathā brāhmaṇacāṇḍālaḥ pūrvadṛṣṭastathaiva saḥ  || 9.87 ||
utkṛṣṭāyābhirūpāya varāya sadṛśāya ca  |
aprāptāmapi tāṃ tasmai kanyāṃ dadyādyathāvidhi  || 9.88 ||
kāmamā maraṇāttiṣṭhedgṛhe kanyā rtumatyapi  |
na caivaināṃ prayaccettu guṇahīnāya karhi cit || 9.89 ||
trīṇi varṣāṇyudīkṣeta kumāryṛtumatī satī  |
ūrdhvaṃ tu kālādetasmādvindeta sadṛśaṃ patim  || 9.90 ||
adīyamānā bhartāramadhigacchedyadi svayam  |
nainaḥ kiṃ cidavāpnoti na ca yaṃ sādhigacchati  || 9.91 ||
alaṅkāraṃ nādadīta pitryaṃ kanyā svayaṃvarā  |
mātṛkaṃ bhrātṛdattaṃ vā stenā syādyadi taṃ haret || 9.92 ||
pitre na dadyācchulkaṃ tu kanyāmṛtumatīṃ haran  |
sa ca svāmyādatikrāmedṛtūnāṃ pratirodhanāt || 9.93 ||
triṃśadvarṣo vahetkanyāṃ hṛdyāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm  |
tryaṣṭavarṣo'ṣṭavarṣāṃ vā dharme sīdati satvaraḥ  || 9.94 ||
devadattāṃ patirbhāryāṃ vindate necchayātmanaḥ  |
tāṃ sādhvīṃ bibhṛyānnityaṃ devānāṃ priyamācaran  || 9.95 ||
prajanārthaṃ striyaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ saṃtānārthaṃ ca mānavaḥ  |
tasmātsādhāraṇo dharmaḥ śrutau patnyā sahoditaḥ  || 9.96 ||
kanyāyāṃ dattaśulkāyāṃ mriyeta yadi śulkadaḥ  |
devarāya pradātavyā yadi kanyānumanyate  || 9.97 ||
ādadīta na śūdro'pi śulkaṃ duhitaraṃ dadan  |
śulkaṃ hi gṛhṇan kurute channaṃ duhitṛvikrayam  || 9.98 ||
etattu na pare cakrurnāpare jātu sādhavaḥ  |
yadanyasya pratijñāya punaranyasya dīyate  || 9.99 ||
nānuśuśruma jātvetatpūrveṣvapi hi janmasu  |
śulkasaṃjñena mūlyena channaṃ duhitṛvikrayam  || 9.100 ||
anyonyasyāvyabhicāro bhavedāmaraṇāntikaḥ  |
eṣa dharmaḥ samāsena jñeyaḥ strīpuṃsayoḥ paraḥ  || 9.101 ||
tathā nityaṃ yateyātāṃ strīpuṃsau tu kṛtakriyau  |
yathā nābhicaretāṃ tau viyuktāvitaretaram  || 9.102 ||
eṣa strīpuṃsayorukto dharmo vo ratisaṃhitaḥ  |
āpadyapatyaprāptiśca dāyadharmaṃ nibodhata  || 9.103 ||
ūrdhvaṃ pituśca mātuśca sametya bhrātaraḥ samam  |
bhajeran paitṛkaṃ rikthamanīśāste hi jīvatoḥ  || 9.104 ||
jyeṣṭha eva tu gṛhṇīyātpitryaṃ dhanamaśeṣataḥ  |
śeṣāstamupajīveyuryathaiva pitaraṃ tathā  || 9.105 ||
jyeṣṭhena jātamātreṇa putrī bhavati mānavaḥ  |
pitṝṇāmanṛṇaścaiva sa tasmātsarvamarhati  || 9.106 ||
yasminnṛṇaṃ saṃnayati yena cānantyamaśnute  |
sa eva dharmajaḥ putraḥ kāmajānitarān viduḥ  || 9.107 ||
piteva pālayetpūtrān jyeṣṭho bhrātṝṇyavīyasaḥ  |
putravaccāpi varteran jyeṣṭhe bhrātari dharmataḥ  || 9.108 ||
jyeṣṭhaḥ kulaṃ vardhayati vināśayati vā punaḥ  |
jyeṣṭhaḥ pūjyatamo loke jyeṣṭhaḥ sadbhiragarhitaḥ  || 9.109 ||
yo jyeṣṭho jyeṣṭhavṛttiḥ syānmāteva sa piteva saḥ  |
ajyeṣṭhavṛttiryastu syātsa saṃpūjyastu bandhuvat || 9.110 ||
evaṃ saha vaseyurvā pṛthagvā dharmakāmyayā  |
pṛthagvivardhate dharmastasmāddharmyā pṛthakkriyā  || 9.111 ||
jyeṣṭhasya viṃśa uddhāraḥ sarvadravyācca yadvaram  |
tato'rdhaṃ madhyamasya syātturīyaṃ tu yavīyasaḥ  || 9.112 ||
jyeṣṭhaścaiva kaniṣṭhaśca saṃharetāṃ yathoditam  |
ye'nye jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhābhyāṃ teṣāṃ syānmadhyamaṃ dhanam  || 9.113 ||
sarveṣāṃ dhanajātānāmādadītāgryamagrajaḥ  |
yacca sātiśayaṃ kiṃ ciddaśataścāpnuyādvaram  || 9.114 ||
uddhāro na daśasvasti saṃpannānāṃ svakarmasu  |
yatkiṃ cideva deyaṃ tu jyāyase mānavardhanam  || 9.115 ||
evaṃ samuddhṛtoddhāre samānaṃśān prakalpayet |
uddhāre'nuddhṛte tveṣāmiyaṃ syādaṃśakalpanā  || 9.116 ||
ekādhikaṃ harejjyeṣṭhaḥ putro'dhyardhaṃ tato'nujaḥ  |
aṃśamaṃśaṃ yavīyāṃsa iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ  || 9.117 ||
svebhyo'ṃśebhyastu kanyābhyaḥ pradadyurbhrātaraḥ pṛthak |
svātsvādaṃśāccaturbhāgaṃ patitāḥ syuraditsavaḥ  || 9.118 ||
ajāvikaṃ saikaśaphaṃ na jātu viṣamaṃ bhajet |
ajāvikaṃ tu viṣamaṃ jyeṣṭhasyaiva vidhīyate  || 9.119 ||
yavīyāñjyeṣṭhabhāryāyāṃ putramutpādayedyadi  |
samastatra vibhāgaḥ syāditi dharmo vyavasthitaḥ  || 9.120 ||
upasarjanaṃ pradhānasya dharmato nopapadyate  |
pitā pradhānaṃ prajane tasmāddharmeṇa taṃ bhajet || 9.121 ||
putraḥ kaniṣṭho jyeṣṭhāyāṃ kaniṣṭhāyāṃ ca pūrvajaḥ  |
kathaṃ tatra vibhāgaḥ syāditi cetsaṃśayo bhavet || 9.122 ||
ekaṃ vṛṣabhamuddhāraṃ saṃhareta sa pūrvajaḥ  |
tato'pare jyeṣṭhavṛṣāstadūnānāṃ svamātṛtaḥ  || 9.123 ||
jyeṣṭhastu jāto jyeṣṭhāyāṃ haredvṛṣabhaṣoḍaśāḥ  |
tataḥ svamātṛtaḥ śeṣā bhajeranniti dhāraṇā  || 9.124 ||
sadṛśastrīṣu jātānāṃ putrāṇāmaviśeṣataḥ  |
na mātṛto jyaiṣṭhyamasti janmato jyaiṣṭhyamucyate  || 9.125 ||
janmajyeṣṭhena cāhvānaṃ subrahmaṇyāsvapi smṛtam  |
yamayoścaiva garbheṣu janmato jyeṣṭhatā smṛtā  || 9.126 ||
aputro'nena vidhinā sutāṃ kurvīta putrikām  |
yadapatyaṃ bhavedasyāṃ tanmama syātsvadhākaram  || 9.127 ||
anena tu vidhānena purā cakre'tha putrikāḥ  |
vivṛddhyarthaṃ svavaṃśasya svayaṃ dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ  || 9.128 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa  |
somāya rājñe satkṛtya prītātmā saptaviṃśatim  || 9.129 ||
yathaivātmā tathā putraḥ putreṇa duhitā samā  |
tasyāmātmani tiṣṭhantyāṃ kathamanyo dhanaṃ haret || 9.130 ||
mātustu yautakaṃ yatsyātkumārībhāga eva saḥ  |
dauhitra eva ca haredaputrasyākhilaṃ dhanam  || 9.131 ||
dauhitro hyakhilaṃ rikthamaputrasya piturharet |
sa eva dadyāddvau piṇḍau pitre mātāmahāya ca  || 9.132 ||
pautradauhitrayorloke na viśeṣo'sti dharmataḥ  |
tayorhi mātāpitarau saṃbhūtau tasya dehataḥ  || 9.133 ||
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro'nujāyate  |
samastatra vibhāgaḥ syājjyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyāḥ  || 9.134 ||
aputrāyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tu putrikāyāṃ kathaṃ cana  |
dhanaṃ tatputrikābhartā haretaivāvicārayan  || 9.135 ||
akṛtā vā kṛtā vāpi yaṃ vindetsadṛśātsutam  |
pautrī mātāmahastena dadyātpiṇḍaṃ hareddhanam  || 9.136 ||
putreṇa lokāñjayati pautreṇānantyamaśnute  |
atha putrasya pautreṇa bradhnasyāpnoti viṣṭapam  || 9.137 ||
puṃnāmno narakādyasmāttrāyate pitaraṃ sutaḥ  |
tasmātputra iti proktaḥ svayameva svayaṃbhuvā  || 9.138 ||
pautradauhitrayorloke viśeṣo nopapadyate  |
dauhitro'pi hyamutrainaṃ saṃtārayati pautravat || 9.139 ||
mātuḥ prathamataḥ piṇḍaṃ nirvapetputrikāsutaḥ  |
dvitīyaṃ tu pitustasyāstṛtīyaṃ tatpituḥ pituḥ  || 9.140 ||
upapanno guṇaiḥ sarvaiḥ putro yasya tu dattrimaḥ  |
sa haretaiva tadrikthaṃ saṃprāpto'pyanyagotrataḥ  || 9.141 ||
gotrarikthe janayiturna hareddattrimaḥ kva cit |
gotrarikthānugaḥ piṇḍo vyapaiti dadataḥ svadhā  || 9.142 ||
aniyuktāsutaścaiva putriṇyāptaśca devarāt |
ubhau tau nārhato bhāgaṃ jārajātakakāmajau  || 9.143 ||
niyuktāyāmapi pumānnāryāṃ jāto'vidhānataḥ  |
naivārhaḥ paitṛkaṃ rikthaṃ patitotpādito hi saḥ  || 9.144 ||
harettatra niyuktāyāṃ jātaḥ putro yathāurasaḥ  |
kṣetrikasya tu tadbījaṃ dharmataḥ prasavaśca saḥ  || 9.145 ||
dhanaṃ yo bibhṛyādbhrāturmṛtasya striyameva ca  |
so'patyaṃ bhrāturutpādya dadyāttasyaiva taddhanam  || 9.146 ||
yā niyuktānyataḥ putraṃ devarādvāpyavāpnuyāt |
taṃ kāmajamarikthīyaṃ vṛthotpannaṃ pracakṣate  || 9.147 ||
etadvidhānaṃ vijñeyaṃ vibhāgasyaikayoniṣu  |
bahvīṣu caikajātānāṃ nānāstrīṣu nibodhata  || 9.148 ||
brāhmaṇasyānupūrvyeṇa catasrastu yadi striyaḥ  |
tāsāṃ putreṣu jāteṣu vibhāge'yaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ  || 9.149 ||
kīnāśo govṛṣo yānamalaṅkāraśca veśma ca  |
viprasyāuddhārikaṃ deyamekāṃśaśca pradhānataḥ  || 9.150 ||
tryaṃśaṃ dāyāddharedvipro dvāvaṃśau kṣatriyāsutaḥ  |
vaiśyājaḥ sārdhamevāṃśamaṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || 9.151 ||
sarvaṃ vā rikthajātaṃ taddaśadhā parikalpya ca  |
dharmyaṃ vibhāgaṃ kurvīta vidhinānena dharmavit || 9.152 ||
caturo'ṃśān haredviprastrīnaṃśān kṣatriyāsutaḥ  |
vaiśyāputro hareddvyaṃśamaṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || 9.153 ||
yadyapi syāttu satputro'pyasatputro'pi vā bhavet |
nādhikaṃ daśamāddadyācchūdrāputrāya dharmataḥ  || 9.154 ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāputro na rikthabhāk |
yadevāsya pitā dadyāttadevāsya dhanaṃ bhavet || 9.155 ||
samavarṇāsu vā jātāḥ sarve putrā dvijanmanām  |
uddhāraṃ jyāyase dattvā bhajerannitare samam  || 9.156 ||
śūdrasya tu savarṇaiva nānyā bhāryā vidhīyate  |
tasyāṃ jātāḥ samāṃśāḥ syuryadi putraśataṃ bhavet || 9.157 ||
putrān dvādaśa yānāha nṝṇāṃ svāyaṃbhuvo manuḥ  |
teṣāṃ ṣaḍbandhudāyādāḥ ṣaḍadāyādabāndhavāḥ  || 9.158 ||
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaścaiva dattaḥ kṛtrima eva ca  |
gūḍhotpanno'paviddhaśca dāyādā bāndhavāśca ṣaṭ || 9.159 ||
kānīnaśca sahoḍhaśca krītaḥ paunarbhavastathā  |
svayaṃdattaśca śaudraśca ṣaḍadāyādabāndhavāḥ  || 9.160 ||
yādṛśaṃ phalamāpnoti kuplavaiḥ saṃtarañjalam  |
tādṛśaṃ phalamāpnoti kuputraiḥ saṃtaraṃstamaḥ  || 9.161 ||
yadyekarikthinau syātāmaurasakṣetrajau sutau  |
yasya yatpaitṛkaṃ rikthaṃ sa tadgṛhṇīta netaraḥ  || 9.162 ||
eka evāurasaḥ putraḥ pitryasya vasunaḥ prabhuḥ  |
śeṣāṇāmānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ pradadyāttu prajīvanam  || 9.163 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣetrajasyāṃśaṃ pradadyātpaitṛkāddhanāt |
auraso vibhajan dāyaṃ pitryaṃ pañcamameva vā  || 9.164 ||
aurasakṣetrajau putrau pitṛrikthasya bhāginau  |
daśāpare tu kramaśo gotrarikthāṃśabhāginaḥ  || 9.165 ||
svakṣetre saṃskṛtāyāṃ tu svayamutpādayeddhi yam  |
tamaurasaṃ vijānīyātputraṃ prāthamakalpikam  || 9.166 ||
yastalpajaḥ pramītasya klībasya vyādhitasya vā  |
svadharmeṇa niyuktāyāṃ sa putraḥ kṣetrajaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 9.167 ||
mātā pitā vā dadyātāṃ yamadbhiḥ putramāpadi  |
sadṛśaṃ prītisaṃyuktaṃ sa jñeyo dattrimaḥ sutaḥ  || 9.168 ||
sadṛśaṃ tu prakuryādyaṃ guṇadoṣavicakṣaṇam  |
putraṃ putraguṇairyuktaṃ sa vijñeyaśca kṛtrimaḥ  || 9.169 ||
utpadyate gṛhe yastu na ca jñāyeta kasya saḥ  |
sa gṛhe gūḍha utpannastasya syādyasya talpajaḥ  || 9.170 ||
mātāpitṛbhyāmutsṛṣṭaṃ tayoranyatareṇa vā  |
yaṃ putraṃ parigṛhṇīyādapaviddhaḥ sa ucyate  || 9.171 ||
pitṛveśmani kanyā tu yaṃ putraṃ janayedrahaḥ  |
taṃ kānīnaṃ vadennāmnā voḍhuḥ kanyāsamudbhavam  || 9.172 ||
yā garbhiṇī saṃskriyate jñātājñātāpi vā satī  |
voḍhuḥ sa garbho bhavati sahoḍha iti cocyate  || 9.173 ||
krīṇīyādyastvapatyārthaṃ mātāpitroryamantikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutastasya sadṛśo'sadṛśo'pi vā  || 9.174 ||
yā patyā vā parityaktā vidhavā vā svayecchayā  |
utpādayetpunarbhūtvā sa paunarbhava ucyate  || 9.175 ||
sā cedakṣatayoniḥ syādgatapratyāgatāpi vā  |
paunarbhavena bhartrā sā punaḥ saṃskāramarhati  || 9.176 ||
mātāpitṛvihīno yastyakto vā syādakāraṇāt |
ātmānamarpayedyasmai svayaṃdattastu sa smṛtaḥ  || 9.177 ||
yaṃ brāhmaṇastu śūdrāyāṃ kāmādutpādayetsutam  |
sa pārayanneva śavastasmātpāraśavaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 9.178 ||
dāsyāṃ vā dāsadāsyāṃ vā yaḥ śūdrasya suto bhavet |
so'nujñāto haredaṃśamiti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ  || 9.179 ||
kṣetrajādīn sutānetānekādaśa yathoditān  |
putrapratinidhīnāhuḥ kriyālopānmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 9.180 ||
ya ete'bhihitāḥ putrāḥ prasaṅgādanyabījajāḥ  |
yasya te bījato jātāstasya te netarasya tu  || 9.181 ||
bhrātṝṇāmekajātānāmekaścetputravān bhavet |
sarvāṃstāṃstena putreṇa putriṇo manurabravīt || 9.182 ||
sarvāsāmekapatnīnāmekā cetputriṇī bhavet |
sarvāstāstena putreṇa prāha putravatīrmanuḥ  || 9.183 ||
śreyasaḥ śreyaso'lābhe pāpīyān rikthamarhati  |
bahavaścettu sadṛśāḥ sarve rikthasya bhāginaḥ  || 9.184 ||
na bhrātaro na pitaraḥ putrā rikthaharāḥ pituḥ  |
pitā haredaputrasya rikthaṃ bhrātara eva ca  || 9.185 ||
trayāṇāmudakaṃ kāryaṃ triṣu piṇḍaḥ pravartate  |
caturthaḥ saṃpradātaiṣāṃ pañcamo nopapadyate  || 9.186 ||
anantaraḥ sapiṇḍādyastasya tasya dhanaṃ bhavet |
ata ūrdhvaṃ sakulyaḥ syādācāryaḥ śiṣya eva vā  || 9.187 ||
sarveṣāmapyabhāve tu brāhmaṇā rikthabhāginaḥ  |
traividyāḥ śucayo dāntāstathā dharmo na hīyate  || 9.188 ||
ahāryaṃ brāhmaṇadravyaṃ rājñā nityamiti sthitiḥ  |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ sarvābhāve harennṛpaḥ  || 9.189 ||
saṃsthitasyānapatyasya sagotrātputramāharet |
tatra yadrikthajātaṃ syāttattasmin pratipādayet || 9.190 ||
dvau tu yau vivadeyātāṃ dvābhyāṃ jātau striyā dhane  |
tayoryadyasya pitryaṃ syāttatsa gṛhṇīta netaraḥ  || 9.191 ||
jananyāṃ saṃsthitāyāṃ tu samaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ  |
bhajeranmātṛkaṃ rikthaṃ bhaginyaśca sanābhayaḥ  || 9.192 ||
yāstāsāṃ syurduhitarastāsāmapi yathārhataḥ  |
mātāmahyā dhanātkiṃ citpradeyaṃ prītipūrvakam  || 9.193 ||
adhyagnyadhyāvāhanikaṃ dattaṃ ca prītikarmaṇi  |
bhrātṛmātṛpitṛprāptaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam  || 9.194 ||
anvādheyaṃ ca yaddattaṃ patyā prītena caiva yat |
patyau jīvati vṛttāyāḥ prajāyāstaddhanaṃ bhavet || 9.195 ||
brāhmadaivārṣagāndharva- prājāpatyeṣu yadvasu  |
aprajāyāmatītāyāṃ bhartureva tadiṣyate  || 9.196 ||
yattvasyāḥ syāddhanaṃ dattaṃ vivāheṣvāsurādiṣu  |
aprajāyāmatītāyāṃ mātāpitrostadiṣyate  || 9.197 ||
striyāṃ tu yadbhavedvittaṃ pitrā dattaṃ kathaṃ cana  |
brāhmaṇī taddharetkanyā tadapatyasya vā bhavet || 9.198 ||
na nirhāraṃ striyaḥ kuryuḥ kuṭumbādbahumadhyagāt |
svakādapi ca vittāddhi svasya bharturanājñayā  || 9.199 ||
patyau jīvati yaḥ strībhiralaṅkāro dhṛto bhavet |
na taṃ bhajeran dāyādā bhajamānāḥ patanti te  || 9.200 ||
anaṃśau klībapatitau jātyandhabadhirau tathā  |
unmattajaḍamūkāśca ye ca ke cinnirindriyāḥ  || 9.201 ||
sarveṣāmapi tu nyāyyaṃ dātuṃ śaktyā manīṣiṇā  |
grāsācchādanamatyantaṃ patito hyadadadbhavet || 9.202 ||
yadyarthitā tu dāraiḥ syātklībādīnāṃ kathaṃ cana  |
teṣāmutpannatantūnāmapatyaṃ dāyamarhati  || 9.203 ||
yatkiṃ citpitari prete dhanaṃ jyeṣṭho'dhigacchati  |
bhāgo yavīyasāṃ tatra yadi vidyānupālinaḥ  || 9.204 ||
avidyānāṃ tu sarveṣāmīhātaśceddhanaṃ bhavet |
samastatra vibhāgaḥ syādapitrya iti dhāraṇā  || 9.205 ||
vidyādhanaṃ tu yadyasya tattasyaiva dhanaṃ bhavet |
maitryamaudvāhikaṃ caiva mādhuparkikameva ca  || 9.206 ||
bhrātṝṇāṃ yastu neheta dhanaṃ śaktaḥ svakarmaṇā  |
sa nirbhājyaḥ svakādaṃśātkiṃ ciddattvopajīvanam  || 9.207 ||
anupaghnan pitṛdravyaṃ śrameṇa yadupārjitam  |
svayamīhitalabdhaṃ tannākāmo dātumarhati  || 9.208 ||
paitṛkaṃ tu pitā dravyamanavāptaṃ yadāpnuyāt |
na tatputrairbhajetsārdhamakāmaḥ svayamarjitam  || 9.209 ||
vibhaktāḥ saha jīvanto vibhajeran punaryadi  |
samastatra vibhāgaḥ syājjyaiṣṭhyaṃ tatra na vidyate  || 9.210 ||
yeṣāṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā hīyetāṃśapradānataḥ  |
mriyetānyataro vāpi tasya bhāgo na lupyate  || 9.211 ||
sodaryā vibhajeraṃstaṃ sametya sahitāḥ samam  |
bhrātaro ye ca saṃsṛṣṭā bhāginyaśca sanābhayaḥ  || 9.212 ||
yo jyeṣṭho vinikurvīta lobhādbhrātṝn yavīyasaḥ  |
so'jyeṣṭhaḥ syādabhāgaśca niyantavyaśca rājabhiḥ  || 9.213 ||
sarva eva vikarmasthā nārhanti bhrātaro dhanam  |
na cādattvā kaniṣṭhebhyo jyeṣṭhaḥ kurvīta yautakam  || 9.214 ||
bhrātṝṇāmavibhaktānāṃ yadyutthānaṃ bhavetsaha  |
na putrabhāgaṃ viṣamaṃ pitā dadyātkathaṃ cana  || 9.215 ||
ūrdhvaṃ vibhāgājjātastu pitryameva hareddhanam  |
saṃsṛṣṭāstena vā ye syurvibhajeta sa taiḥ saha  || 9.216 ||
anapatyasya putrasya mātā dāyamavāpnuyāt |
mātaryapi ca vṛttāyāṃ piturmātā hareddhanam  || 9.217 ||
ṛṇe dhane ca sarvasmin pravibhakte yathāvidhi  |
paścāddṛśyeta yatkiṃ cittatsarvaṃ samatāṃ nayet || 9.218 ||
vastraṃ patramalaṅkāraṃ kṛtānnamudakaṃ striyaḥ  |
yogakṣemaṃ pracāraṃ ca na vibhājyaṃ pracakṣate  || 9.219 ||
ayamukto vibhāgo vaḥ putrāṇāṃ ca kriyāvidhiḥ  |
kramaśaḥ kṣetrajādīnāṃ dyūtadharmaṃ nibodhata  || 9.220 ||
dyūtaṃ samāhvayaṃ caiva rājā rāṣṭrānnivārayet |
rājāntakaraṇāvetau dvau doṣau pṛthivīkṣitām  || 9.221 ||
prakāśametattāskaryaṃ yaddevanasamāhvayau  |
tayornityaṃ pratīghāte nṛpatiryatnavān bhavet || 9.222 ||
aprāṇibhiryatkriyate talloke dyūtamucyate  |
prāṇibhiḥ kriyate yastu sa vijñeyaḥ samāhvayaḥ  || 9.223 ||
dyūtaṃ samāhvayaṃ caiva yaḥ kuryātkārayeta vā  |
tān sarvān ghātayedrājā śūdrāṃśca dvijaliṅginaḥ  || 9.224 ||
kitavān kuśīlavān krūrān pāṣaṇḍasthāṃśca mānavān  |
vikarmasthān śauṇḍikāṃśca kṣipraṃ nirvāsayetpurāt || 9.225 ||
ete rāṣṭre vartamānā rājñaḥ prachannataskarāḥ  |
vikarmakriyayā nityaṃ bādhante bhadrikāḥ prajāḥ  || 9.226 ||
dyūtametatpurā kalpe dṛṣṭaṃ vairakaraṃ mahat |
tasmāddyūtaṃ na seveta hāsyārthamapi buddhimān  || 9.227 ||
pracchannaṃ vā prakāśaṃ vā tanniṣeveta yo naraḥ  |
tasya daṇḍavikalpaḥ syādyatheṣṭaṃ nṛpatestathā  || 9.228 ||
kṣatraviṭśūdrayonistu daṇḍaṃ dātumaśaknuvan  |
ānṛṇyaṃ karmaṇā gacchedvipro dadyācchanaiḥ śanaiḥ  || 9.229 ||
strībālonmattavṛddhānāṃ daridrāṇāṃ ca rogiṇām  |
śiphāvidalarajjvādyairvidadhyānnṛpatirdamam  || 9.230 ||
ye niyuktāstu kāryeṣu hanyuḥ kāryāṇi kāryiṇām  |
dhanoṣmaṇā pacyamānāstānniḥsvān kārayennṛpaḥ  || 9.231 ||
kūṭaśāsanakartṝṃśca prakṛtīnāṃ ca dūṣakān  |
strībālabrāhmaṇaghnāṃśca hanyāddviṭsevinastathā  || 9.232 ||
tīritaṃ cānuśiṣṭaṃ ca yatra kva cana yadbhavet |
kṛtaṃ taddharmato vidyānna tadbhūyo nivartayet || 9.233 ||
amātyāḥ prāḍvivāko vā yatkuryuḥ kāryamanyathā  |
tatsvayaṃ nṛpatiḥ kuryāttān sahasraṃ ca daṇḍayet || 9.234 ||
brahmahā ca surāpaśca steyī ca gurutalpagaḥ  |
ete sarve pṛthagjñeyā mahāpātakino narāḥ  || 9.235 ||
caturṇāmapi caiteṣāṃ prāyaścittamakurvatām  |
śārīraṃ dhanasaṃyuktaṃ daṇḍaṃ dharmyaṃ prakalpayet || 9.236 ||
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ  |
steye ca śvapadaṃ kāryaṃ brahmahaṇyaśirāḥ pumān  || 9.237 ||
asaṃbhojyā hyasaṃyājyā asaṃpāṭhyā'vivāhinaḥ  |
careyuḥ pṛthivīṃ dīnāḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtāḥ  || 9.238 ||
jñātisaṃbandhibhistvete tyaktavyāḥ kṛtalakṣaṇāḥ  |
nirdayā nirnamaskārāstanmanoranuśāsanam  || 9.239 ||
prāyaścittaṃ tu kurvāṇāḥ sarvavarṇā yathoditam  |
nāṅkyā rājñā lalāṭe syurdāpyāstūttamasāhasam  || 9.240 ||
āgaḥsu brāhmaṇasyaiva kāryo madhyamasāhasaḥ  |
vivāsyo vā bhavedrāṣṭrātsadravyaḥ saparicchadaḥ  || 9.241 ||
itare kṛtavantastu pāpānyetānyakāmataḥ  |
sarvasvahāramarhanti kāmatastu pravāsanam  || 9.242 ||
nādadīta nṛpaḥ sādhurmahāpātakino dhanam  |
ādadānastu tallobhāttena doṣeṇa lipyate  || 9.243 ||
apsu praveśya taṃ daṇḍaṃ varuṇāyopapādayet |
śrutavṛttopapanne vā brāhmaṇe pratipādayet || 9.244 ||
īśo daṇḍasya varuṇo rājñāṃ daṇḍadharo hi saḥ  |
īśaḥ sarvasya jagato brāhmaṇo vedapāragaḥ  || 9.245 ||
yatra varjayate rājā pāpakṛdbhyo dhanāgamam  |
tatra kālena jāyante mānavā dīrghajīvinaḥ  || 9.246 ||
niṣpadyante ca sasyāni yathoptāni viśāṃ pṛthak |
bālāśca na pramīyante vikṛtaṃ ca na jāyate  || 9.247 ||
brāhmaṇān bādhamānaṃ tu kāmādavaravarṇajam  |
hanyāccitrairvadhopāyairudvejanakarairnṛpaḥ  || 9.248 ||
yāvānavadhyasya vadhe tāvān vadhyasya mokṣaṇe  |
adharmo nṛpaterdṛṣṭo dharmastu viniyacchataḥ  || 9.249 ||
udito'yaṃ vistaraśo mitho vivādamānayoḥ  |
aṣṭādaśasu mārgeṣu vyavahārasya nirṇayaḥ  || 9.250 ||
evaṃ dharmyāṇi kāryāṇi samyakkurvanmahīpatiḥ  |
deśānalabdhāṃllipseta labdhāṃśca paripālayet || 9.251 ||
samyaṅniviṣṭadeśastu kṛtadurgaśca śāstrataḥ  |
kaṇṭakoddharaṇe nityamātiṣṭhedyatnamuttamam  || 9.252 ||
rakṣanādāryavṛttānāṃ kaṇṭakānāṃ ca śodhanāt |
narendrāstridivaṃ yānti prajāpālanatatparāḥ  || 9.253 ||
aśāsaṃstaskarān yastu baliṃ gṛhṇāti pārthivaḥ  |
tasya prakṣubhyate rāṣṭraṃ svargācca parihīyate  || 9.254 ||
nirbhayaṃ tu bhavedyasya rāṣṭraṃ bāhubalāśritam  |
tasya tadvardhate nityaṃ sicyamāna iva drumaḥ  || 9.255 ||
dvividhāṃstaskarān vidyātparadravyāpahārakān  |
prakāśāṃścāprakāśāṃśca cāracakṣurmahīpatiḥ  || 9.256 ||
prakāśavañcakāsteṣāṃ nānāpaṇyopajīvinaḥ  |
pracchannavañcakāstvete ye stenāṭavikādayaḥ  || 9.257 ||
utkocakāścāupadhikā vañcakāḥ kitavāstathā  |
maṅgalādeśavṛttāśca bhadrāścekṣaṇikaiḥ saha  || 9.258 ||
asamyakkāriṇaścaiva mahāmātrāścikitsakāḥ  |
śilpopacārayuktāśca nipuṇāḥ paṇyayoṣitaḥ  || 9.259 ||
evamādīn vijānīyātprakāśāṃllokakaṇṭakān  |
nigūḍhacāriṇaścānyānanāryānāryaliṅginaḥ  || 9.260 ||
tān viditvā sucaritairgūḍhaistatkarmakāribhiḥ  |
cāraiścānekasaṃsthānaiḥ protsādya vaśamānayet || 9.261 ||
teṣāṃ doṣānabhikhyāpya sve sve karmaṇi tattvataḥ  |
kurvīta śāsanaṃ rājā samyaksārāparādhataḥ  || 9.262 ||
na hi daṇḍādṛte śakyaḥ kartuṃ pāpavinigrahaḥ  |
stenānāṃ pāpabuddhīnāṃ nibhṛtaṃ caratāṃ kṣitau  || 9.263 ||
sabhāprapāpūpaśālā- veśamadyānnavikrayāḥ  |
catuṣpathāṃścaityavṛkṣāḥ samājāḥ prekṣaṇāni ca  || 9.264 ||
jīrṇodyānānyaraṇyāni kārukāveśanāni ca  |
śūnyāni cāpyagārāṇi vanānyupavanāni ca  || 9.265 ||
evaṃvidhānnṛpo deśān gulmaiḥ sthāvarajaṅgamaiḥ  |
taskarapratiṣedhārthaṃ cāraiścāpyanucārayet || 9.266 ||
tatsahāyairanugatairnānākarmapravedibhiḥ  |
vidyādutsādayeccaiva nipuṇaiḥ pūrvataskaraiḥ  || 9.267 ||
bhakṣyabhojyopadeśaiśca brāhmaṇānāṃ ca darśanaiḥ  |
śauryakarmāpadeśaiśca kuryusteṣāṃ samāgamam  || 9.268 ||
ye tatra nopasarpeyurmūlapraṇihitāśca ye  |
tān prasahya nṛpo hanyātsamitrajñātibāndhavān  || 9.269 ||
na hoḍhena vinā cauraṃ ghātayeddhārmiko nṛpaḥ  |
sahoḍhaṃ sopakaraṇaṃ ghātayedavicārayan  || 9.270 ||
grāmeṣvapi ca ye ke ciccaurāṇāṃ bhaktadāyakāḥ  |
bhāṇḍāvakāśadāścaiva sarvāṃstānapi ghātayet || 9.271 ||
rāṣṭreṣu rakṣādhikṛtān sāmantāṃścaiva coditān  |
abhyāghāteṣu madhyasthāñśiṣyāccaurāniva drutam  || 9.272 ||
yaścāpi dharmasamayātpracyuto dharmajīvanaḥ  |
daṇḍenaiva tamapyoṣetsvakāddharmāddhi vicyutam  || 9.273 ||
grāmaghāte hitābhaṅge pathi moṣābhidarśane  |
śaktito nābhidhāvanto nirvāsyāḥ saparicchadāḥ  || 9.274 ||
rājñaḥ kośāpahartṝṃśca pratikūleṣu ca sthitān  |
ghātayedvividhairdaṇḍairarīṇāṃ copajāpakān  || 9.275 ||
saṃdhiṃ chittvā tu ye cauryaṃ rātrau kurvanti taskarāḥ  |
teṣāṃ chittvā nṛpo hastau tīkṣṇe śūle niveśayet || 9.276 ||
aṅgulīrgranthibhedasya chedayetprathame grahe  |
dvitīye hastacaraṇau tṛtīye vadhamarhati  || 9.277 ||
agnidān bhaktadāṃścaiva tathā śastrāvakāśadān  |
saṃnidhātṝṃśca moṣasya hanyāccauramiveśvaraḥ  || 9.278 ||
taḍāgabhedakaṃ hanyādapsu śuddhavadhena vā  |
yadvāpi pratisaṃskuryāddāpyastūttamasāhasam  || 9.279 ||
koṣṭhāgārāyudhāgāra- devatāgārabhedakān  |
hastyaśvarathahartṝṃśca hanyādevāvicārayan  || 9.280 ||
yastu pūrvaniviṣṭasya taḍāgasyodakaṃ haret |
āgamaṃ vāpyapāṃ bhindyātsa dāpyaḥ pūrvasāhasam  || 9.281 ||
samutsṛjedrājamārge yastvamedhyamanāpadi  |
sa dvau kārṣāpaṇau dadyādamedhyaṃ cāśu śodhayet || 9.282 ||
āpadgato'tha vā vṛddhā garbhiṇī bāla eva vā  |
paribhāṣaṇamarhanti tacca śodhyamiti sthitiḥ  || 9.283 ||
cikitsakānāṃ sarveṣāṃ mithyāpracaratāṃ damaḥ  |
amānuṣeṣu prathamo mānuṣeṣu tu madhyamaḥ  || 9.284 ||
saṃkramadhvajayaṣṭīnāṃ pratimānāṃ ca bhedakaḥ  |
pratikuryācca tatsarvaṃ pañca dadyācchatāni ca  || 9.285 ||
adūṣitānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ dūṣaṇe bhedane tathā  |
maṇīnāmapavedhe ca daṇḍaḥ prathamasāhasaḥ  || 9.286 ||
samairhi viṣamaṃ yastu caredvai mūlyato'pi vā  |
samāpnuyāddamaṃ pūrvaṃ naro madhyamameva vā  || 9.287 ||
bandhanāni ca sarvāṇi rājā mārge niveśayet |
duḥkhitā yatra dṛśyeran vikṛtāḥ pāpakāriṇah || 9.288 ||
prākārasya ca bhettāraṃ parikhāṇāṃ ca pūrakam  |
dvārāṇāṃ caiva bhaṅktāraṃ kṣiprameva pravāsayet || 9.289 ||
abhicāreṣu sarveṣu kartavyo dviśato damaḥ  |
mūlakarmaṇi cānāpteḥ kṛtyāsu vividhāsu ca  || 9.290 ||
abījavikrayī caiva bījotkṛṣṭā tathaiva ca  |
maryādābhedakaścaiva vikṛtaṃ prāpnuyādvadham  || 9.291 ||
sarvakaṇṭakapāpiṣṭhaṃ hemakāraṃ tu pārthivaḥ  |
pravartamānamanyāye chedayellavaśaḥ kṣuraiḥ  || 9.292 ||
sītādravyāpaharaṇe śastrāṇāmauṣadhasya ca  |
kālamāsādya kāryaṃ ca rājā daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet || 9.293 ||
svāmyamātyau puraṃ rāṣṭraṃ kośadaṇḍau suhṛttathā  |
sapta prakṛtayo hyetāḥ saptāṅgaṃ rājyamucyate  || 9.294 ||
saptānāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ tu rājyasyāsāṃ yathākramam  |
pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ gurutaraṃ jānīyādvyasanaṃ mahat || 9.295 ||
saptāṅgasyeha rājyasya viṣṭabdhasya tridaṇḍavat |
anyonyaguṇavaiśeṣyānna kiṃ cidatiricyate  || 9.296 ||
teṣu teṣu tu kṛtyeṣu tattadaṅgaṃ viśiṣyate  |
yena yatsādhyate kāryaṃ tattasmiñśreṣṭhamucyate  || 9.297 ||
cāreṇotsāhayogena kriyayaiva ca karmaṇām  |
svaśaktiṃ paraśaktiṃ ca nityaṃ vidyānmahīpatiḥ  || 9.298 ||
pīḍanāni ca sarvāṇi vyasanāni tathaiva ca  |
ārabheta tataḥ kāryaṃ saṃcintya gurulāghavam  || 9.299 ||
ārabhetaiva karmāṇi śrāntaḥ śrāntaḥ punaḥ punaḥ  |
karmāṇyārabhamāṇaṃ hi puruṣaṃ śrīrniṣevate  || 9.300 ||
kṛtaṃ tretāyugaṃ caiva dvāparaṃ kalireva ca  |
rājño vṛttāni sarvāṇi rājā hi yugamucyate  || 9.301 ||
kaliḥ prasupto bhavati sa jāgraddvāparaṃ yugam  |
karmasvabhyudyatastretā vicaraṃstu kṛtaṃ yugam  || 9.302 ||
indrasyārkasya vāyośca yamasya varuṇasya ca  |
candrasyāgneḥ pṛthivyāśca tejovṛttaṃ nṛpaścaret || 9.303 ||
vārṣikāṃścaturo māsān yathendro'bhipravarṣati  |
tathābhivarṣetsvaṃ rāṣṭraṃ kāmairindravrataṃ caran  || 9.304 ||
aṣṭau māsān yathādityastoyaṃ harati raśmibhiḥ  |
tathā haretkaraṃ rāṣṭrānnityamarkavrataṃ hi tat || 9.305 ||
praviśya sarvabhūtāni yathā carati mārutaḥ  |
tathā cāraiḥ praveṣṭavyaṃ vratametaddhi mārutam  || 9.306 ||
yathā yamaḥ priyadveṣyau prāpte kāle niyacchati  |
tathā rājñā niyantavyāḥ prajāstaddhi yamavratam  || 9.307 ||
varuṇena yathā pāśairbaddha evābhidṛśyate  |
tathā pāpānnigṛhṇīyādvratametaddhi vāruṇam  || 9.308 ||
paripūrṇaṃ yathā candraṃ dṛṣṭvā hṛṣyanti mānavāḥ  |
tathā prakṛtayo yasmin sa cāndravratiko nṛpaḥ  || 9.309 ||
pratāpayuktastejasvī nityaṃ syātpāpakarmasu  |
duṣṭasāmantahiṃsraśca tadāgneyaṃ vrataṃ smṛtam  || 9.310 ||
yathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni dharā dhārayate samam  |
tathā sarvāṇi bhūtāni bibhrataḥ pārthivaṃ vratam  || 9.311 ||
etairupāyairanyaiśca yukto nityamatandritaḥ  |
stenān rājā nigṛhṇīyātsvarāṣṭre para eva ca  || 9.312 ||
parāmapyāpadaṃ prāpto brāhmaṇānna prakopayet |
te hyenaṃ kupitā hanyuḥ sadyaḥ sabalavāhanam  || 9.313 ||
yaiḥ kṛtaḥ sarvabhakṣyo'gnirapeyaśca mahodadhiḥ  |
kṣayī cāpyāyitaḥ somaḥ ko na naśyetprakopya tān  || 9.314 ||
lokānanyān sṛjeyurye lokapālāṃśca kopitāḥ  |
devān kuryuradevāṃśca kaḥ kṣiṇvaṃstān samṛdhnuyāt || 9.315 ||
yānupāśritya tiṣṭhanti lokā devāśca sarvadā  |
brahma caiva dhanaṃ yeṣāṃ ko hiṃsyāttāñjijīviṣuḥ  || 9.316 ||
avidvāṃścaiva vidvāṃśca brāhmaṇo daivataṃ mahat |
praṇītaścāpraṇītaśca yathāgnirdaivataṃ mahat || 9.317 ||
śmaśāneṣvapi tejasvī pāvako naiva duṣyati  |
hūyamānaśca yajñeṣu bhūya evābhivardhate  || 9.318 ||
evaṃ yadyapyaniṣṭeṣu vartante sarvakarmasu  |
sarvathā brāhmaṇāḥ pūjyāḥ paramaṃ daivataṃ hi tat || 9.319 ||
kṣatrasyātipravṛddhasya brāhmaṇān prati sarvaśaḥ  |
brahmaiva saṃniyantṛ syātkṣatraṃ hi brahmasaṃbhavam  || 9.320 ||
adbhyo'gnirbrahmataḥ kṣatramaśmano lohamutthitam  |
teṣāṃ sarvatragaṃ tejaḥ svāsu yoniṣu śāmyati  || 9.321 ||
nābrahma kṣatramṛdhnoti nākṣatraṃ brahma vardhate  |
brahma kṣatraṃ ca saṃpṛktamiha cāmutra vardhate  || 9.322 ||
dattvā dhanaṃ tu viprebhyaḥ sarvadaṇḍasamutthitam  |
putre rājyaṃ samāsṛjya kurvīta prāyaṇaṃ raṇe  || 9.323 ||
evaṃ caran sadā yukto rājadharmeṣu pārthivaḥ  |
hiteṣu caiva lokasya sarvān bhṛtyānniyojayet || 9.324 ||
eṣo'khilaḥ karmavidhirukto rājñaḥ sanātanaḥ  |
imaṃ karmavidhiṃ vidyātkramaśo vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ  || 9.325 ||
vaiśyastu kṛtasaṃskāraḥ kṛtvā dāraparigraham  |
vārtāyāṃ nityayuktaḥ syātpaśūnāṃ caiva rakṣaṇe  || 9.326 ||
prajāpatirhi vaiśyāya sṛṣṭvā paridade paśūn  |
brāhmaṇāya ca rājñe ca sarvāḥ paridade prajāḥ  || 9.327 ||
na ca vaiśyasya kāmaḥ syānna rakṣeyaṃ paśūniti  |
vaiśye cecchati nānyena rakṣitavyāḥ kathaṃ cana  || 9.328 ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ lohānāṃ tāntavasya ca  |
gandhānāṃ ca rasānāṃ ca vidyādarghabalābalam  || 9.329 ||
bījānāmuptivicca syātkṣetradoṣaguṇasya ca  |
mānayogaṃ ca jānīyāttulāyogāṃśca sarvaśaḥ  || 9.330 ||
sārāsāraṃ ca bhāṇḍānāṃ deśānāṃ ca guṇāguṇān  |
lābhālābhaṃ ca paṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ parivardhanam  || 9.331 ||
bhṛtyānāṃ ca bhṛtiṃ vidyādbhāṣāśca vividhā nṛṇāṃ  |
dravyāṇāṃ sthānayogāṃśca krayavikrayameva ca  || 9.332 ||
dharmeṇa ca dravyavṛddhāvātiṣṭhedyatnamuttamam  |
dadyācca sarvabhūtānāmannameva prayatnataḥ  || 9.333 ||
viprāṇāṃ vedaviduṣāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ yaśasvinām  |
śuśrūṣaiva tu śūdrasya dharmo naiśreyasaḥ paraḥ  || 9.334 ||
śucirutkṛṣṭaśuśrūṣurmṛduvāganahaṃkṛtaḥ  |
brāhmaṇādyāśrayo nityamutkṛṣṭāṃ jātimaśnute  || 9.335 ||
eṣo'nāpadi varṇānāmuktaḥ karmavidhiḥ śubhaḥ  |
āpadyapi hi yasteṣāṃ kramaśastannibodhata  || 9.336 ||

adhīyīraṃstrayo varṇāḥ svakarmasthā dvijātayaḥ  |
prabrūyādbrāhmaṇastveṣāṃ netarāviti niścayaḥ  || 10.1 ||
sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇo vidyādvṛttyupāyān yathāvidhi  |
prabrūyāditarebhyaśca svayaṃ caiva tathā bhavet || 10.2 ||
vaiśeṣyātprakṛtiśraiṣṭhyānniyamasya ca dhāraṇāt |
saṃskārasya viśeṣācca varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ prabhuḥ  || 10.3 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyastrayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ  |
caturtha ekajātistu śūdro nāsti tu pañcamaḥ  || 10.4 ||
sarvavarṇeṣu tulyāsu patnīṣvakṣatayoniṣu  |
ānulomyena saṃbhūtā jātyā jñeyāsta eva te  || 10.5 ||
strīṣvanantarajātāsu dvijairutpāditān sutān  |
sadṛśāneva tānāhurmātṛdoṣavigarhitān  || 10.6 ||
anantarāsu jātānāṃ vidhireṣa sanātanaḥ  |
dvyekāntarāsu jātānāṃ dharmyaṃ vidyādimaṃ vidhim  || 10.7 ||
brāhmaṇādvaiśyakanyāyāmambaṣṭho nāma jāyate  |
niṣādaḥ śūdrakanyāyāṃ yaḥ pāraśava ucyate  || 10.8 ||
kṣatriyācchūdrakanyāyāṃ krūrācāravihāravān  |
kṣatraśūdravapurjanturugro nāma prajāyate  || 10.9 ||
viprasya triṣu varṇeṣu nṛpatervarṇayordvayoḥ  |
vaiśyasya varṇe caikasmin ṣaḍete'pasadāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 10.10 ||
kṣatriyādviprakanyāyāṃ sūto bhavati jātitaḥ  |
vaiśyānmāgadhavaidehau rājaviprāṅganāsutau  || 10.11 ||
śūdrādāyogavaḥ kṣattā caṇḍālaścādhamo nṛṇām  |
vaiśyarājanyaviprāsu jāyante varṇasaṃkarāḥ  || 10.12 ||
ekāntare tvānulomyādambaṣṭhograu yathā smṛtau  |
kṣattṛvaidehakau tadvatprātilomye'pi janmani  || 10.13 ||
putrā ye'nantarastrījāḥ krameṇoktā dvijanmanām  |
tānanantaranāmnastu mātṛdoṣātpracakṣate  || 10.14 ||
brāhmaṇādugrakanyāyāmāvṛto nāma jāyate  |
ābhīro'mbaṣṭhakanyāyāmāyogavyāṃ tu dhigvaṇaḥ  || 10.15 ||
āyogavaśca kṣattā ca caṇḍālaścādhamo nṛṇām  |
prātilomyena jāyante śūdrādapasadāstrayaḥ  || 10.16 ||
vaiśyānmāgadhavaidehau kṣatriyātsūta eva tu  |
pratīpamete jāyante pare'pyapasadāstrayaḥ  || 10.17 ||
jāto niṣādācchūdrāyāṃ jātyā bhavati pukkasaḥ  |
śūdrājjāto niṣādyāṃ tu sa vai kukkuṭakaḥ smṛtaḥ  || 10.18 ||
kṣatturjātastathogrāyāṃ śvapāka iti kīrtyate  |
vaidehakena tvambaṣṭhyāmutpanno veṇa ucyate  || 10.19 ||
dvijātayaḥ savarṇāsu janayantyavratāṃstu yān  |
tān sāvitrīparibhraṣṭān vrātyāniti vinirdiśet || 10.20 ||
vrātyāttu jāyate viprātpāpātmā bhūrjakaṇṭakaḥ  |
āvantyavāṭadhānau ca puṣpadhaḥ śaikha eva ca  || 10.21 ||
jhallo mallaśca rājanyādvrātyānnicchivireva ca  |
naṭaśca karaṇaścaiva khaso draviḍa eva ca  || 10.22 ||
vaiśyāttu jāyate vrātyātsudhanvācārya eva ca  |
kāruṣaśca vijanmā ca maitraḥ sātvata eva ca  || 10.23 ||
vyabhicāreṇa varṇānāmavedyāvedanena ca  |
svakarmaṇāṃ ca tyāgena jāyante varṇasaṃkarāḥ  || 10.24 ||
saṃkīrṇayonayo ye tu pratilomānulomajāḥ  |
anyonyavyatiṣaktāśca tān pravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 10.25 ||
sūto vaidehakaścaiva caṇḍālaśca narādhamaḥ  |
māgadhaḥ tathāyogava eva ca kṣatrajātiśca  || 10.26 ||
ete ṣaṭsadṛśān varṇāñjanayanti svayoniṣu  |
mātṛjātyāṃ prasūyante pravārāsu ca yoniṣu  || 10.27 ||
yathā trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ dvayorātmāsya jāyate  |
ānantaryātsvayonyāṃ tu tathā bāhyeṣvapi kramāt || 10.28 ||
te cāpi bāhyān subahūṃstato'pyadhikadūṣitān  |
parasparasya dāreṣu janayanti vigarhitān  || 10.29 ||
yathaiva śūdro brāhmaṇyāṃ bāhyaṃ jantuṃ prasūyate  |
tathā bāhyataraṃ bāhyaścāturvarṇye prasūyate  || 10.30 ||
pratikūlaṃ vartamānā bāhyā bāhyatarān punaḥ  |
hīnā hīnān prasūyante varṇān pañcadaśaiva tu  || 10.31 ||
prasādhanopacārajñamadāsaṃ dāsajīvanam  |
sairindhraṃ vāgurāvṛttiṃ sūte dasyurayogave  || 10.32 ||
maitreyakaṃ tu vaideho mādhūkaṃ saṃprasūyate  |
nṝn praśaṃsatyajasraṃ yo ghaṇṭātāḍo'ruṇodaye  || 10.33 ||
niṣādo mārgavaṃ sūte dāsaṃ naukarmajīvinam  |
kaivartamiti yaṃ prāhurāryāvartanivāsinaḥ  || 10.34 ||
mṛtavastrabhṛtsvnārīṣu garhitānnāśanāsu ca  |
bhavantyāyogavīṣvete jātihīnāḥ pṛthaktrayaḥ  || 10.35 ||
kārāvaro niṣādāttu carmakāraḥ prasūyate  |
vaidehikādandhramedau bahirgrāmapratiśrayau  || 10.36 ||
caṇḍālātpāṇḍusopākastvaksāravyavahāravān  |
āhiṇḍiko niṣādena vaidehyāmeva jāyate  || 10.37 ||
caṇḍālena tu sopāko mūlavyasanavṛttimān  |
pukkasyāṃ jāyate pāpaḥ sadā sajjanagarhitaḥ  || 10.38 ||
niṣādastrī tu caṇḍālātputramantyāvasāyinam  |
śmaśānagocaraṃ sūte bāhyānāmapi garhitam  || 10.39 ||
saṃkare jātayastvetāḥ pitṛmātṛpradarśitāḥ  |
prachannā vā prakāśā vā veditavyāḥ svakarmabhiḥ  || 10.40 ||
svajātijānantarajāḥ ṣaṭsutā dvijadharmiṇaḥ  |
śūdrāṇāṃ tu sadharmāṇaḥ sarve'padhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 10.41 ||
tapobījaprabhāvaistu te gacchanti yuge yuge  |
utkarṣaṃ cāpakarṣaṃ ca manuṣyeṣviha janmataḥ  || 10.42 ||
śanakaistu kriyālopādimāḥ kṣatriyajātayaḥ  |
vṛṣalatvaṃ gatā loke brāhmaṇādarśanena ca  || 10.43 ||
pauṇḍrakāścauḍradraviḍāḥ kāmbojā yavanāḥ śakāḥ  |
pāradāpahlavāścīnāḥ kirātā daradāḥ khaśāḥ  || 10.44 ||
mukhabāhūrupajjānāṃ yā loke jātayo bahiḥ  |
mlecchavācaścāryavācaḥ sarve te dasyavaḥ smṛtāḥ  || 10.45 ||
ye dvijānāmapasadā ye cāpadhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ  |
te ninditairvartayeyurdvijānāmeva karmabhiḥ  || 10.46 ||
sūtānāmaśvasārathyamambaṣṭhānāṃ cikitsanam  |
vaidehakānāṃ strīkāryaṃ māgadhānāṃ vaṇikpathaḥ  || 10.47 ||
matsyaghāto niṣādānāṃ tvaṣṭistvāyogavasya ca  |
medāndhracuñcumadgūnāmāraṇyapaśuhiṃsanam  || 10.48 ||
kṣattrugrapukkasānāṃ tu bilaukovadhabandhanam  |
dhigvaṇānāṃ carmakāryaṃ veṇānāṃ bhāṇḍavādanam  || 10.49 ||
caityadrumaśmaśāneṣu śaileṣūpavaneṣu ca  |
vaseyurete vijñātā vartayantaḥ svakarmabhiḥ  || 10.50 ||
caṇḍālaśvapacānāṃ tu bahirgrāmātpratiśrayaḥ  |
apapātrāśca kartavyā dhanameṣāṃ śvagardabham  || 10.51 ||
vāsāṃsi mṛtacailāni bhinnabhāṇḍeṣu bhojanam  |
kārṣṇāyasamalaṅkāraḥ parivrajyā ca nityaśaḥ  || 10.52 ||
na taiḥ samayamanvicchetpuruṣo dharmamācaran  |
vyavahāro mithasteṣāṃ vivāhaḥ sadṛśaiḥ saha  || 10.53 ||
annameṣāṃ parādhīnaṃ deyaṃ syādbhinnabhājane  |
rātrau na vicareyuste grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca  || 10.54 ||
divā careyuḥ kāryārthaṃ cihnitā rājaśāsanaiḥ  |
abāndhavaṃ śavaṃ caiva nirhareyuriti sthitiḥ  || 10.55 ||
vadhyāṃśca hanyuḥ satataṃ yathāśāstraṃ nṛpājñayā  |
vadhyavāsāṃsi gṛhṇīyuḥ śayyāścābharaṇāni ca  || 10.56 ||
varṇāpetamavijñātaṃ naraṃ kaluṣayonijam  |
āryarūpamivānāryaṃ karmabhiḥ svairvibhāvayet || 10.57 ||
anāryatā niṣṭhuratā krūratā niṣkriyātmatā  |
puruṣaṃ vyañjayantīha loke kaluṣayonijam  || 10.58 ||
pitryaṃ vā bhajate śīlaṃ māturvobhayameva vā  |
na kathaṃ cana duryoniḥ prakṛtiṃ svāṃ niyacchati  || 10.59 ||
kule mukhye'pi jātasya yasya syādyonisaṃkaraḥ  |
saṃśrayatyeva tacchīlaṃ naro'lpamapi vā bahu  || 10.60 ||
yatra tvete paridhvaṃsājjāyante varṇadūṣakāḥ  |
rāṣṭrikaiḥ saha tadrāṣṭraṃ kṣiprameva vinaśyati  || 10.61 ||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā dehatyāgo'nupaskṛtaḥ  |
strībālābhyupapattau ca bāhyānāṃ siddhikāraṇam  || 10.62 ||
ahiṃsā satyamasteyaṃ śaucamindriyanigrahaḥ  |
etaṃ sāmāsikaṃ dharmaṃ cāturvarṇye'bravīnmanuḥ  || 10.63 ||
śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇājjātaḥ śreyasā cetprajāyate  |
aśreyān śreyasīṃ jātiṃ gacchatyā saptamādyugāt || 10.64 ||
śūdro brāhmaṇatāmeti brāhmaṇaścaiti śūdratām  |
kṣatriyājjātamevaṃ tu vidyādvaiśyāttathaiva ca  || 10.65 ||
anāryāyāṃ samutpanno brāhmaṇāttu yadṛcchayā  |
brāhmaṇyāmapyanāryāttu śreyastvaṃ kveti cedbhavet || 10.66 ||
jāto nāryāmanāryāyāmāryādāryo bhavedguṇaiḥ  |
jāto'pyanāryādāryāyāmanārya iti niścayaḥ  || 10.67 ||
tāvubhāvapyasaṃskāryāviti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ  |
vaiguṇyājjanmanaḥ pūrva uttaraḥ pratilomataḥ  || 10.68 ||
subījaṃ caiva sukṣetre jātaṃ saṃpadyate yathā  |
tathāryājjāta āryāyāṃ sarvaṃ saṃskāramarhati  || 10.69 ||
bījameke praśaṃsanti kṣetramanye manīṣiṇaḥ  |
bījakṣetre tathaivānye tatreyaṃ tu vyavasthitiḥ  || 10.70 ||
akṣetre bījamutsṛṣṭamantaraiva vinaśyati  |
abījakamapi kṣetraṃ kevalaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ bhavet || 10.71 ||
yasmādbījaprabhāveṇa tiryagjā ṛṣayo'bhavan  |
pūjitāśca praśastāśca tasmādbījaṃ praśasyate  || 10.72 ||
anāryamāryakarmāṇamāryaṃ cānāryakarmiṇam  |
saṃpradhāryābravīddhātā na samau nāsamāviti  || 10.73 ||
brāhmaṇā brahmayonisthā ye svakarmaṇyavasthitāḥ  |
te samyagupajīveyuḥ ṣaṭkarmāṇi yathākramam  || 10.74 ||
adhyāpanamadhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā  |
dānaṃ pratigrahaścaiva ṣaṭkarmāṇyagrajanmanaḥ  || 10.75 ||
ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇāmasya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā  |
yājanādhyāpane caiva viśuddhācca pratigrahaḥ  || 10.76 ||
trayo dharmā nivartante brāhmaṇātkṣatriyaṃ prati  |
adhyāpanaṃ yājanaṃ ca tṛtīyaśca pratigrahaḥ  || 10.77 ||
vaiśyaṃ prati tathaivaite nivarteranniti sthitiḥ  |
na tau prati hi tān dharmānmanurāha prajāpatiḥ  || 10.78 ||
śastrāstrabhṛttvaṃ kṣatrasya vaṇikpaśukṛṣirviṣaḥ  |
ājīvanārthaṃ dharmastu dānamadhyayanaṃ yajiḥ  || 10.79 ||
vedābhyāso brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya ca rakṣaṇam  |
vārtākarmaiva vaiśyasya viśiṣṭāni svakarmasu  || 10.80 ||
ajīvaṃstu yathoktena brāhmaṇaḥ svena karmaṇā  |
jīvetkṣatriyadharmeṇa sa hyasya pratyanantaraḥ  || 10.81 ||
