ubhābhyāmapyajīvaṃstu kathaṃ syāditi cedbhavet |
kṛṣigorakṣamāsthāya jīvedvaiśyasya jīvikām  || 10.82 ||
vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvaṃstu brāhmaṇaḥ ksatriyo'pi vā  |
hiṃsāprāyāṃ parādhīnāṃ kṛṣiṃ yatnena varjayet || 10.83 ||
kṛṣiṃ sādhviti manyante sā vṛttiḥ sadvigarhitāḥ  |
bhūmiṃ bhūmiśayāṃścaiva hanti kāṣṭhamayomukham  || 10.84 ||
idaṃ tu vṛttivaikalyāttyajato dharmanaipuṇaṃ  |
viṭpaṇyamuddhṛtoddhāraṃ vikreyaṃ vittavardhanam  || 10.85 ||
sarvān rasānapoheta kṛtānnaṃ ca tilaiḥ saha  |
aśmano lavaṇaṃ caiva paśavo ye ca mānuṣāḥ  || 10.86 ||
sarvaṃ ca tāntavaṃ raktaṃ śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca  |
api cetsyuraraktāni phalamūle tathauṣadhīḥ  || 10.87 ||
apaḥ śastraṃ viṣaṃ māṃsaṃ somaṃ gandhāṃśca sarvaśaḥ  |
kṣīraṃ kṣaudraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ madhu guḍaṃ kuśān  || 10.88 ||
āraṇyāṃśca paśūn sarvān daṃṣṭriṇaśca vayāṃsi ca  |
madyaṃ nīliṃ ca lākṣāṃ ca sarvāṃścaikaśaphāṃstathā  || 10.89 ||
kāmamutpādya kṛṣyāṃ tu svayameva kṛṣīvalaḥ  |
vikrīṇīta tilāñśūdrān dharmārthamacirasthitān  || 10.90 ||
bhojanābhyañjanāddānādyadanyatkurute tilaiḥ  |
kṛmibhūtaḥ śvaviṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati  || 10.91 ||
sadyaḥ patati māṃsena lākṣayā lavaṇena ca  |
tryaheṇa śūdro bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ kṣīravikrayāt || 10.92 ||
itareṣāṃ tu paṇyānāṃ vikrayādiha kāmataḥ  |
brāhmaṇaḥ saptarātreṇa vaiśyabhāvaṃ niyacchati  || 10.93 ||
rasā rasairnimātavyā na tveva lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ  |
kṛtānnaṃ ca kṛtānnena tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ  || 10.94 ||
jīvedetena rājanyaḥ sarveṇāpyanayaṃ gataḥ  |
na tveva jyāyaṃsīṃ vṛttimabhimanyeta karhi cit || 10.95 ||
yo lobhādadhamo jātyā jīvedutkṛṣṭakarmabhiḥ  |
taṃ rājā nirdhanaṃ kṛtvā kṣiprameva pravāsayet || 10.96 ||
varaṃ svadharmo viguṇo na pārakyaḥ svanuṣṭhitaḥ  |
paradharmeṇa jīvan hi sadyaḥ patati jātitaḥ  || 10.97 ||
vaiśyo'jīvan svadharmeṇa śūdravṛttyāpi vartayet |
anācarannakāryāṇi nivarteta ca śaktimān  || 10.98 ||
aśaknuvaṃstu śuśrūṣāṃ śūdraḥ kartuṃ dvijanmanām  |
putradārātyayaṃ prāpto jīvetkārukakarmabhiḥ  || 10.99 ||
yaiḥ karmabhiḥ pracaritaiḥ śuśrūṣyante dvijātayaḥ  |
tāni kārukakarmāṇi śilpāni vividhāni ca  || 10.100 ||
vaiśyavṛttimanātiṣṭhan brāhmaṇaḥ sve pathi sthitaḥ  |
avṛttikarṣitaḥ sīdannimaṃ dharmaṃ samācaret || 10.101 ||
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyādbrāhmaṇastvanayaṃ gataḥ  |
pavitraṃ duṣyatītyetaddharmato nopapadyate  || 10.102 ||
nādhyāpanādyājanādvā garhitādvā pratigrahāt |
doṣo bhavati viprāṇāṃ jvalanāmbusamā hi te  || 10.103 ||
jīvitātyayamāpanno yo'nnamatti tatastataḥ  |
ākāśamiva paṅkena na sa pāpena lipyate  || 10.104 ||
ajīgartaḥ sutaṃ hantumupāsarpadbubhukṣitaḥ  |
na cālipyata pāpena kṣutpratīkāramācaran  || 10.105 ||
śvamāṃsamicchanārto'ttuṃ dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ  |
prāṇānāṃ parirakṣārthaṃ vāmadevo na liptavān  || 10.106 ||
bharadvājaḥ kṣudhārtastu saputro vijane vane  |
bahvīrgāḥ pratijagrāha vṛdhostakṣṇo mahātapāḥ  || 10.107 ||
kṣudhārtaścāttumabhyāgādviśvāmitraḥ śvajāghanīm  |
caṇḍālahastādādāya dharmādharmavicakṣaṇaḥ  || 10.108 ||
pratigrahādyājanādvā tathaivādhyāpanādapi  |
pratigrahaḥ pratyavaraḥ pretya viprasya garhitaḥ  || 10.109 ||
yājanādhyāpane nityaṃ kriyete saṃskṛtātmanām  |
pratigrahastu kriyate śūdrādapyantyajanmanaḥ  || 10.110 ||
japahomairapaityeno yājanādhyāpanaiḥ kṛtam  |
pratigrahanimittaṃ tu tyāgena tapasaiva ca  || 10.111 ||
śiloñchamapyādadīta vipro'jīvan yatastataḥ  |
pratigrahācchilaḥ śreyāṃstato'pyuñchaḥ praśasyate  || 10.112 ||
sīdadbhiḥ kupyamicchadbhirdhane vā pṛthivīpatiḥ  |
yācyaḥ syātsnātakairviprairaditsaṃstyāgamarhati  || 10.113 ||
akṛtaṃ ca kṛtātkṣetrādgaurajāvikameva ca  |
hiraṇyaṃ dhānyamannaṃ ca pūrvaṃ pūrvamadoṣavat || 10.114 ||
sapta vittāgamā dharmyā dāyo lābhaḥ krayo jayaḥ  |
prayogaḥ karmayogaśca satpratigraha eva ca  || 10.115 ||
vidyā śilpaṃ bhṛtiḥ sevā gorakṣyaṃ vipaṇiḥ kṛṣiḥ  |
dhṛtirbhaikṣaṃ kusīdaṃ ca daśa jīvanahetavaḥ  || 10.116 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vāpi vṛddhiṃ naiva prayojayet |
kāmaṃ tu khalu dharmārthaṃ dadyātpāpīyase'lpikām  || 10.117 ||
caturthamādadāno'pi kṣatriyo bhāgamāpadi  |
prajā rakṣan paraṃ śaktyā kilbiṣātpratimucyate  || 10.118 ||
svadharmo vijayastasya nāhave syātparāṅmukhaḥ  |
śastreṇa vaiśyān rakṣitvā dharmyamāhārayedbalim  || 10.119 ||
dhānye'ṣṭamaṃ viśāṃ śulkaṃ viṃśaṃ kārṣāpaṇāvaram  |
karmopakaraṇāḥ śūdrāḥ kāravaḥ śilpinastathā  || 10.120 ||
śūdrastu vṛttimākāṅkṣan kṣatramārādhayedyadi  |
dhaninaṃ vāpyupārādhya vaiśyaṃ śūdro jijīviṣet || 10.121 ||
svargārthamubhayārthaṃ vā viprānārādhayettu saḥ  |
jātabrāhmaṇaśabdasya sā hyasya kṛtakṛtyatā  || 10.122 ||
viprasevaiva śūdrasya viśiṣṭaṃ karma kīrtyate  |
yadato'nyaddhi kurute tadbhavatyasya niṣphalam  || 10.123 ||
prakalpyā tasya tairvṛttiḥ svakuṭumbādyathārhataḥ  |
śaktiṃ cāvekṣya dākṣyaṃ ca bhṛtyānāṃ ca parigraham  || 10.124 ||
ucchiṣṭamannaṃ dātavyaṃ jīrṇāni vasanāni ca  |
pulākāścaiva dhānyānāṃ jīrṇāścaiva paricchadāḥ  || 10.125 ||
na śūdre pātakaṃ kiṃ cinna ca saṃskāramarhati  |
nāsyādhikāro dharme'sti na dharmātpratiṣedhanam  || 10.126 ||
dharmepsavastu dharmajñāḥ satāṃ vṛttamanuṣṭhitāḥ  |
mantravarjyaṃ na duṣyanti praśaṃsāṃ prāpnuvanti ca  || 10.127 ||
yathā yathā hi sadvṛttamātiṣṭhatyanasūyakaḥ  |
tathā tathemaṃ cāmuṃ ca lokaṃ prāpnotyaninditaḥ  || 10.128 ||
śaktenāpi hi śūdreṇa na kāryo dhanasaṃcayaḥ  |
śūdro hi dhanamāsādya brāhmaṇāneva bādhate  || 10.129 ||
ete caturṇāṃ varṇānāmāpaddharmāḥ prakīrtitāḥ  |
yān samyaganutiṣṭhanto vrajanti paramaṃ gatim  || 10.130 ||
eṣa dharmavidhiḥ kṛtsnaścāturvarṇyasya kīrtitaḥ  |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi prāyaścittavidhiṃ śubham  || 10.131 ||

sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇamadhvagaṃ sārvavedasam  |
gurvarthaṃ pitṛmātrarthaṃ svādhyāyārthyupatāpinaḥ  || 11.1 ||
na vai tān snātakān vidyādbrāhmaṇān dharmabhikṣukān  |
niḥsvebhyo deyametebhyo dānaṃ vidyāviśeṣataḥ  || 11.2 ||
etebhyo hi dvijāgryebhyo deyamannaṃ sadakṣiṇam  |
itarebhyo bahirvedi kṛtānnaṃ deyamucyate  || 11.3 ||
sarvaratnāni rājā tu yathārhaṃ pratipādayet |
brāhmaṇān vedaviduṣo yajñārthaṃ caiva dakṣiṇām  || 11.4 ||
kṛtadāro'parān dārān bhikṣitvā yo'dhigacchati  |
ratimātraṃ phalaṃ tasya dravyadātustu saṃtatiḥ  || 11.5 ||
dhanāni tu yathāśakti vipreṣu pratipādayet |
vedavitsu vivikteṣu pretya svargaṃ samaśnute  || 11.6 ||
yasya traivārṣikaṃ bhaktaṃ paryāptaṃ bhṛtyavṛttaye  |
adhikaṃ vāpi vidyeta sa somaṃ pātumarhati  || 11.7[06 ] ||
ataḥ svalpīyasi dravye yaḥ somaṃ pibati dvijaḥ  |
sa pītasomapūrvo'pi na tasyāpnoti tatphalam  || 11.8[07 ] ||
śaktaḥ parajane dātā svajane duḥkhajīvini  |
madhvāpāto viṣāsvādaḥ sa dharmapratirūpakaḥ  || 11.9[08 ] ||
bhṛtyānāmuparodhena yatkarotyaurdhvadehikam  |
tadbhavatyasukhodarkaṃ jīvataśca mṛtasya ca  || 11.10[09 ] ||
yajñaścetpratiruddhaḥ syādekenāṅgena yajvanaḥ  |
brāhmaṇasya viśeṣena dhārmike sati rājani  || 11.11[10 ] ||
yo vaiśyaḥ syādbahupaśurhīnakraturasomapaḥ  |
kuṭumbāttasya taddravyamāharedyajñasiddhaye  || 11.12[11 ] ||
āharettrīṇi vā dve vā kāmaṃ śūdrasya veśmanaḥ  |
na hi śūdrasya yajñeṣu kaścidasti parigrahaḥ  || 11.13[12 ] ||
yo'nāhitāgniḥ śatagurayajvā ca sahasraguḥ  |
tayorapi kuṭumbābhyāmāharedavicārayan  || 11.14[13 ] ||
ādānanityāccādāturāharedaprayacchataḥ  |
tathā yaśo'sya prathate dharmaścaiva pravardhate  || 11.15[14 ] ||
tathāaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣaḍanaśnatā  |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇaḥ  || 11.16[15 ] ||
khalātkṣetrādagārādvā yato vāpyupalabhyate  |
ākhyātavyaṃ tu tattasmai pṛcchate yadi pṛcchati  || 11.17[16 ] ||
brāhmaṇasvaṃ na hartavyaṃ kṣatriyeṇa kadā cana  |
dasyuniṣkriyayostu svamajīvan hartumarhati  || 11.18[17 ] ||
yo'sādhubhyo'rthamādāya sādhubhyaḥ saṃprayacchati  |
sa kṛtvā plavamātmānaṃ saṃtārayati tāvubhau  || 11.19[18 ] ||
yaddhanaṃ yajñaśīlānāṃ devasvaṃ tadvidurbudhāḥ  |
ayajvanāṃ tu yadvittamāsurasvaṃ taducyate  || 11.20[19 ] ||
na tasmin dhārayeddaṇḍaṃ dhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ  |
kṣatriyasya hi bāliśyādbrāhmaṇaḥ sīdati kṣudhā  || 11.21[20 ] ||
tasya bhṛtyajanaṃ jñātvā svakuṭumbānmahīpatiḥ  |
śrutaśīle ca vijñāya vṛttiṃ dharmyāṃ prakalpayet || 11.22[21 ] ||
kalpayitvāsya vṛttiṃ ca rakṣedenaṃ samantataḥ  |
rājā hi dharmaṣaḍbhāgaṃ tasmātprāpnoti rakṣitāt || 11.23[22 ] ||
na yajñārthaṃ dhanaṃ śūdrādvipro bhikṣeta karhi cit |
yajamāno hi bhikṣitvā caṇḍālaḥ pretya jāyate  || 11.24[23 ] ||
yājñārthamarthaṃ bhikṣitvā yo na sarvaṃ prayacchati  |
sa yāti bhāsatāṃ vipraḥ kākatāṃ vā śataṃ samāḥ  || 11.25[24 ] ||
devasvaṃ brāhmaṇasvaṃ vā lobhenopahinasti yaḥ  |
sa pāpātmā pare loke gṛdhrocchiṣṭena jīvati  || 11.26[25 ] ||
iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ nityaṃ nirvapedabdaparyaye  |
kḷptānāṃ paśusomānāṃ niṣkṛtyarthamasaṃbhave  || 11.27[26 ] ||
āpatkalpena yo dharmaṃ kurute'nāpadi dvijaḥ  |
sa nāpnoti phalaṃ tasya paratreti vicāritam  || 11.28[27 ] ||
viśvaiśca devaiḥ sādhyaiśca brāhmaṇaiśca maharṣibhiḥ  |
āpatsu maraṇādbhītairvidheḥ pratinidhiḥ kṛtaḥ  || 11.29[28 ] ||
prabhuḥ prathamakalpasya yo'nukalpena vartate  |
na sāṃparāyikaṃ tasya durmatervidyate phalam  || 11.30[29 ] ||
na brāhmaṇo vedayeta kiṃ cidrājani dharmavit |
svavīryeṇaiva tāñśiṣyānmānavānapakāriṇaḥ  || 11.31[30 ] ||
svavīryādrājavīryācca svavīryaṃ balavattaram  |
tasmātsvenaiva vīryeṇa nigṛhṇīyādarīn dvijaḥ  || 11.32[31 ] ||
śrutīratharvāṅgirasīḥ kuryādityavicārayan  |
vākśastraṃ vai brāhmaṇasya tena hanyādarīn dvijaḥ  || 11.33[32 ] ||
kṣatriyo bāhuvīryeṇa taredāpadamātmanaḥ  |
dhanena vaiśyaśūdrau tu japahomairdvijottamaḥ  || 11.34[33 ] ||
vidhātā śāsitā vaktā maitro brāhmaṇa ucyate  |
tasmai nākuśalaṃ brūyānna śuṣkāṃ giramīrayet || 11.35[34 ] ||
na vai kanyā na yuvatirnālpavidyo na bāliśaḥ  |
hotā syādagnihotrasya nārto nāsaṃskṛtastathā  || 11.36[35 ] ||
narake hi patantyete juhvantaḥ sa ca yasya tat |
tasmādvaitānakuśalo hotā syādvedapāragaḥ  || 11.37[36 ] ||
prājāpatyamadattvāśvamagnyādheyasya dakṣiṇām  |
anāhitāgnirbhavati brāhmaṇo vibhave sati  || 11.38[37 ] ||
puṇyānyanyāni kurvīta śraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ  |
na tvalpadakṣiṇairyajñairyajeteha kathaṃ cana  || 11.39[38 ] ||
indriyāṇi yaśaḥ svargamāyuḥ kīrtiṃ prajāḥ paśūn  |
hantyalpadakṣiṇo yajñastasmānnālpadhano yajet || 11.40[39 ] ||
agnihotryapavidhyāgnīn brāhmaṇaḥ kāmakārataḥ  |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ carenmāsaṃ vīrahatyāsamaṃ hi tat || 11.41[40 ] ||
ye śūdrādadhigamyārthamagnihotramupāsate  |
ṛtvijaste hi śūdrāṇāṃ brahmavādiṣu garhitāḥ  || 11.42[41 ] ||
teṣāṃ satatamajñānāṃ vṛṣalāgnyupasevinām  |
padā mastakamākramya dātā durgāṇi saṃtaret || 11.43[42 ] ||
akurvan vihitaṃ karma ninditaṃ ca samācaran  |
prasaktaścendriyārtheṣu prāyaścittīyate naraḥ  || 11.44[43 ] ||
akāmataḥ kṛte pāpe prāyaścittaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  |
kāmakārakṛte'pyāhureke śrutinidarśanāt || 11.45[44 ] ||
akāmataḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vedābhyāsena śudhyati  |
kāmatastu kṛtaṃ mohātprāyaścittaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ  || 11.46[45 ] ||
prāyaścittīyatāṃ prāpya daivātpūrvakṛtena vā  |
na saṃsargaṃ vrajetsadbhiḥ prāyaścitte'kṛte dvijaḥ  || 11.47[46 ] ||
iha duścaritaiḥ ke citke citpūrvakṛtaistathā  |
prāpnuvanti durātmāno narā rūpaviparyayam  || 11.48[47 ] ||
suvarṇacauraḥ kaunakhyaṃ surāpaḥ śyāvadantatām  |
brahmahā kṣayarogitvaṃ dauścarmyaṃ gurutalpagaḥ  || 11.49[48 ] ||
piśunaḥ pautināsikyaṃ sūcakaḥ pūtivaktratām  |
dhānyacauro'ṅgahīnatvamātiraikyaṃ tu miśrakaḥ  || 11.50[49 ] ||
annahartāmayāvitvaṃ maukyaṃ vāgapahārakaḥ  |
vastrāpahārakaḥ śvaitryaṃ paṅgutāmaśvahārakaḥ  || 11.51[50 ] ||
evaṃ karmaviśeṣeṇa jāyante sadvigarhitāḥ  |
jaḍamūkāndhabadhirā vikṛtākṛtayastathā  || 11.52[51 ] ||
caritavyamato nityaṃ prāyaścittaṃ viśuddhaye  |
nindyairhi lakṣaṇairyuktā jāyante'niṣkṛtainasaḥ  || 11.53[52 ] ||
brahmahatyā surāpānaṃ steyaṃ gurvaṅganāgamaḥ  |
mahānti pātakānyāhuḥ saṃsargaścāpi taiḥ saha  || 11.54[53 ] ||
anṛtaṃ ca samutkarṣe rājagāmi ca paiśunam  |
guroścālīkanirbandhaḥ samāni brahmahatyayā  || 11.55[54 ] ||
brahmojjhatā vedanindā kauṭasākṣyaṃ suhṛdvadhaḥ  |
garhitānādyayorjagdhiḥ surāpānasamāni ṣaṭ || 11.56[55 ] ||
nikṣepasyāpaharaṇaṃ narāśvarajatasya ca  |
bhūmivajramaṇīnāṃ ca rukmasteyasamaṃ smṛtam  || 11.57[56 ] ||
retaḥsekaḥ svayonīṣu kumārīṣvantyajāsu ca  |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu gurutalpasamaṃ viduḥ  || 11.58[57 ] ||
govadho'yājyasaṃyājyaṃ pāradāryātmavikrayaḥ  |
gurumātṛpitṛtyāgaḥ svādhyāyāgnyoḥ sutasya ca  || 11.59[58 ] ||
parivittitānuje'nūḍhe parivedanameva ca  |
tayordānaṃ ca kanyāyāstayoreva ca yājanam  || 11.60[59 ] ||
kanyāyā dūṣaṇaṃ caiva vārdhuṣyaṃ vratalopanam  |
taḍāgārāmadārāṇāmapatyasya ca vikrayaḥ  || 11.61[60 ] ||
vrātyatā bāndhavatyāgo bhṛtyādhyāpanameva ca  |
bhṛtyā cādhyayanādānamapaṇyānāṃ ca vikrayaḥ  || 11.62[61 ] ||
sarvākāreṣvadhīkāro mahāyantrapravartanam  |
hiṃsauṣadhīnāṃ stryājīvo'bhicāro mūlakarma ca  || 11.63[62 ] ||
indhanārthamaśuṣkāṇāṃ drumāṇāmavapātanam  |
ātmārthaṃ ca kriyārambho ninditānnādanaṃ tathā  || 11.64[63 ] ||
anāhitāgnitā steyamṛṇānāmanapakriyā  |
asacchāṣṭrādhigamanaṃ kauśīlavyasya ca kriyā  || 11.65[64 ] ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyaṃ madyapastrīniṣevaṇam  |
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho nāstikyaṃ copapātakam  || 11.66[65 ] ||
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ kṛtvā ghrātiraghreyamadyayoḥ  |
jaihmyaṃ ca maithunaṃ puṃsi jātibhraṃśakaraṃ smṛtam  || 11.67[66 ] ||
kharāśvoṣṭramṛgebhānāmajāvikavadhastathā  |
saṃkarīkaraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ mīnāhimahiṣasya ca  || 11.68[67 ] ||
ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ śūdrasevanam  |
apātrīkaraṇaṃ jñeyamasatyasya ca bhāṣaṇam  || 11.69[68 ] ||
kṛmikīṭavayohatyā madyānugatabhojanam  |
phalaidhaḥkusumasteyamadhairyaṃ ca malāvaham  || 11.70[69 ] ||
etānyenāṃsi sarvāṇi yathoktāni pṛthakpṛthak |
yairyairvratairapohyante tāni samyaṅnibodhata  || 11.71[70 ] ||
brahmahā dvādaśa samāḥ kuṭīṃ kṛtvā vane vaset |
bhaikṣāśyātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ kṛtvā śavaśiro dhvajam  || 11.72[71 ] ||
lakṣyaṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ vā syādviduṣāmicchayātmanaḥ  |
prāsyedātmānamagnau vā samiddhe triravākśirāḥ  || 11.73[72 ] ||
yajeta vāśvamedhena svarjitā gosavena vā  |
abhijidviśvajidbhyāṃ vā trivṛtāgniṣṭutāpi vā  || 11.74[73 ] ||
japan vānyatamaṃ vedaṃ yojanānāṃ śataṃ vrajet |
brahmahatyāpanodāya mitabhuṅniyatendriyaḥ  || 11.75[74 ] ||
sarvasvaṃ vedaviduṣe brāhmaṇāyopapādayet |
dhanaṃ hi jīvanāyālaṃ gṛhaṃ vā saparicchadam  || 11.76[75 ] ||
haviṣyabhugvānusaretpratisrotaḥ sarasvatīm  |
japedvā niyatāhārastrirvai vedasya saṃhitām  || 11.77[76 ] ||
kṛtavāpano nivasedgrāmānte govraje'pi vā  |
āśrame vṛkṣamūle vā gobrāhmaṇahite rataḥ  || 11.78[77 ] ||
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā sadyaḥ prāṇān parityajet |
mucyate brahmahatyāyā goptā gorbrāhmaṇasya ca  || 11.79[78 ] ||
trivāraṃ pratiroddhā vā sarvasvamavajitya vā  |
viprasya tannimitte vā prāṇālābhe vimucyate  || 11.80[79 ] ||
evaṃ dṛḍhavrato nityaṃ brahmacārī samāhitaḥ  |
samāpte dvādaśe varṣe brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati  || 11.81[80 ] ||
śiṣṭvā vā bhūmidevānāṃ naradevasamāgame  |
svameno'vabhṛthasnāto hayamedhe vimucyate  || 11.82[81 ] ||
dharmasya brāhmaṇo mūlamagraṃ rājanya ucyate  |
tasmātsamāgame teṣāmeno vikhyāpya śudhyati  || 11.83[82 ] ||
brahmaṇaḥ saṃbhavenaiva devānāmapi daivatam  |
pramāṇaṃ caiva lokasya brahmātraiva hi kāraṇam  || 11.84[83 ] ||
teṣāṃ vedavido brūyustrayo'pyenaḥ suniṣkṛtim  |
sā teṣāṃ pāvanāya syātpavitrā viduṣāṃ hi vāk || 11.85[84 ] ||
ato'nyatamamāsthāya vidhiṃ vipraḥ samāhitaḥ  |
brahmahatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vyapohatyātmavattayā  || 11.86[85 ] ||
hatvā garbhamavijñātametadeva vrataṃ caret |
rājanyavaiśyau cejānāvātreyīmeva ca striyam  || 11.87[86 ] ||
uktvā caivānṛtaṃ sākṣye pratirudhya guruṃ tathā  |
apahṛtya ca niḥkṣepaṃ kṛtvā ca strīsuhṛdvadham  || 11.88[87 ] ||
iyaṃ viśuddhiruditā pramāpyākāmato dvijam  |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtirna vidhīyate  || 11.89[88 ] ||
surāṃ pītvā dvijo mohādagnivarṇāṃ surāṃ pibet |
tayā sa kāye nirdagdhe mucyate kilbiṣāttataḥ  || 11.90[89 ] ||
gomūtramagnivarṇaṃ vā pibedudakameva vā  |
payo ghṛtaṃ vā maraṇādgośakṛdrasameva vā  || 11.91[90 ] ||
kaṇān vā bhakṣayedabdaṃ piṇyākaṃ vā sakṛnniśi  |
surāpānāpanuttyarthaṃ vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī  || 11.92[91 ] ||
surā vai malamannānāṃ pāpmā ca malamucyate  |
tasmādbrāhmaṇarājanyau vaiśyaśca na surāṃ pibet || 11.93[92 ] ||
gauḍī paiṣṭī ca mādhvī ca vijñeyā trividhā surā  |
yathaivaikā tathā sarvā na pātavyā dvijottamaiḥ  || 11.94[93 ] ||
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācānnaṃ madyaṃ māṃsaṃ surāsavam  |
tadbrāhmaṇena nāttavyaṃ devānāmaśnatā haviḥ  || 11.95[94 ] ||
amedhye vā patenmatto vaidikaṃ vāpyudāharet |
akāryamanyatkuryādvā brāhmaṇo madamohitaḥ  || 11.96[95 ] ||
yasya kāyagataṃ brahma madyenāplāvyate sakṛt |
tasya vyapaiti brāhmaṇyaṃ śūdratvaṃ ca sa gacchati  || 11.97[96 ] ||
eṣā vicitrābhihitā surāpānasya niṣkṛtiḥ  |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi suvarṇasteyaniṣkṛtim  || 11.98[97 ] ||
suvarṇasteyakṛdvipro rājānamabhigamya tu  |
svakarma khyāpayan brūyānmāṃ bhavānanuśāstviti  || 11.99[98 ] ||
gṛhītvā musalaṃ rājā sakṛddhanyāttu taṃ svayam  |
vadhena śudhyati steno brāhmaṇastapasaiva tu  || 11.100[99 ] ||
tapasāpanunutsustu suvarṇasteyajaṃ malam  |
cīravāsā dvijo'raṇye caredbrahmahaṇo vratam  || 11.101[100 ] ||
etairvratairapoheta pāpaṃ steyakṛtaṃ dvijaḥ  |
gurustrīgamanīyaṃ tu vratairebhirapānudet || 11.102[101 ] ||
gurutalpyabhibhāṣyainastapte svapyādayomaye  |
sūrmīṃ jvalantīṃ svāśliṣyenmṛtyunā sa viśudhyati  || 11.103[102 ] ||
svayaṃ vā śiṣṇavṛṣaṇāvutkṛtyādhāya cāñjalau  |
nairṛtīṃ diśamātiṣṭhedā nipātādajihmagaḥ  || 11.104[103 ] ||
khaṭvāṅgī cīravāsā vā śmaśrulo vijane vane  |
prājāpatyaṃ caretkṛcchramabdamekaṃ samāhitaḥ  || 11.105[104 ] ||
cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā trīnmāsānabhyasyenniyatendriyaḥ  |
haviṣyeṇa yavāgvā vā gurutalpāpanuttaye  || 11.106[105 ] ||
etairvratairapoheyurmahāpātakino malam  |
upapātakinastvevamebhirnānāvidhairvrataiḥ  || 11.107[106 ] ||
upapātakasaṃyukto goghno māsaṃ yavān pibet |
kṛtavāpo vasedgoṣṭhe carmaṇā tena saṃvṛtaḥ  || 11.108[107 ] ||
caturthakālamaśnīyādakṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam  |
gomūtreṇācaretsnānaṃ dvau māsau niyatendriyaḥ  || 11.109[108 ] ||
divānugacchedgāstāstu tiṣṭhannūrdhvaṃ rajaḥ pibet |
śuśrūṣitvā namaskṛtya rātrau vīrāsanaṃ vaset || 11.110[109 ] ||
tiṣṭhantīṣvanutiṣṭhettu vrajantīṣvapyanuvrajet |
āsīnāsu tathāsīno niyato vītamatsaraḥ  || 11.111[110 ] ||
āturāmabhiśastāṃ vā cauravyāghrādibhirbhayaiḥ  |
patitāṃ paṅkalagnaṃ vā sarvopāyairvimocayet || 11.112[111 ] ||
uṣṇe varṣati śīte vā mārute vāti vā bhṛśam  |
na kurvītātmanastrāṇaṃ gorakṛtvā tu śaktitaḥ  || 11.113[112 ] ||
ātmano yadi vānyeṣāṃ gṛhe kṣetre'tha vā khale  |
bhakṣayantīṃ na kathayetpibantaṃ caiva vatsakam  || 11.114[113 ] ||
anena vidhinā yastu goghno gāmanugacchati  |
sa gohatyākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tribhirmāsairvyapohati  || 11.115[114 ] ||
vṛṣabhaikādaśā gāśca dadyātsucaritavrataḥ  |
avidyamāne sarvasvaṃ vedavidbhyo nivedayet || 11.116[115 ] ||
etadeva vrataṃ kuryurupapātakino dvijāḥ  |
avakīrṇivarjyaṃ śuddhyarthaṃ cāndrāyaṇamathāpi vā  || 11.117[116 ] ||
avakīrṇī tu kāṇena gardabhena catuṣpathe  |
pākayajñavidhānena yajeta nirṛtiṃ niśi  || 11.118[117 ] ||
hutvāgnau vidhivaddhomānantataśca sametyṛcā  |
vātendraguruvahnīnāṃ juhuyātsarpiṣāhutīḥ  || 11.119[118 ] ||
kāmato retasaḥ sekaṃ vratasthasya dvijanmanaḥ  |
atikramaṃ vratasyāhurdharmajñā brahmavādinaḥ  || 11.120[119 ] ||
mārutaṃ puruhūtaṃ ca guruṃ pāvakameva ca  |
caturo vratino'bhyeti brāhmaṃ tejo'vakīrṇinaḥ  || 11.121[120 ] ||
etasminnenasi prāpte vasitvā gardabhājinam  |
saptāgārāṃścaredbhaikṣaṃ svakarma parikīrtayan  || 11.122[121 ] ||
tebhyo labdhena bhaikṣeṇa vartayannekakālikam  |
upaspṛśaṃstriṣavaṇaṃ tvabdena sa viśudhyati  || 11.123[122 ] ||
jātibhraṃśakaraṃ karma kṛtvānyatamamicchayā  |
caretsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyamanicchayā  || 11.124[123 ] ||
saṃkarāpātrakṛtyāsu māsaṃ śodhanamaindavam  |
malinīkaraṇīyeṣu taptaḥ syādyāvakaistryaham  || 11.125[124 ] ||
turīyo brahmahatyāyāḥ kṣatriyasya vadhe smṛtaḥ  |
vaiśye'ṣṭamāṃśo vṛttasthe śūdre jñeyastu ṣoḍaśaḥ  || 11.126[125 ] ||
akāmatastu rājanyaṃ vinipātya dvijottamaḥ  |
vṛṣabhaikasahasrā gā dadyātsucaritavrataḥ  || 11.127[126 ] ||
tryabdaṃ caredvā niyato jaṭī brahmahaṇo vratam  |
vasan dūratare grāmādvṛkṣamūlaniketanaḥ  || 11.128[127 ] ||
etadeva caredabdaṃ prāyaścittaṃ dvijottamaḥ  |
pramāpya vaiśyaṃ vṛttasthaṃ dadyāccaikaśataṃ gavām  || 11.129[128 ] ||
etadeva vrataṃ kṛtsnaṃ ṣaṇmāsāñśūdrahā caret |
vṛṣabhaikādaśā vāpi dadyādviprāya gāḥ sitāḥ  || 11.130[129 ] ||
mārjāranakulau hatvā cāṣaṃ maṇḍūkameva ca  |
śvagodholūkakākāṃśca śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || 11.131[130 ] ||
payaḥ pibettrirātraṃ vā yojanaṃ vādhvano vrajet |
upaspṛśetsravantyāṃ vā sūktaṃ vābdaivataṃ japet || 11.132[131 ] ||
abhriṃ kārṣṇāyasīṃ dadyātsarpaṃ hatvā dvijottamaḥ  |
palālabhārakaṃ ṣaṇḍhe saisakaṃ caikamāṣakam  || 11.133[132 ] ||
ghṛtakumbhaṃ varāhe tu tiladroṇaṃ tu tittirau  |
śuke dvihāyanaṃ vatsaṃ krauñcaṃ hatvā trihāyanam  || 11.134[133 ] ||
hatvā haṃsaṃ balākāṃ ca bakaṃ barhiṇameva ca  |
vānaraṃ śyenabhāsau ca sparśayedbrāhmaṇāya gām  || 11.135[134 ] ||
vāso dadyāddhayaṃ hatvā pañca nīlān vṛṣān gajam  |
ajameṣāvanaḍvāhaṃ kharaṃ hatvaikahāyanam  || 11.136[135 ] ||
kravyādāṃstu mṛgān hatvā dhenuṃ dadyātpayasvinīm  |
akravyādān vatsatarīmuṣṭraṃ hatvā tu kṛṣṇalam  || 11.137[136 ] ||
jīnakārmukabastāvīn pṛthagdadyādviśuddhaye  |
caturṇāmapi varṇānāṃ nārīrhatvānavasthitāḥ  || 11.138[137 ] ||
dānena vadhanirṇekaṃ sarpādīnāmaśaknuvan  |
ekaikaśaścaretkṛcchraṃ dvijaḥ pāpāpanuttaye  || 11.139[138 ] ||
asthimatāṃ tu sattvānāṃ sahasrasya pramāpaṇe  |
pūrṇe cānasyanasthnāṃ tu śūdrahatyāvrataṃ caret || 11.140[139 ] ||
kiṃ cideva tu viprāya dadyādasthimatāṃ vadhe  |
anasthnāṃ caiva hiṃsāyāṃ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati  || 11.141[140 ] ||
phaladānāṃ tu vṛkṣāṇāṃ chedane japyamṛkśatam  |
gulmavallīlatānāṃ ca puṣpitānāṃ ca vīrudhām  || 11.142[141 ] ||
annādyajānāṃ sattvānāṃ rasajānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  |
phalapuṣpodbhavānāṃ ca ghṛtaprāśo viśodhanam  || 11.143[142 ] ||
kṛṣṭajānāmoṣadhīnāṃ jātānāṃ ca svayaṃ vane  |
vṛthālambhe'nugacchedgāṃ dinamekaṃ payovrataḥ  || 11.144[143 ] ||
etairvratairapohyaṃ syādeno hiṃsāsamudbhavam  |
jñānājñānakṛtaṃ kṛtsnaṃ śṛṇutānādyabhakṣaṇe  || 11.145[144 ] ||
ajñānādvāruṇīṃ pītvā saṃskāreṇaiva śudhyati  |
matipūrvamanirdeśyaṃ prāṇāntikamiti sthitiḥ  || 11.146[145 ] ||
apaḥ surābhājanasthā madyabhāṇḍasthitāstathā  |
pañcarātraṃ pibetpītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīśṛtaṃ payaḥ  || 11.147[146 ] ||
spṛṣṭva dattvā ca madirāṃ vidhivatpratigṛhya ca  |
śūdrocchiṣṭāśca pītvāpaḥ kuśavāri pibettryaham  || 11.148[147 ] ||
brāhmaṇastu surāpasya gandhamāghrāya somapaḥ  |
prāṇānapsu trirāyamya ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati  || 11.149[148 ] ||
ajñānātprāśya viṇmūtraṃ surāsaṃspṛṣṭameva ca  |
punaḥ saṃskāramarhanti trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ  || 11.150[149 ] ||
vapanaṃ mekhalā daṇḍo bhaikṣacaryā vratāni ca  |
nivartante dvijātīnāṃ punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi  || 11.151[150 ] ||
abhojyānāṃ tu bhuktvānnaṃ strīśūdrocchiṣṭameva ca  |
jagdhvā māṃsamabhakṣyaṃ ca saptarātraṃ yavān pibet || 11.152[151 ] ||
śuktāni ca kaṣāyāṃśca pītvā medhyānyapi dvijaḥ  |
tāvadbhavatyaprayato yāvattanna vrajatyadhaḥ  || 11.153[152 ] ||
viḍvarāhakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gomāyoḥ kapikākayoḥ  |
prāśya mūtrapurīṣāṇi dvijaścāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 11.154[153 ] ||
śuṣkāṇi bhuktvā māṃsāni bhaumāni kavakāni ca  |
ajñātaṃ caiva sūnāsthametadeva vrataṃ caret || 11.155[154 ] ||
kravyādasūkaroṣṭrāṇāṃ kukkuṭānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe  |
narakākakharāṇāṃ ca taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam  || 11.156[155 ] ||
māsikānnaṃ tu yo'śnīyādasamāvartako dvijaḥ  |
sa trīṇyahānyupavasedekāhaṃ codake vaset || 11.157[156 ] ||
brahmacārī tu yo'śnīyānmadhu māṃsaṃ kathaṃ cana  |
sa kṛtvā prākṛtaṃ kṛcchraṃ vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet || 11.158[157 ] ||
biḍālakākākhūcchiṣṭaṃ jagdhvā śvanakulasya ca  |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca pibedbrahmasuvarcalām  || 11.159[158 ] ||
abhojyamannaṃ nāttavyamātmanaḥ śuddhimicchatā  |
ajñānabhuktaṃ tūttāryaṃ śodhyaṃ vāpyāśu śodhanaiḥ  || 11.160[159 ] ||
eṣo'nādyādanasyokto vratānāṃ vividho vidhiḥ  |
steyadoṣāpahartṝṇāṃ vratānāṃ śrūyatāṃ vidhiḥ  || 11.161[160 ] ||
dhānyānnadhanacauryāṇi kṛtvā kāmāddvijottamaḥ  |
svajātīyagṛhādeva kṛcchrābdena viśudhyati  || 11.162[161 ] ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ tu haraṇe strīṇāṃ kṣetragṛhasya ca  |
kūpavāpījalānāṃ ca śuddhiścāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 11.163[162 ] ||
dravyāṇāmalpasārāṇāṃ steyaṃ kṛtvānyaveśmataḥ  |
caretsāṃtapanaṃ kṛcchraṃ tanniryātyātmaśuddhaye  || 11.164[163 ] ||
bhakṣyabhojyāpaharaṇe yānaśayyāsanasya ca  |
puṣpamūlaphalānāṃ ca pañcagavyaṃ viśodhanam  || 11.165[164 ] ||
tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāṃ ca śuṣkānnasya guḍasya ca  |
celacarmāmiṣāṇāṃ ca trirātraṃ syādabhojanam  || 11.166[165 ] ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ tāmrasya rajatasya ca  |
ayaḥkāṃsyopalānāṃ ca dvādaśāhaṃ kaṇānnatā  || 11.167[166 ] ||
kārpāsakīṭajorṇānāṃ dviśaphaikaśaphasya ca  |
pakṣigandhauṣadhīnāṃ ca rajjvāścaiva tryahaṃ payaḥ  || 11.168[167 ] ||
etairvratairapoheta pāpaṃ steyakṛtaṃ dvijaḥ  |
agamyāgamanīyaṃ tu vratairebhirapānudet || 11.169[168 ] ||
gurutalpavrataṃ kuryādretaḥ siktvā svayoniṣu  |
sakhyuḥ putrasya ca strīṣu kumārīṣvantyajāsu ca  || 11.170[169 ] ||
paitṛsvaseyīṃ bhaginīṃ svasrīyāṃ mātureva ca  |
mātuśca bhrātustanayāṃ gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || 11.171[170 ] ||
etāstisrastu bhāryārthe nopayacchettu buddhimān  |
jñātitvenānupeyāstāḥ patati hyupayannadhaḥ  || 11.172[171 ] ||
amānuṣīṣū puruṣa udakyāyāmayoniṣu  |
retaḥ siktvā jale caiva kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ caret || 11.173[172 ] ||
maithunaṃ tu samāsevya puṃsi yoṣiti vā dvijaḥ  |
goyāne'psu divā caiva savāsāḥ snānamācaret || 11.174[173 ] ||
caṇḍālāntyastriyo gatvā bhuktvā ca pratigṛhya ca  |
patatyajñānato vipro jñānātsāmyaṃ tu gacchati  || 11.175[174 ] ||
vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ bhartā nirundhyādekaveśmani  |
yatpuṃsaḥ paradāreṣu taccaināṃ cārayedvratam  || 11.176[175 ] ||
sā cetpunaḥ praduṣyettu sadṛśenopamantritā  |
kṛcchraṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ caiva tadasyāḥ pāvanaṃ smṛtam  || 11.177[176 ] ||
yatkarotyekarātreṇa vṛṣalīsevanāddvijaḥ  |
tadbhaikṣabhugjapannityaṃ tribhirvarṣairvyapohati  || 11.178[177 ] ||
eṣā pāpakṛtāmuktā caturṇāmapi niṣkṛtiḥ  |
patitaiḥ saṃprayuktānāmimāḥ śṛṇuta niṣkṛtīḥ  || 11.179[178 ] ||
saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran  |
yājanādhyāpanādyaunānna tu yānāsanāśanāt || 11.180[179 ] ||
yo yena patitenaiṣāṃ saṃsargaṃ yāti mānavaḥ  |
sa tasyaiva vrataṃ kuryāttatsaṃsargaviśuddhaye  || 11.181[180 ] ||
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍairbāndhavairbahiḥ  |
nindite'hani sāyāhne jñātirtviggurusaṃnidhau  || 11.182[181 ] ||
dāsī ghaṭamapāṃ pūrṇaṃ paryasyetpretavatpadā  |
ahorātramupāsīrannaśaucaṃ bāndhavaiḥ saha  || 11.183[182 ] ||
nivarteraṃśca tasmāttu saṃbhāṣaṇasahāsane  |
dāyādyasya pradānaṃ ca yātrā caiva hi laukikī  || 11.184[183 ] ||
jyeṣṭhatā ca nivarteta jyeṣṭhāvāpyaṃ ca yaddhanam  |
jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ prāpnuyāccāsya yavīyān guṇato'dhikaḥ  || 11.185[184 ] ||
prāyaścitte tu carite pūrṇakumbhamapāṃ navam  |
tenaiva sārdhaṃ prāsyeyuḥ snātvā puṇye jalāśaye  || 11.186[185 ] ||
sa tvapsu taṃ ghaṭaṃ prāsya praviśya bhavanaṃ svakam  |
sarvāṇi jñātikāryāṇi yathāpūrvaṃ samācaret || 11.187[186 ] ||
etadeva vidhiṃ kuryādyoṣitsu patitāsvapi  |
vastrānnapānaṃ deyaṃ tu vaseyuśca gṛhāntike  || 11.188[187 ] ||
enasvibhiranirṇiktairnārthaṃ kiṃ citsahācaret |
kṛtanirṇejanāṃścaiva na jugupseta karhi cit || 11.189[188 ] ||
bālaghnāṃśca kṛtaghnāṃśca viśuddhānapi dharmataḥ  |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃśca strīhantṝṃśca na saṃvaset || 11.190[189 ] ||
yeṣāṃ dvijānāṃ sāvitrī nānūcyeta yathāvidhi  |
tāṃścārayitvā trīn kṛcchrān yathāvidhyupanāyayet || 11.191[190 ] ||
prāyaścittaṃ cikīrṣanti vikarmasthāstu ye dvijāḥ  |
brahmaṇā ca parityaktāsteṣāmapyetadādiśet || 11.192[191 ] ||
yadgarhitenārjayanti karmaṇā brāhmaṇā dhanam  |
tasyotsargeṇa śudhyanti japyena tapasaiva ca  || 11.193[192 ] ||
japitvā trīṇi sāvitryāḥ sahasrāṇi samāhitaḥ  |
māsaṃ goṣṭhe payaḥ pītvā mucyate'satpratigrahāt || 11.194[193 ] ||
upavāsakṛśaṃ taṃ tu govrajātpunarāgatam  |
praṇataṃ prati pṛccheyuḥ sāmyaṃ saumyecchasīti kim  || 11.195[194 ] ||
satyamuktvā tu vipreṣu vikiredyavasaṃ gavām  |
gobhiḥ pravartite tīrthe kuryustasya parigraham  || 11.196[195 ] ||
vrātyānāṃ yājanaṃ kṛtvā pareṣāmantyakarma ca  |
abhicāramahīnaṃ ca tribhiḥ kṛcchrairvyapohati  || 11.197[196 ] ||
śaraṇāgataṃ parityajya vedaṃ viplāvya ca dvijaḥ  |
saṃvatsaraṃ yavāhārastatpāpamapasedhati  || 11.198[197 ] ||
śvaśṛgālakharairdaṣṭo grāmyaiḥ kravyādbhireva ca  |
narāśvoṣṭravarāhaiśca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati   || 11.199[198 ] ||
ṣaṣṭhānnakālatā māsaṃ saṃhitājapa eva vā  |
homāśca sakalā nityamapāṅktyānāṃ viśodhanam  || 11.200[199 ] ||
uṣṭrayānaṃ samāruhya kharayānaṃ tu kāmataḥ  |
snātvā tu vipro digvāsāḥ prāṇāyāmena śudhyati  || 11.201[200 ] ||
vinādbhirapsu vāpyārtaḥ śārīraṃ saṃniṣevya ca  |
sacailo bahirāplutya gāmālabhya viśudhyati  || 11.202[201 ] ||
vedoditānāṃ nityānāṃ karmaṇāṃ samatikrame  |
snātakavratalope ca prāyaścittamabhojanam  || 11.203[202 ] ||
huṅkāraṃ brāhmaṇasyoktvā tvamkāraṃ ca garīyasaḥ  |
snātvānaśnannahaḥ śeṣamabhivādya prasādayet || 11.204[203 ] ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi kaṇṭhe vābadhya vāsasā  |
vivāde vā vinirjitya praṇipatya prasādayet || 11.205[204 ] ||
avagūrya tvabdaśataṃ sahasramabhihatya ca  |
jighāṃsayā brāhmaṇasya narakaṃ pratipadyate  || 11.206[205 ] ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāti mahītale  |
tāvantyabdasahasrāṇi tatkartā narake vaset || 11.207[206 ] ||
avagūrya caretkṛcchramatikṛcchraṃ nipātane  |
kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kurvīta viprasyotpādya śoṇitam  || 11.208[207 ] ||
anuktaniṣkṛtīnāṃ tu pāpānāmapanuttaye  |
śaktiṃ cāvekṣya pāpaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ prakalpayet || 11.209[208 ] ||
yairabhyupāyairenāṃsi mānavo vyapakarṣati  |
tān vo'bhyupāyān vakṣyāmi devarṣipitṛsevitān  || 11.210[209 ] ||
tryahaṃ prātastryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryahamadyādayācitam  |
tryahaṃ paraṃ ca nāśnīyātprājāpatyaṃ caran dvijaḥ  || 11.211[210 ] ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam  |
ekarātropavāsaśca kṛcchraṃ sāṃtapanaṃ smṛtam  || 11.212[211 ] ||
ekaikaṃ grāsamaśnīyāttryahāṇi trīṇi pūrvavat |
tryahaṃ copavasedantyamatikṛcchraṃ caran dvijaḥ  || 11.213[212 ] ||
taptakṛcchraṃ caran vipro jalakṣīraghṛtānilān  |
pratitryahaṃ pibeduṣṇān sakṛtsnāyī samāhitaḥ  || 11.214[213 ] ||
yatātmano'pramattasya dvādaśāhamabhojanam  |
parāko nāma kṛcchro'yaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanaḥ  || 11.215[214 ] ||
ekaikaṃ hrāsayetpiṇḍaṃ kṛṣṇe śukle ca vardhayet |
upaspṛśaṃstriṣavaṇametaccāṇdrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 11.216[215 ] ||
etameva vidhiṃ kṛtsnamācaredyavamadhyame  |
śuklapakṣādiniyataścaraṃścāndrāyaṇaṃ vratam  || 11.217[216 ] ||
aṣṭāvaṣṭau samaśnīyātpiṇḍānmadhyaṃdine sthite  |
niyatātmā haviṣyāśī yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ caran  || 11.218[217 ] ||
caturaḥ prātaraśnīyātpiṇḍān vipraḥ samāhitaḥ  |
caturo'stamite sūrye śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ smṛtam  || 11.219[218 ] ||
yathā kathaṃ citpiṇḍānāṃ tisro'śītīḥ samāhitaḥ  |
māsenāśnan haviṣyasya candrasyaiti salokatām  || 11.220[219 ] ||
etadrudrāstathādityā vasavaścācaran vratam  |
sarvākuśalamokṣāya marutaśca maharṣibhiḥ  || 11.221[220 ] ||
mahāvyāhṛtibhirhomaḥ kartavyaḥ svayamanvaham  |
ahiṃsā satyamakrodhamārjavaṃ ca samācaret || 11.222[221 ] ||
trirahnastrirniśāyāṃ ca savāsā jalamāviśet |
strīśūdrapatitāṃścaiva nābhibhāṣeta karhi cit || 11.223[222 ] ||
sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharedaśakto'dhaḥ śayīta vā  |
brahmacārī vratī ca syādgurudevadvijārcakaḥ  || 11.224[223 ] ||
sāvitrīṃ ca japennityaṃ pavitrāṇi ca śaktitaḥ  |
sarveṣveva vrateṣvevaṃ prāyaścittārthamādṛtaḥ  || 11.225[224 ] ||
etairdvijātayaḥ śodhyā vratairāviṣkṛtainasaḥ  |
anāviṣkṛtapāpāṃstu mantrairhomaiśca śodhayet || 11.226[225 ] ||
khyāpanenānutāpena tapasādhyayanena ca  |
pāpakṛnmucyate pāpāttathā dānena cāpadi  || 11.227[226 ] ||
yathā yathā naro'dharmaṃ svayaṃ kṛtvānubhāṣate  |
tathā tathā tvacevāhistenādharmeṇa mucyate  || 11.228[227 ] ||
yathā yathā manastasya duṣkṛtaṃ karma garhati  |
tathā tathā śarīraṃ tattenādharmeṇa mucyate  || 11.229[228 ] ||
kṛtvā pāpaṃ hi saṃtapya tasmātpāpātpramucyate  |
naivaṃ kuryāṃ punariti nivṛttyā pūyate tu saḥ  || 11.230[229 ] ||
evaṃ saṃcintya manasā pretya karmaphalodayam  |
manovāṅgūrtibhirnityaṃ śubhaṃ karma samācaret || 11.231[230 ] ||
ajñānādyadi vā jñānātkṛtvā karma vigarhitam  |
tasmādvimuktimanvicchan dvitīyaṃ na samācaret || 11.232[231 ] ||
yasmin karmaṇyasya kṛte manasaḥ syādalāghavam  |
tasmiṃstāvattapaḥ kuryādyāvattuṣṭikaraṃ bhavet || 11.233[232 ] ||
tapomūlamidaṃ sarvaṃ daivamānuṣakaṃ sukham  |
tapomadhyaṃ budhaiḥ proktaṃ tapo'ntaṃ vedadarśibhiḥ  || 11.234[233 ] ||
brāhmaṇasya tapo jñānaṃ tapaḥ kṣatrasya rakṣaṇam  |
vaiśyasya tu tapo vārtā tapaḥ śūdrasya sevanam  || 11.235[234 ] ||
ṛṣayaḥ saṃyatātmānaḥ phalamūlānilāśanāḥ  |
tapasaiva prapaśyanti trailokyaṃ sacarācaram  || 11.236[235 ] ||
auṣadhānyagado vidyā daivī ca vividhā sthitiḥ  |
tapasaiva prasidhyanti tapasteṣāṃ hi sādhanam  || 11.237[236 ] ||
yaddustaraṃ yaddurāpaṃ yaddurgaṃ yacca duṣkaram  |
sarvaṃ tu tapasā sādhyaṃ tapo hi duratikramam  || 11.238[237 ] ||
mahāpātakinaścaiva śeṣāścākāryakāriṇaḥ  |
tapasaiva sutaptena mucyante kilbiṣāttataḥ  || 11.239[238 ] ||
kītāścāhipataṃgāśca paśavaśca vayāṃsi ca  |
sthāvarāṇi ca bhūtāni divaṃ yānti tapobalāt || 11.240[239 ] ||
yatkiṃ cidenaḥ kurvanti manovāṅgūrtibhirjanāḥ  |
tatsarvaṃ nirdahantyāśu tapasaiva tapodhanāḥ  || 11.241[240 ] ||
tapasaiva viśuddhasya brāhmaṇasya divaukasaḥ  |
ijyāśca pratigṛhṇanti kāmān saṃvardhayanti ca  || 11.242[241 ] ||
prajāpatiridaṃ śāstraṃ tapasaivāsṛjatprabhuḥ  |
tathaiva vedānṛṣayastapasā pratipedire  || 11.243[242 ] ||
ityetattapaso devā mahābhāgyaṃ pracakṣate  |
sarvasyāsya prapaśyantastapasaḥ puṇyamuttamam  || 11.244[243 ] ||
vedābhyāso'nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyā kṣamā  |
nāśayantyāśu pāpāni mahāpātakajānyapi  || 11.245[244 ] ||
yathaidhastejasā vahniḥ prāptaṃ nirdahati kṣaṇāt |
tathā jñānāgninā pāpaṃ sarvaṃ dahati vedavit || 11.246[245 ] ||
ityetadenasāmuktaṃ prāyaścittaṃ yathāvidhi  |
ata ūrdhvaṃ rahasyānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ nibodhata  || 11.247[246 ] ||
savyāhṛtipraṇavakāḥ prāṇāyāmāstu ṣoḍaśa  |
api bhrūṇahanaṃ māsātpunantyaharahaḥ kṛtāḥ  || 11.248[247 ] ||
kautsaṃ japtvāpa ityetadvasiṣṭhaṃ ca pratītyṛcam  |
māhitraṃ śuddhavatyaśca surāpo'pi viśudhyati  || 11.249[248 ] ||
sakṛjjaptvāsyavāmīyaṃ śivasaṃkalpameva ca  |
apahṛtya suvarṇaṃ tu kṣaṇādbhavati nirmalaḥ  || 11.250[249 ] ||
haviṣpāntīyamabhyasya na tamaṃ ha itīti ca  |
japitvā pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ mucyate gurutalpagaḥ  || 11.251[250 ] ||
enasāṃ sthūlasūkṣmāṇāṃ cikīrṣannapanodanam  |
avetyṛcaṃ japedabdaṃ yatkiṃ cedamitīti vā  || 11.252[251 ] ||
pratigṛhyāpratigrāhyaṃ bhuktvā cānnaṃ vigarhitam  |
japaṃstaratsamandīyaṃ pūyate mānavastryahāt || 11.253[252 ] ||
somāraudraṃ tu bahvenāḥ māsamabhyasya śudhyati  |
sravantyāmācaran snānamaryamṇāmiti ca tṛcam  || 11.254[253 ] ||
abdārdhamindramityetadenasvī saptakaṃ japet |
apraśastaṃ tu kṛtvāpsu māsamāsīta bhaikṣabhuk || 11.255[254 ] ||
mantraiḥ śākalahomīyairabdaṃ hutvā ghṛtaṃ dvijaḥ  |
sugurvapyapahantyeno japtvā vā nama ityṛcam  || 11.256[255 ] ||
mahāpātakasaṃyukto'nugacchedgāḥ samāhitaḥ  |
abhyasyābdaṃ pāvamānīrbhaikṣāhāro viśudhyati  || 11.257[256 ] ||
araṇye vā trirabhyasya prayato vedasaṃhitām  |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaiḥ parākaiḥ śodhitastribhiḥ  || 11.258[257 ] ||
tryahaṃ tūpavasedyuktastrirahno'bhyupayannapaḥ  |
mucyate pātakaiḥ sarvaistrirjapitvāghamarṣaṇam  || 11.259[258 ] ||
yathāśvamedhaḥ kraturāṭsarvapāpāpanodanaḥ  |
tathāghamarṣaṇaṃ sūktaṃ sarvapāpāpanodanam  || 11.260[259 ] ||
hatvā lokānapīmāṃstrīnaśnannapi yatastataḥ  |
ṛgvedaṃ dhārayan vipro nainaḥ prāpnoti kiṃ cana  || 11.261[260 ] ||
ṛksaṃhitāṃ trirabhyasya yajuṣāṃ vā samāhitaḥ  |
sāmnāṃ vā sarahasyānāṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate  || 11.262[261 ] ||
yathā mahāhradaṃ prāpya kṣiptaṃ loṣṭaṃ vinaśyati  |
tathā duścaritaṃ sarvaṃ vede trivṛti majjati  || 11.263[262 ] ||
ṛco yajūṃṣi cānyāni sāmāni vividhāni ca  |
eṣa jñeyastrivṛdvedo yo vedainaṃ sa vedavit || 11.264[263 ] ||
ādyaṃ yattryakṣaraṃ brahma trayī yasmin pratiṣṭhitā  |
sa guhyo'nyastrivṛdvedo yastaṃ veda sa vedavit || 11.265[264 ] ||

cāturvarṇyasya kṛtsno'yamukto dharmastvayānaghaḥ  |
karmaṇāṃ phalanirvṛttiṃ śaṃsa nastattvataḥ parām  || 12.1 ||
sa tānuvāca dharmātmā maharṣīnmānavo bhṛguḥ  |
asya sarvasya śṛṇuta karmayogasya nirṇayam  || 12.2 ||
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ karma manovāgdehasaṃbhavam  |
karmajā gatayo nṝṇāmuttamādhamamadhyamaḥ  || 12.3 ||
tasyeha trividhasyāpi tryadhiṣṭhānasya dehinaḥ  |
daśalakṣaṇayuktasya mano vidyātpravartakam  || 12.4 ||
paradravyeṣvabhidhyānaṃ manasāniṣṭacintanam  |
vitathābhiniveśaśca trividhaṃ karma mānasam  || 12.5 ||
pāruṣyamanṛtaṃ caiva paiśunyaṃ cāpi sarvaśaḥ  |
asaṃbaddhapralāpaśca vāṅmayaṃ syāccaturvidham  || 12.6 ||
adattānāmupādānaṃ hiṃsā caivāvidhānataḥ  |
paradāropasevā ca śārīraṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam  || 12.7 ||
mānasaṃ manasaivāyamupabhuṅkte śubhāśubham  |
vācā vācā kṛtaṃ karma kāyenaiva ca kāyikam  || 12.8 ||
śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣairyāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ  |
vācikaiḥ pakṣimṛgatāṃ mānasairantyajātitām  || 12.9 ||
vāgdaṇḍo'tha manodaṇḍaḥ kāyadaṇḍastathaiva ca  |
yasyaite nihitā buddhau tridaṇḍīti sa ucyate  || 12.10 ||
tridaṇḍametannikṣipya sarvabhūteṣu mānavaḥ  |
kāmakrodhau tu saṃyamya tataḥ siddhiṃ niyacchati  || 12.11 ||
yo'syātmanaḥ kārayitā taṃ kṣetrajñaṃ pracakṣate  |
yaḥ karoti tu karmāṇi sa bhūtātmocyate budhaiḥ  || 12.12 ||
jīvasaṃjño'ntarātmānyaḥ sahajaḥ sarvadehinām  |
yena vedayate sarvaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca janmasu  || 12.13 ||
tāvubhau bhūtasaṃpṛktau mahān kṣetrajña eva ca  |
uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu sthitaṃ taṃ vyāpya tiṣṭhataḥ  || 12.14 ||
asaṃkhyā mūrtayastasya niṣpatanti śarīrataḥ  |
uccāvacāni bhūtāni satataṃ ceṣṭayanti yāḥ  || 12.15 ||
pañcabhya eva mātrābhyaḥ pretya duṣkṛtināṃ nṛṇām  |
śarīraṃ yātanārthīyamanyadutpadyate dhruvam  || 12.16 ||
tenānubhūya tā yāmīḥ śarīreṇeha yātanāḥ  |
tāsveva bhūtamātrāsu pralīyante vibhāgaśaḥ  || 12.17 ||
so'nubhūyāsukhodarkān doṣān viṣayasaṅgajān  |
vyapetakalmaṣo'bhyeti tāvevobhau mahaujasau  || 12.18 ||
tau dharmaṃ paśyatastasya pāpaṃ cātandritau saha  |
yābhyāṃ prāpnoti saṃpṛktaḥ pretyeha ca sukhāsukham  || 12.19 ||
yadyācarati dharmaṃ sa prāyaśo'dharmamalpaśaḥ  |
taireva cāvṛto bhūtaiḥ svarge sukhamupāśnute  || 12.20 ||
yadi tu prāyaśo'dharmaṃ sevate dharmamalpaśaḥ  |
tairbhūtaiḥ sa parityakto yāmīḥ prāpnoti yātanāḥ  || 12.21 ||
yāmīstā yātanāḥ prāpya sa jīvo vītakalmaṣaḥ  |
tānyeva pañca bhūtāni punarapyeti bhāgaśaḥ  || 12.22 ||
etā dṛṣṭvāsya jīvasya gatīḥ svenaiva cetasā  |
dharmato'dharmataścaiva dharme dadhyātsadā manaḥ  || 12.23 ||
sattvaṃ rajastamaścaiva trīn vidyādātmano guṇān  |
yairvyāpyemān sthito bhāvānmahān sarvānaśeṣataḥ  || 12.24 ||
yo yadaiṣāṃ guṇo dehe sākalyenātiricyate  |
sa tadā tadguṇaprāyaṃ taṃ karoti śarīriṇam  || 12.25 ||
sattvaṃ jñānaṃ tamo'jñānaṃ rāgadveṣau rajaḥ smṛtam  |
etadvyāptimadeteṣāṃ sarvabhūtāśritaṃ vapuḥ  || 12.26 ||
tatra yatprītisaṃyuktaṃ kiṃ cidātmani lakṣayet |
praśāntamiva śuddhābhaṃ sattvaṃ tadupadhārayet || 12.27 ||
yattu duḥkhasamāyuktamaprītikaramātmanaḥ  |
tadrajo pratīpaṃ vidyātsatataṃ hāri dehinām  || 12.28 ||
yattu syānmohasaṃyuktamavyaktaṃ viṣayātmakam  |
apratarkyamavijñeyaṃ tamastadupadhārayet || 12.29 ||
trayāṇāmapi caiteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ yaḥ phalodayaḥ  |
agryo madhyo jaghanyaśca taṃ pravakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ  || 12.30 ||
vedābhyāsastapo jñānaṃ śaucamindriyanigrahaḥ  |
dharmakriyātmacintā ca sāttvikaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam  || 12.31 ||
ārambharucitādhairyamasatkāryaparigrahaḥ  |
viṣayopasevā cājasraṃ rājasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam  || 12.32 ||
lobhaḥ svapno'dhṛtiḥ krauryaṃ nāstikyaṃ bhinnavṛttitā  |
yāciṣṇutā pramādaśca tāmasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam  || 12.33 ||
trayāṇāmapi caiteṣāṃ guṇānāṃ triṣu tiṣṭhatām  |
idaṃ sāmāsikaṃ jñeyaṃ kramaśo guṇalakṣaṇam  || 12.34 ||
yatkarma kṛtvā kurvaṃśca kariṣyaṃścaiva lajjati  |
tajjñeyaṃ viduṣā sarvaṃ tāmasaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam  || 12.35 ||
yenāsmin karmanā loke khyātimicchati puṣkalām  |
na ca śocatyasaṃpattau tadvijñeyaṃ tu rājasam  || 12.36 ||
yatsarveṇecchati jñātuṃ yanna lajjati cācaran  |
yena tuṣyati cātmāsya tatsattvaguṇalakṣaṇam  || 12.37 ||
tamaso lakṣaṇaṃ kāmo rajasastvartha ucyate  |
sattvasya lakṣaṇaṃ dharmaḥ śraiṣṭhyameṣāṃ yathottaram  || 12.38 ||
yena yastu guṇenaiṣāṃ saṃsarān pratipadyate  |
tān samāsena vakṣyāmi sarvasyāsya yathākramam  || 12.39 ||
devatvaṃ sāttvikā yānti manuṣyatvaṃ ca rājasāḥ  |
tiryaktvaṃ tāmasā nityamityeṣā trividhā gatiḥ  || 12.40 ||
trividhā trividhaiṣā tu vijñeyā gauṇikī gatiḥ  |
adhamā madhyamāgryā ca karmavidyāviśeṣataḥ  || 12.41 ||
sthāvarāḥ kṛmikīṭāśca matsyāḥ sarpāḥ sakacchapāḥ  |
paśavaśca mṛgāścaiva jaghanyā tāmasī gatiḥ  || 12.42 ||
hastinaśca turaṅgāśca śūdrā mlecchāśca garhitāḥ  |
siṃhā vyāghrā varāhāśca madhyamā tāmasī gatiḥ  || 12.43 ||
cāraṇāśca suparṇāśca puruṣāścaiva dāmbhikāḥ  |
rakṣāṃsi ca piśācāśca tāmasīṣūttamā gatiḥ  || 12.44 ||
jhallā mallā naṭāścaiva puruṣāḥ śastravṛttayaḥ  |
dyūtapānaprasaktāśca jaghanyā rājasī gatiḥ  || 12.45 ||
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāścaiva rājñāṃ caiva purohitāḥ  |
vādayuddhapradhānāśca madhyamā rājasī gatiḥ  || 12.46 ||
gandharvā guhyakā yakṣā vibudhānucarāśca ye  |
tathaivāpsarasaḥ sarvā rājasīṣūttamā gatiḥ  || 12.47 ||
tāpasā yatayo viprā ye ca vaimānikā gaṇāḥ  |
nakṣatrāṇi ca daityāśca prathamā sāttvikī gatiḥ  || 12.48 ||
yajvāna ṛṣayo devā vedā jyotīṃṣi vatsarāḥ  |
pitaraścaiva sādhyāśca dvitīyā sāttvikī gatiḥ  || 12.49 ||
brahmā viśvasṛjo dharmo mahānavyaktameva ca  |
uttamāṃ sāttvikīmetāṃ gatimāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ  || 12.50 ||
eṣa sarvaḥ samuddiṣṭastriprakārasya karmaṇaḥ  |
trividhastrividhaḥ kṛtsnaḥ saṃsāraḥ sārvabhautikaḥ  || 12.51 ||
indriyāṇāṃ prasaṅgena dharmasyāsevanena ca  |
pāpān saṃyānti saṃsārānavidvāṃso narādhamāḥ  || 12.52 ||
yāṃ yāṃ yoniṃ tu jīvo'yaṃ yena yeneha karmaṇā  |
kramaśo yāti loke'smiṃstattatsarvaṃ nibodhata  || 12.53 ||
bahūn varṣagaṇān ghorānnarakān prāpya tatkṣayāt |
saṃsārān pratipadyante mahāpātakinastvimān  || 12.54 ||
śvasūkarakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ go'jāvimṛgapakṣiṇāṃ  |
caṇḍālapukkasānāṃ ca brahmahā yonimṛcchati  || 12.55 ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgānāṃ viḍbhujāṃ caiva pakṣiṇām  |
hiṃsrāṇāṃ caiva sattvānāṃ surāpo brāhmaṇo vrajet || 12.56 ||
lūtāhisaraṭānāṃ ca tiraścāṃ cāmbucāriṇām  |
hiṃsrāṇāṃ ca piśācānāṃ steno vipraḥ sahasraśaḥ  || 12.57 ||
tṛṇagulmalatānāṃ ca kravyādāṃ daṃṣṭriṇāmapi  |
krūrakarmakṛtāṃ caiva śataśo gurutalpagaḥ  || 12.58 ||
hiṃsrā bhavanti kravyādāḥ kṛmayo'medhyabhakṣiṇaḥ  |
parasparādinaḥ stenāḥ pretyāntyastrīniṣeviṇaḥ  || 12.59 ||
saṃyogaṃ patitairgatvā parasyaiva ca yoṣitam  |
apahṛtya ca viprasvaṃ bhavati brahmarākṣasaḥ  || 12.60 ||
maṇimuktāpravālāni hṛtvā lobhena mānavaḥ  |
vividhāṇi ca ratnāni jāyate hemakartṛṣu  || 12.61 ||
dhānyaṃ hṛtvā bhavatyākhuḥ kāṃsyaṃ haṃso jalaṃ plavaḥ  |
madhu daṃśaḥ payaḥ kāko rasaṃ śvā nakulo ghṛtam  || 12.62 ||
māṃsaṃ gṛdhro vapāṃ madgustailaṃ tailapakaḥ khagaḥ  |
cīrīvākastu lavaṇaṃ balākā śakunirdadhi  || 12.63 ||
kauśeyaṃ tittirirhṛtvā kṣaumaṃ hṛtvā tu darduraḥ  |
kārpāsatāntavaṃ krauñco godhā gāṃ vāggudo guḍam  || 12.64 ||
chucchundariḥ śubhān gandhān patraśākaṃ tu barhiṇaḥ  |
śvāvitkṛtānnaṃ vividhamakṛtānnaṃ tu śalyakaḥ  || 12.65 ||
bako bhavati hṛtvāgniṃ gṛhakārī hyupaskaram  |
raktāni hṛtvā vāsāṃsi jāyate jīvajīvakaḥ  || 12.66 ||
vṛko mṛgebhaṃ vyāghro'śvaṃ phalamūlaṃ tu markaṭaḥ  |
strīmṛkṣaḥ stokako vāri yānānyuṣṭraḥ paśūnajaḥ  || 12.67 ||
yadvā tadvā paradravyamapahṛtya balānnaraḥ  |
avaśyaṃ yāti tiryaktvaṃ jagdhvā caivāhutaṃ haviḥ  || 12.68 ||
striyo'pyetena kalpena hṛtvā doṣamavāpnuyuḥ  |
eteṣāmeva jantūnāṃ bhāryātvamupayānti tāḥ  || 12.69 ||
svebhyaḥ svebhyastu karmabhyaścyutā varṇā hyanāpadi  |
pāpān saṃsṛtya saṃsārān preṣyatāṃ yānti śatruṣu  || 12.70 ||
vāntāśyulkāmukhaḥ preto vipro dharmātsvakāccyutaḥ  |
amedhyakuṇapāśī ca kṣatriyaḥ kaṭapūtanaḥ  || 12.71 ||
maitrākṣajyotikaḥ preto vaiśyo bhavati pūyabhuk |
cailāśakaśca bhavati śūdro dharmātsvakāccyutaḥ  || 12.72 ||
yathā yathā niṣevante viṣayān viṣayātmakāḥ  |
tathā tathā kuśalatā teṣāṃ teṣūpajāyate  || 12.73 ||
te'bhyāsātkarmaṇāṃ teṣāṃ pāpānāmalpabuddhayaḥ  |
saṃprāpnuvanti duḥkhāni tāsu tāsviha yoniṣu  || 12.74 ||
tāmisrādiṣu cogreṣu narakeṣu vivartanam  |
asipatravanādīni bandhanachedanāni ca  || 12.75 ||
vividhāścaiva saṃpīḍāḥ kākolūkaiśca bhakṣaṇam  |
karambhavālukātāpān kumbhīpākāṃśca dāruṇān  || 12.76 ||
saṃbhavāṃśca viyonīṣu duḥkhaprāyāsu nityaśaḥ  |
śītātapābhighātāṃśca vividhāni bhayāni ca  || 12.77 ||
asakṛdgarbhavāseṣu vāsaṃ janma ca dāruṇam  |
bandhanāni ca kāṣṭhāni parapreṣyatvameva ca  || 12.78 ||
bandhupriyaviyogāṃśca saṃvāsaṃ caiva durjanaiḥ  |
dravyārjanaṃ ca nāśaṃ ca mitrāmitrasya cārjanam  || 12.79 ||
jarāṃ caivāpratīkārāṃ vyādhibhiścopapīḍanam  |
kleśāṃśca vividhāṃstāṃstānmṛtyumeva ca durjayam  || 12.80 ||
yādṛśena tu bhāvena yadyatkarma niṣevate  |
tādṛśena śarīreṇa tattatphalamupāśnute  || 12.81 ||
eṣa sarvaḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ karmaṇāṃ vaḥ phalodayaḥ  |
naiḥśreyasakaraṃ karma viprasyedaṃ nibodhata  || 12.82 ||
vedābhyāsastapo jñānamindriyāṇāṃ ca saṃyamaḥ  |
ahiṃsā gurusevā ca niḥśreyasakaraṃ param  || 12.83 ||
sarveṣāmapi caiteṣāṃ śubhānāmiha karmaṇām  |
kiṃ cicchreyaskarataraṃ karmoktaṃ puruṣaṃ prati  || 12.84 ||
sarveṣāmapi caiteṣāmātmajñānaṃ paraṃ smṛtam  |
taddhyagryaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ prāpyate hyamṛtaṃ tataḥ  || 12.85 ||
ṣaṇṇāmeṣāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ pretya ceha ca  |
śreyaskarataraṃ jñeyaṃ sarvadā karma vaidikam  || 12.86 ||
vaidike karmayoge tu sarvāṇyetānyaśeṣataḥ  |
antarbhavanti kramaśastasmiṃstasmin kriyāvidhau  || 12.87 ||
sukhābhyudayikaṃ caiva naiḥśreyasikameva ca  |
pravṛttaṃ ca nivṛttaṃ ca dvividhaṃ karma vaidikam  || 12.88 ||
iha cāmutra vā kāmyaṃ pravṛttaṃ karma kīrtyate  |
niṣkāmaṃ jñātapūrvaṃ tu nivṛttamupadiśyate  || 12.89 ||
pravṛttaṃ karma saṃsevyaṃ devānāmeti sāmyatām  |
nivṛttaṃ sevamānastu bhūtānyatyeti pañca vai  || 12.90 ||
sarvabhūteṣu cātmānaṃ sarvabhūtāni cātmani  |
samaṃ paśyannātmayājī svārājyamadhigacchati  || 12.91 ||
yathoktānyapi karmāṇi parihāya dvijottamaḥ  |
ātmajñāne śame ca syādvedābhyāse ca yatnavān  || 12.92 ||
etaddhi janmasāphalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya viśeṣataḥ  |
prāpyaitatkṛtakṛtyo hi dvijo bhavati nānyathā  || 12.93 ||
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇāṃ vedaścakṣuḥ sanātanam  |
aśakyaṃ cāprameyaṃ ca vedaśāstramiti sthitiḥ  || 12.94 ||
yā vedabāhyāḥ smṛtayo yāśca kāśca kudṛṣṭayaḥ  |
sarvāstā niṣphalāḥ pretya tamoniṣṭhā hi tāḥ smṛtāḥ  || 12.95 ||
utpadyante cyavante ca yānyato'nyāni kāni cit |
tānyarvākkālikatayā niṣphalānyanṛtāni ca  || 12.96 ||
cāturvarṇyaṃ trayo lokāścatvāraścāśramāḥ pṛthak |
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca sarvaṃ vedātprasidhyati  || 12.97 ||
śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaśca pañcamaḥ  |
vedādeva prasūyante prasūtirguṇakarmataḥ  || 12.98 ||
bibharti sarvabhūtāni vedaśāstraṃ sanātanam  |
tasmādetatparaṃ manye yajjantorasya sādhanam  || 12.99 ||
senāpatyaṃ ca rājyaṃ ca daṇḍanetṛtvameva ca  |
sarvalokādhipatyaṃ ca vedaśāstravidarhati  || 12.100 ||
yathā jātabalo vahnirdahatyārdrānapi drumān  |
tathā dahati vedajñaḥ karmajaṃ doṣamātmanaḥ  || 12.101 ||
vedaśāstrārthatattvajño yatra tatrāśrame vasan  |
ihaiva loke tiṣṭhan sa brahmabhūyāya kalpate  || 12.102 ||
ajñebhyo granthinaḥ śreṣṭhā granthibhyo dhāriṇo varāḥ  |
dhāribhyo jñāninaḥ śreṣṭhā jñānibhyo vyavasāyinaḥ  || 12.103 ||
tapo vidyā ca viprasya niḥśreyasakaraṃ param  |
tapasā kilbiṣaṃ hanti vidyayāmṛtamaśnute  || 12.104 ||
pratyakṣaṃ cānumānaṃ ca śāstraṃ ca vividhāgamam  |
trayaṃ suviditaṃ kāryaṃ dharmaśuddhimabhīpsatā  || 12.105 ||
ārṣaṃ dharmopadeśaṃ ca vedaśāstrāvirodhinā  |
yastarkeṇānusaṃdhatte sa dharmaṃ veda netaraḥ  || 12.106 ||
naiḥśreyasamidaṃ karma yathoditamaśeṣataḥ  |
mānavasyāsya śāstrasya rahasyamupadiśyate  || 12.107 ||
anāmnāteṣu dharmeṣu kathaṃ syāditi cedbhavet |
yaṃ śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā brūyuḥ sa dharmaḥ syādaśaṅkitaḥ  || 12.108 ||
dharmeṇādhigato yaistu vedaḥ saparibṛṃhaṇaḥ  |
te śiṣṭā brāhmaṇā jñeyāḥ śrutipratyakṣahetavaḥ  || 12.109 ||
daśāvarā vā pariṣadyaṃ dharmaṃ parikalpayet |
tryavarā vāpi vṛttasthā taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet || 12.110 ||
traividyo hetukastarkī nairukto dharmapāṭhakaḥ  |
trayaścāśramiṇaḥ pūrve pariṣatsyāddaśāvarā  || 12.111 ||
ṛgvedavidyajurvicca sāmavedavideva ca  |
tryavarā pariṣajjñeyā dharmasaṃśayanirṇaye  || 12.112 ||
eko'pi vedaviddharmaṃ yaṃ vyavasyeddvijottamaḥ  |
sa vijñeyaḥ paro dharmo nājñānāmudito'yutaiḥ  || 12.113 ||
avratānāmamantrāṇāṃ jātimātropajīvinām  |
sahasraśaḥ sametānāṃ pariṣattvaṃ na vidyate  || 12.114 ||
yaṃ vadanti tamobhūtā mūrkhā dharmamatadvidaḥ  |
tatpāpaṃ śatadhā bhūtvā tadvaktṝnanugacchati  || 12.115 ||
etadvo'bhihitaṃ sarvaṃ niḥśreyasakaraṃ param  |
asmādapracyuto vipraḥ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim  || 12.116 ||
evaṃ sa bhagavān devo lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā  |
dharmasya paramaṃ guhyaṃ mamedaṃ sarvamuktavān  || 12.117 ||
sarvamātmani saṃpaśyetsaccāsacca samāhitaḥ  |
sarvaṃ hyātmani saṃpaśyannādharme kurute manaḥ  || 12.118 ||
ātmaiva devatāḥ sarvāḥ sarvamātmanyavasthitam  |
ātmā hi janayatyeṣāṃ karmayogaṃ śarīriṇām  || 12.119 ||
khaṃ saṃniveśayetkheṣu ceṣṭanasparśane'nilam  |
paktidṛṣṭyoḥ paraṃ tejaḥ snehe'po gāṃ ca mūrtiṣu  || 12.120 ||
manasīnduṃ diśaḥ śrotre krānte viṣṇuṃ bale haram  |
vācyagniṃ mitramutsarge prajane ca prajāpatim  || 12.121 ||
praśāsitāraṃ sarveṣāmaṇīyāṃsamaṇorapi  |
rukmābhaṃ svapnadhīgamyaṃ vidyāttaṃ puruṣaṃ param  || 12.122 ||
etameke vadantyagniṃ manumanye prajāpatim  |
indrameke pare prāṇamapare brahma śāśvatam  || 12.123 ||
eṣa sarvāṇi bhūtāni pañcabhirvyāpya mūrtibhiḥ  |
janmavṛddhikṣayairnityaṃ saṃsārayati cakravat || 12.124 ||
evaṃ yaḥ sarvabhūteṣu paśyatyātmānamātmanā  |
sa sarvasamatāmetya brahmābhyeti paraṃ padam  || 12.125 ||
ityetanmānavaṃ śāstraṃ bhṛguproktaṃ paṭhan dvijaḥ  |
bhavatyācāravānnityaṃ yatheṣṭāṃ prāpnuyādgatim  || 12.126 ||
